import {
  Color,
  JsonAsset,
  Material,
  Mesh,
  MeshRenderer,
  Node,
  Quat,
  Vec3,
  isValid,
  resources,
  utils,
} from 'cc';
import { AIRFRAME, WEAPONS } from './core/Data';
import { aircraftRotation, cabinPoint } from './core/Flight';
type Geometry = {
  positions: number[];
  normals: number[];
  colors: number[];
  indices: number[];
};
const RESOURCE = 'models/aircraft/cabin';
function validate(payload: unknown): Geometry {
  if (!payload || typeof payload !== 'object')
    throw Error('Expected a geometry object');
  const g = payload as Record<keyof Geometry, unknown>;
  if (
    !Array.isArray(g.positions) ||
    !Array.isArray(g.normals) ||
    !Array.isArray(g.colors) ||
    !Array.isArray(g.indices)
  )
    throw Error('positions, normals, colors and indices must be arrays');
  const { positions, normals, colors, indices } = g;
  const vertices = positions.length / 3;
  // Cocos 3.8.8 utils.createMesh writes 16-bit indices, as in World.mesh.
  if (!Number.isInteger(vertices) || vertices < 3 || vertices > 65535)
    throw Error('positions must contain 3..65535 complete XYZ vertices');
  if (normals.length !== positions.length || colors.length !== vertices * 4)
    throw Error('Expected one XYZ normal and one RGBA color per vertex');
  if (!indices.length || indices.length % 3 !== 0 || indices.length > 8000 * 3)
    throw Error('indices must contain 1..8000 complete triangles');
  for (const [name, values] of Object.entries({
    positions,
    normals,
    colors,
    indices,
  }))
    for (const value of values)
      if (
        typeof value !== 'number' ||
        !Number.isFinite(value) ||
        !Number.isFinite(Math.fround(value))
      )
        throw Error(`${name} must contain finite float32 numbers`);
  if (colors.some((v) => v < 0 || v > 1))
    throw Error('RGBA colors must be normalized to [0, 1]');
  if (indices.some((i) => !Number.isInteger(i) || i < 0 || i >= vertices))
    throw Error('indices must be integers within the vertex array');
  return { positions, normals, colors, indices };
}
/** Explicitly owned runtime model; the main World supplies the Simulation pose. */
export class AircraftModel {
  public readonly node: Node;
  public readonly ready: Promise<void>;
  public status: 'loading' | 'ready' | 'error' | 'disposed' = 'loading';
  public error = '';
  public triangleCount = 0;
  public direction: -1 | 1 = 1;
  private settleReady!: () => void;
  private mesh: Mesh | null = null;
  private material: Material | null = null;
  private renderer: MeshRenderer | null = null;
  private bay: Node;
  private weapon = 0;
  constructor(parent: Node) {
    this.node = new Node('asset:aircraft.cabin');
    this.bay = new Node('Port gun bay (metre-authored detail)');
    // The terrain sensor excludes near-field cabin geometry; muzzle transforms remain physical.
    this.bay.layer = 1 << 29;
    this.node.addChild(this.bay);
    this.bay.setScale(
      AIRFRAME.cabinScale,
      AIRFRAME.cabinScale,
      AIRFRAME.cabinScale,
    );
    this.setWeapon(0);
    // Always resolves, including failure/cancellation; callers must inspect status.
    this.ready = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
      this.settleReady = resolve;
    });
    this.node.once(Node.EventType.NODE_DESTROYED, this.finishDisposal, this);
    if (!isValid(parent, true)) {
      this.dispose();
      return;
    }
    try {
      parent.addChild(this.node);
      if (!this.alive()) return;
      resources.load(RESOURCE, JsonAsset, (error, asset) => {
        let retained: JsonAsset | null = null;
        try {
          // Also balance late callbacks: never force-release a shared cached asset.
          if (asset) {
            asset.addRef();
            retained = asset;
          }
          if (!this.alive()) return;
          if (error) throw error;
          if (!asset) throw Error('Loader returned no JsonAsset');
          const geometry = validate(asset.json);
          this.mesh = new Mesh();
          utils.createMesh(geometry, this.mesh, { calculateBounds: true });
          this.material = new Material();
          this.material.initialize({
            effectName: 'builtin-unlit',
            defines: { USE_VERTEX_COLOR: true },
          });
          this.material.setProperty('mainColor', Color.WHITE);
          this.renderer = this.bay.addComponent(MeshRenderer);
          this.renderer.mesh = this.mesh;
          this.renderer.setMaterial(this.material, 0);
          this.triangleCount = geometry.indices.length / 3;
          this.status = 'ready';
        } catch (cause) {
          this.fail(cause);
        } finally {
          // createMesh copies into its own buffers; the JSON is no longer needed.
          retained?.decRef();
          this.settleReady();
        }
      });
    } catch (cause) {
      this.fail(cause);
    }
  }
  /** Uses Flight's shared Ry(yaw) * Rx(-pitch) * Rz(bank); direction never mirrors port/starboard. */
  public update(
    position: { x: number; y: number; z: number; bank?: number },
    yaw: number,
    pitch: number,
    direction: -1 | 1,
    bank = position.bank ?? 0,
    weapon = 0,
  ): void {
    if (!this.alive()) return;
    this.direction = direction;
    this.setWeapon(weapon);
    this.node.setPosition(position.x, position.y, position.z);
    const q = aircraftRotation({ yaw, pitch, bank });
    this.node.setRotation(new Quat(q.x, q.y, q.z, q.w));
  }
  private setWeapon(weapon: number) {
    this.weapon = WEAPONS[weapon] ? weapon : 0;
    const origin = cabinPoint({ x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }, this.weapon);
    this.bay.setPosition(origin.x, origin.y, origin.z);
  }
  /** Includes the parent world transform; available during loading, null after disposal. */
  public get worldMuzzle(): Vec3 | null {
    if (!this.alive()) return null;
    const mount = WEAPONS[this.weapon].muzzle;
    return Vec3.transformMat4(
      new Vec3(),
      new Vec3(mount.x, mount.y, mount.z),
      this.node.worldMatrix,
    );
  }
  public dispose(): void {
    if (this.status === 'disposed') return;
    this.node.off(Node.EventType.NODE_DESTROYED, this.finishDisposal, this);
    this.finishDisposal();
    if (isValid(this.node, true)) this.node.destroy();
  }
  private alive(): boolean {
    if (this.status === 'disposed') return false;
    // Parent/ancestor destroy() is deferred; its children may still look valid this frame.
    for (let node: Node | null = this.node; node; node = node.parent)
      if (!isValid(node, true)) {
        this.dispose();
        return false;
      }
    return true;
  }
  private fail(cause: unknown): void {
    if (this.status === 'disposed') return;
    this.status = 'error';
    this.error = cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause);
    this.releaseGraphics();
    console.error(
      `[AircraftModel] Failed to load ${RESOURCE}: ${this.error}`,
      cause,
    );
    this.settleReady();
  }
  // A node destruction event only releases ownership; do not destroy an in-flight node again.
  private finishDisposal(): void {
    if (this.status === 'disposed') return;
    this.status = 'disposed';
    this.releaseGraphics();
    this.settleReady();
  }
  private releaseGraphics(): void {
    if (this.renderer && isValid(this.renderer, true)) {
      this.renderer.mesh = null;
      this.renderer.setMaterial(null, 0);
    }
    if (this.mesh && isValid(this.mesh, true)) this.mesh.destroy();
    if (this.material && isValid(this.material, true)) this.material.destroy();
    this.renderer = null;
    this.mesh = null;
    this.material = null;
    this.triangleCount = 0;
  }
}
import type {} from '../../scripts/dev-mode.js';
import type { Overwatch } from './Overwatch';
import { MISSIONS, type MissionId } from './core/MissionCatalog.ts';
/** Package mode and server capability are independent; all asset writes use the normal journal. */
export function installDeveloperTools(game: Overwatch) {
  const scope = globalThis as unknown as Window;
  const dev = scope.SmallGamesDev;
  const enabled = () =>
    scope.__SMALL_GAMES_BUILD__?.type === 'development' && dev?.isEnabled();
  if (!enabled()) return () => {};
  const ready = () => {
    if (!enabled()) throw Error('当前包未启用开发调试');
    if (
      game.sim.phase === 'playing' ||
      game.online?.room ||
      game.online?.active ||
      game.online?.busy ||
      game.quartermaster?.busy ||
      game.quartermaster?.viewing
    )
      throw Error('请先正常结束当前行动、离开联机房间并等待操作完成');
    const q = game.quartermaster;
    if (!q) throw Error('军需服务尚未就绪');
    return q;
  };
  const adjust = async (field: 'coinsDelta' | 'xpDelta', value?: number) => {
    const q = ready();
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || Math.abs(value!) > 1e9)
      throw Error('请输入 ±1000000000 范围内的整数');
    if (!q.assets?.devToolsEnabled)
      throw Error('服务器未启用开发调试，请连接已启用开发能力的测试服');
    if (!(await q.execute({ kind: 'dev-adjust', [field]: value })))
      throw Error(q.message[0] || '操作未完成');
  };
  const select = (id: MissionId, index: number) => {
    const q = ready();
    q.page = '';
    game.online?.show('');
    game.selectMission(id);
    game.hud.missionsOpen = id !== 'training-60';
    game.hud.missionPage = Math.floor(index / 3);
    game.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
  };
  const stopActions = dev.registerActions([
    {
      id: 'night-coins',
      label: '调整测试金币',
      input: {
        label: '金币增减（负数扣减）',
        min: -1e9,
        max: 1e9,
        step: 1,
        value: 1000,
      },
      run: (value) => adjust('coinsDelta', value),
    },
    {
      id: 'night-xp',
      label: '调整测试经验',
      input: {
        label: '经验增减（负数扣减）',
        min: -1e9,
        max: 1e9,
        step: 1,
        value: 200,
      },
      run: (value) => adjust('xpDelta', value),
    },
    {
      id: 'night-mission',
      label: '选择试玩任务',
      input: {
        label: `任务序号（1–${MISSIONS.length}）`,
        min: 1,
        max: MISSIONS.length,
        step: 1,
        value: 1,
      },
      run(value) {
        if (!Number.isInteger(value) || value! < 1 || value! > MISSIONS.length)
          throw Error('任务序号超出范围');
        select(MISSIONS[value! - 1].id, value! - 1);
      },
    },
    {
      id: 'night-training',
      label: '选择火控热身',
      run: () => select('training-60', 0),
    },
    {
      id: 'night-assets-refresh',
      label: '刷新服务端资产',
      async run() {
        const q = ready();
        await q.refresh();
        if (q.message[0]) throw Error(q.message[0]);
      },
    },
    {
      id: 'night-assets-retry',
      label: '重试待确认操作',
      async run() {
        const q = ready();
        if (!q.pending) throw Error('没有待确认的操作');
        if (!(await q.execute())) throw Error(q.message[0] || '操作未完成');
      },
    },
  ]);
  const stopSnapshot = dev.registerSnapshot(() => game.snapshot());
  return () => {
    stopActions();
    stopSnapshot();
  };
}
import { Color, Graphics, view } from 'cc';
import { FLIGHT, WEAPONS } from './core/Data';
import type { Point } from './core/Data';
import type { Simulation } from './core/Simulation';
import type { World } from './World';
type ScreenPoint = { x: number; y: number };
export type EffectsFrame = {
  smoke?: { id: number; age: number; kind: 'damage' | 'wreck' }[];
  fires?: { id: number; age: number; life: number }[];
  lights?: number[];
  impacts: {
    id: number;
    weapon: number;
    outcome?: string;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    radius: number;
    age: number;
    primitives: number;
  }[];
  projectiles: {
    id: number;
    weapon: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    width: number;
    length: number;
    speed: number;
  }[];
};
const TAU = Math.PI * 2;
const clamp = (n: number) => Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
/**
 * Append after Graphics.clear(), before target/aim markers; preserves their Graphics styles.
 * Budget: 16 rounds, 24 large / 8 small impacts, 8 attacks, 12 smoking vehicles.
 * With <=96px lobe radii and an estimated <=128 vertices/ellipse, allow ~46k FX vertices
 * including fixed-segment strokes/ribbons (Creator's default tessellation; estimate, not measured).
 */
export function drawEffects(
  g: Graphics,
  s: Simulation,
  world: World,
  width: number,
  height: number,
  reduced = false,
): EffectsFrame {
  const frame: EffectsFrame = {
    impacts: [],
    projectiles: [],
    smoke: [],
    fires: [],
    lights: [],
  };
  let primitives = 0;
  const fill = g.fillColor.clone(),
    stroke = g.strokeColor.clone(),
    lineWidth = g.lineWidth;
  const sx = view.getScaleX(),
    sy = view.getScaleY();
  const project = (p: Point, y = 0): ScreenPoint => {
    const q = world.project(p, y);
    return { x: q.x / sx - width / 2, y: q.y / sy - height / 2 };
  };
  const airborne = (p: { x: number; y: number; z: number }): ScreenPoint => {
    const q = world.projectAir(p);
    return { x: q.x / sx - width / 2, y: q.y / sy - height / 2 };
  };
  // Perspective size comes from each effect's ground location.
  const scaleAt = (p: Point) => {
    const base = project(p),
      x = project({ x: p.x + 1, z: p.z }),
      z = project({ x: p.x, z: p.z + 1 });
    // The X axis can point straight away from the camera; use both axes so the blast cannot collapse.
    return Math.min(
      30,
      Math.max(
        Math.hypot(x.x - base.x, x.y - base.y),
        Math.hypot(z.x - base.x, z.y - base.y),
      ),
    );
  };
  const ink = new Color();
  const color = (heat: number, alpha: number, smoke = false): Color => {
    heat = clamp(heat);
    const gray = Math.round(60 + heat * 195);
    ink.set(
      world.thermal ? gray : smoke ? 72 + heat * 35 : 255,
      world.thermal ? gray : smoke ? 75 + heat * 32 : 95 + heat * 140,
      world.thermal ? gray : smoke ? 79 + heat * 28 : 25 + heat * 175,
      Math.round(clamp(alpha) * 255),
    );
    return ink;
  };
  const blob = (
    p: ScreenPoint,
    rx: number,
    ry: number,
    heat: number,
    alpha: number,
    smoke = false,
  ) => {
    if (
      alpha <= 0.005 ||
      rx <= 0 ||
      ry <= 0 ||
      ![p.x, p.y, rx, ry].every(Number.isFinite)
    )
      return;
    // ponytail: cap billboard lobes at 96 UI pixels; subdivide them if giant close-up clouds are needed.
    rx = Math.min(96, rx);
    ry = Math.min(96, ry);
    if (Math.abs(p.x) > width / 2 + rx || Math.abs(p.y) > height / 2 + ry)
      return;
    g.fillColor = color(heat, alpha, smoke);
    g.ellipse(p.x, p.y, rx, ry);
    g.fill();
    primitives++;
  };
  const line = (
    a: ScreenPoint,
    b: ScreenPoint,
    heat: number,
    alpha: number,
    weight: number,
    friendly = false,
  ) => {
    if (![a.x, a.y, b.x, b.y].every(Number.isFinite)) return;
    if (
      Math.max(a.x, b.x) < -width / 2 ||
      Math.min(a.x, b.x) > width / 2 ||
      Math.max(a.y, b.y) < -height / 2 ||
      Math.min(a.y, b.y) > height / 2
    )
      return;
    g.strokeColor = color(heat, alpha);
    if (friendly && !world.thermal)
      g.strokeColor = new Color(145, 230, 203, Math.round(alpha * 255));
    g.lineWidth = weight;
    g.moveTo(a.x, a.y);
    g.lineTo(b.x, b.y);
    g.stroke();
    primitives++;
  };
  const ring = (
    p: Point,
    radius: number,
    heat: number,
    alpha: number,
    dashed = false,
  ) => {
    if (alpha <= 0.005) return;
    const q = project(p),
      x = project({ x: p.x + radius, z: p.z });
    const z = project({ x: p.x, z: p.z + radius });
    const extent = Math.max(
      Math.hypot(x.x - q.x, x.y - q.y),
      Math.hypot(z.x - q.x, z.y - q.y),
    );
    if (
      Math.abs(q.x) > width / 2 + extent ||
      Math.abs(q.y) > height / 2 + extent ||
      extent > 2000
    )
      return;
    g.strokeColor = color(heat, alpha);
    g.lineWidth = 1;
    // Project both ground axes, including temporary zoom; 24 segments also bound stroke geometry.
    for (let i = 0; i < (dashed ? 24 : 25); i++) {
      const a = (i * TAU) / 24;
      const px = q.x + Math.cos(a) * (x.x - q.x) + Math.sin(a) * (z.x - q.x);
      const py = q.y + Math.cos(a) * (x.y - q.y) + Math.sin(a) * (z.y - q.y);
      if (i === 0 || (dashed && i % 2 === 0)) g.moveTo(px, py);
      else g.lineTo(px, py);
    }
    if (!dashed) g.close();
    g.stroke();
    primitives++;
  };
  const patch = (points: ScreenPoint[], tint: Color) => {
    if (
      points.some(
        (p) =>
          !Number.isFinite(p.x + p.y) ||
          Math.abs(p.x) > width * 3 ||
          Math.abs(p.y) > height * 3,
      )
    )
      return;
    g.fillColor = tint;
    points.forEach((p, i) => (i ? g.lineTo(p.x, p.y) : g.moveTo(p.x, p.y)));
    g.close();
    g.fill();
  };
  for (const zone of s.fireZones) {
    const age = s.time - zone.born,
      left = zone.expires - s.time;
    if (age < 0 || left <= 0) continue;
    const fade = Math.min(1, left / 2),
      scale = scaleAt(zone);
    patch(
      Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, i) => {
        const angle = (i * TAU) / 24;
        return project(
          {
            x: zone.x + Math.cos(angle) * zone.radius,
            z: zone.z + Math.sin(angle) * zone.radius,
          },
          0.06,
        );
      }),
      world.thermal
        ? new Color(235, 235, 235, 45 * fade)
        : new Color(201, 69, 19, 80 * fade),
    );
    ring(zone, zone.radius, 0.45, 0.9 * fade);
    // Connected sheets keep the burning area readable without a field of isolated sparks.
    for (let band = 0; band < (reduced ? 2 : 3); band++) {
      const z = zone.z + (band - 1) * zone.radius * 0.52;
      const span = Math.sqrt(zone.radius ** 2 - (z - zone.z) ** 2) * 0.95;
      const base = [],
        crest = [];
      for (let i = 0; i <= 16; i++) {
        const t = i / 16,
          x = zone.x + (t * 2 - 1) * span;
        const edge = Math.sin(t * Math.PI),
          wave =
            0.65 +
            0.35 * Math.sin(t * Math.PI * 8 + age * (reduced ? 1.8 : 3) + band);
        base.push(project({ x, z: z + zone.radius * 0.32 }, 0.08));
        crest.push(project({ x, z }, 0.15 + edge * (1.5 + wave * 2)));
      }
      patch(
        [...base, ...crest.reverse()],
        world.thermal
          ? new Color(245, 245, 235, 165 * fade)
          : new Color(255, 94 + band * 37, 24, (155 + band * 20) * fade),
      );
    }
    frame.fires!.push({ id: zone.id, age, life: zone.expires - zone.born });
  }
  for (const u of s.units) {
    const at = project(u);
    if (Math.abs(at.x) > width / 2 + 40 || Math.abs(at.y) > height / 2 + 40)
      continue;
    const radius = u.kind === 'heavy' ? 0.5 : 0.35;
    for (let layer = 0; layer < 3; layer++) {
      const r = radius + layer * 0.14;
      patch(
        Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, j) => {
          const a = (j * TAU) / 12;
          return project(
            {
              x: u.x + 0.3 + Math.cos(a) * r,
              z: u.z + 0.25 + Math.sin(a) * r * 1.6,
            },
            0.012 + layer * 0.004,
          );
        }),
        new Color(3, 8, 12, world.thermal ? 12 : 28),
      );
    }
    if (
      u.hp <= 0 ||
      u.kind === 'turret' ||
      world.thermal ||
      world.light === 'day'
    )
      continue;
    const c = Math.cos(u.heading),
      sn = Math.sin(u.heading);
    const point = (side: number, forward: number, y = 0.025) =>
      project(
        {
          x: u.x + c * side + sn * forward,
          z: u.z - sn * side + c * forward,
        },
        y,
      );
    for (const side of [-0.16, 0.16]) {
      patch(
        [
          point(side - 0.06, 0.45),
          point(side - 0.75, 5.5),
          point(side + 0.75, 5.5),
          point(side + 0.06, 0.45),
        ],
        new Color(255, 222, 157, reduced ? 13 : 22),
      );
      patch(
        [
          point(side - 0.04, 0.45),
          point(side - 0.35, 2.7),
          point(side + 0.35, 2.7),
          point(side + 0.04, 0.45),
        ],
        new Color(255, 229, 171, 34),
      );
      const p = point(side, 0.48, 0.23);
      blob(p, 2.7, 2.3, 0.75, 0.18);
      blob(p, 1.1, 1, 1, 0.95);
    }
    frame.lights!.push(u.id);
  }
  // ponytail: inspect 64 units and render 12 plumes; spatial pooling if missions grow.
  let wrecks = 0;
  for (
    let i = s.units.length - 1, end = Math.max(0, s.units.length - 64);
    i >= end && wrecks < 12;
    i--
  ) {
    const u = s.units[i],
      dead = u.hp <= 0,
      age = dead ? s.time - u.deadAt : s.time;
    if (dead ? u.deadAt < 0 || age < 0 || age >= 35 : u.hp / u.maxHp > 0.55)
      continue;
    wrecks++;
    const scale = scaleAt(u);
    const fade = dead
      ? Math.min(1, age * 2) * Math.min(1, (35 - age) / 10)
      : 0.6;
    for (let j = reduced ? 0 : 3; j >= 0; j--) {
      const drift = (age * 0.35 + j * 0.7) % 3;
      const r = (0.16 + drift * 0.14) * scale;
      const p = project(
        { x: u.x + drift * 0.25 + Math.sin(u.id + j) * 0.08, z: u.z },
        0.3 + drift * 0.6,
      );
      blob(
        p,
        Math.max(1.5, r),
        Math.max(2, r * 1.2),
        dead ? 0.6 * Math.max(0, 1 - age / 30) : 0.3,
        fade * (1 - drift / 3) * 0.6,
        true,
      );
    }
    frame.smoke!.push({ id: u.id, age, kind: dead ? 'wreck' : 'damage' });
    if (dead && age < 18) {
      const p = project(u, 0.3),
        flicker = 0.75 + Math.sin(age * 13 + u.id) * 0.2;
      blob(
        p,
        Math.max(1.5, scale * 0.2),
        Math.max(3, scale * 0.5 * flicker),
        0.85,
        (1 - age / 18) * 0.9,
      );
      frame.fires!.push({ id: u.id, age, life: 18 });
    }
  }
  // Special weapons remain presentation-only; authoritative hits come from the simulation.
  for (const beam of s.lasers) {
    const unit = s.units.find((unit) => unit.id === beam.targetId);
    if (!unit || unit.hp <= 0) continue;
    const origin = airborne(beam.origin),
      target = airborne(unit),
      pulse = 0.82 + Math.sin(s.time * 36) * 0.12;
    if (!reduced) line(origin, target, 0.7, 0.25 * pulse, 7, true);
    line(origin, target, 1, pulse, reduced ? 1.5 : 2.5, true);
    blob(target, 5, 5, 1, pulse);
  }
  for (const drone of s.drones.slice(0, 6)) {
    const p = airborne(drone),
      size = Math.max(4, Math.min(12, scaleAt(drone) * 0.45)),
      falling = s.time >= drone.expires;
    const angle = falling ? (s.time - drone.expires) * 9 : 0,
      cos = Math.cos(angle),
      sin = Math.sin(angle);
    const rotor = (x: number, y: number) => ({
      x: p.x + x * cos - y * sin,
      y: p.y + x * sin + y * cos,
    });
    for (const sign of [-1, 1])
      line(
        rotor(-size, sign * size * 0.45),
        rotor(size, -sign * size * 0.45),
        0.8,
        0.9,
        2,
        true,
      );
    for (const dx of [-1, 1])
      for (const dy of [-1, 1]) {
        const r = rotor(dx * size, dy * size * 0.45);
        g.strokeColor = falling
          ? new Color(245, 168, 91, 210)
          : new Color(143, 222, 230, 210);
        g.lineWidth = 1;
        g.ellipse(r.x, r.y, size * 0.45, size * 0.15);
        g.stroke();
      }
    blob(p, 2, 2, 1, 0.9);
    if (falling)
      for (let i = 0; i < (reduced ? 1 : 3); i++)
        blob(
          { x: p.x + i * 2, y: p.y + size + i * 5 },
          3 + i,
          4 + i,
          0.25,
          0.5 - i * 0.12,
          true,
        );
    const target = s.units.find((u) => u.id === drone.target);
    if (
      !falling &&
      target &&
      s.time > drone.nextAttack - 0.12 &&
      s.time <= drone.nextAttack
    )
      line(p, project(target, target.y + 0.2), 1, 0.65, 1, true);
  }
  for (const unit of s.units)
    if ((unit.rootedUntil ?? 0) > s.time) {
      const p = project(unit, unit.y + 0.5),
        r = Math.max(5, Math.min(30, scaleAt(unit) * 0.8));
      g.strokeColor = new Color(112, 213, 255, 210);
      g.lineWidth = 1.5;
      g.ellipse(p.x, p.y, r, r * 0.45);
      g.stroke();
      if (!reduced) {
        g.moveTo(p.x - r * 0.3, p.y + r);
        g.lineTo(p.x + r * 0.2, p.y + r * 0.4);
        g.lineTo(p.x - r * 0.15, p.y);
        g.lineTo(p.x + r * 0.3, p.y - r * 0.6);
        g.stroke();
      }
    }
  // Every terrain impact explodes, including misses. Kill events use wreck smoke, not a second explosion.
  let attacks = 0;
  for (const e of [
    ...world.impactClouds,
    ...s.events.filter((event) => event.type === 'attack'),
  ]) {
    const age = s.time - e.time;
    if (age < 0) continue;
    if (e.type === 'attack') {
      if (age >= 0.3 || attacks++ >= 8) continue;
      const target = e.target || s.units.find((u) => u.id === e.unit);
      if (!target) continue;
      const a = project(e, 0.3),
        b = project(target, 0.25),
        t = clamp(age / 0.12);
      line(a, b, 0.45, (1 - age / 0.3) * 0.55, 1, e.friendly);
      blob(
        { x: a.x + (b.x - a.x) * t, y: a.y + (b.y - a.y) * t },
        2,
        2,
        1,
        1 - age / 0.3,
      );
      continue;
    }
    if (e.type !== 'impact') continue;
    const weapon = e.weapon,
      life = weapon === 0 ? 1.5 : weapon === 1 ? 10 : 16;
    if (age >= life) continue;
    const before = primitives;
    const scale = scaleAt(e);
    const radius = WEAPONS[weapon].radius;
    const burnLife =
      weapon === 0
        ? 0.35
        : e.outcome === 'destroyed'
          ? 8
          : weapon === 2
            ? 5
            : 2.8;
    const t = age / life,
      fade = 1 - t,
      seed = (e.id * 0.61803398875) % 1;
    const wave = clamp(age / (weapon === 0 ? 0.2 : 0.75));
    // The pressure ring stops at the gameplay blast radius; dust can drift beyond it.
    ring(e, radius * (0.2 + wave * 0.8), 0.9 - t * 0.65, (1 - wave) * 0.8);
    if (weapon > 0) {
      ring(e, radius * (0.12 + wave * 0.88), 0.5, (1 - wave) * 0.4);
      if (reduced) {
        const r = Math.max(1.5, radius * (0.15 + t * 0.3) * scale);
        blob(
          project(e, radius * 0.15),
          r,
          r * 0.6,
          fade * 0.5,
          fade * 0.45,
          true,
        );
      }
      const lobes = reduced ? 0 : weapon === 2 ? 5 : 3;
      // Cool outer dust behind fewer, smaller fire lobes. All motion is a function of event id and age.
      for (let j = lobes - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
        const a = seed * TAU + j * 2.39996323;
        const spread = radius * (0.1 + t * 0.55);
        const p = project(
          {
            x: e.x + Math.cos(a) * spread + age * 0.12,
            z: e.z + Math.sin(a) * spread,
          },
          radius * (0.08 + t * (0.65 + j * 0.08)),
        );
        const r = radius * (0.16 + t * 0.42 + (j % 2) * 0.05) * scale;
        blob(p, r, r * 1.12, fade * 0.55, fade * 0.48, true);
      }
      for (let j = 0; j < (reduced ? 0 : weapon === 2 ? 3 : 2); j++) {
        const a = seed * TAU + (j * TAU) / 3,
          burn = clamp(1 - age / burnLife);
        const p = project(
          {
            x: e.x + Math.cos(a) * radius * 0.2,
            z: e.z + Math.sin(a) * radius * 0.2,
          },
          radius * (0.1 + t * 0.7),
        );
        const r =
          radius *
          (0.16 + Math.sin(Math.min(1, t * 2) * Math.PI) * 0.2) *
          scale;
        blob(p, r, r * 1.1, burn, burn * 0.78);
      }
    } else {
      const p = project(e, 0.2);
      blob(p, (0.35 + age) * scale, (0.25 + age) * scale, fade, fade * 0.45);
    }
    if (weapon > 0 && age < burnLife) {
      const fire =
        (1 - age / burnLife) * (0.8 + Math.sin(age * 17 + e.id) * 0.15);
      const p = project(e, radius * 0.08);
      blob(
        p,
        Math.max(3, radius * 0.3 * scale),
        Math.max(2, radius * 0.13 * scale),
        0.65,
        fire * 0.24,
      );
      blob(
        p,
        Math.max(1.5, radius * 0.12 * scale),
        Math.max(3, radius * 0.28 * scale),
        0.95,
        fire * 0.9,
      );
      frame.fires!.push({ id: e.id, age, life: burnLife });
    }
    const flash = clamp(1 - age / (weapon === 2 ? 0.24 : 0.16));
    const core = Math.max(weapon === 0 ? 1 : 2, radius * 0.3 * scale);
    blob(project(e, 0.25), core, core, 1, flash * 0.9);
    const count = reduced ? 1 : weapon === 0 ? 3 : weapon === 1 ? 5 : 7;
    if (age < (weapon === 0 ? 0.28 : 0.85)) {
      for (let j = 0; j < count; j++) {
        const a = seed * TAU + j * 2.39996323;
        const speed = (1.7 + ((e.id + j * 13) % 7) * 0.22) * radius;
        const fragment = (time: number) =>
          project(
            {
              x: e.x + Math.cos(a) * speed * time,
              z: e.z + Math.sin(a) * speed * time,
            },
            Math.max(0, 0.15 + radius * 1.7 * time - 5 * time * time),
          );
        line(
          fragment(Math.max(0, age - 0.045)),
          fragment(age),
          fade,
          clamp(1 - age / (weapon === 0 ? 0.28 : 0.85)) * 0.9,
          weapon === 2 ? 1.6 : 1,
        );
      }
    }
    if (primitives > before) {
      const p = project(e);
      frame.impacts.push({
        id: e.id,
        weapon,
        outcome: e.outcome,
        x: p.x + width / 2,
        y: height / 2 - p.y,
        radius: radius * scale,
        age,
        primitives: primitives - before,
      });
    }
  }
  // ponytail: last 16 rounds cover current cadence; raise this budget if weapons gain simultaneous volleys.
  for (let i = Math.max(0, s.shots.length - 16); i < s.shots.length; i++) {
    const shot = s.shots[i],
      age = s.time - shot.born,
      flight = shot.due - shot.born;
    if (flight <= 0 || age < 0 || age >= flight) continue;
    const t = clamp(age / flight),
      weapon = shot.weapon,
      spec = WEAPONS[weapon],
      missile = shot.guidance?.kind === 'consumable';
    const position = (progress: number): ScreenPoint =>
      airborne(s.shotPosition(shot, shot.born + progress * flight));
    if (!missile)
      ring(
        shot,
        WEAPONS[weapon].radius * (1 + (1 - t) * 0.65),
        0.75,
        0.22 + t * 0.25,
        true,
      );
    // A single tapered ribbon per round, seven samples along the actual origin-to-impact parabola.
    const tail: ScreenPoint[] = [];
    const start = Math.max(0, t - (missile ? 0.45 : spec.tracerTime) / flight);
    for (let j = 0; j <= 6; j++)
      tail.push(position(start + ((t - start) * j) / 6));
    if (
      tail.some(
        (p) =>
          !Number.isFinite(p.x) ||
          !Number.isFinite(p.y) ||
          Math.abs(p.x) > width * 3 ||
          Math.abs(p.y) > height * 3,
      )
    )
      continue;
    const location = s.shotPosition(shot),
      projected = airborne(location);
    const localScale = Math.min(
      200,
      Math.max(
        ...[
          { x: location.x + 1, y: location.y, z: location.z },
          { x: location.x, y: location.y + 1, z: location.z },
          { x: location.x, y: location.y, z: location.z + 1 },
        ].map((p) => {
          const q = airborne(p);
          return Math.hypot(q.x - projected.x, q.y - projected.y);
        }),
      ),
    );
    // Keep distant rounds readable in UI pixels without changing their ballistic path.
    const bodyWidth = missile
      ? 5
      : Math.min(5, Math.max(1.5 + weapon * 0.65, spec.calibre * localScale));
    const bodyLength = missile
      ? 12
      : Math.min(12, Math.max(4 + weapon * 1.5, spec.length * localScale));
    g.fillColor = color(0.8, 0.7);
    for (let side = 0; side < 2; side++) {
      for (let n = 0; n <= 6; n++) {
        const j = side === 0 ? n : 6 - n,
          p = tail[j];
        const a = tail[Math.max(0, j - 1)],
          b = tail[Math.min(6, j + 1)];
        const length = Math.max(0.001, Math.hypot(b.x - a.x, b.y - a.y));
        const w = (0.08 + (j / 6) * bodyWidth * 0.5) * (side === 0 ? 1 : -1);
        const x = p.x - ((b.y - a.y) / length) * w,
          y = p.y + ((b.x - a.x) / length) * w;
        if (side === 0 && n === 0) g.moveTo(x, y);
        else g.lineTo(x, y);
      }
    }
    g.close();
    g.fill();
    const head = tail[6],
      last = tail[5],
      heading = Math.atan2(head.y - last.y, head.x - last.x);
    const beforeHead = primitives;
    const body = (along: number, across: number) => ({
      x: head.x + Math.cos(heading) * along - Math.sin(heading) * across,
      y: head.y + Math.sin(heading) * along + Math.cos(heading) * across,
    });
    // A pointed nose and tapered tail remain readable even when a round is only a few pixels long.
    patch(
      [
        body(1, 0),
        body(-bodyLength * 0.32, bodyWidth * 0.5),
        body(-bodyLength * 0.82, bodyWidth * 0.5),
        body(-bodyLength, bodyWidth * 0.24),
        body(-bodyLength, -bodyWidth * 0.24),
        body(-bodyLength * 0.82, -bodyWidth * 0.5),
        body(-bodyLength * 0.32, -bodyWidth * 0.5),
      ],
      color(0.85, 0.98).clone(),
    );
    primitives++;
    if (missile)
      for (const side of [-1, 1])
        patch(
          [
            body(-bodyLength * 0.6, side * bodyWidth * 0.3),
            body(-bodyLength, side * bodyWidth),
            body(-bodyLength * 0.9, 0),
          ],
          color(0.5, 0.9).clone(),
        );
    line(
      body(-bodyLength * 0.75, -bodyWidth * 0.12),
      body(-bodyLength * 0.25, -bodyWidth * 0.12),
      1,
      0.8,
      Math.max(0.6, bodyWidth * 0.2),
    );
    if (primitives > beforeHead)
      frame.projectiles.push({
        id: shot.id,
        weapon,
        x: head.x + width / 2,
        y: height / 2 - head.y,
        width: bodyWidth,
        length: bodyLength,
        speed:
          (shot.timeScale ?? 1) *
          Math.hypot(
            shot.velocity.x,
            shot.velocity.y -
              (shot.guidance
                ? 0
                : FLIGHT.gravity * age * (shot.timeScale ?? 1)),
            shot.velocity.z,
          ),
      });
    const flash = shot.timeScale ? 0 : clamp(1 - age / 0.085);
    if (flash > 0) {
      const origin = airborne(shot.origin);
      blob(origin, 7 + weapon * 3, 5 + weapon * 2, 0.65, flash * 0.35);
      blob(origin, 2.5 + weapon, 2.5 + weapon, 1, flash);
    }
  }
  g.fillColor = fill;
  g.strokeColor = stroke;
  g.lineWidth = lineWidth;
  return frame;
}

const C = {
  ink: '#081725f5',
  line: '#456780',
  white: '#e5edf3',
  dim: '#9bb4c8',
  mint: '#82c9ed',
  amber: '#e5b65c',
  action: '#ffe3a4',
  red: '#ff525b',
};
const col = (hex: string) => new Color().fromHEX(hex);
const movableControls = [
  'weapon0',
  'weapon1',
  'weapon2',
  'weapon3',
  'homing',
  'weapon4',
  'skillUse',
  'weapon5',
];
type ControlPosition = { x: number; y: number };
export type ButtonRect = {
  id: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  w: number;
  h: number;
  label: Label;
  visual?: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number };
  disabled?: boolean;
  clip?: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number };
};
export class HUD {
  listAreas: Array<{
    key: string;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    w: number;
    h: number;
    max: number;
    axis: 'x' | 'y';
  }> = [];
  listScroll: Record<string, number> = {};
  missionFocus: number | undefined;
  safeRevision = 0;
  private nameFits = new Map<string, string>();
  nativeCapsule = false;
  private semanticKey = '';
  listAt(x: number, y: number) {
    return [...this.listAreas]
      .reverse()
      .find((a) => x >= a.x && x <= a.x + a.w && y >= a.y && y <= a.y + a.h);
  }
  moveList(key: string, amount: number) {
    const a = this.listAreas.find((a) => a.key === key);
    if (!a) return;
    const states = key.startsWith('qm:')
      ? this.quartermaster!.scrolls
      : this.listScroll;
    states[key] = Math.max(0, Math.min(a.max, (states[key] || 0) + amount));
    this.modalKey = 'rebuild';
  }
  listViewport(
    parent: Node,
    key: string,
    area: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number },
    total: number,
    axis: 'x' | 'y' = 'y',
  ) {
    const max = Math.max(0, total - (axis === 'x' ? area.w : area.h)),
      states = key.startsWith('qm:')
        ? this.quartermaster!.scrolls
        : this.listScroll;
    const offset = (states[key] = Math.max(0, Math.min(max, states[key] || 0)));
    this.listAreas.push({ ...area, key, max, axis });
    const viewport = this.node('Scroll:' + key, parent),
      mx = area.x + area.w / 2 - this.w / 2,
      my = this.h / 2 - area.y - area.h / 2;
    viewport.setPosition(mx, my);
    viewport.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(area.w, area.h);
    viewport.addComponent(Mask).type = Mask.Type.GRAPHICS_RECT;
    const n = this.node('ScrollContent', viewport);
    n.setPosition(-mx, -my);
    const surfaces = this.node('Surfaces', n),
      g = this.node('Ink', n).addComponent(Graphics),
      start = this.buttons.length;
    return {
      n,
      surfaces,
      g,
      offset,
      finish: () => {
        for (const b of this.buttons.slice(start)) b.clip = area;
        if (max && axis === 'x') {
          const ink = this.node('ScrollIndicator', parent).addComponent(
              Graphics,
            ),
            track = area.w - 32,
            thumb = Math.max(20, (track * area.w) / total),
            y = area.y + area.h - 5;
          this.rect(ink, area.x + 16, y, track, 3, '#253e50');
          this.rect(
            ink,
            area.x + 16 + ((track - thumb) * offset) / max,
            y - 1,
            thumb,
            5,
            C.amber,
          );
          if (offset > 1)
            this.label(
              parent,
              '‹',
              area.x + 5,
              area.y + area.h / 2,
              25,
              14,
              32,
              C.white,
            );
          if (offset < max - 1)
            this.label(
              parent,
              '›',
              area.x + area.w - 5,
              area.y + area.h / 2,
              25,
              14,
              32,
              C.white,
            );
        }
        if (max && axis === 'y') {
          const ink = this.node('ScrollIndicator', parent).addComponent(
              Graphics,
            ),
            x = area.x + area.w - 5,
            track = Math.max(4, area.h - 32),
            thumb = Math.max(20, (track * area.h) / total);
          this.rect(ink, x, area.y + 16, 3, track, '#253e50');
          this.rect(
            ink,
            x - 1,
            area.y + 16 + ((track - thumb) * offset) / max,
            5,
            thumb,
            C.amber,
          );
          const arrow = (y: number, down: boolean) => {
            ink.strokeColor = col(C.white);
            ink.lineWidth = 2;
            const xx = x - this.w / 2,
              yy = this.h / 2 - y,
              d = down ? -1 : 1;
            ink.moveTo(xx - 4, yy - d * 2);
            ink.lineTo(xx, yy + d * 2);
            ink.lineTo(xx + 4, yy - d * 2);
            ink.stroke();
          };
          if (offset > 1) arrow(area.y + 6, false);
          if (offset < max - 1) arrow(area.y + area.h - 7, true);
        }
      },
    };
  }
  quartermaster?: Quartermaster;
  progressionArt: ProgressionArt;
  private progressionRevision = '';
  root: Node;
  camera: Camera;
  g: Graphics;
  marks: Graphics;
  globalControls: Node;
  globalGraphics: Graphics;
  homeBackground: Node;
  homeArtwork: Sprite;
  private titleArtwork?: SpriteFrame;
  private missionArtwork: SpriteFrame[] = [];
  private dayArtwork: SpriteFrame[] = [];
  private artworkFrames: SpriteFrame[] = [];
  w = 960;
  h = 540;
  lang: Language = 'zh';
  radioText = '';
  touch =
    sys.isMobile ||
    (sys.isBrowser && window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches);
  // UI coordinates from real mouse input; the controller clears this on leave/touch/blur.
  mousePointer?: { x: number; y: number };
  pressedButton = '';
  private uiFrames = new Map<string, SpriteFrame>();
  private artworkRetries = new Map<string, () => void>();
  private surfaces?: Node;
  retryArtwork() {
    this.progressionArt.retry();
    for (const [name, retry] of Array.from(this.artworkRetries)) {
      this.artworkRetries.delete(name);
      retry();
    }
    this.modalKey = 'rebuild';
  }
  get interfaceArtworkReady() {
    return this.quartermaster
      ? this.progressionArt.homeReady
      : this.uiFrames.size === 3 &&
          !!this.titleArtwork &&
          this.missionArtwork.length === 3 &&
          this.dayArtwork.length === 3;
  }
  effects: EffectsFrame = { impacts: [], projectiles: [] };
  buttons: ButtonRect[] = [];
  labels = new Map<string, Label>();
  modal?: Node;
  modalKey = '';
  private onlineRevision = -1;
  private modalFamily = '';
  screenControl = false;
  screenActive = false;
  missionsOpen = false;
  online?: Online;
  reportOpen = false;
  equipmentOpen = false;
  timeComparison?: TimeComparison;
  missionPage = 0;
  missionProgress: MissionProgress = newMissionProgress();
  developer = false;
  helpGroup: 'basic' | 'advanced' | 'rules' = 'basic';
  helpTouch = false;
  scroll = 0;
  scrollMax = 0;
  scrollNode?: Node;
  scrollArea = { x: 0, y: 0, w: 0, h: 0 };
  tutorial = true;
  muted = false;
  voicePrompts = true;
  autoPriority = true;
  reducedEffects = false;
  toolsOpen = false;
  zoomOpen = false;
  zoomLimit = 10;
  rewardsAvailable = false;
  supplyOpen = false;
  exitConfirm = false;
  supplySelection: SupplyReward = 'tracking';
  supplyMessage = '';
  pendingSupply = false;
  advert?: { kind: RewardKind; mock: boolean };
  lockedTargets: { id: number; x: number; y: number }[] = [];
  toast = '';
  toastUntil = 0;
  trainingBest?: TrainingRecord;
  unitLabels = new Map<number, Label>();
  lastHelpLine = '';
  safe = { left: 0, right: 0, top: 0, bottom: 0 };
  controlPositions: Record<string, ControlPosition> = {};
  controlBounds(id: string) {
    const b = this.buttons.find((b) => b.id === id)!;
    return {
      x: this.safe.left,
      y: this.safe.top,
      w: Math.max(0, this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - b.w),
      h: Math.max(0, this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - b.h),
    };
  }
  private positionControl(b: ButtonRect, x: number, y: number, width = b.w) {
    b.x = x;
    b.y = y;
    b.w = width;
    b.visual = { x: x + 2, y: y + 2, w: width - 4, h: b.h - 4 };
    b.label.node.setPosition(
      x + width / 2 - this.w / 2,
      this.h / 2 - y - b.h / 2,
    );
    b.label.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(width - 8, b.h - 2);
    b.label.overflow = Label.Overflow.SHRINK;
  }
  placeControl(id: string, position?: ControlPosition) {
    if (position)
      this.controlPositions[id] = {
        x: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, position.x)),
        y: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, position.y)),
      };
    else delete this.controlPositions[id];
    this.layoutCombatControls();
  }
  controlAt(x: number, y: number) {
    const button = this.hit(x, y, true);
    return button && movableControls.includes(button.id) ? button : undefined;
  }
  private avoidFixedControls(id: string) {
    const b = this.buttons.find((b) => b.id === id)!,
      bounds = this.controlBounds(id);
    if (!b.label.node.activeInHierarchy) return;
    for (const fixed of this.buttons
      .filter(
        (b) =>
          !movableControls.includes(b.id) &&
          !b.disabled &&
          b.label.node.activeInHierarchy,
      )
      .sort((a, b) => b.x - a.x)) {
      if (
        b.x >= fixed.x + fixed.w ||
        b.x + b.w <= fixed.x ||
        b.y >= fixed.y + fixed.h ||
        b.y + b.h <= fixed.y
      )
        continue;
      const above = fixed.y > this.h / 2 && fixed.y - b.h - 4 >= bounds.y;
      const x =
        !above && fixed.x - b.w - 4 >= bounds.x ? fixed.x - b.w - 4 : b.x;
      const y = above
        ? fixed.y - b.h - 4
        : x !== b.x
          ? b.y
          : Math.min(bounds.y + bounds.h, fixed.y + fixed.h + 4);
      this.positionControl(b, x, y);
      this.controlPositions[id] = {
        x: (x - bounds.x) / Math.max(1, bounds.w),
        y: (y - bounds.y) / Math.max(1, bounds.h),
      };
    }
  }
  private layoutCombatControls() {
    const left = 12 + this.safe.left,
      right = this.w - 12 - this.safe.right,
      by = this.h - this.footer - this.safe.bottom;
    const normal = this.buttons.filter((b) =>
      movableControls.slice(0, 5).includes(b.id),
    );
    const support = ['weapon4', 'skillUse', 'weapon5']
      .map((id) => this.buttons.find((b) => b.id === id)!)
      .filter((b) => b.label.node.activeInHierarchy);
    const visible = normal.filter((b) => b.label.node.activeInHierarchy),
      fire = this.buttons.find((b) => b.id === 'fire')!;
    const cardW = Math.min(
      this.compact ? 64 : 78,
      (right - left - 146) / 5 - 6,
    );
    let regularW = cardW,
      supportW = 100;
    if (support.length) {
      const room = fire.x - left - 12,
        gaps =
          Math.max(0, visible.length - 1) * 6 + 8 + (support.length - 1) * 6;
      supportW = Math.min(
        100,
        Math.max(72, (room - visible.length * cardW - gaps) / support.length),
      );
      regularW = Math.max(
        44,
        Math.min(
          cardW,
          (room - support.length * supportW - gaps) /
            Math.max(1, visible.length),
        ),
      );
    }
    normal.forEach((b, index) =>
      this.positionControl(b, left + index * (cardW + 6), by + 8, cardW),
    );
    if (support.length)
      visible.forEach((b, index) =>
        this.positionControl(
          b,
          left + index * (regularW + 6),
          by + 8,
          regularW,
        ),
      );
    const dockX = left + visible.length * (regularW + 6) + 2;
    support.forEach((b, index) =>
      this.positionControl(b, dockX + index * (supportW + 6), by + 8, supportW),
    );
    const state = this.labels.get('weaponState')!,
      stateW = Math.max(
        60,
        Math.min(132, right - 68 - (left + 5 * (cardW + 6)) - 12),
      );
    const crowded =
      support.length > 0 &&
      dockX + support.length * (supportW + 6) - 6 > fire.x - stateW - 16;
    state.node.setPosition(
      (crowded ? right - stateW / 2 : fire.x - 8 - stateW / 2) - this.w / 2,
      this.h / 2 -
        (crowded ? fire.y - 8 - (this.footer - 14) / 2 : fire.y + fire.h / 2),
    );
    state.node
      .getComponent(UITransform)!
      .setContentSize(stateW, this.footer - 14);
    for (const id of movableControls) {
      const position = this.controlPositions[id];
      if (!position) continue;
      const bounds = this.controlBounds(id),
        b = this.buttons.find((b) => b.id === id)!;
      this.positionControl(
        b,
        bounds.x + bounds.w * position.x,
        bounds.y + bounds.h * position.y,
      );
      this.avoidFixedControls(id);
    }
  }
  get compact() {
    return this.h < 500;
  }
  get footer() {
    return 60;
  }
  get desktop() {
    return !this.compact && this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right >= 900;
  }
  get mouseDesktop() {
    return this.desktop && !this.touch;
  }
  get editing() {
    return (
      this.modal
        ?.getComponentsInChildren(EditBox)
        .some((field) => field.isFocused()) ?? false
    );
  }
  get minimapLayout() {
    const w = this.compact ? 112 : 156;
    return {
      x: this.w - 12 - this.safe.right - w,
      y: this.safe.top + 64,
      w,
      h: this.compact ? 84 : 108,
    };
  }
  minimapPoint(x: number, y: number): Point | undefined {
    if (this.modal || ![x, y].every(Number.isFinite)) return;
    const map = this.minimapLayout,
      left = map.x + 8,
      top = map.y + 25,
      width = map.w - 16,
      height = map.h - 33;
    if (x < left || x > left + width || y < top || y > top + height) return;
    return {
      x: ((x - left) / width) * MAP.halfWidth * 2 - MAP.halfWidth,
      z: ((y - top) / height) * MAP.halfDepth * 2 - MAP.halfDepth,
    };
  }
  constructor(parent: Node) {
    this.root = new Node('OverwatchHUD');
    parent.addChild(this.root);
    this.root.layer = Layers.Enum.UI_2D;
    this.progressionArt = new ProgressionArt(this);
    const unwatch = watchSafeArea(() => this.safeRevision++);
    this.root.once(Node.EventType.NODE_DESTROYED, unwatch);
    this.root.once(Node.EventType.NODE_DESTROYED, () =>
      this.progressionArt.destroy(),
    );
    this.root.once(Node.EventType.NODE_DESTROYED, () => {
      for (const frame of this.artworkFrames) frame.destroy();
    });
    this.root.addComponent(UITransform);
    const canvas = this.root.addComponent(Canvas),
      cn = new Node('UICamera');
    parent.addChild(cn);
    this.camera = cn.addComponent(Camera);
    this.camera.projection = Camera.ProjectionType.ORTHO;
    this.camera.near = 0.1;
    this.camera.far = 2000;
    this.camera.priority = 10;
    this.camera.clearFlags = Camera.ClearFlag.DEPTH_ONLY;
    this.camera.visibility = Layers.Enum.UI_2D;
    canvas.cameraComponent = this.camera;
    this.homeBackground = this.node('HomeCover', this.root);
    this.homeBackground.addComponent(Graphics);
    this.homeArtwork = this.node(
      'HomeArtwork',
      this.homeBackground,
    ).addComponent(Sprite);
    this.homeArtwork.sizeMode = Sprite.SizeMode.CUSTOM;
    // These are original unlettered illustrations and a separate transparent wordmark.
    // All mission data, interaction and status text remain live Cocos UI.
    for (const asset of ['title-metal', 'mission-atlas', 'mission-day-atlas']) {
      const load = () =>
        resources.load(
          'ui/' + asset + '/texture',
          Texture2D,
          (error, texture) => {
            if (!isValid(this.root)) return;
            if (error) {
              this.artworkRetries.set(asset, load);
              if (!this.editing) this.modalKey = 'rebuild';
              return;
            }
            const count = asset === 'title-metal' ? 1 : 3;
            for (let index = 0; index < count; index++) {
              const frame = new SpriteFrame();
              frame.texture = texture;
              if (count === 3)
                frame.rect = new Rect(
                  (index * texture.width) / 3,
                  0,
                  texture.width / 3,
                  texture.height,
                );
              this.artworkFrames.push(frame);
              if (asset === 'mission-day-atlas') this.dayArtwork.push(frame);
              else if (count === 3) this.missionArtwork.push(frame);
              else this.titleArtwork = frame;
            }
            if (!this.editing) this.modalKey = 'rebuild';
          },
        );
      load();
    }
    for (const name of ['panel', 'button', 'insignia']) {
      const load = () =>
        resources.load(`ui/${name}/texture`, Texture2D, (error, texture) => {
          if (!isValid(this.root)) return;
          if (error) {
            this.artworkRetries.set(name, load);
            if (!this.editing) this.modalKey = 'rebuild';
            return;
          }
          const frame = new SpriteFrame();
          frame.texture = texture;
          if (name !== 'insignia') {
            const edge = Math.round(
              Math.min(texture.width, texture.height) * 0.065,
            );
            frame.insetLeft =
              frame.insetRight =
              frame.insetTop =
              frame.insetBottom =
                edge;
          }
          this.uiFrames.set(name, frame);
          this.artworkFrames.push(frame);
          if (!this.editing) this.modalKey = 'rebuild';
        });
      load();
    }
    this.g = this.node('Backdrop', this.root).addComponent(Graphics);
    this.marks = this.node('Telemetry', this.root).addComponent(Graphics);
    this.globalControls = this.node('GlobalControls', this.root);
    this.globalGraphics = this.globalControls.addComponent(Graphics);
    this.resize();
  }
  t(zh: string, en: string) {
    return this.lang === 'zh' ? zh : en;
  }
  node(name: string, parent: Node) {
    const n = new Node(name);
    n.layer = Layers.Enum.UI_2D;
    parent.addChild(n);
    n.addComponent(UITransform);
    return n;
  }
  resize() {
    this.mousePointer = undefined;
    this.pressedButton = '';
    const size = view.getFrameSize();
    this.w = size.width;
    this.h = size.height;
    view.setDesignResolutionSize(this.w, this.h, ResolutionPolicy.EXACT_FIT);
    const rect = sys.getSafeAreaRect(false);
    const merged = safeArea(this.w, this.h, {
      left: Math.max(0, rect.x),
      right: Math.max(0, this.w - rect.x - rect.width),
      bottom: Math.max(0, rect.y),
      top: Math.max(0, this.h - rect.y - rect.height),
    });
    this.nativeCapsule = merged.capsule;
    this.safe = {
      left: merged.left,
      right: merged.right,
      top: merged.top,
      bottom: merged.bottom,
    };
    this.root.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(this.w, this.h);
    this.root.setPosition(this.w / 2, this.h / 2, 0);
    this.camera.node.setPosition(this.w / 2, this.h / 2, 1000);
    this.camera.orthoHeight = this.h / 2;
    for (const b of this.buttons) b.label.node.destroy();
    this.buttons = [];
    for (const l of Array.from(this.labels.values())) l.node.destroy();
    this.labels.clear();
    for (const l of Array.from(this.unitLabels.values())) l.node.destroy();
    this.unitLabels.clear();
    this.modal?.destroy();
    this.modal = undefined;
    this.modalKey = '';
    this.buildControls();
  }
  label(
    parent: Node,
    str: string,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    size: number,
    width: number,
    height: number,
    hex = C.white,
  ) {
    const n = this.node(str.slice(0, 20), parent);
    n.setPosition(x - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y);
    n.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(width, height);
    const l = n.addComponent(Label);
    l.string = str;
    l.fontFamily = 'Microsoft YaHei';
    l.fontSize = size;
    l.lineHeight = size * 1.45;
    l.color = col(hex);
    l.overflow = Label.Overflow.CLAMP;
    l.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.CENTER;
    l.verticalAlign = Label.VerticalAlign.CENTER;
    return l;
  }
  button(
    id: string,
    str: string,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h = 44,
    parent = this.root,
  ) {
    const l = this.label(
      parent,
      str,
      x + w / 2,
      y + h / 2,
      this.compact ? 13 : 14,
      w - 8,
      h - 2,
    );
    this.buttons.push({
      id,
      x,
      y,
      w,
      h,
      label: l,
      visual: { x: x + 2, y: y + 2, w: w - 4, h: h - 4 },
    });
    return l;
  }
  artwork(
    name: string,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
    tone?: string,
  ) {
    const frame = this.uiFrames.get(name);
    if (!frame || !this.surfaces) return false;
    let parent = this.surfaces;
    if (name === 'button') {
      const crop = this.node('ButtonSilhouette', parent);
      crop.setPosition(x + w / 2 - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y - h / 2);
      crop.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(w, h);
      const mask = crop.addComponent(Mask);
      mask.type = Mask.Type.GRAPHICS_STENCIL;
      const silhouette =
        (mask.subComp as Graphics) ||
        crop.getComponent(Graphics) ||
        crop.addComponent(Graphics);
      const cut = Math.min(8, h * 0.18),
        l = -w / 2,
        r = w / 2,
        t = h / 2,
        b = -h / 2;
      silhouette.clear();
      silhouette.fillColor = col('#ffffff');
      silhouette.moveTo(l + cut, t);
      silhouette.lineTo(r - cut, t);
      silhouette.lineTo(r, t - cut);
      silhouette.lineTo(r, b + cut);
      silhouette.lineTo(r - cut, b);
      silhouette.lineTo(l + cut, b);
      silhouette.lineTo(l, b + cut);
      silhouette.lineTo(l, t - cut);
      silhouette.close();
      silhouette.fill();
      parent = crop;
    }
    const n = this.node('Skin:' + name, parent),
      sprite = n.addComponent(Sprite);
    const scale = name === 'button' ? 0.18 : name === 'panel' ? 0.2 : 1;
    n.setPosition(
      name === 'button' ? 0 : x + w / 2 - this.w / 2,
      name === 'button' ? 0 : this.h / 2 - y - h / 2,
    );
    n.setScale(scale, scale, 1);
    sprite.sizeMode = Sprite.SizeMode.CUSTOM;
    sprite.type = name === 'insignia' ? Sprite.Type.SIMPLE : Sprite.Type.SLICED;
    sprite.spriteFrame = frame;
    n.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(w / scale, h / scale);
    sprite.color = col(tone || (name === 'panel' ? '#b6c6d4' : '#ffffff'));
    return true;
  }
  controlIcon(
    g: Graphics,
    kind: string,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    size = 18,
    color = C.dim,
  ) {
    const cx = x - this.w / 2,
      cy = this.h / 2 - y,
      k = size / 20;
    const line = (points: number[][], close = false) => {
      points.forEach(([xx, yy], i) =>
        i
          ? g.lineTo(cx + xx * k, cy - yy * k)
          : g.moveTo(cx + xx * k, cy - yy * k),
      );
      if (close) g.close();
      g.stroke();
    };
    g.strokeColor = col(color);
    g.lineWidth = 1.5;
    if (kind === 'close') {
      line([
        [-5, -5],
        [5, 5],
      ]);
      line([
        [5, -5],
        [-5, 5],
      ]);
    } else if (kind === 'back' || kind === 'next')
      line(
        kind === 'back'
          ? [
              [3, -6],
              [-3, 0],
              [3, 6],
            ]
          : [
              [-3, -6],
              [3, 0],
              [-3, 6],
            ],
      );
    else if (kind === 'plus') {
      line([
        [-7, 0],
        [7, 0],
      ]);
      line([
        [0, -7],
        [0, 7],
      ]);
    } else if (kind === 'time' || kind === 'history') {
      g.circle(cx, cy, 8 * k);
      g.stroke();
      line([
        [0, -5],
        [0, 0],
        [4, 2],
      ]);
      if (kind === 'time')
        line([
          [-3, -11],
          [3, -11],
        ]);
    } else if (kind === 'star') {
      const points = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => {
        const a = -Math.PI / 2 + (i * Math.PI) / 5,
          r = i % 2 ? 4 : 9;
        return [Math.cos(a) * r, Math.sin(a) * r];
      });
      line(points, true);
    } else if (kind === 'guide') {
      line([
        [0, 7],
        [0, -7],
        [-8, -9],
        [-8, 5],
        [0, 7],
        [8, 5],
        [8, -9],
        [0, -7],
      ]);
    } else if (kind === 'profile') {
      g.circle(cx, cy + 4 * k, 4 * k);
      g.stroke();
      line([
        [-8, 8],
        [-7, 3],
        [-3, 0],
        [3, 0],
        [7, 3],
        [8, 8],
      ]);
    } else if (kind === 'ranking') {
      line([
        [-7, 8],
        [-7, 0],
        [-3, 0],
        [-3, 8],
      ]);
      line([
        [-2, 8],
        [-2, -7],
        [2, -7],
        [2, 8],
      ]);
      line([
        [3, 8],
        [3, -3],
        [7, -3],
        [7, 8],
      ]);
    } else if (kind === 'mission')
      line([
        [-9, -6],
        [-3, -9],
        [3, -6],
        [9, -9],
        [9, 6],
        [3, 9],
        [-3, 6],
        [-9, 9],
        [-9, -6],
        [-3, -9],
        [-3, 6],
        [3, 9],
        [3, -6],
      ]);
    else if (kind === 'lobby') {
      line([
        [-7, 8],
        [6, -8],
        [8, -3],
        [3, -2],
      ]);
      line([
        [7, 8],
        [-6, -8],
        [-8, -3],
        [-3, -2],
      ]);
    } else if (kind === 'sound' || kind === 'muted') {
      line(
        [
          [-8, -3],
          [-4, -3],
          [1, -7],
          [1, 7],
          [-4, 3],
          [-8, 3],
        ],
        true,
      );
      if (kind === 'sound')
        line([
          [5, -5],
          [8, 0],
          [5, 5],
        ]);
      else {
        line([
          [4, -4],
          [10, 4],
        ]);
        line([
          [10, -4],
          [4, 4],
        ]);
      }
    } else if (kind === 'language') {
      g.circle(cx, cy, 8 * k);
      g.stroke();
      line([
        [-8, 0],
        [8, 0],
      ]);
      line([
        [0, -8],
        [-3, -3],
        [-3, 3],
        [0, 8],
        [3, 3],
        [3, -3],
        [0, -8],
      ]);
    } else if (kind === 'effects') {
      line(
        [
          [-9, -7],
          [9, -7],
          [9, 5],
          [-9, 5],
        ],
        true,
      );
      line([
        [0, 5],
        [0, 9],
        [-5, 9],
        [5, 9],
      ]);
    } else if (kind === 'refresh') {
      line([
        [-7, 1],
        [-7, -4],
        [-3, -7],
        [3, -7],
        [7, -3],
        [7, 0],
      ]);
      line([
        [3, -2],
        [7, 0],
        [9, -4],
      ]);
      line([
        [7, 3],
        [4, 7],
        [-3, 7],
        [-7, 4],
      ]);
    } else if (kind === 'join') {
      line([
        [-2, -8],
        [8, -8],
        [8, 8],
        [-2, 8],
      ]);
      line([
        [-9, 0],
        [3, 0],
        [-1, -4],
      ]);
      line([
        [3, 0],
        [-1, 4],
      ]);
    } else if (kind === 'bot') {
      line(
        [
          [-8, -5],
          [8, -5],
          [8, 7],
          [-8, 7],
        ],
        true,
      );
      line([
        [0, -5],
        [0, -9],
      ]);
      g.circle(cx - 3 * k, cy, 1.5 * k);
      g.circle(cx + 3 * k, cy, 1.5 * k);
      g.stroke();
    } else if (kind === 'match') {
      line([
        [-8, -5],
        [-4, -5],
        [5, 5],
        [9, 5],
        [6, 2],
      ]);
      line([
        [-8, 5],
        [-4, 5],
        [5, -5],
        [9, -5],
        [6, -8],
      ]);
    }
  }
  styleButton(
    g: Graphics,
    b: ButtonRect,
    kind: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'quiet' = 'secondary',
    icon = '',
  ) {
    const v = (b.visual = { x: b.x + 2, y: b.y + 4, w: b.w - 4, h: b.h - 8 });
    if (kind === 'primary') {
      if (
        !this.artwork(
          'button',
          v.x,
          v.y,
          v.w,
          v.h,
          b.disabled ? '#75808c99' : undefined,
        )
      )
        this.rect(
          g,
          v.x,
          v.y,
          v.w,
          v.h,
          b.disabled ? '#242d35' : '#4a3d24',
          C.amber,
          3,
        );
    } else if (kind === 'secondary')
      this.rect(
        g,
        v.x,
        v.y,
        v.w,
        v.h,
        b.disabled ? '#14233188' : '#263e50ed',
        b.disabled ? '#72899655' : '#8ba9ba',
        3,
      );
    b.label.color = col(
      b.disabled ? '#72818a' : kind === 'primary' ? '#fff0d0' : C.white,
    );
    b.label.isBold = kind === 'primary';
    if (icon) {
      this.controlIcon(
        g,
        icon,
        b.x + (b.w <= 54 ? b.w / 2 : 20),
        b.y + b.h / 2,
        18,
        b.disabled ? '#72818a' : kind === 'primary' ? C.action : C.dim,
      );
      if (b.w > 54) {
        b.label.node.setPosition(
          b.x + 32 + (b.w - 38) / 2 - this.w / 2,
          this.h / 2 - b.y - b.h / 2,
        );
        b.label.node
          .getComponent(UITransform)!
          .setContentSize(b.w - 40, b.h - 4);
      }
    }
  }
  menuButton(
    n: Node,
    g: Graphics,
    id: string,
    caption: string,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    kind: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'quiet' = 'secondary',
    icon = '',
    h = 44,
    disabled = false,
  ) {
    const label = this.button(id, caption, x, y, w, h, n);
    this.buttons[this.buttons.length - 1].disabled = disabled;
    this.styleButton(g, this.buttons[this.buttons.length - 1], kind, icon);
    return label;
  }
  buttonFeedback() {
    const b = this.pressedButton
      ? this.buttons.find((b) => b.id === this.pressedButton)
      : this.mousePointer
        ? this.hit(this.mousePointer.x, this.mousePointer.y)
        : undefined;
    if (
      !b ||
      b.disabled ||
      !b.label.node.activeInHierarchy ||
      (this.modal &&
        b.label.node.parent !== this.modal &&
        !this.isGlobalAction(b.id))
    )
      return;
    const v = b.visual || { x: b.x + 3, y: b.y + 4, w: b.w - 6, h: b.h - 8 };
    this.rect(
      this.globalGraphics,
      v.x,
      v.y,
      v.w,
      v.h,
      this.pressedButton ? '#e5d5b433' : '#e0efff14',
      this.pressedButton ? C.amber : '#bdcfd6aa',
      3,
    );
  }
  live(
    id: string,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    size: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
    hex = C.white,
  ) {
    const l = this.label(this.root, '', x, y, size, w, h, hex);
    this.labels.set(id, l);
    return l;
  }
  set(id: string, str: string) {
    const l = this.labels.get(id);
    if (l && l.string !== str) l.string = str;
  }
  buildControls() {
    const w = this.w,
      h = this.h,
      left = 12 + this.safe.left,
      right = w - 12 - this.safe.right,
      top = this.safe.top;
    const small = this.mouseDesktop,
      settingsX =
        right -
        44 -
        (!sys.isBrowser && this.touch && !this.nativeCapsule ? 88 : 0) -
        (this.screenControl ? 52 : 0);
    const sensorX = settingsX - 56,
      statusX = sensorX - 86,
      statsX = left + 64;
    this.live(
      'health',
      (statsX + statusX - 6) / 2,
      top + 24,
      this.compact ? 11 : 13,
      Math.max(80, statusX - statsX - 6),
      28,
    );
    this.labels.get('health')!.enableWrapText = false;
    this.labels.get('health')!.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
    this.labels.get('health')!.overflow = Label.Overflow.SHRINK;
    for (const side of [0, 1])
      this.live(
        'spectator:' + side,
        left + 100 + side * 198,
        top + 64,
        14,
        182,
        36,
        C.white,
      );
    this.live('progress', left + 120, top + 58, 11, 240, 20, C.dim);
    this.live('status', statusX + 40, top + 24, 11, 80, 28, C.white);
    this.button('sensor', '', sensorX, top + 2, 50, 44).fontSize = 11;
    this.button(
      'settings',
      '',
      settingsX,
      top + 2,
      44,
      44,
      this.globalControls,
    );
    if (this.screenControl)
      this.button(
        'screen',
        '',
        settingsX + 52,
        top + 2,
        44,
        44,
        this.globalControls,
      );
    this.live(
      'connectionNotice',
      left + 150,
      top + 68,
      12,
      Math.min(300, right - left - 140),
      28,
      C.red,
    ).node.parent = this.globalControls;
    this.live(
      'buff',
      w / 2,
      top + 61,
      11,
      Math.min(180, w - 200),
      24,
      C.mint,
    ).node.parent = this.globalControls;
    const toastWidth = Math.min(420, settingsX - left - 12);
    this.live(
      'globalToast',
      left + toastWidth / 2,
      top + 26,
      12,
      toastWidth,
      36,
      C.amber,
    ).node.parent = this.globalControls;
    const by = h - this.footer - this.safe.bottom;
    const cardW = Math.min(
      this.compact ? 64 : 78,
      (right - left - 146) / 5 - 6,
    );
    const fireW = 68;
    const cardsLeft = left;
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      const label = this.button(
        'weapon' + i,
        '',
        cardsLeft + i * (cardW + 6),
        by + 8,
        cardW,
        44,
      );
      if (small) {
        label.fontSize = 12;
        label.lineHeight = 17;
      }
    }
    this.button('homing', '', cardsLeft + 4 * (cardW + 6), by + 8, cardW, 44);
    for (const id of ['weapon4', 'skillUse', 'weapon5'])
      this.button(id, '', left, by + 8, 100, 44);
    this.button(
      'fire',
      '',
      right - fireW,
      h - this.safe.bottom - fireW - 10,
      fireW,
      fireW,
    );
    const stateW = Math.min(132, right - fireW - (left + 5 * (cardW + 6)) - 12);
    this.live(
      'weaponState',
      right - fireW - 8 - stateW / 2,
      h - this.safe.bottom - fireW / 2 - 10,
      small ? 12 : this.compact ? 12 : 14,
      Math.max(60, stateW),
      this.footer - 14,
      C.dim,
    );
    this.live(
      'notice',
      w / 2,
      by - 17,
      this.compact ? 12 : 15,
      this.compact ? w - 40 : Math.min(w - 260, 560),
      this.compact ? 28 : 36,
      C.amber,
    );
    this.live(
      'friendWarning',
      w / 2,
      by - 17,
      this.compact ? 13 : 16,
      this.compact ? w - 40 : Math.min(w - 260, 560),
      28,
      C.red,
    ).isBold = true;
    this.live(
      'tutorial',
      w / 2,
      by - 17,
      this.compact ? 11 : 13,
      w - 40,
      28,
      C.mint,
    );
    this.live('target', w / 2, by - 45, 12, 156, 24, C.amber);
    this.live('flight', w / 2, h / 2, 12, 130, 24, C.white);
    const priority = this.button('priority', '', 0, 0, 96, 68);
    priority.fontSize = 12;
    priority.color = col(C.red);
    priority.isBold = true;
    this.labels.set('priorityMarker', priority);
    const map = this.minimapLayout;
    this.button(
      'battleReport',
      this.t('战况', 'REPORT'),
      left,
      top + 2,
      56,
      44,
    ).fontSize = 11;
    this.live(
      'mapLegend',
      map.x + map.w / 2,
      map.y + 11,
      10,
      map.w - 4,
      20,
      C.dim,
    );
  }
  rect(
    g: Graphics,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
    fill: string,
    stroke?: string,
    radius = 0,
  ) {
    if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
    const path = () =>
      radius > 0
        ? g.roundRect(
            x - this.w / 2,
            this.h / 2 - y - h,
            w,
            h,
            Math.min(radius, w / 2, h / 2),
          )
        : g.rect(x - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y - h, w, h);
    g.fillColor = col(fill);
    path();
    g.fill();
    if (stroke) {
      g.lineWidth = 1;
      g.strokeColor = col(stroke);
      path();
      g.stroke();
    }
  }
  image(
    parent: Node,
    name: string,
    frame: SpriteFrame | undefined,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
  ) {
    if (!frame || w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
    const node = this.node(name, parent),
      sprite = node.addComponent(Sprite);
    sprite.sizeMode = Sprite.SizeMode.CUSTOM;
    sprite.spriteFrame = frame;
    node.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(w, h);
    node.setPosition(x + w / 2 - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y - h / 2);
    return sprite;
  }
  // A separate overlay after a Sprite preserves the border/text layer ordering.
  coverImage(
    parent: Node,
    name: string,
    frame: SpriteFrame,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
  ) {
    const crop = this.node(name, parent);
    crop.setPosition(x + w / 2 - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y - h / 2);
    crop.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(w, h);
    crop.addComponent(Mask).type = Mask.Type.GRAPHICS_RECT;
    const picture = this.node(name + '-Art', crop),
      sprite = picture.addComponent(Sprite);
    sprite.sizeMode = Sprite.SizeMode.CUSTOM;
    sprite.spriteFrame = frame;
    const rect = frame.rect,
      fw = rect?.width || frame.originalSize.width,
      fh = rect?.height || frame.originalSize.height;
    const scale = Math.max(w / fw, h / fh);
    picture.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(fw * scale, fh * scale);
    return crop;
  }
  artOverlay(parent: Node, name: string) {
    return this.node(name, parent).addComponent(Graphics);
  }
  mix(a: string, b: string, t: number) {
    const channels = (value: string) =>
      [1, 3, 5, 7].map((offset) =>
        parseInt(value.slice(offset, offset + 2) || 'ff', 16),
      );
    return (
      '#' +
      channels(a)
        .map((value, index) =>
          Math.round(value + (channels(b)[index] - value) * t)
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, '0'),
        )
        .join('')
    );
  }
  gradient(
    g: Graphics,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
    top: string,
    bottom: string,
    cut = 0,
    steps = 12,
  ) {
    for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
      const ya = (h * i) / steps,
        yb = (h * (i + 1)) / steps;
      const inset = (v: number) => Math.max(0, cut - v, cut - (h - v));
      g.fillColor = col(this.mix(top, bottom, (i + 0.5) / steps));
      g.moveTo(x + inset(ya) - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y - ya);
      g.lineTo(x + w - inset(ya) - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y - ya);
      g.lineTo(x + w - inset(yb) - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y - yb);
      g.lineTo(x + inset(yb) - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y - yb);
      g.close();
      g.fill();
    }
  }
  plate(
    g: Graphics,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
    fill: string,
    stroke = C.line,
    cut = 7,
  ) {
    if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
    const gold = fill === C.amber,
      active = fill === C.mint || stroke === C.mint,
      emphasis = gold || fill === C.mint;
    if (emphasis) stroke = gold ? C.amber : C.mint;
    cut = Math.min(cut, w / 5, h / 4);
    const path = (inset = 0, dy = 0) => {
      const l = x - this.w / 2 + inset,
        r = x + w - this.w / 2 - inset,
        t = this.h / 2 - y - inset + dy,
        b = this.h / 2 - y - h + inset + dy,
        c = Math.max(2, cut - inset);
      g.moveTo(l + c, t);
      g.lineTo(r - c, t);
      g.lineTo(r, t - c);
      g.lineTo(r, b + c);
      g.lineTo(r - c, b);
      g.lineTo(l + c, b);
      g.lineTo(l, b + c);
      g.lineTo(l, t - c);
      g.close();
    };
    const skinned =
      gold &&
      g.node.parent === this.modal &&
      this.artwork('button', x + 2, y + 2, w - 4, h - 4);
    if (!skinned) {
      g.fillColor = col('#01070cb0');
      path(0, -3);
      g.fill();
    }
    if (!skinned)
      this.gradient(
        g,
        x,
        y,
        w,
        h,
        gold
          ? '#b88c3cf2'
          : fill === '#382730'
            ? '#492c37'
            : active
              ? '#244b65ed'
              : '#20384aed',
        gold ? '#443018fa' : fill === '#382730' ? '#21111d' : '#07121ff2',
        cut,
        h > 90 ? 16 : 8,
      );
    if (gold) {
      g.lineWidth = 5;
      g.strokeColor = col('#f3c36624');
      path();
      g.stroke();
      g.lineWidth = 3;
      g.strokeColor = col('#ffe2a04d');
      path();
      g.stroke();
    }
    g.lineWidth = gold ? 1.5 : 1;
    g.strokeColor = col(stroke);
    path();
    g.stroke();
    g.strokeColor = col(gold ? '#ffe8ada0' : '#a5cbe229');
    path(2);
    g.stroke();
    this.rect(
      g,
      x + cut + 3,
      y + 3,
      w - cut * 2 - 6,
      1,
      gold ? '#ffe2a0a0' : '#d3e9f34d',
    );
    this.rect(g, x + cut + 3, y + h - 3, w - cut * 2 - 6, 1, '#010812a0');
    if (gold) {
      for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
        const cx = x + (side < 0 ? 9 : w - 9) - this.w / 2,
          cy = this.h / 2 - y - h / 2;
        g.strokeColor = col('#efd39388');
        g.lineWidth = 1;
        g.moveTo(cx - side * 2, cy + 3);
        g.lineTo(cx, cy);
        g.lineTo(cx - side * 2, cy - 3);
        g.stroke();
      }
    }
  }
  inset(
    g: Graphics,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
    accent = C.line,
  ) {
    this.gradient(g, x, y, w, h, '#030c17f2', '#1a3041ed', 3, 12);
    g.strokeColor = col('#02070de6');
    g.lineWidth = 2;
    g.rect(x - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y - h, w, h);
    g.stroke();
    g.strokeColor = col(accent);
    g.lineWidth = 1;
    g.rect(x + 2 - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y - h + 2, w - 4, h - 4);
    g.stroke();
    this.rect(g, x + 7, y + h - 1, w - 14, 1, '#aec8d047');
    this.rect(g, x + 8, y + 6, 18, 2, accent);
  }
  emblem(g: Graphics, x: number, y: number, radius: number, color = C.amber) {
    const cx = x - this.w / 2,
      cy = this.h / 2 - y;
    g.strokeColor = col(color);
    g.lineWidth = 1.5;
    g.circle(cx, cy, radius * 0.8);
    g.stroke();
    for (const side of [-1, 1])
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        g.fillColor = col(this.mix(color, '#142231', 0.15 + i * 0.16));
        g.moveTo(cx + side * radius * 0.7, cy + radius * 0.15 - i * 5);
        g.lineTo(
          cx + side * (radius * 1.6 - i * 5),
          cy + radius * 0.15 - i * 5,
        );
        g.lineTo(
          cx + side * (radius * 1.4 - i * 5),
          cy - radius * 0.02 - i * 5,
        );
        g.lineTo(cx + side * radius * 0.65, cy - radius * 0.02 - i * 5);
        g.close();
        g.fill();
      }
    g.fillColor = col(color);
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
      const angle = Math.PI / 2 + (i * Math.PI) / 4,
        r = i % 2 ? radius * 0.25 : radius;
      const px = cx + Math.cos(angle) * r,
        py = cy + Math.sin(angle) * r;
      if (i) g.lineTo(px, py);
      else g.moveTo(px, py);
    }
    g.close();
    g.fill();
    g.fillColor = col('#fff1ca');
    g.moveTo(cx, cy + radius);
    g.lineTo(cx, cy);
    g.lineTo(cx - radius * 0.25, cy + radius * 0.25);
    g.close();
    g.fill();
  }
  icon(
    g: Graphics,
    kind: string,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    size = 16,
    color = C.mint,
  ) {
    const cx = x - this.w / 2,
      cy = this.h / 2 - y,
      r = size / 2;
    g.strokeColor = col(color);
    g.fillColor = col(color);
    g.lineWidth = 1.8;
    if (kind === 'mountain') {
      g.moveTo(cx - r, cy - r * 0.65);
      g.lineTo(cx - r * 0.1, cy + r);
      g.lineTo(cx + r * 0.35, cy + r * 0.1);
      g.lineTo(cx + r * 0.7, cy + r * 0.65);
      g.lineTo(cx + r * 1.3, cy - r * 0.65);
      g.close();
      g.fill();
    } else if (kind === 'ranking') {
      for (const [i, h] of [r, r * 1.7, r * 1.3].entries()) {
        g.rect(cx - r + i * r * 0.7, cy - r, r * 0.45, h);
        g.fill();
      }
    } else if (kind === 'book') {
      for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
        g.moveTo(cx + side * 1.5, cy - r * 0.8);
        g.lineTo(cx + side * r, cy - r * 0.5);
        g.lineTo(cx + side * r, cy + r);
        g.lineTo(cx + side * 1.5, cy + r * 0.7);
        g.close();
        g.fill();
        g.strokeColor = col('#173147');
        g.lineWidth = 1;
        for (const row of [0, 0.4]) {
          g.moveTo(cx + side * 3, cy + r * row);
          g.lineTo(cx + side * (r - 2), cy + r * (row + 0.15));
          g.stroke();
        }
      }
    } else if (kind === 'battle') {
      for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
        g.moveTo(cx - side * r * 0.5, cy - r * 0.7);
        g.lineTo(cx + side * r * 0.8, cy + r * 0.65);
        g.lineTo(cx + side * r, cy + r);
        g.lineTo(cx + side * r * 0.6, cy + r * 0.8);
        g.lineTo(cx - side * r * 0.7, cy - r * 0.5);
        g.close();
        g.fill();
        g.lineWidth = 2.6;
        g.strokeColor = col(color);
        g.moveTo(cx - side * r * 0.9, cy - r * 0.25);
        g.lineTo(cx - side * r * 0.25, cy - r * 0.9);
        g.moveTo(cx - side * r * 0.6, cy - r * 0.6);
        g.lineTo(cx - side * r, cy - r);
        g.stroke();
      }
    } else if (kind === 'clock') {
      g.circle(cx, cy, r);
      g.stroke();
      g.moveTo(cx, cy + r * 0.65);
      g.lineTo(cx, cy);
      g.lineTo(cx + r * 0.45, cy - r * 0.2);
      g.stroke();
    } else {
      g.circle(cx, cy, r);
      g.stroke();
      g.moveTo(cx - r * 1.35, cy);
      g.lineTo(cx + r * 1.35, cy);
      g.moveTo(cx, cy - r * 1.35);
      g.lineTo(cx, cy + r * 1.35);
      g.stroke();
    }
  }
  isGlobalAction(id: string) {
    return id === 'settings' || id === 'screen';
  }
  inModal(node: Node) {
    for (let current: Node | null = node; current; current = current.parent)
      if (current === this.modal) return true;
    return false;
  }
  hit(x: number, y: number, includeDisabled = false) {
    const buttons = [...this.buttons].reverse();
    return [
      ...buttons.filter((b) => this.isGlobalAction(b.id)),
      ...buttons.filter((b) => !this.isGlobalAction(b.id)),
    ].find(
      (b) =>
        (includeDisabled || !b.disabled) &&
        (!b.clip ||
          (x >= b.clip.x &&
            x <= b.clip.x + b.clip.w &&
            y >= b.clip.y &&
            y <= b.clip.y + b.clip.h)) &&
        x >= b.x &&
        x <= b.x + b.w &&
        y >= b.y &&
        y <= b.y + b.h &&
        b.label.node.activeInHierarchy &&
        (!this.modal ||
          this.inModal(b.label.node) ||
          this.isGlobalAction(b.id)),
    );
  }
  blocksBattlefield(x: number, y: number, ignorePriority = false) {
    const map = this.minimapLayout,
      control = this.buttons.some(
        (b) =>
          !(ignorePriority && b.id === 'priority') &&
          b.label.node.activeInHierarchy &&
          (!this.modal ||
            this.inModal(b.label.node) ||
            this.isGlobalAction(b.id)) &&
          x >= b.x &&
          x <= b.x + b.w &&
          y >= b.y &&
          y <= b.y + b.h,
      );
    return (
      control ||
