      y < 46 + this.safe.top ||
      (x >= map.x && x <= map.x + map.w && y >= map.y && y <= map.y + map.h)
    );
  }
  scrollBy(amount: number) {
    this.scroll = Math.max(0, Math.min(this.scrollMax, this.scroll + amount));
    if (this.scrollNode) this.scrollNode.setPosition(0, this.scroll, 0);
  }
  routeGraphic(
    g: Graphics,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
    progress = 0,
    map: BattlefieldId = 'valley',
    light: MissionLight = 'day',
    enemies: readonly Point[] = [],
  ) {
    if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
    const route = mapRoute(map),
      point = (p: Point) => ({
        x: x + ((p.x / MAP.halfWidth + 1) * w) / 2 - this.w / 2,
        y: this.h / 2 - y - ((p.z / MAP.halfDepth + 1) * h) / 2,
      });
    const colors =
      light === 'night'
        ? [
            [17, 39, 53],
            [70, 100, 117],
          ]
        : light === 'dusk'
          ? [
              [67, 61, 48],
              [165, 125, 80],
            ]
          : map === 'highland'
            ? [
                [78, 91, 70],
                [187, 181, 147],
              ]
            : [
                [43, 76, 58],
                [156, 177, 117],
              ];
    // Sample the same height field as the battlefield so ridges and roads stay in place.
    const cols = 32,
      rows = 20,
      dx = (MAP.halfWidth * 2) / cols,
      dz = (MAP.halfDepth * 2) / rows;
    for (let j = 0; j < rows; j++)
      for (let i = 0; i < cols; i++) {
        const wx = -MAP.halfWidth + i * dx,
          wz = -MAP.halfDepth + j * dz;
        const corners = [
          { x: wx, z: wz },
          { x: wx + dx, z: wz },
          { x: wx + dx, z: wz + dz },
          { x: wx, z: wz + dz },
        ];
        for (const indices of [
          [0, 1, 2],
          [0, 2, 3],
        ]) {
          const tri = indices.map((k) => corners[k]),
            cx = tri.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.x, 0) / 3,
            cz = tri.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.z, 0) / 3;
          const elevation = terrainHeight(cx, cz, map);
          const shade = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(
              1,
              (elevation / (map === 'highland' ? 22 : 10)) * 0.75 +
                (elevation - terrainHeight(cx - 4, cz - 4, map)) * 0.18 +
                0.12,
            ),
          );
          g.fillColor = col(
            '#' +
              colors[0]
                .map((v, k) =>
                  Math.round(v + (colors[1][k] - v) * shade)
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(2, '0'),
                )
                .join(''),
          );
          tri.forEach((p, k) => {
            const q = point(p);
            if (k) g.lineTo(q.x, q.y);
            else g.moveTo(q.x, q.y);
          });
          g.close();
          g.fill();
        }
      }
    if (map === 'valley') {
      for (const [width, color] of [
        [Math.max(7, w * 0.032), light === 'night' ? '#254656' : '#7c9c87'],
        [Math.max(3, w * 0.017), light === 'night' ? '#396785' : '#4e9eac'],
      ] as const) {
        g.lineWidth = width;
        g.strokeColor = col(color);
        for (let j = 0; j <= 24; j++) {
          const z = -MAP.halfDepth + (j / 24) * MAP.halfDepth * 2,
            q = point({ x: riverX(z), z });
          if (j) g.lineTo(q.x, q.y);
          else g.moveTo(q.x, q.y);
        }
        g.stroke();
      }
    }
    if (h >= 60)
      for (let j = 1; j < 7; j++)
        for (let i = 1; i < 11; i++) {
          const p = {
            x: ((i / 11) * 2 - 1) * MAP.halfWidth + Math.sin(i * 7 + j) * 7,
            z: ((j / 7) * 2 - 1) * MAP.halfDepth,
          };
          if (
            (i + j * 3) % 4 === 0 ||
            (map === 'valley' && Math.abs(p.x - riverX(p.z)) < 10) ||
            route.some((q) => Math.hypot(q.x - p.x, q.z - p.z) < 12)
          )
            continue;
          const q = point(p),
            r = Math.max(2, Math.min(4, w / 90));
          g.fillColor = col(
            light === 'night'
              ? '#132d36'
              : light === 'dusk'
                ? '#474936'
                : '#254f3b',
          );
          g.circle(q.x + 1, q.y - 1, r + 1);
          g.fill();
          g.fillColor = col(
            light === 'night'
              ? '#254351'
              : light === 'dusk'
                ? '#717148'
                : '#55794a',
          );
          g.circle(q.x, q.y + 1, r);
          g.fill();
        }
    for (const [width, color] of [
      [6, '#17262b'],
      [3, '#c2b58c'],
      [1.5, C.amber],
    ] as const) {
      g.lineWidth = width;
      g.strokeColor = col(color);
      route.forEach((p, i) => {
        const q = point(p);
        if (i) g.lineTo(q.x, q.y);
        else g.moveTo(q.x, q.y);
      });
      g.stroke();
    }
    for (const post of FRIENDLY_POSTS) {
      const q = point(post);
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        g.fillColor = col('#172c37');
        g.rect(q.x - 7 + i * 5, q.y - 4, 5, 8);
        g.fill();
        g.fillColor = col(light === 'night' ? '#d0ad69' : '#adad91');
        g.rect(q.x - 8 + i * 5, q.y - 2, 4, 6);
        g.fill();
      }
      g.strokeColor = col(C.mint);
      g.lineWidth = 1;
      g.rect(q.x - 10, q.y - 6, 19, 13);
      g.stroke();
    }
    for (const enemy of enemies) {
      const q = point(enemy),
        r = h < 70 ? 2.5 : 4;
      g.fillColor = col('#14242b');
      g.strokeColor = col(C.red);
      g.lineWidth = 1.5;
      g.moveTo(q.x, q.y + r);
      g.lineTo(q.x + r, q.y);
      g.lineTo(q.x, q.y - r);
      g.lineTo(q.x - r, q.y);
      g.close();
      g.fill();
      g.stroke();
    }
    for (const p of [route[0], route.at(-1)!]) {
      const q = point(p);
      g.fillColor = col('#1c2a2e');
      g.circle(q.x, q.y, 5);
      g.fill();
      g.strokeColor = col(C.mint);
      g.lineWidth = 2;
      g.circle(q.x, q.y, 5);
      g.stroke();
    }
    const q = point(routePoint(progress, map));
    g.fillColor = col(C.amber);
    g.rect(q.x - 3, q.y - 2, 6, 4);
    g.fill();
  }
  supplyIcon(g: Graphics, kind: string, x: number, y: number) {
    x -= this.w / 2;
    y = this.h / 2 - y;
    g.strokeColor = col(kind === 'rate' ? C.amber : C.mint);
    g.lineWidth = 2;
    if (kind === 'tracking') {
      g.circle(x, y, 9);
      g.stroke();
      for (const sign of [-1, 1]) {
        g.moveTo(x + sign * 5, y);
        g.lineTo(x + sign * 14, y);
        g.moveTo(x, y + sign * 5);
        g.lineTo(x, y + sign * 14);
      }
    } else if (kind === 'ammo') {
      g.moveTo(x - 12, y - 7);
      g.lineTo(x + 13, y + 9);
      g.lineTo(x + 4, y - 5);
      g.lineTo(x - 10, y - 10);
      g.close();
      g.moveTo(x - 4, y - 4);
      g.lineTo(x - 10, y + 4);
    } else {
      for (const dy of [-5, 5]) {
        g.moveTo(x - 13, y + dy - 3);
        g.lineTo(x + 7, y + dy - 3);
        g.lineTo(x + 13, y + dy);
        g.lineTo(x + 7, y + dy + 3);
        g.lineTo(x - 13, y + dy + 3);
        g.close();
      }
    }
    g.stroke();
  }
  renderHome(n: Node, g: Graphics, s: Simulation) {
    const w = this.w,
      h = this.h,
      left = this.safe.left + 24,
      right = w - this.safe.right - 24;
    const light = (_number: number) => this.t('夜航', 'NIGHT OPS');
    const number =
      Math.max(
        0,
        MISSIONS.findIndex((mission) => mission.id === s.mission.id),
      ) + 1;
    this.rect(
      this.modal?.getChildByName('Backdrop')?.getComponent(Graphics) || g,
      0,
      0,
      this.w,
      this.h,
      '#07121c28',
    );
    if (!this.missionsOpen) {
      const compact = h < 420,
        wide = right - left >= 680,
        menuY = h - this.safe.bottom - (wide ? 76 : 114),
        titleW = Math.min(wide ? 500 : 390, (right - left) * 0.62),
        titleH = titleW / 3,
        titleY = Math.min(
          this.safe.top + (compact ? 70 : 112),
          menuY - titleH - 46,
        );
      // The title is an authored transparent metal wordmark, not a font approximation.
      if (this.titleArtwork && this.lang === 'zh')
        this.image(
          n,
          'MetalWordmark',
          this.titleArtwork,
          left - 8,
          titleY,
          titleW,
          titleH,
        );
      else {
        const title = this.label(
          n,
          this.t('夜航守望', 'OVERWATCH'),
          left + titleW / 2,
          titleY + titleH / 2,
          compact ? 42 : 62,
          titleW,
          Math.min(titleH - 20, 76),
          C.white,
        );
        title.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
        title.isBold = true;
      }
      const strap = this.label(
        n,
        'N I G H T   O V E R W A T C H',
        left + titleW / 2,
        titleY + titleH + 2,
        compact ? 10 : 12,
        titleW,
        24,
        C.action,
      );
      strap.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
      const subtitle = this.label(
        n,
        this.t('长夜之上，守住归途。', 'ABOVE THE NIGHT. BRING THEM HOME.'),
        left + titleW / 2,
        titleY + titleH + 28,
        12,
        titleW,
        24,
        '#b8cbd8',
      );
      subtitle.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
      const menuW = wide
        ? (right - left - 36) / 4
        : Math.min(248, (right - left - 12) / 2);
      const entries = [
        ['missions', this.t('单人模式', 'SINGLE PLAYER'), 'mountain'],
        ['online:menu', this.t('对战大厅', 'BATTLE LOBBY'), 'battle'],
        ['online:ranking', this.t('排行榜', 'LEADERBOARDS'), 'ranking'],
        ['help', this.t('飞行手册', 'FIELD GUIDE'), 'book'],
      ];
      for (const [i, [id, caption, symbol]] of entries.entries()) {
        const x = left + (wide ? i : i % 2) * (menuW + 12),
          y = menuY + (wide ? 0 : Math.floor(i / 2) * 54),
          primary = i === 0;
        this.plate(
          g,
          x,
          y,
          menuW,
          48,
          primary ? C.amber : '#111d21dc',
          primary ? C.amber : '#8ba9ba',
          5,
        );
        if (!primary) {
          this.rect(g, x + 6, y + 5, menuW - 12, 38, '#59778b33');
          this.rect(g, x + 6, y + 12, 2, 24, C.mint);
        }
        this.icon(g, symbol, x + 23, y + 24, 18, primary ? C.action : C.mint);
        const button = this.button(id, caption + '  ›', x, y, menuW, 48, n);
        button.node.setPosition(x + menuW / 2 + 11 - w / 2, h / 2 - y - 24);
        button.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(menuW - 42, 44);
        this.buttons[this.buttons.length - 1].visual = {
          x: x + 2,
          y: y + 2,
          w: menuW - 4,
          h: 44,
        };
        button.fontSize = menuW < 170 ? 13 : 15;
        button.color = col(primary ? C.action : C.white);
        button.isBold = true;
      }
      if (this.online) {
        const settingsLeft = this.buttons.find(
          (button) => button.id === 'settings',
        )!.x;
        const profileW = Math.min(264, settingsLeft - left - 118);
        this.plate(
          g,
          left,
          this.safe.top + 8,
          profileW,
          44,
          '#111d21ed',
          C.line,
          6,
        );
        this.button(
          'online:profile',
          playerLabel(this.online.identity) + '  ✎',
          left,
          this.safe.top + 8,
          profileW,
          44,
          n,
        ).fontSize = 12;
        this.plate(
          g,
          left + profileW + 10,
          this.safe.top + 8,
          92,
          44,
          '#111d21ed',
          C.line,
          6,
        );
        this.button(
          'online:history',
          this.t('对战记录', 'RECORDS'),
          left + profileW + 10,
          this.safe.top + 8,
          92,
          44,
          n,
        ).fontSize = 11;
      }
      return;
    }
    this.rect(
      this.modal?.getChildByName('Backdrop')?.getComponent(Graphics) || g,
      0,
      0,
      this.w,
      this.h,
      '#071a2d55',
    );
    this.label(
      n,
      this.t('任务地图', 'MISSION MAP'),
      left + 78,
      this.safe.top + 80,
      22,
      156,
      42,
    ).isBold = true;
    const top = this.safe.top + 104,
      bottom = h - this.safe.bottom - 58,
      gap = 12,
      cw = Math.min(300, Math.max(160, (right - left) / 3.2)),
      ch = Math.max(100, bottom - top - 22);
    if (this.missionFocus !== undefined) {
      this.listScroll.missions = this.missionFocus * (cw + gap);
      this.missionFocus = undefined;
    }
    const parent = n,
      originalG = g,
      list = this.listViewport(
        n,
        'missions',
        { x: left, y: top, w: right - left, h: bottom - top - 8 },
        MISSIONS.length * (cw + gap) - gap,
        'x',
      );
    n = list.n;
    g = list.g;
    for (const [i, mission] of MISSIONS.entries()) {
      const x = left + i * (cw + gap) - list.offset,
        y = top,
        selected = mission.id === s.mission.id,
        number = i + 1;
      if (x + cw < left || x > right) continue;
      const unlocked =
          this.developer || missionUnlocked(this.missionProgress, mission.id),
        smallCard = ch < 180;
      this.plate(g, x, y, cw, ch, '#101e2c', selected ? C.mint : C.line, 3);
      const artTop = y + 4,
        artH = ch - 8;
      const artIndex = mission.map === 'highland' ? 1 : 2;
      const artwork = this.missionArtwork[artIndex];
      if (artwork)
        this.coverImage(
          n,
          'MissionIllustration-' + mission.id,
          artwork,
          x + 4,
          artTop,
          cw - 8,
          artH,
        );
      else
        this.routeGraphic(
          g,
          x + 4,
          artTop,
          cw - 8,
          artH,
          0,
          mission.map,
          'night',
          mission.events,
        );
      const overlay = this.artOverlay(n, 'MissionSurface-' + mission.id);
      this.gradient(
        overlay,
        x + 4,
        artTop,
        cw - 8,
        Math.min(40, artH),
        '#04131bca',
        '#04131b00',
      );
      this.gradient(
        overlay,
        x + 4,
        artTop + artH * 0.25,
        cw - 8,
        artH * 0.75,
        '#04131b00',
        '#04131bf2',
        0,
        24,
      );
      this.gradient(
        overlay,
        x + 4,
        y + ch - (smallCard ? 66 : 90),
        cw - 8,
        smallCard ? 62 : 86,
        '#07131f00',
        '#07131fc9',
        0,
        18,
      );
      if (selected) {
        overlay.strokeColor = col('#6ad4ff55');
        overlay.lineWidth = 5;
        overlay.rect(x - w / 2 + 2, h / 2 - y - ch + 2, cw - 4, ch - 4);
        overlay.stroke();
      }
      overlay.strokeColor = col(selected ? '#b9edff' : '#58788d');
      overlay.lineWidth = selected ? 2 : 1;
      overlay.rect(x - w / 2 + 2, h / 2 - y - ch + 2, cw - 4, ch - 4);
      overlay.stroke();
      this.rect(
        overlay,
        x + 14,
        y + ch - 3,
        cw - 28,
        2,
        selected ? C.mint : '#45678055',
      );
      if (selected) {
        const bx = x + cw - 16 - w / 2,
          by = h / 2 - y - 17;
        overlay.fillColor = col(C.mint);
        overlay.circle(bx, by, 8);
        overlay.fill();
        overlay.strokeColor = col('#092032');
        overlay.lineWidth = 2;
        overlay.moveTo(bx - 4, by);
        overlay.lineTo(bx - 1, by - 3);
        overlay.lineTo(bx + 4, by + 3);
        overlay.stroke();
      }
      this.label(
        n,
        String(number).padStart(3, '0'),
        x + 29,
        y + 18,
        15,
        42,
        25,
        C.action,
      ).isBold = true;
      this.label(
        n,
        selected
          ? this.t('当前', 'SELECTED')
          : unlocked
            ? light(number)
            : this.t('锁定', 'LOCKED'),
        x + cw - (selected ? 62 : 48),
        y + 18,
        10,
        selected ? 66 : 80,
        22,
        selected ? C.mint : C.white,
      );
      const label = this.button(
        'mission:' + mission.id,
        text(mission.name, this.lang),
        x,
        y,
        cw,
        ch,
        n,
      );
      this.buttons[this.buttons.length - 1].disabled = !unlocked;
      label.node.setPosition(
        x + cw / 2 - w / 2,
        h / 2 - y - ch + (smallCard ? 48 : 61),
      );
      label.node
        .getComponent(UITransform)!
        .setContentSize(cw - 20, smallCard ? 24 : 26);
      label.fontSize = cw < 180 ? 12 : smallCard ? 15 : 19;
      label.color = col(unlocked ? C.white : C.dim);
      label.isBold = true;
      const tier =
        number <= 3
          ? this.t('入门', 'INTRO')
          : number <= 60
            ? this.t('进阶', 'ADVANCED')
            : this.t('挑战', 'EXPERT');
      this.label(
        n,
        `${light(number)} · ${tier} · ` +
          this.t(
            `敌方 ${mission.events.length}`,
            `${mission.events.length} HOSTILES`,
          ),
        x + cw / 2,
        y + ch - (smallCard ? 27 : 36),
        cw < 180 ? 9 : 11,
        cw - 16,
        smallCard ? 16 : 20,
        '#bdd0dd',
      );
      const status = this.missionProgress.cleared.includes(mission.id)
        ? this.t('已完成', 'CLEARED')
        : unlocked
          ? this.developer
            ? this.t('试玩 · 不计进度', 'PRACTICE')
            : this.t('可出动', 'AVAILABLE')
          : this.t('完成上一关解锁', 'COMPLETE PREVIOUS');
      this.label(
        n,
        status,
        x + cw / 2,
        y + ch - (smallCard ? 10 : 15),
        smallCard ? 9 : 11,
        cw - 12,
        smallCard ? 16 : 19,
        unlocked ? C.mint : C.dim,
      );
    }
    list.finish();
    n = parent;
    g = originalG;
    const bw = (right - left - 24) / 3;
    for (const [i, id] of ['homeMenu', 'training', 'start'].entries()) {
      const x = left + i * (bw + 12),
        primary = id === 'start',
        selected = id === 'training' && s.mission.mode === 'training';
      this.plate(
        g,
        x,
        bottom,
        bw,
        44,
        primary ? C.amber : '#1c2a2e',
        selected ? C.mint : C.line,
        3,
      );
      const label = this.button(
        id,
        [
          this.t('返回', 'BACK'),
          this.t('火控热身', 'FIRE CONTROL'),
          s.mission.mode === 'training'
            ? this.t('开始热身 →', 'START WARMUP →')
            : this.t(
                `出发 ${String(number).padStart(3, '0')} →`,
                `DEPLOY ${String(number).padStart(3, '0')} →`,
              ),
        ][i],
        x,
        bottom,
        bw,
        44,
        n,
      );
      this.buttons[this.buttons.length - 1].disabled =
        primary &&
        s.mission.mode !== 'training' &&
        !this.developer &&
        !missionUnlocked(this.missionProgress, s.mission.id);
      label.color = col(primary ? C.action : selected ? C.mint : C.white);
      label.isBold = primary;
    }
  }
  panelFrame(
    g: Graphics,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
    accent = C.mint,
    dim = '#050f1d55',
    header = true,
  ) {
    const backdrop =
      this.modal?.getChildByName('Backdrop')?.getComponent(Graphics) || g;
    this.rect(backdrop, 0, 0, this.w, this.h, dim);
    const skinned = this.artwork('panel', x, y, w, h);
    // Separate load-bearing frame, recessed display and machined corner hardware.
    if (!skinned)
      this.plate(g, x - 4, y + 3, w + 8, h + 2, '#162838', '#020b12', 18);
    if (!skinned) this.gradient(g, x, y, w, h, '#35454b', '#101a21', 15, 22);
    this.gradient(
      g,
      x + 5,
      y + 5,
      w - 10,
      h - 10,
      skinned ? '#17282f33' : '#17282f',
      skinned ? '#070f1644' : '#070f16',
      11,
      22,
    );
    if (header)
      this.gradient(
        g,
        x + 9,
        y + 9,
        w - 18,
        36,
        '#55656725',
        '#17282f00',
        7,
        10,
      );
    if (header) this.rect(g, x + 14, y + 47, w - 28, 1, '#b7b09488');
    this.rect(g, x + 14, y + h - 66, w - 28, 1, '#45677c66');
    // Quiet instrument grid and a wing planform live behind the readable controls.
    for (let xx = x + 22; xx < x + w - 20; xx += 24)
      this.rect(g, xx, y + 51, 1, Math.max(0, h - 120), '#a3c6df07');
    for (let yy = y + 58; yy < y + h - 68; yy += 24)
      this.rect(g, x + 18, yy, w - 36, 1, '#a3c6df07');
    const cx = x + w / 2 - this.w / 2,
      cy = this.h / 2 - y - h * 0.54;
    g.strokeColor = col('#8eafc414');
    g.lineWidth = 1;
    g.moveTo(cx, cy + h * 0.21);
    g.lineTo(cx + 8, cy + 5);
    g.lineTo(cx + w * 0.3, cy - h * 0.06);
    g.lineTo(cx + w * 0.3, cy - h * 0.1);
    g.lineTo(cx + 7, cy - 12);
    g.lineTo(cx + 5, cy - h * 0.2);
    g.lineTo(cx + w * 0.09, cy - h * 0.24);
    g.lineTo(cx - w * 0.09, cy - h * 0.24);
    g.lineTo(cx - 5, cy - h * 0.2);
    g.lineTo(cx - 7, cy - 12);
    g.lineTo(cx - w * 0.3, cy - h * 0.1);
    g.lineTo(cx - w * 0.3, cy - h * 0.06);
    g.lineTo(cx - 8, cy + 5);
    g.close();
    g.stroke();
    for (const side of [-1, 1])
      for (const end of [-1, 1]) {
        const xx = side < 0 ? x + 4 : x + w - 35,
          yy = end < 0 ? y + 4 : y + h - 24;
        this.gradient(
          g,
          xx,
          yy,
          31,
          20,
          skinned ? '#4b5a6070' : '#4b5a60',
          skinned ? '#1b2c3444' : '#1b2c34',
          5,
          6,
        );
        this.rect(g, xx + 5, yy + 3, 21, 1, '#c5d5d363');
        g.fillColor = col('#07131b');
        g.circle(xx + 8 - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - yy - 10, 2.5);
        g.fill();
        g.strokeColor = col('#d8ba6c80');
        g.lineWidth = 1;
        g.circle(xx + 8 - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - yy - 10, 2.5);
        g.stroke();
      }
    this.rect(g, x + 45, y + 3, w - 90, 1, '#e4d19995');
    if (header) this.rect(g, x + w / 2 - 30, y + 47, 60, 2, accent);
    this.rect(g, x + 44, y + h - 5, w - 88, 1, '#c6ac7080');
  }
  renderSettings(
    n: Node,
    g: Graphics,
    s: Simulation,
    world: World,
    key: string,
  ) {
    const flight = this.toolsOpen,
      zoom = this.zoomOpen,
      playing = s.phase === 'playing';
    const short = this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom < 360;
    const pw = Math.min(
      flight || zoom ? Math.max(344, Math.min(420, this.w * 0.5)) : 640,
      this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - 32,
    );
    const ph = Math.min(
      zoom ? 248 : short ? 288 : flight ? 304 : 360,
      this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - 32,
    );
    const px =
        this.safe.left + (this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - pw) / 2,
      py = this.safe.top + (this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - ph) / 2;
    this.panelFrame(
      g,
      px,
      py,
      pw,
      ph,
      C.mint,
      flight || zoom ? '#050f1d18' : '#050f1d45',
    );
    const title = flight
      ? this.t('飞行 / 视野', 'FLIGHT / VIEW')
      : zoom
        ? this.t('画面 / 倍率', 'DISPLAY / ZOOM')
        : this.t(
            playing ? '战斗设置' : '设置',
            playing ? 'BATTLE SETTINGS' : 'SETTINGS',
          );
    const heading = this.label(
      n,
      title,
      px + 16 + (pw - 112) / 2,
      py + 20,
      19,
      pw - 112,
      30,
    );
    heading.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
    heading.isBold = true;
    const control = (
      id: string,
      caption: string,
      x: number,
      y: number,
      w: number,
      active = false,
      disabled = false,
    ) => {
      const label = this.menuButton(
        n,
        g,
        id,
        caption,
        x,
        y,
        w,
        'secondary',
        '',
        44,
        disabled,
      );
      if (active) this.rect(g, x + 12, y + 39, w - 24, 2, C.mint);
      label.fontSize = w < 100 ? 11 : 13;
      label.lineHeight = 17;
      label.color = col(disabled ? C.dim : active ? C.mint : C.white);
      label.isBold = active;
      return label;
    };
    if (flight)
      control('locate', this.t('定位', 'LOCATE'), px + pw - 80, py - 5, 64);
    else
      this.label(
        n,
        s.duelMode
          ? this.t('对战继续', 'BATTLE LIVE')
          : this.t('已暂停', 'PAUSED'),
        px + pw - 48,
        py + 20,
        10,
        64,
        24,
        C.mint,
      );
    if ((flight || zoom) && px - this.safe.left > 160) {
      const x = this.safe.left + 16,
        w = Math.min(190, px - x - 16);
      this.plate(g, x, py, w, 28, '#111d21dc', undefined, 6);
      this.label(
        n,
        s.duelMode
          ? this.t('对战继续 · 操作已松开', 'BATTLE LIVE · FIRE RELEASED')
          : this.t('实时画面 · 战斗暂停', 'LIVE VIEW · BATTLE PAUSED'),
        x + w / 2,
        py + 14,
        10,
        w - 8,
        20,
        C.mint,
      );
    }
    if (flight) {
      const a = s.aircraft,
        cell = (pw - 40) / 2,
        row = short ? 64 : 86,
        top = py + (short ? 44 : 54);
      const groups = [
        [
          this.t('飞行高度 / m', 'ALTITUDE / m'),
          'altitudeDown',
          'altitudeUp',
          String(Math.round(a.altitude * FLIGHT.metersPerUnit)),
          a.altitude <= FLIGHT.minAltitude,
          a.altitude >= FLIGHT.maxAltitude,
        ],
        [
          this.t('盘旋半径 / m', 'ORBIT RADIUS / m'),
          'radiusIn',
          'radiusOut',
          String(Math.round(a.radius * FLIGHT.metersPerUnit)),
          a.radius <= FLIGHT.minRadius,
          a.radius >= FLIGHT.maxRadius,
        ],
        [
          this.t('观察角度', 'VIEW ANGLE'),
          'rotateLeft',
          'rotateRight',
          `${Math.round(world.cameraSnapshot?.sensorRotation || 0)}°`,
          false,
          false,
        ],
      ] as const;
      groups.forEach(([caption, down, up, value, min, max], i) => {
        const x = px + 16 + (i % 2) * (cell + 8),
          y = top + Math.floor(i / 2) * row;
        this.label(n, caption, x + cell / 2, y + 8, 10, cell, 16, C.dim);
        control(down, '−', x, y + 18, 44, false, min);
        control(up, '+', x + cell - 44, y + 18, 44, false, max);
        this.label(
          n,
          value,
          x + cell / 2,
          y + 40,
          16,
          cell - 92,
          30,
          C.white,
        ).isBold = true;
      });
      const x = px + 24 + cell,
        y = top + row,
        half = (cell - 6) / 2;
      this.label(
        n,
        this.t('盘旋方向', 'ORBIT DIRECTION'),
        x + cell / 2,
        y + 8,
        10,
        cell,
        16,
        C.dim,
      );
      control(
        'orbitLeft',
        this.t('逆时针', 'CCW'),
        x,
        y + 18,
        half,
        a.direction === -1,
      );
      control(
        'orbitRight',
        this.t('顺时针', 'CW'),
        x + half + 6,
        y + 18,
        half,
        a.direction === 1,
      );
      if (!short)
        this.label(
          n,
          s.duelMode
            ? this.t(
                '服务器持续计时，关闭菜单后继续操作',
                'SERVER CLOCK CONTINUES WHILE IN MENU',
              )
            : this.t(
                '调整立即生效，战斗计时保持暂停',
                'CHANGES APPLY NOW · BATTLE STAYS PAUSED',
              ),
          px + pw / 2,
          py + ph - 73,
          10,
          pw - 32,
          20,
          C.mint,
        );
    } else if (zoom) {
      const y = py + 44,
        step = short ? 46 : 48,
        inner = pw - 32,
        half = (inner - 8) / 2;
      control('lensOut', '−', px + 16, y, 44, false, world.zoom <= 0.65);
      control(
        'lensIn',
        '+',
        px + pw - 60,
        y,
        44,
        false,
        world.zoom >= this.zoomLimit,
      );
      this.label(
        n,
        `${world.zoom.toFixed(1)}×`,
        px + pw / 2,
        y + 22,
        26,
        inner - 104,
        40,
        C.white,
      ).isBold = true;
      const viewW = (inner - 12) / 3;
      for (const [i, mode] of (
        ['night', 'enhanced', 'thermal'] as const
      ).entries())
        control(
          'view:' + mode,
          this.t(
            { night: '夜景', enhanced: '增强', thermal: '热像' }[mode],
            mode.toUpperCase(),
          ),
          px + 16 + i * (viewW + 6),
          y + step,
          viewW,
          (world.viewMode || 'night') === mode,
        );
      control(
        'effects',
        this.reducedEffects
          ? this.t('特效 · 精简', 'FX · REDUCED')
          : this.t('特效 · 完整', 'FX · FULL'),
        px + 16,
        y + step * 2,
        half,
        !this.reducedEffects,
      );
      const limit = nextZoomLimit(this.zoomLimit);
      if (this.rewardsAvailable && limit)
        control(
          'zoomUpgrade',
          s.buff
            ? this.t('支援生效中', 'SUPPORT ACTIVE')
            : this.pendingSupply
              ? this.t('先领取补给', 'CLAIM SUPPLY')
              : this.t(`解锁 ${limit}× · 广告`, `UNLOCK ${limit}× · AD`),
          px + 24 + half,
          y + step * 2,
          half,
          false,
          !!s.buff || this.pendingSupply,
        );
      else
        this.label(
          n,
          this.t(`上限 ${this.zoomLimit}×`, `LIMIT ${this.zoomLimit}×`),
          px + 24 + half + half / 2,
          y + step * 2 + 22,
          11,
          half,
          28,
          C.dim,
        );
    } else {
      const x = px + 16,
        inner = pw - 32,
        top = py + (short ? 34 : 44),
        step = short ? 44 : 48;
      if (playing) {
        const half = (inner - 8) / 2;
        control(
          'flightControls',
          this.t('飞行 / 视野  ›', 'FLIGHT / VIEW  ›'),
          x,
          top,
          half,
          true,
        );
        control(
          'zoomControls',
          this.t('画面 / 倍率  ›', 'DISPLAY / ZOOM  ›'),
          x + half + 8,
          top,
          half,
          true,
        );
      }
      const items = [
        [
          'sound',
          this.muted
            ? this.t('游戏声音 关', 'SOUND OFF')
            : this.t('游戏声音 开', 'SOUND ON'),
        ],
        [
          'voicePrompts',
          this.voicePrompts
            ? this.t('语音提示 开', 'VOICE ON')
            : this.t('语音提示 关', 'VOICE OFF'),
        ],
        [
          'autoPriority',
          this.autoPriority
            ? this.t('优先标注 开', 'AUTO MARK ON')
            : this.t('优先标注 关', 'AUTO MARK OFF'),
        ],
        ['language', this.lang === 'zh' ? '语言 · 中文' : 'LANGUAGE · EN'],
        ['help', this.t('飞行手册', 'FIELD GUIDE')],
        ...(playing
          ? [
              [
                'convoy',
                s.convoy === 'moving'
                  ? this.t('车队等待', 'HOLD CONVOY')
                  : s.convoy === 'holdRequested'
                    ? this.t('取消等待', 'CANCEL HOLD')
                    : this.t('车队继续', 'CONVOY GO'),
              ],
              ['mission', this.t('任务详情', 'MISSION BRIEF')],
              ['supply', this.supplyLabel(s)],
            ]
          : [
              ['online:profile', this.t('玩家昵称', 'PLAYER NAME')],
              [
                'effects',
                this.reducedEffects
                  ? this.t('特效 · 精简', 'FX · REDUCED')
                  : this.t('特效 · 完整', 'FX · FULL'),
              ],
            ]),
      ].filter(
        ([id]) =>
          !(s.duelMode && ['convoy', 'mission', 'supply'].includes(id)) &&
          !(id === 'convoy' && s.mission.mode === 'training') &&
          !(id === 'supply' && !this.rewardsAvailable && !this.pendingSupply),
      );
      const cols = short ? 3 : 2,
        cw = (inner - (cols - 1) * 16) / cols;
      items.forEach(([id, caption], i) => {
        const bx = x + (i % cols) * (cw + 16),
          by = top + (playing ? step : 0) + Math.floor(i / cols) * step;
        const glyph = (
          {
            sound: this.muted ? 'muted' : 'sound',
            voicePrompts: this.voicePrompts ? 'sound' : 'muted',
            autoPriority: 'mission',
            language: 'language',
            help: 'guide',
            'online:profile': 'profile',
            effects: 'effects',
            mission: 'mission',
            convoy: 'next',
            supply: 'plus',
          } as Record<string, string>
        )[id];
        const label = this.menuButton(
          n,
          g,
          id,
          caption,
          bx,
          by,
          cw,
          'quiet',
          glyph,
        );
        label.fontSize = cw < 160 ? 11 : 13;
        label.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
        this.rect(g, bx + 8, by + 43, cw - 16, 1, '#8ba0ab28');
        if (['sound', 'voicePrompts', 'autoPriority'].includes(id)) {
          const enabled =
            id === 'sound'
              ? !this.muted
              : id === 'voicePrompts'
                ? this.voicePrompts
                : this.autoPriority;
          const sw = cw > 220 ? 34 : 20,
            sx = bx + cw - sw - 10,
            sy = by + 16;
          this.rect(
            g,
            sx,
            sy,
            sw,
            12,
            enabled ? '#34766f' : '#283b45',
            enabled ? C.mint : C.line,
            6,
          );
          g.fillColor = col(enabled ? C.mint : C.dim);
          g.circle(
            sx + (enabled ? sw - 6 : 6) - this.w / 2,
            this.h / 2 - sy - 6,
            4,
          );
          g.fill();
          label.node
            .getComponent(UITransform)!
            .setContentSize(cw - 42 - sw, 40);
          label.node.setPosition(
            bx + 30 + (cw - 42 - sw) / 2 - this.w / 2,
            this.h / 2 - by - 22,
          );
          label.fontSize = cw < 180 ? 10 : 12;
        } else if (cw > 220)
          this.controlIcon(g, 'next', bx + cw - 22, by + 22, 16);
        if (id === 'supply' && s.buff) {
          this.buttons[this.buttons.length - 1].disabled = true;
          label.color = col(C.dim);
        }
      });
    }
    const by = py + ph - 52,
      bw = (pw - 40) / 2;
    if (!short) this.rect(g, px + 16, by - 7, pw - 32, 1, '#4c605e77');
    if (playing)
      this.menuButton(
        n,
        g,
        flight || zoom ? 'close' : 'home',
        flight || zoom
          ? this.t('返回设置', 'BACK')
          : this.t('返回基地', 'BASE'),
        px + 16,
        by,
        bw,
        'quiet',
        'back',
      );
    const width = Math.min(playing ? bw : 160, 200),
      bx = px + pw - 16 - width;
    const primary =
      playing && (flight || zoom || key === 'pause') ? 'resume' : 'close';
    const label = this.menuButton(
      n,
      g,
      primary,
      playing ? this.t('继续战斗', 'RESUME BATTLE') : this.t('返回', 'BACK'),
      bx,
      by,
      width,
      playing ? 'primary' : 'secondary',
      playing ? 'next' : 'back',
    );
    label.color = col(C.action);
    label.isBold = true;
  }
  renderGuide(n: Node, g: Graphics) {
    const pw = Math.min(760, this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - 32),
      ph = Math.min(450, this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - 74),
      px =
        this.safe.left + (this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - pw) / 2,
      py =
        this.safe.top +
        58 +
        (this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - 58 - ph) / 2;
    this.panelFrame(g, px, py, pw, ph);
    const title = this.label(
      n,
      this.t('飞行手册', 'FIELD GUIDE'),
      px + 100,
      py + 20,
      20,
      168,
      30,
    );
    title.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
    title.isBold = true;
    if (pw >= 420)
      this.label(
        n,
        'NIGHT 07 / ' + this.t('操作指引', 'FLIGHT NOTES'),
        px + pw - 110,
        py + 20,
        10,
        188,
        22,
        C.dim,
      );
    const tabW = (pw - 122) / 3,
      tabY = py + 44;
    for (const [i, [id, caption]] of [
      ['basic', this.t('基础操作', 'BASICS')],
      ['advanced', this.t('飞行视野', 'FLIGHT')],
      ['rules', this.t('任务规则', 'MISSION')],
    ].entries()) {
      const x = px + 16 + i * (tabW + 4),
        active = this.helpGroup === id;
      this.plate(
        g,
        x,
        tabY,
        tabW,
        44,
        active ? '#29423c' : '#1c2a2e',
        undefined,
        6,
      );
      const label = this.button('tab:' + id, caption, x, tabY, tabW, 44, n);
      label.color = col(active ? C.mint : C.dim);
      label.isBold = active;
      if (active) this.rect(g, x + 12, tabY + 41, tabW - 24, 2, C.mint);
    }
    this.plate(g, px + pw - 94, tabY, 78, 44, '#1c2a2e', C.line, 6);
    this.button(
      'input',
      this.helpTouch ? this.t('触控', 'TOUCH') : this.t('键鼠', 'KEYS'),
      px + pw - 94,
      tabY,
      78,
      44,
      n,
    );
    const vh = ph - 154,
      vw = pw - 44,
      mask = this.node('HelpViewport', n);
    mask.setPosition(px + pw / 2 - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - (py + 94 + vh / 2));
    mask.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(vw, vh);
    mask.addComponent(Mask).type = Mask.Type.GRAPHICS_RECT;
    this.scrollArea = { x: px + 22, y: py + 94, w: vw, h: vh };
    const content = this.node('Instructions', mask);
    this.scrollNode = content;
    const lines = content.addComponent(Graphics),
      rows = ACTIONS.filter(
        (a) =>
          a.group === this.helpGroup &&
          !(this.helpTouch && ['previous', 'next', 'focus'].includes(a.id)),
      );
    let offset = 0;
    rows.forEach((a, i) => {
      const description = text(a.description, this.lang),
        font = pw < 600 ? 12 : 14,
        width = vw - 46,
        units = [...description].reduce(
          (sum, c) => sum + (c.charCodeAt(0) > 255 ? 1 : 0.6),
          0,
        ),
        bodyH =
          Math.max(2, Math.ceil((units * font) / width) + 1) * (font * 1.45),
        rowH = bodyH + 38;
      const entry = (
        str: string,
        x: number,
        yy: number,
        size: number,
        w: number,
        h: number,
        color: string,
      ) => {
        const label = this.label(
          content,
          str,
          this.w / 2,
          0,
          size,
          w,
          h,
          color,
        );
        label.node.setPosition(x, vh / 2 - offset - yy);
        label.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
        return label;
      };
      entry(
        String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0'),
        -vw / 2 + 16,
        17,
        10,
        28,
        22,
        C.mint,
      );
      entry(
        text(a.name, this.lang),
        -vw / 2 + 42 + width * 0.22,
        17,
        14,
        width * 0.44,
        26,
        C.white,
      ).isBold = true;
      const binding = entry(
        this.helpTouch
          ? text(a.touch, this.lang)
          : bindingLabel(a.id, this.lang),
        -vw / 2 + 42 + width * 0.73,
        17,
        10,
        width * 0.5,
        26,
        C.mint,
      );
      binding.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.RIGHT;
      entry(
        description,
        -vw / 2 + 42 + width / 2,
        32 + bodyH / 2,
        font,
        width,
        bodyH,
        C.dim,
      );
      lines.fillColor = col('#36566e66');
      lines.rect(-vw / 2 + 42, vh / 2 - offset - rowH + 1, width, 1);
      lines.fill();
      offset += rowH;
    });
    this.scrollMax = Math.max(0, offset - vh);
    this.lastHelpLine = text(rows.at(-1)!.description, this.lang);
    const bottom = py + ph - 52;
    for (const [id, caption, x, width, primary] of [
      [
        'tutorial',
        this.t('重玩教学', 'COACH'),
        px + 16,
        pw < 400 ? 80 : 100,
        false,
      ],
      [
        'language',
        this.lang === 'zh' ? 'EN' : '中文',
        px + (pw < 400 ? 100 : 124),
        44,
        false,
      ],
      [
        'close',
        this.t('返回', 'BACK'),
        px + pw - (pw < 400 ? 96 : 116),
        pw < 400 ? 80 : 100,
        true,
      ],
    ] as const) {
      this.plate(
        g,
        x,
        bottom,
        width,
        44,
        primary ? C.amber : '#1c2a2e',
        primary ? undefined : C.line,
        6,
      );
      const label = this.button(id, caption, x, bottom, width, 44, n);
      label.color = col(primary ? C.action : C.white);
      label.isBold = primary;
    }
    if (pw >= 420)
      this.label(
        n,
        this.t('上下滑动查看更多', 'SCROLL FOR MORE'),
        px + 176 + (pw - 300) / 2,
        bottom + 22,
        10,
        pw - 308,
        26,
        C.dim,
      );
  }
  inputField(
    n: Node,
    g: Graphics,
    key: 'draftName' | 'draftCode' | 'draftPassword',
    caption: string,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    width: number,
  ) {
    this.label(n, caption, x + 46, y + 22, 12, 92, 40, C.dim);
    this.inset(g, x + 102, y, width - 102, 44);
    const field = this.node(key, n);
    // Configure before preload creates the native input and default labels.
    field.active = false;
    field.setPosition(
      x + 102 + (width - 102) / 2 - this.w / 2,
      this.h / 2 - y - 22,
    );
    field.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(width - 122, 44);
    const edit = field.addComponent(EditBox);
    edit.textLabel = this.label(
      field,
      '',
      this.w / 2,
      this.h / 2,
      14,
      width - 122,
      42,
    );
    const placeholder =
      key === 'draftCode'
        ? this.t('6位房间号', 'ROOM NUMBER')
        : key === 'draftPassword'
          ? this.t('公开房无需密码', 'OPTIONAL FOR PUBLIC ROOM')
          : '';
    edit.placeholderLabel = this.label(
      field,
      placeholder,
      this.w / 2,
      this.h / 2,
      12,
      width - 122,
      42,
      C.dim,
    );
    edit.inputMode = EditBox.InputMode.SINGLE_LINE;
    for (const label of [edit.textLabel, edit.placeholderLabel]) {
      label.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.setAnchorPoint(0, 1);
      label.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
      // Label setters use the initial multiline mode; restore single-line alignment.
      label.verticalAlign = Label.VerticalAlign.CENTER;
    }
    edit.maxLength = key === 'draftName' ? 16 : key === 'draftCode' ? 12 : 64;
    edit.inputFlag =
      key === 'draftPassword'
        ? EditBox.InputFlag.PASSWORD
        : EditBox.InputFlag.DEFAULT;
    edit.string = this.online![key];
    field.on(EditBox.EventType.TEXT_CHANGED, () => {
      this.online![key] = edit.string;
    });
    field.active = true;
  }
  private lobbyBody?: Node;
  private lobbyComposer?: EditBox;
  private lobbyStamp = '';
  private chatLayout: Array<{ id: string; y: number; h: number }> = [];
  private chatUnread = false;
  private lobbyOwner = '';
  private lobbySkin(
    parent: Node,
    key: string,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
  ) {
    const frame = this.progressionArt.get(key);
    if (!frame) return;
    const n = this.node('LobbySkin', parent),
      sp = n.addComponent(Sprite);
    n.setPosition(x + w / 2 - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y - h / 2);
    n.setScale(0.24, 0.24, 1);
    n.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(w / 0.24, h / 0.24);
    sp.sizeMode = Sprite.SizeMode.CUSTOM;
    sp.type = Sprite.Type.SLICED;
    sp.spriteFrame = frame;
  }
  private renderLobby(n: Node, initial = false) {
    const o = this.online!,
      roomy = this.w >= 1000,
      l = this.safe.left + 12,
      r = this.w - this.safe.right - 12,
      top = this.safe.top + 56,
      b = this.h - this.safe.bottom - 12,
      gap = 14,
      lw = (r - l - gap) * 0.44,
      rx = l + lw + gap,
      rw = r - rx,
      inputY = b - 48;
    if (initial) {
      this.lobbySkin(n, 'panel', l, top, lw, b - top);
      this.lobbySkin(n, 'panel', rx, top, rw, b - top);
      this.button(
        'online:back',
        '‹',
        l,
        this.safe.top + 5,
        44,
        44,
        n,
      ).fontSize = 31;
      this.label(
        n,
        this.t('全局大厅', 'GLOBAL LOBBY'),
        l + 145,
        this.safe.top + 27,
        25,
        190,
        40,
        C.white,
      ).isBold = true;
      this.label(
        n,
        this.t('大厅交流', 'LOBBY CHAT'),
        rx + rw * 0.32,
        top + 21,
        19,
        rw * 0.61,
        32,
        C.white,
      ).isBold = true;
      const field = this.node('LobbyComposer', n),
        fw = Math.max(90, rw - 142);
      field.active = false;
      field.setPosition(
        rx + 10 + fw / 2 - this.w / 2,
        this.h / 2 - inputY - 22,
      );
      field.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(fw, 44);
      this.lobbySkin(n, 'panel', rx + 8, inputY, fw + 4, 44);
      field.setSiblingIndex(n.children.length - 1);
      const edit = (this.lobbyComposer = field.addComponent(EditBox));
      edit.textLabel = this.label(
        field,
        '',
        this.w / 2,
        this.h / 2,
        roomy ? 18 : 13,
        fw - 12,
        40,
        C.white,
      );
      edit.placeholderLabel = this.label(
        field,
        this.t('说点什么…', 'Say something…'),
        this.w / 2,
        this.h / 2,
        roomy ? 17 : 12,
        fw - 12,
        40,
        C.dim,
      );
      edit.inputMode = EditBox.InputMode.SINGLE_LINE;
      edit.inputFlag = EditBox.InputFlag.DEFAULT;
      edit.maxLength = 240;
      edit.string = o.chatDraft;
      for (const label of [edit.textLabel, edit.placeholderLabel]) {
        label.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.setAnchorPoint(0, 1);
        label.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
        label.verticalAlign = Label.VerticalAlign.CENTER;
      }
      field.on(EditBox.EventType.TEXT_CHANGED, () => {
        o.chatDraft = edit.string;
      });
      field.active = true;
      this.lobbySkin(n, 'blue', r - 122, inputY, 54, 44);
      this.button(
        'online:chatInvite',
        this.t('邀请', 'INVITE'),
        r - 122,
        inputY,
        54,
        44,
        n,
      ).fontSize = 12;
      this.lobbySkin(n, 'gold', r - 64, inputY, 56, 44);
      this.button(
        'online:chatSend',
        this.t('发送', 'SEND'),
        r - 64,
        inputY,
        56,
        44,
        n,
      ).fontSize = 14;
      if (this.lobbyOwner !== o.authenticatedId) {
        this.lobbyOwner = o.authenticatedId;
        this.chatLayout = [];
        this.chatUnread = false;
        this.listScroll.chat = 0;
      }
    }
    if (this.lobbyBody && isValid(this.lobbyBody)) {
      const old = this.lobbyBody;
      this.buttons = this.buttons.filter((b) => {
        let node: Node | null = b.label.node;
        while (node) {
          if (node === old) return false;
          node = node.parent;
        }
        return true;
      });
      old.destroy();
    }
    this.listAreas = this.listAreas.filter(
      (a) => !['rooms', 'chat'].includes(a.key),
    );
    const body = (this.lobbyBody = this.node('LobbyLiveContent', n)),
      g = this.node('Ink', body).addComponent(Graphics);
    const btn = (
      id: string,
      caption: string,
      x: number,
      y: number,
      w: number,
      kind = 'blue',
      disabled = false,
      parent = body,
    ) => {
      this.lobbySkin(parent, kind, x, y, w, 44);
      const label = this.button('online:' + id, caption, x, y, w, 44, parent);
      label.fontSize = Math.min(14, Math.max(11, w / 6));
      label.isBold = true;
      this.buttons.at(-1)!.disabled = disabled;
    };
    const roomTop = top + 8,
      roomBottom = b - 98,
      rowH = this.h < 420 ? 112 : 136,
      rooms = o.rooms;
    const roomList = this.listViewport(
      body,
      'rooms',
      {
        x: l + 7,
        y: roomTop,
        w: lw - 14,
        h: Math.max(44, roomBottom - roomTop),
      },
      rooms.length * (rowH + 8),
    );
    rooms.forEach((room, i) => {
      const y = roomTop + i * (rowH + 8) - roomList.offset;
      if (y + rowH < roomTop || y > roomBottom) return;
      this.lobbySkin(roomList.n, 'panel', l + 9, y, lw - 24, rowH);
      const frame = this.progressionArt.get('duel'),
        iw = roomy ? Math.min(154, lw * 0.29) : Math.min(92, lw * 0.34),
        ih = roomy ? 96 : 70;
      if (frame)
        this.coverImage(roomList.n, 'RoomArt', frame, l + 16, y + 10, iw, ih);
      const tx = l + iw + 26,
        tw = lw - iw - (roomy ? 148 : 44);
      const title = this.label(
        roomList.n,
        this.t('房间 ', 'ROOM ') + room.code,
        tx + tw / 2,
        y + (roomy ? 29 : 20),
        roomy ? 24 : 13,
        tw,
        32,
        C.white,
      );
      title.isBold = true;
      title.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
      this.label(
        roomList.n,
        room.players.map((p) => p.nickname || p.name || '飞行员').join(' / '),
        tx + tw / 2,
        y + (roomy ? 64 : 42),
        roomy ? 17 : 12,
        tw,
        26,
        C.dim,
      ).horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
      this.label(
        roomList.n,
        `${room.playerCount}/2 · ` +
          this.t(
            room.status === 'waiting'
              ? room.canJoin
                ? '等待加入'
                : '等待开战'
              : '进行中',
            room.status === 'waiting' ? 'WAITING' : 'PLAYING',
          ),
        tx + tw / 2,
        y + (roomy ? 97 : 63),
        roomy ? 18 : 12,
        tw,
        26,
        room.canJoin ? C.mint : C.amber,
      ).horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
      const bw = roomy ? 94 : Math.min(82, lw * 0.37);
      btn(
        (room.canJoin ? 'joinCode:' : 'watchCode:') + room.code,
        this.t(room.canJoin ? '加入' : '观战', room.canJoin ? 'JOIN' : 'WATCH'),
        l + lw - bw - 18,
        y + (roomy ? (rowH - 44) / 2 : rowH - 45),
        bw,
        room.canJoin ? 'gold' : 'blue',
        !room.canJoin && !room.canSpectate,
        roomList.n,
      );
    });
    roomList.finish();
    if (!rooms.length)
      this.label(
        body,
        o.message || this.t('暂无公开房间', 'No public rooms'),
        l + lw / 2,
        roomTop + 42,
        14,
        lw - 25,
        70,
        C.dim,
      );
    const ww = (lw - 24) / 2;
    btn(
      o.room ? 'roomReturn' : 'friend',
      this.t(o.room ? '返回房间' : '创建房间', o.room ? 'MY ROOM' : 'CREATE'),
      l + 8,
      b - 92,
      ww,
      'gold',
      o.busy,
    );
    btn(
      'join',
      this.t('房号加入', 'JOIN CODE'),
      l + 16 + ww,
      b - 92,
      ww,
      'blue',
      o.busy,
    );
    btn(
      'match',
      this.t('快速匹配', 'MATCH'),
      l + 8,
      b - 46,
      ww,
      'blue',
      o.busy,
    );
    btn(
      'bot',
      this.t('人机练习', 'PRACTICE'),
      l + 16 + ww,
      b - 46,
      ww,
      'panel',
      o.busy,
    );
    const area = {
        x: rx + 8,
        y: top + 43,
        w: rw - 16,
        h: Math.max(44, inputY - top - 68),
      },
      messages = o.visibleChat;
    const old = this.chatLayout,
      oldOffset = this.listScroll.chat || 0,
      oldTotal = old.length ? old.at(-1)!.y + old.at(-1)!.h + 8 : 0,
      atBottom = old.length === 0 || oldOffset >= oldTotal - area.h - 3;
    const anchor = old.find((row) => row.y + row.h > oldOffset),
      relative = anchor ? oldOffset - anchor.y : 0;
    const messageFont = roomy ? 18 : 13,
      avatar = roomy ? 38 : 28,
      textX = rx + avatar + 20,
      maxBubble = rw - avatar - 66;
    let total = 0;
    this.chatLayout = messages.map((m) => {
      const lines = Math.max(
          1,
          Math.ceil(
            Array.from(m.text).length /
              Math.max(6, Math.floor((maxBubble - 20) / messageFont)),
          ),
        ),
        h =
          (m.room ? (roomy ? 170 : 174) : roomy ? 80 : 68) +
          (lines - 1) * messageFont * 1.4;
      const row = { id: m.id, y: total, h };
      total += h + 8;
      return row;
    });
    const changed =
      old.map((r) => r.id).join() !== this.chatLayout.map((r) => r.id).join();
    if (changed && atBottom) this.listScroll.chat = Math.max(0, total - area.h);
    else if (changed) {
      const row = this.chatLayout.find((r) => r.id === anchor?.id);
      if (row) this.listScroll.chat = row.y + relative;
      this.chatUnread = true;
    }
    if (this.listScroll.chat >= total - area.h - 3) this.chatUnread = false;
    const chat = this.listViewport(body, 'chat', area, total);
    messages.forEach((m, i) => {
      const row = this.chatLayout[i],
        y = area.y + row.y - chat.offset;
      if (y + row.h < area.y || y > area.y + area.h) return;
      const own = m.playerId === o.authenticatedId,
        date = new Date(m.at),
        stamp = `${String(date.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(date.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
      this.rect(
        chat.g,
        rx + 10,
        y + 4,
        avatar,
        avatar,
        own ? '#1c4559' : '#203347',
        '#8a7959',
        6,
      );
      this.label(
        chat.n,
        Array.from(m.nickname)[0] || '航',
        rx + 10 + avatar / 2,
        y + 4 + avatar / 2,
        roomy ? 22 : 17,
        avatar,
        avatar,
        own ? C.mint : C.amber,
      ).isBold = true;
      this.label(
        chat.n,
        (own ? this.t('你 · ', 'YOU · ') : '') + m.nickname + '  ' + stamp,
        textX + maxBubble / 2,
        y + 14,
        roomy ? 15 : 11,
        maxBubble,
        25,
        own ? C.mint : C.white,
      ).horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
      const bubbleW = m.room
          ? maxBubble
          : Math.min(
              maxBubble,
              Math.max(82, Array.from(m.text).length * messageFont + 24),
            ),
        bubbleY = y + 32,
        textH = row.h - (m.room ? (roomy ? 133 : 142) : 38);
      this.rect(
        chat.g,
        textX,
        bubbleY,
        bubbleW,
        row.h - 36,
        own ? '#174358ee' : '#142b3bee',
        '#456477',
        7,
      );
      const label = this.label(
        chat.n,
        m.text,
        textX + bubbleW / 2,
        bubbleY + textH / 2 + 3,
        messageFont,
        bubbleW - 20,
        textH,
        C.white,
      );
      label.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
      this.button(
        'online:chatMenu:' + m.id,
        '⋮',
        r - 46,
        y + 20,
        44,
        44,
        chat.n,
      ).fontSize = 24;
      if (m.room) {
        const cardY = bubbleY + textH + 8,
          iw = roomy ? 90 : 44,
          ih = roomy ? 62 : 42,
          scene = this.progressionArt.get('duel');
        if (scene)
          this.coverImage(
            chat.n,
            'ChatInvitationArt',
            scene,
            textX + 8,
            cardY,
            iw,
            ih,
          );
        const tx = textX + iw + 17,
          tw = Math.max(50, bubbleW - iw - 28);
        this.label(
          chat.n,
          this.t('房间 ', 'ROOM ') + m.room.code,
          tx + tw / 2,
          cardY + 12,
          roomy ? 20 : 12,
          tw,
          24,
          m.room.canJoin ? C.mint : C.dim,
        ).horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
        this.label(
          chat.n,
          this.t(
            m.room.canJoin ? '公开房间 · 等待加入' : '已满或已关闭',
            m.room.canJoin ? 'PUBLIC · OPEN' : 'FULL OR CLOSED',
          ),
          tx + tw / 2,
          cardY + 35,
          roomy ? 14 : 10,
          tw,
          22,
          C.dim,
        ).horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
        btn(
          'chatJoin:' + m.id,
          this.t(
            m.room.canJoin ? '加入' : '已失效',
            m.room.canJoin ? 'JOIN' : 'CLOSED',
          ),
          textX + bubbleW - (roomy ? 104 : 72),
          y + row.h - 48,
          roomy ? 96 : 68,
          'blue',
          !m.room.canJoin,
          chat.n,
        );
      }
      if (o.chatMenu === m.id) {
        const menuY = y + 24;
        this.lobbySkin(chat.n, 'panel', r - 146, menuY, 100, 92);
        btn(
          'chatBlock:' + m.playerId,
          this.t('屏蔽', 'BLOCK'),
          r - 144,
          menuY,
          96,
          'panel',
          own,
          chat.n,
        );
        btn(
          'chatReport:' + m.id,
          this.t('举报', 'REPORT'),
          r - 144,
          menuY + 46,
          96,
          'panel',
          own,
          chat.n,
        );
      }
    });
    chat.finish();
    if (!o.chatAvailable || !messages.length)
      this.label(
        body,
        !o.chatAvailable
          ? this.t('大厅交流暂不可用', 'CHAT UNAVAILABLE')
          : this.t('和大厅飞行员打个招呼', 'Say hello to other pilots'),
        rx + rw / 2,
        area.y + 42,
        13,
        rw - 30,
        70,
        C.dim,
      );
    if (this.chatUnread)
      btn(
        'chatLatest',
        this.t('新消息 ↓', 'NEW ↓'),
        r - 108,
        top + 1,
        98,
        'blue',
      );
    this.label(
      body,
      o.chatStatus ||
        this.t(
          '文字交流 · 120字 · 发言间隔3秒',
          'TEXT · 120 CHARS · 3s INTERVAL',
        ),
      rx + rw / 2,
      inputY - 12,
      10,
      rw - 18,
      21,
      C.dim,
    );
    if (o.blockedPlayers.size)
      btn(
        'chatBlocks',
        this.t(
          `屏蔽(${o.blockedPlayers.size})`,
          `BLOCKS (${o.blockedPlayers.size})`,
        ),
        rx + rw - 105,
        area.y + area.h - 45,
        96,
        'panel',
      );
    if (o.chatMenu === 'blocks') {
      this.lobbySkin(
        body,
        'panel',
        rx + 8,
        area.y,
        rw - 16,
        Math.min(area.h, 52 * o.blockedPlayers.size + 12),
      );
      Array.from(o.blockedPlayers)
        .slice(0, 5)
        .forEach((id, i) =>
          btn(
            'chatBlock:' + id,
            this.t('恢复 ', 'UNBLOCK ') + id.slice(-6),
            rx + 14,
            area.y + 6 + i * 50,
            rw - 28,
            'blue',
          ),
        );
    }
    if (this.lobbyComposer && this.lobbyComposer.string !== o.chatDraft)
      this.lobbyComposer.string = o.chatDraft;
    const send = this.buttons.find((b) => b.id === 'online:chatSend');
    if (send) {
      send.disabled = !o.chatAvailable || o.chatSending;
      send.label.string = o.chatSending
        ? this.t('发送中', 'SENDING')
        : this.t('发送', 'SEND');
    }
    this.lobbyStamp = `${o.revision}:${o.chatRevision}`;
  }
  renderOnline(n: Node, g: Graphics, s: Simulation, result: boolean) {
    const online = this.online!;
    if (online.panel === 'menu' && !result) {
      this.renderLobby(n, true);
      return;
    }
    const pw = Math.min(
        result ? 560 : 760,
        this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - 32,
      ),
      ph = Math.min(
        result ? 360 : online.panel === 'ranking' ? 620 : 440,
        this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - 28,
      ),
      px = (this.w - pw) / 2,
      py = (this.h - ph) / 2;
    this.panelFrame(
      g,
      px,
      py,
      pw,
      ph,
      result ? C.amber : C.mint,
      result ? '#050f1d66' : '#050f1d55',
      !result && !(online.panel === 'ranking' && ph < 280),
    );
    const label = (str: string, y: number, size = 14, color = C.white) =>
      this.label(n, str, px + pw / 2, py + y, size, pw - 40, 26, color);
    const button = (
      id: string,
      caption: string,
      x: number,
      y: number,
      width: number,
      primary = false,
      quiet = false,
      glyph = '',
      disabled = false,
    ) => {
      disabled ||=
        online.busy &&
        [
          'saveName',
          'createConfirm',
          'joinConfirm',
          'match',
          'friend',
          'ready',
        ].includes(id);
      const selected =
        primary &&
        (id === 'public' ||
          id === 'private' ||
          id.startsWith('board:') ||
          id.startsWith('category:'));
      const text = this.menuButton(
        n,
        g,
        'online:' + id,
        caption,
        px + x,
        py + y,
        width,
        primary && !selected ? 'primary' : quiet ? 'quiet' : 'secondary',
        glyph,
        44,
        disabled,
      );
      if (selected) {
        text.color = col(C.mint);
        text.isBold = true;
        this.rect(g, px + x + 12, py + y + 39, width - 24, 2, C.mint);
      }
      text.fontSize = pw < 600 ? 11 : 13;
      return this.buttons[this.buttons.length - 1];
    };
    const bottom = ph - 54,
      half = (pw - 44) / 2,
      count =
        online.panel === 'menu'
          ? Math.max(1, Math.min(4, Math.floor((bottom - 141) / 52)))
          : ph < 340
            ? 2
            : 3;
    online.pageSize = count;
    if (online.panel === 'menu')
      online.roomPage = Math.min(
        online.roomPage,
        Math.max(0, Math.ceil(online.rooms.length / count) - 1),
      );
    if (online.panel === 'history')
      online.historyPage = Math.min(
        online.historyPage,
        Math.max(
          0,
          Math.ceil((online.history?.matches.length || 0) / count) - 1,
        ),
      );
    const titles = {
      menu: ['对战大厅', 'BATTLE LOBBY'],
      ranking: ['排行榜', 'RANKINGS'],
      room: ['作战准备室', 'READY ROOM'],
      profile: ['玩家昵称', 'PLAYER NAME'],
      create: ['创建房间', 'CREATE ROOM'],
      join: ['加入房间', 'JOIN ROOM'],
      history: ['对战记录', 'BATTLE RECORDS'],
      '': ['', ''],
    };
    if (!result) {
      const headingW = online.panel === 'ranking' ? pw - 294 : pw - 130;
      const heading = this.label(
        n,
        text(titles[online.panel], this.lang),
        px + 54 + headingW / 2,
        py + 26,
        21,
        headingW,
        32,
      );
      heading.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
      heading.isBold = true;
      this.controlIcon(
        g,
        online.panel === 'menu'
          ? 'lobby'
          : online.panel === 'ranking'
            ? 'ranking'
            : online.panel === 'history'
              ? 'history'
              : 'profile',
        px + 31,
        py + 25,
        22,
        C.amber,
      );
    }
    if (result || !['ranking', 'menu', 'history'].includes(online.panel))
      button('dismiss', '', pw - 58, 4, 44, false, true, 'close');
    if (result) {
      const short = ph < 320,
        cramped = ph < 280,
        mid = Math.min(short ? 165 : 205, bottom - 66);
      const ownSeat = online.spectator ? -1 : (online.room?.you ?? 0);
      const outcome = (seat: number) =>
        online.room?.players[seat]?.settlement?.outcome ||
        (seat === ownSeat ? online.room?.settlement?.outcome : undefined) ||
        (online.view?.winner === null
          ? 'draw'
          : online.view?.winner === seat
            ? 'win'
            : online.view?.winner !== undefined
              ? 'loss'
              : '');
      const outcomeText = (value: string) =>
        value === 'win'
          ? this.t('胜利', 'WIN')
          : value === 'loss'
            ? this.t('失败', 'LOSS')
            : value === 'draw'
              ? this.t('平局', 'DRAW')
              : this.t('结算中', 'PENDING');
      const outcomeColor = (value: string) =>
        value === 'win' ? '#75db9e' : value === 'loss' ? C.red : C.amber;
      const ownOutcome = outcome(ownSeat);
      if (!cramped)
        this.artwork(
          'insignia',
          px + pw / 2 - (short ? 50 : 65),
          py + 12,
          short ? 100 : 130,
          short ? 37 : 48,
        );
      label(
        online.spectator
          ? this.t('观战结束', 'MATCH ENDED')
          : this.t('战斗', 'BATTLE ') + outcomeText(ownOutcome),
        cramped ? 44 : short ? 70 : 88,
        short ? 28 : 34,
        online.spectator ? C.amber : outcomeColor(ownOutcome),
      ).isBold = true;
      for (let seat = 0; seat < 2; seat++) {
        const player =
            online.room?.players[seat] ||
            (seat === 0 ? online.identity : online.opponent),
          result = outcome(seat);
        label(
          (seat === 0 ? this.t('A方', 'SIDE A') : this.t('B方', 'SIDE B')) +
            (seat === ownSeat ? this.t(' · 你', ' · YOU') : '') +
            ' · ' +
            playerLabel(player) +
            ' · ' +
            outcomeText(result),
          (cramped ? 70 : short ? 99 : 120) + seat * (cramped ? 22 : 23),
          11,
          outcomeColor(result),
        );
      }
      const settlement = online.room?.settlement;
      const cw = (pw - 56) / 2;
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++)
        this.inset(
          g,
          px + 20 + i * (pw / 2 - 10),
          py + mid - 28,
          pw / 2 - 30,
          86,
          i ? '#ac8e4d99' : '#5b859d99',
        );
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
        const cx = px + 28 + cw * (i + 0.5);
        this.controlIcon(
          g,
          i ? 'star' : 'time',
          cx - cw * 0.32,
          py + mid + 8,
          26,
          i ? C.amber : C.dim,
        );
        this.label(
          n,
          i
            ? this.t('本局积分', 'POINTS EARNED')
            : this.t('作战用时', 'OPERATION TIME'),
          cx + 14,
          py + mid - 14,
          11,
          cw - 40,
          22,
          C.dim,
        );
        this.label(
          n,
          i
            ? settlement
              ? '+' + settlement.points
              : '—'
            : formatTime(s.time * 1000),
          cx + 14,
          py + mid + 13,
          short ? 24 : 30,
          cw - 40,
          40,
          i ? C.action : C.white,
        ).isBold = true;
        if (i)
          this.label(
            n,
            settlement?.totalPoints !== undefined
              ? this.t('总积分 ', 'TOTAL ') + settlement.totalPoints
              : online.spectator
                ? this.t('观战不计分', 'SPECTATING · NO POINTS')
                : online.room
                  ? this.t('积分暂不可用', 'POINTS UNAVAILABLE')
                  : this.t('本地练习', 'LOCAL PRACTICE'),
            cx + 14,
            py + mid + 43,
            11,
            cw - 20,
            22,
            C.dim,
          );
      }
      this.rect(g, px + 32, py + bottom - 7, pw - 64, 1, '#8ba0ab44');
      const bw = Math.min(210, pw - 64);
      button(
        'backLobby',
        this.t('返回大厅', 'BACK TO LOBBY'),
        (pw - bw) / 2,
        bottom,
        bw,
        true,
        false,
        'back',
      );
      return;
    }
    if (['profile', 'create', 'join'].includes(online.panel)) {
      if (online.panel === 'profile') {
        this.inputField(
          n,
          g,
          'draftName',
          this.t('玩家昵称', 'PLAYER NAME'),
          px + 18,
          py + 83,
          pw - 36,
        );
        label(
          this.t(
            '2–16个字符，昵称会显示在房间和战绩中',
            '2–16 CHARACTERS · SHOWN IN ROOMS AND RECORDS',
          ),
          151,
          11,
          C.dim,
        );
      } else if (online.panel === 'create') {
        button(
          'public',
          this.t('公开房间', 'PUBLIC'),
          18,
          65,
          half,
          !online.privateRoom,
        );
        button(
          'private',
          this.t('好友私密房', 'PRIVATE'),
          26 + half,
          65,
          half,
          online.privateRoom,
        );
        if (online.privateRoom)
          this.inputField(
            n,
            g,
            'draftPassword',
            this.t('房间密码', 'PASSWORD'),
            px + 18,
            py + 127,
            pw - 36,
          );
        else
          label(
            this.t(
              '房间将显示在大厅，其他玩家可加入',
              'OTHER PILOTS CAN FIND AND JOIN THIS ROOM',
            ),
            151,
            13,
            C.dim,
          );
      } else {
        this.inputField(
          n,
          g,
          'draftCode',
          this.t('房间号', 'ROOM'),
          px + 18,
          py + 73,
          pw - 36,
        );
        this.inputField(
          n,
          g,
          'draftPassword',
          this.t('房间密码', 'PASSWORD'),
          px + 18,
          py + 129,
          pw - 36,
        );
      }
      label(
        online.message ||
          (online.busy
            ? this.t('正在处理…', 'PLEASE WAIT…')
            : online.panel === 'create' && online.privateRoom
              ? this.t(
                  '设置4–64位密码，分享时另行告知好友',
                  'SET A 4–64 CHARACTER PASSWORD',
                )
              : ''),
        bottom - 24,
        11,
        C.amber,
      );
      button(
        'back',
        this.t('返回', 'BACK'),
        18,
        bottom,
        100,
        false,
        true,
        'back',
      );
      button(
        online.panel === 'profile'
          ? 'saveName'
          : online.panel === 'create'
            ? 'createConfirm'
            : 'joinConfirm',
        online.busy
          ? this.t('请稍候', 'PLEASE WAIT')
          : this.t('确定', 'CONFIRM'),
        pw - 198,
        bottom,
        180,
        online.panel !== 'profile',
      );
      return;
    }
    if (online.panel === 'ranking') {
      const solo = online.board === 'clears' || online.board === 'level',
        level = online.board === 'level';
      button(
        'category:solo',
        this.t('单人模式', 'SOLO'),
        pw - 234,
        4,
        104,
        solo,
      );
      button(
        'category:duel',
        this.t('对战模式', 'VERSUS'),
        pw - 122,
        4,
        104,
        !solo,
      );
      const tabW = Math.min(140, (pw - 64) / 4);
      const boards = solo
        ? [
            ['clears', this.t('通关榜', 'CLEARS')],
            ['level', this.t('关卡榜', 'LEVEL')],
          ]
        : [
            ['points', this.t('积分榜', 'POINTS')],
            ['win-rate', this.t('胜率榜', 'WIN RATE')],
          ];
      boards.forEach(([id, name], i) =>
        button(
          'board:' + id,
          name,
          18 + i * (tabW + 8),
          52,
          tabW,
          online.board === id,
        ),
      );
      const detailX = 34 + 2 * tabW,
        detailW = pw - detailX - 18;
      if (level) {
        button(
          'previous',
          '',
          detailX,
          52,
          44,
          false,
          true,
          'back',
          online.level === 0,
        );
        button(
          'next',
          '',
          pw - 62,
          52,
          44,
          false,
          true,
          'next',
          online.level === MISSIONS.length - 1,
        );
      }
      const descriptions = {
        clears: this.t('最高通关', 'HIGHEST CLEAR'),
        level: this.t(`第 ${online.level + 1} 关`, `LEVEL ${online.level + 1}`),
        points: this.t('累计积分', 'TOTAL POINTS'),
        'win-rate': this.t('真人对战胜率', 'HUMAN WIN RATE'),
      };
      this.label(
        n,
        descriptions[online.board],
        px + detailX + detailW / 2,
        py + 74,
        11,
        detailW - (level ? 88 : 0),
        26,
        C.dim,
      );
      const value = (row: NonNullable<Online['ranking']>['top'][number]) =>
        level
          ? formatTime(row.secondary)
          : online.board === 'win-rate'
            ? Math.round(
                (row.games ? (row.wins || 0) / row.games : row.score) * 1000,
              ) /
                10 +
              '%'
            : online.board === 'clears'
              ? this.t('第', '') + row.score + this.t('关', '')
              : row.score + this.t('分', '');
      const metric = (row: NonNullable<Online['ranking']>['top'][number]) =>
        (level
          ? this.t('最快 ', 'BEST ')
          : online.board === 'clears'
            ? this.t('最高通关 ', 'LEVEL ')
            : online.board === 'points'
              ? this.t('积分 ', 'POINTS ')
              : this.t('胜率 ', 'WIN RATE ')) + value(row);
      const rows = online.ranking?.top || [],
        rowH = 36,
        vw = pw - 36,
        vh = bottom - 144;
      const mask = this.node('RankingViewport', n);
      mask.setPosition(
        px + pw / 2 - this.w / 2,
        this.h / 2 - (py + 104 + vh / 2),
      );
      mask.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(vw, vh);
      mask.addComponent(Mask).type = Mask.Type.GRAPHICS_RECT;
      this.scrollArea = { x: px + 18, y: py + 104, w: vw, h: vh };
      const content = this.node('RankingRows', mask);
      this.scrollNode = content;
      const ink = content.addComponent(Graphics),
        originX = this.w / 2 - vw / 2,
        originY = this.h / 2 - vh / 2;
      rows.forEach((row, i) => {
        const own = playerId(row) === playerId(online.identity),
          y = originY + rowH * (i + 0.5);
        if (own) this.plate(ink, originX, y - 16, vw, 32, '#29423c', C.mint, 4);
        else this.rect(ink, originX, y + 17, vw, 1, '#8ba0ab22');
        if (row.rank >= 1 && row.rank <= 3) {
          const medal = this.node('RankMedal:' + row.rank, content),
            medalInk = medal.addComponent(Graphics);
          medal.setPosition(-vw / 2 + 12, vh / 2 - rowH * (i + 0.5));
          medalInk.strokeColor = col('#6b8499');
          medalInk.lineWidth = 5;
          for (const x of [-4, 4]) {
            medalInk.moveTo(x, -3);
            medalInk.lineTo(x * 1.5, -13);
            medalInk.stroke();
          }
          medalInk.fillColor = col(
            ['#e5b65c', '#c4d5e2', '#ca926c'][row.rank - 1],
          );
          medalInk.circle(0, 1, 9);
          medalInk.fill();
          medalInk.strokeColor = col('#fff0cf');
          medalInk.lineWidth = 1;
          medalInk.circle(0, 1, 6);
          medalInk.stroke();
        }
        const nameW = vw - 244,
          maxName = Math.max(2, Math.floor((nameW - 95) / 12)),
          name = Array.from(row.nickname || row.name || '飞行员');
        const caption = playerLabel({
          ...row,
          nickname:
            name.length > maxName
              ? name.slice(0, maxName).join('') + '…'
              : name.join(''),
        });
        for (const [captionText, x, width] of [
          [this.t('第', 'RANK ') + row.rank + this.t('名', ''), 26, 60],
          [caption, 92, nameW],
          [metric(row), vw - 146, 142],
        ] as const) {
          this.label(
            content,
            captionText,
            originX + x + width / 2,
            y,
            12,
            width,
            30,
            own ? C.mint : C.white,
          ).horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
        }
      });
      this.scrollMax = Math.max(0, rows.length * rowH - vh);
      if (!rows.length)
        label(
          online.message ||
            (online.ranking
              ? this.t('暂无成绩，等你创造纪录', 'SET THE FIRST RECORD')
              : this.t('正在读取…', 'LOADING…')),
          104 + vh / 2,
          12,
          C.dim,
        );
      const me = online.ranking?.me;
      label(
        me
          ? this.t('我的排名 第', 'MY RANK ') +
              me.rank +
              this.t('名 · ', ' · ') +
              metric(me)
          : this.t('你 · 尚无上榜成绩', 'YOU · NOT YET RANKED'),
        bottom - 24,
        11,
        C.mint,
      );
      button(
        'back',
        this.t('返回', 'BACK'),
        18,
        bottom,
        90,
        false,
        true,
        'back',
      );
      button(
        'refresh',
        this.t('刷新', 'REFRESH'),
        114,
        bottom,
        94,
        false,
        true,
        'refresh',
      );
      if (level)
        button(
          'ruleVersion',
          online.rankingVersion === 'night-campaign-v2'
            ? this.t('当前梯度版 ▾', 'CURRENT ▾')
            : online.rankingVersion === 'night-growth-v1'
              ? this.t('旧成长版 ▾', 'OLD GROWTH ▾')
              : this.t('原始历史版 ▾', 'ORIGINAL ▾'),
          pw - 160,
          bottom,
          142,
        );
      else
        this.label(
          n,
          this.t(
            `已加载 ${rows.length} 名 · 上下滑动`,
            `${rows.length} LOADED · SCROLL`,
          ),
          px + 216 + (pw - 234) / 2,
          py + bottom + 22,
          11,
          pw - 234,
          26,
          C.dim,
        );
      return;
    }
    if (online.panel === 'history') {
      const summary = online.history?.summary;
      label(
        summary
          ? this.t('真人对战 ', 'MATCHES ') +
              summary.games +
              this.t(' · 胜 ', ' · W ') +
              summary.wins +
              this.t('  负 ', '  L ') +
              summary.losses +
              this.t('  平 ', '  D ') +
              summary.draws +
              ' · ' +
              Math.round(summary.winRate * 1000) / 10 +
              '%'
          : this.t('仅记录开局双方都是真人的对局', 'HUMAN MATCHES ONLY'),
        63,
        12,
        C.mint,
      );
      const rows = online.history?.matches || [],
        parent = n,
        oldG = g,
        list = this.listViewport(
          n,
          'history',
          { x: px + 18, y: py + 85, w: pw - 36, h: bottom - 91 },
          rows.length * 66,
        );
      n = list.n;
      g = list.g;
      rows.forEach((match, i) => {
        const y = 86 + i * 66 - list.offset,
          outcome =
            match.outcome === 'win'
              ? this.t('胜利', 'WIN')
              : match.outcome === 'loss'
                ? this.t('失败', 'LOSS')
                : this.t('平局', 'DRAW');
        this.plate(g, px + 18, py + y, pw - 36, 52, '#1c2a2e', C.line, 5);
        this.label(
          n,
          outcome +
            ' · ' +
            (match.opponent.nickname || match.opponent.name || '飞行员') +
            '  +' +
            match.points,
          px + pw / 2,
          py + y + 15,
          13,
          pw - 60,
          26,
          match.outcome === 'win'
            ? '#75db9e'
            : match.outcome === 'loss'
              ? C.red
              : C.amber,
        );
        const date = new Date(match.finishedAt).toLocaleString(
          this.lang === 'zh' ? 'zh-CN' : 'en-GB',
          {
            month: '2-digit',
            day: '2-digit',
            hour: '2-digit',
            minute: '2-digit',
          },
        );
        this.label(
          n,
          date +
            ' · ' +
            formatTime(match.durationMs) +
            (match.reason === 'disconnect'
              ? this.t(' · 掉线结算', ' · DISCONNECT')
              : match.reason === 'abandoned'
                ? this.t(' · 双方离开', ' · ABANDONED')
                : ''),
          px + pw / 2,
          py + y + 36,
          10,
          pw - 60,
          22,
          C.dim,
        );
      });
      list.finish();
      n = parent;
      g = oldG;
      if (!rows.length)
        label(
          online.message ||
            (online.busy
              ? this.t('正在读取…', 'LOADING…')
              : this.t('暂无真人对战记录', 'NO HUMAN MATCHES YET')),
          144,
          13,
          C.dim,
        );
      button(
        'back',
        this.t('返回', 'BACK'),
        18,
        bottom,
        90,
        false,
        true,
        'back',
      );
      button(
        'history',
        this.t('刷新', 'REFRESH'),
        114,
        bottom,
        94,
        false,
        true,
        'refresh',
      );
      if (online.history?.nextCursor)
        button(
          'historyMore',
          this.t('加载更多', 'LOAD MORE'),
          pw - 150,
          bottom,
          132,
          false,
          true,
          'next',
        );
      return;
    }
    if (online.panel === 'menu') {
      const waiting = online.rooms.filter(
        (room) => room.status === 'waiting',
      ).length;
      label(
        online.message ||
          this.t('等待中 ', 'WAITING ') +
            waiting +
            this.t(' · 对战中 ', ' · PLAYING ') +
            (online.rooms.length - waiting),
        61,
        12,
        C.amber,
      );
      const rows = online.rooms.slice(
        online.roomPage * count,
        (online.roomPage + 1) * count,
      );
      rows.forEach((room, i) => {
        const y = 81 + i * 52;
        this.rect(g, px + 20, py + y, pw - 40, 48, '#1a2d3944');
        if (this.missionArtwork[0])
          this.coverImage(
            n,
            'RoomTerrain-' + room.code,
            this.missionArtwork[0],
            px + 24,
            py + y + 5,
            38,
            38,
          );
        else
          this.controlIcon(
            g,
            room.canJoin ? 'lobby' : 'mission',
            px + 43,
            py + y + 23,
            22,
            room.canJoin ? C.amber : C.mint,
          );
        const names = room.players
            .map((player) => player.nickname || player.name || '飞行员')
            .join(' / '),
