          textW = pw - 176;
        const name = this.label(
          n,
          names,
          px + 70 + textW / 2,
          py + y + 14,
          13,
          textW,
          24,
          C.white,
        );
        name.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
        const detail = this.label(
          n,
          '#' +
            room.code +
            '  ·  ' +
            room.playerCount +
            '/2  ·  ' +
            (room.status === 'waiting'
              ? room.canJoin
                ? this.t('等待加入', 'WAITING')
                : this.t('等待开战', 'READY ROOM')
              : this.t('对战中', 'IN BATTLE')),
          px + 70 + textW / 2,
          py + y + 35,
          10,
          textW,
          20,
          C.dim,
        );
        detail.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
        button(
          (room.canJoin ? 'joinCode:' : 'watchCode:') + room.code,
          room.canJoin ? this.t('加入', 'JOIN') : this.t('观战', 'WATCH'),
          pw - 102,
          y + 2,
          82,
        );
      });
      if (!rows.length) {
        const yy = ph < 280 ? 78 : ph < 340 ? 108 : 144;
        if (ph >= 280)
          this.controlIcon(g, 'lobby', px + pw / 2, py + yy, 34, C.line);
        label(
          this.t('空域待命，等你出发', 'THE SKIES ARE QUIET'),
          yy + 32,
          16,
          C.white,
        );
        if (ph >= 340)
          label(
            this.t(
              '创建房间邀请好友，或先进行人机练习',
              'CREATE A ROOM FOR FRIENDS OR PRACTICE WITH A BOT',
            ),
            yy + 58,
            11,
            C.dim,
          );
      }
      const cw = (pw - 60) / 4;
      for (const [i, [id, name]] of [
        ['friend', this.t('创建房间', 'CREATE')],
        ['join', this.t('加入房间', 'JOIN ROOM')],
        ['match', this.t('随机匹配', 'RANDOM')],
        ['bot', this.t('人机对战', 'BOT')],
      ].entries())
        button(
          id,
          name,
          18 + i * (cw + 8),
          bottom - 52,
          cw,
          true,
          false,
          pw >= 660 ? ['plus', 'join', 'match', 'bot'][i] : '',
        );
      this.rect(g, px + 20, py + bottom - 4, pw - 40, 1, '#8ba0ab33');
      button(
        'back',
        this.t('返回', 'BACK'),
        18,
        bottom,
        82,
        false,
        true,
        'back',
      );
      button(
        'history',
        this.t('对战记录', 'RECORDS'),
        106,
        bottom,
        108,
        false,
        true,
        'history',
      );
      button(
        online.resumeCode ? 'resumeRoom' : 'roomsRefresh',
        online.resumeCode
          ? this.t('重连', 'REJOIN')
          : pw < 500
            ? ''
            : this.t('刷新', 'REFRESH'),
        224,
        bottom,
        Math.min(online.resumeCode ? 110 : 80, pw - 382),
        false,
        true,
        online.resumeCode ? '' : 'refresh',
      );
      button(
        'roomPrev',
        '',
        pw - 150,
        bottom,
        44,
        false,
        false,
        'back',
        online.roomPage === 0,
      );
      button(
        'roomNext',
        '',
        pw - 62,
        bottom,
        44,
        false,
        false,
        'next',
        online.rooms.length <= (online.roomPage + 1) * count,
      );
      this.label(
        n,
        `${online.roomPage + 1}/${Math.max(1, Math.ceil(online.rooms.length / count))}`,
        px + pw - 84,
        py + bottom + 22,
        11,
        44,
        24,
        C.dim,
      );
      return;
    }
    const room = online.room;
    label(
      room
        ? this.t('房间号 ', 'ROOM ') +
            room.code +
            (room.visibility === 'private'
              ? this.t(' · 私密', ' · PRIVATE')
              : '')
        : online.busy
          ? this.t('正在连接…', 'CONNECTING…')
          : '',
      78,
      20,
      C.amber,
    );
    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
      const player = room?.players[i],
        own = player && playerId(player) === playerId(online.identity),
        y = 117 + i * 34;
      if (own)
        this.plate(g, px + 18, py + y - 14, pw - 36, 30, '#29423c', C.mint, 5);
      label(
        player
          ? playerLabel(player) +
              (own ? this.t(' · 你', ' · YOU') : '') +
              ' · ' +
              (player.ready
                ? this.t('已准备', 'READY')
                : this.t('未准备', 'NOT READY'))
          : this.t('等待另一位飞行员', 'WAITING FOR ANOTHER PILOT'),
        y,
        13,
        own ? C.mint : C.white,
      );
    }
    label(
      online.message ||
        this.t(
          '把房间号告诉好友，双方准备后开始',
          'TELL YOUR FRIEND THE ROOM NUMBER · BOTH READY TO START',
        ),
      bottom - 25,
      11,
      C.dim,
    );
    const cw = Math.min(180, (pw - 52) / 3);
    button(
      'backLobby',
      this.t('离开房间', 'LEAVE'),
      18,
      bottom,
      cw,
      false,
      false,
      'back',
    );
    if (room?.status === 'waiting' && !online.spectator) {
      button('chatInvite', this.t('大厅邀请', 'INVITE'), 26 + cw, bottom, cw);
      button(
        'ready',
        room.players[room.you]?.ready
          ? this.t('取消准备', 'CANCEL READY')
          : this.t('准备', 'READY UP'),
        pw - 18 - cw,
        bottom,
        cw,
      );
    }
  }
  renderModal(key: string, s: Simulation, world: World) {
    const family = key.startsWith('online:')
      ? key.split(':').slice(0, 2).join(':')
      : key.split(':')[0];
    if (
      family === 'online:menu' &&
      this.modalFamily === family &&
      this.modal &&
      this.lobbyComposer &&
      isValid(this.lobbyComposer.node)
    ) {
      if (
        key !== this.modalKey ||
        this.lobbyStamp !==
          `${this.online!.revision}:${this.online!.chatRevision}`
      ) {
        this.renderLobby(this.modal);
        this.modalKey = key;
      }
      return;
    }
    if (family === this.modalFamily && this.editing) return;
    if (
      s.phase !== 'playing' &&
      this.online &&
      this.onlineRevision !== this.online.revision
    ) {
      this.onlineRevision = this.online.revision;
      this.modalKey = '';
    }
    if (key === this.modalKey) return;
    this.buttons = this.buttons.filter((b) => {
      if (b.label.node.parent !== this.root && !this.isGlobalAction(b.id)) {
        return false;
      }
      return true;
    });
    const q = this.quartermaster,
      semantic = [
        ['settings', 'help', 'supply', 'advert'].includes(family)
          ? key
          : family,
        this.lang,
        family === 'online:ranking'
          ? [
              this.online?.board,
              this.online?.level,
              this.online?.rankingVersion,
            ].join(':')
          : '',
        family === 'online:create' ? this.online?.privateRoom : '',
        key.startsWith('progression:') ? q?.page : '',
        q?.section,
        q?.term,
        q?.mode,
        q?.selectedSkill,
        q?.detail,
        q?.info,
        !!q?.confirmation,
        q?.leaveTo !== undefined,
        q?.gift
          ? q.gift.started
            ? 'opening'
            : q.gift.opened
              ? 'rewards'
              : 'received'
          : '',
      ].join('|');
    const animate = !this.reducedEffects && semantic !== this.semanticKey;
    this.semanticKey = semantic;
    this.listAreas = [];
    const previous = this.modal;
    if (previous) {
      for (const field of previous.getComponentsInChildren(EditBox))
        field.enabled = false;
      const opacity = previous.getComponent(UIOpacity);
      if (animate && opacity) {
        Tween.stopAllByTarget(opacity);
        tween(opacity)
          .to(0.12, { opacity: 0 })
          .call(() => {
            if (isValid(previous)) previous.destroy();
          })
          .start();
      } else previous.destroy();
    }
    this.pressedButton = '';
    this.modalFamily = family;
    this.modal = undefined;
    this.surfaces = undefined;
    this.scrollNode = undefined;
    this.scroll = 0;
    this.scrollMax = 0;
    this.modalKey = key;
    if (!key) return;
    const n = this.node('Panel', this.root);
    this.modal = n;
    const opacity = n.addComponent(UIOpacity);
    if (animate) {
      opacity.opacity = 0;
      tween(opacity).to(0.16, { opacity: 255 }, { easing: 'quadOut' }).start();
    }
    this.node('Backdrop', n).addComponent(Graphics);
    this.surfaces = this.node('Surfaces', n);
    const g = this.node('Ink', n).addComponent(Graphics);
    if (
      this.quartermaster &&
      (key === 'home' ||
        key.startsWith('progression:') ||
        key === 'success' ||
        key === 'failure' ||
        key === 'duelResult')
    ) {
      new ProgressionView(
        this,
        this.quartermaster,
        this.progressionArt,
        n,
        g,
        this.surfaces,
      ).render(s, ['success', 'failure', 'duelResult'].includes(key));
      return;
    }
    if (
      this.quartermaster &&
      (key === 'missions' || key.startsWith('online:'))
    ) {
      const missions = key === 'missions',
        required = key.startsWith('online:menu')
          ? ['skins', 'night-ui-serif', '03-mode-selection']
          : [];
      if (
        new ProgressionView(
          this,
          this.quartermaster,
          this.progressionArt,
          n,
          g,
          this.surfaces,
        ).loading(
          required,
          missions ? 'homeMenu' : 'online:back',
          missions &&
            (this.missionArtwork.length !== 3 || this.dayArtwork.length !== 3),
          missions &&
            ['mission-atlas', 'mission-day-atlas'].some((name) =>
              this.artworkRetries.has(name),
            ),
        )
      )
        return;
    }
    if (key === 'entering') {
      const pw = Math.min(480, this.w - 48),
        px = (this.w - pw) / 2,
        py = this.h / 2 - 75;
      this.panelFrame(g, px, py, pw, 150, C.amber, '#050f1d38');
      this.label(
        n,
        this.t('正在进入战区', 'ENTERING THE BATTLEFIELD'),
        this.w / 2,
        py + 54,
        25,
        pw - 30,
        46,
        C.amber,
      ).isBold = true;
      this.label(
        n,
        this.t(
          '火控系统就绪 · 祝行动顺利',
          'FIRE CONTROL READY · GOOD HUNTING',
        ),
        this.w / 2,
        py + 105,
        13,
        pw - 30,
        32,
        C.mint,
      );
      return;
    }
    if (key.startsWith('online:') || key === 'duelResult') {
      this.renderOnline(n, g, s, key === 'duelResult');
      return;
    }
    if (key === 'home' || key === 'missions') {
      this.renderHome(n, g, s);
      return;
    }
    if (key.startsWith('report:')) {
      const pw = Math.min(1060, this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - 24),
        ph = Math.min(590, this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - 20),
        px =
          this.safe.left + (this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - pw) / 2,
        py =
          this.safe.top + (this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - ph) / 2;
      this.panelFrame(g, px, py, pw, ph, C.mint, '#071724f5');
      this.label(
        n,
        this.t('战况', 'BATTLE REPORT'),
        px + (pw - 96) / 2,
        py + 24,
        20,
        pw - 120,
        32,
        C.mint,
      ).isBold = true;
      this.button(
        'close',
        this.t('关闭', 'CLOSE'),
        px + pw - 82,
        py + 4,
        70,
        44,
        n,
      ).fontSize = 12;
      for (const [i, id] of ['reportOverview', 'equipment'].entries()) {
        const x = px + 12 + i * 110,
          selected = this.equipmentOpen === (i === 1);
        this.plate(
          g,
          x,
          py + 49,
          104,
          44,
          selected ? '#23404b' : '#0b1d2a',
          selected ? C.mint : C.line,
          3,
        );
        this.button(
          id,
          i ? this.t('装配', 'LOADOUT') : this.t('概况', 'OVERVIEW'),
          x,
          py + 49,
          104,
          44,
          n,
        ).fontSize = 13;
      }
      if (!this.equipmentOpen) {
        if (s.duelMode && this.online) {
          this.online.combatants.forEach((player, i) => {
            const cw = (pw - 36) / 2,
              x = px + 12 + i * (cw + 12),
              units = s.units.filter((u) =>
                i === 0 ? u.friendly : !u.friendly,
              ),
              alive = units.filter((u) => u.hp > 0).length;
            this.plate(
              g,
              x,
              py + 102,
              cw,
              ph - 116,
              '#071725',
              i ? C.amber : C.mint,
              4,
            );
            this.label(
              n,
              playerLabel(player).split(' #')[0],
              x + cw / 2,
              py + 137,
              16,
              cw - 24,
              58,
              i ? C.amber : C.mint,
            );
            this.label(
              n,
              this.t(
                `已损失 ${units.length - alive} · 剩余 ${alive}`,
                `LOST ${units.length - alive} · REMAINING ${alive}`,
              ),
              x + cw / 2,
              py + 189,
              14,
              cw - 24,
              44,
              C.white,
            );
          });
          return;
        }
        const columns = [0.17, 0.41, 0.57, 0.73, 0.9],
          headings =
            this.lang === 'zh'
              ? ['类型', '总数', '存活', '消灭', '未出场']
              : ['TYPE', 'TOTAL', 'ALIVE', 'LOST', 'PENDING'];
        headings.forEach((v, i) =>
          this.label(
            n,
            v,
            px + pw * columns[i],
            py + 113,
            12,
            pw * 0.18,
            22,
            C.dim,
          ),
        );
        const names = {
          light: ['轻车', 'LIGHT'],
          turret: ['炮台', 'TURRET'],
          heavy: ['重甲', 'ARMOR'],
          rescue: ['救援车', 'RESCUE'],
          escort: ['护卫', 'ESCORT'],
        };
        battleCounts(s, false, s.duelMode).forEach((row, i) =>
          [
            text(names[row.kind], this.lang),
            row.total,
            row.alive,
            row.destroyed,
            row.pending,
          ].forEach((v, j) =>
            this.label(
              n,
              String(v),
              px + pw * columns[j],
              py + 140 + i * 23,
              13,
              pw * 0.18,
              23,
              j === 3 ? C.mint : C.white,
            ),
          ),
        );
        this.label(
          n,
          this.t('查看时战斗继续', 'BATTLE CONTINUES'),
          px + pw / 2,
          py + ph - 17,
          11,
          pw - 24,
          22,
          C.amber,
        );
        return;
      }
      const gap = 12,
        cw = (pw - 36) / 2,
        bodyH = ph - 110,
        font = ph < 380 ? 11 : 14;
      const sides = [s.pilotState(), this.online?.view?.enemyEquipment];
      sides.forEach((pilot, side) => {
        const x = px + 12 + side * (cw + gap),
          title =
            s.duelMode && this.online
              ? playerLabel(this.online.combatants[side]).split(' #')[0]
              : side
                ? this.t('对方', 'OPPONENT')
                : this.t('我方', 'YOU');
        this.plate(g, x, py + 100, cw, bodyH, '#0b1d2ae8', C.line, 4);
        let lines: string[] = [title];
        if (!pilot) {
          lines.push(
            this.t(
              s.duelMode ? '对方尚未提供装配信息' : '地面敌军 · 无军需插槽',
              s.duelMode
                ? 'Opponent equipment is not available'
                : 'Ground forces · no loadout slots',
            ),
          );
        } else {
          const load = pilot.loadout,
            effects = load.effects,
            w = WEAPONS[pilot.selected],
            ordinary = pilot.selected < 4,
            preview = attackPreview(ordinary ? effects : []),
            shots = preview.shots;
          const names = effects.map((effect) =>
            this.lang === 'zh'
              ? CATALOG.find((p) => p.effect === effect)!.name
              : effectEnglish[effect],
          );
          const damage = ordinary
            ? w.damage *
              (1 +
                (load.level >= 3 ? 0.05 : 0) +
                (effects.includes('power') ? 0.15 : 0))
            : w.damage;
          const interval = ordinary
            ? (w.interval *
                (effects.includes('rate') ? 0.88 : 1) *
                (load.level >= 15 ? 0.95 : 1)) /
              (pilot.buff?.kind === 'rate' ? 1.3 : 1)
            : w.interval;
          const radius =
            pilot.selected >= 1 &&
            pilot.selected <= 3 &&
            effects.includes('radius')
              ? w.radius * 1.15
              : w.radius;
          const heat = w.heat * preview.heatMultiplier;
          const fmt = (v: number) => Number(v.toFixed(2)).toString(),
            skill = effects.includes('tracking')
              ? 'tracking'
              : effects.includes('emp')
                ? 'emp'
                : undefined;
          const remaining = Math.max(0, pilot.skill.activeUntil - s.time),
            cooldown = Math.max(0, pilot.skill.readyAt - s.time);
          const gun = pilot.guns[pilot.selected];
          lines = [
            title + ` · Lv.${load.level}`,
            this.t('装配：', 'Equipped: ') +
              (names.join(' / ') || this.t('默认配置', 'Default')),
            this.t('装配武器：', 'Equipped weapon: ') + text(w.name, this.lang),
            this.t('默认 → 当前：', 'Default → current: ') +
              this.t(
                `每轮 1 → ${shots} 弹 · 耗弹 1`,
                `Projectiles 1 → ${shots} · ammo 1`,
              ),
            pilot.selected === 5
              ? this.t(
                  '持续3秒 · 每秒80 · 总240 · 重甲×0.85',
                  '3s beam · 80/s · total 240 · armor ×0.85',
                )
              : pilot.selected === 4
                ? this.t(
                    '3架无人机 · 12秒后坠毁 · 每架最多144伤害',
                    '3 drones · crash after 12s · up to 144 damage each',
                  )
                : shots > 1
                  ? this.t(
                      `主弹 ${fmt(damage)} ＋ ${shots - 1}枚副弹各 ${fmt(damage * preview.secondaryStrength)}`,
                      `Primary ${fmt(damage)} + ${shots - 1} secondary × ${fmt(damage * preview.secondaryStrength)}`,
                    )
                  : this.t(
                      `基础伤害 ${fmt(w.damage)} → ${fmt(damage)} / 发`,
                      `Base damage ${fmt(w.damage)} → ${fmt(damage)} / shot`,
                    ),
            this.t(
              `间隔 ${fmt(w.interval)} → ${fmt(interval)} 秒`,
              `Interval ${fmt(w.interval)} → ${fmt(interval)} s`,
            ),
            this.t(
              `热量 ${fmt(w.heat)} → ${fmt(heat)} / 次 · 范围 ${fmt(w.radius)} → ${fmt(radius)}`,
              `Heat ${fmt(w.heat)} → ${fmt(heat)} / attack · radius ${fmt(w.radius)} → ${fmt(radius)}`,
            ),
            this.t(
              `实时热量 ${Math.round(gun.heat)} · 武器冷却 ${fmt(gun.cooldown)} 秒`,
              `Heat ${Math.round(gun.heat)} · weapon cooldown ${fmt(gun.cooldown)} s`,
            ),
            this.t('武器 / 剩余弹药：', 'Weapons / ammo: ') +
              WEAPONS.filter(
                (_, i) =>
                  i < 4 || effects.includes(i === 4 ? 'drone' : 'laser'),
              )
                .map((weapon) => {
                  const i = WEAPONS.indexOf(weapon);
                  return (
                    text(weapon.name, this.lang) +
                    ' ' +
                    (weapon.ammo === Infinity ? '∞' : pilot.guns[i].ammo)
                  );
                })
                .join(' / '),
            this.t('技能：', 'Skill: ') +
              (skill
                ? (this.lang === 'zh'
                    ? CATALOG.find((p) => p.effect === skill)!.name
                    : effectEnglish[skill]) +
                  this.t(
                    remaining
                      ? ` · 生效 ${Math.ceil(remaining)} 秒`
                      : cooldown
                        ? ` · 冷却 ${Math.ceil(cooldown)} 秒`
                        : ' · 就绪',
                    remaining
                      ? ` · active ${Math.ceil(remaining)}s`
                      : cooldown
                        ? ` · cooldown ${Math.ceil(cooldown)}s`
                        : ' · ready',
                  )
                : this.t('未装配', 'None')),
            this.t('临时增益：', 'Temporary buff: ') +
              (pilot.buff
                ? this.t(
                    pilot.buff.kind === 'rate' ? '射速 +30%' : '追踪',
                    pilot.buff.kind === 'rate' ? 'Fire rate +30%' : 'Tracking',
                  ) + ` ${Math.ceil(pilot.buff.remaining)}s`
                : this.t('无', 'None')),
          ];
          if (load.skipped.length)
            lines.push(
              this.t('未生效：', 'Unavailable: ') +
                load.skipped
                  .map((id) =>
                    this.lang === 'zh'
                      ? CATALOG.find((p) => p.id === id)?.name || id
                      : id,
                  )
                  .join(' / '),
            );
        }
        const label = this.label(
          n,
          lines.join('\n'),
          x + cw / 2,
          py + 100 + bodyH / 2,
          font,
          cw - 20,
          bodyH - 12,
          C.white,
        );
        label.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
        label.verticalAlign = Label.VerticalAlign.TOP;
        label.lineHeight = font * 1.45;
        label.overflow = Label.Overflow.SHRINK;
      });
      return;
    }
    if (key.startsWith('help')) {
      this.renderGuide(n, g);
      return;
    }
    if (key.startsWith('countdown:')) {
      const x =
          this.safe.left + (this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right) / 2,
        y = this.safe.top + (this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom) / 2,
        r = Math.min(88, (this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - 100) / 2);
      this.rect(
        this.modal?.getChildByName('Backdrop')?.getComponent(Graphics) || g,
        0,
        0,
        this.w,
        this.h,
        '#05090c55',
      );
      g.strokeColor = col('#91e6cb66');
      g.lineWidth = 2;
      g.circle(x - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y, r);
      g.stroke();
      this.label(n, key.slice(10), x, y - 14, 72, r * 2, 104, C.mint).isBold =
        true;
      this.label(
        n,
        this.t('准备继续', 'GET READY'),
        x,
        y + 45,
        18,
        r * 2,
        32,
        C.mint,
      );
      return;
    }
    if (key.startsWith('supply:')) {
      const choice = this.pendingSupply,
        pw = Math.min(760, this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - 32),
        ph = Math.min(320, this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - 74),
        px =
          this.safe.left + (this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - pw) / 2,
        py =
          this.safe.top +
          58 +
          (this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - 58 - ph) / 2;
      this.panelFrame(g, px, py, pw, ph, C.amber);
      const offers = [
        [
          'tracking',
          this.t('自动追踪', 'AUTO TRACKING'),
          this.t(
            '普通炮弹自动追踪敌人\n持续 60 秒',
            'Regular rounds track foes\n60 seconds',
          ),
        ],
        [
          'ammo',
          this.t('追踪弹 +2', 'HOMING +2'),
          this.t(
            '点选敌人，再按开火\n立即补充 2 发',
            'Tap an enemy, then FIRE\nAdds 2 rounds',
          ),
        ],
        [
          'rate',
          this.t('射速提升', 'FIRE RATE'),
          this.t(
            '普通炮射速提升 30%\n持续 60 秒',
            'Fire rate +30%\n60 seconds',
          ),
        ],
        [
          'restock',
          this.t('弹药补给', 'RESTOCK'),
          this.t(
            '榴弹 +60 · 重炮 +24\n燃烧弹 +6',
            'HE +60 · HEAVY +24\nFIRE +6',
          ),
        ],
      ];
      const selected = offers.find(([id]) => id === this.supplySelection)!;
      this.label(
        n,
        this.t('选择战斗支援', 'CHOOSE COMBAT SUPPORT'),
        px + pw / 2,
        py + 19,
        20,
        pw - 32,
        28,
        C.white,
      ).isBold = true;
      this.label(
        n,
        s.buff
          ? this.t(
              '支援已生效，结束后可再次领取',
              'SUPPORT ACTIVE · WAIT UNTIL IT ENDS',
            )
          : choice
            ? this.t(
                '支援已获得 · 选择后直接启用',
                'SUPPORT EARNED · SELECT AND ACTIVATE',
              )
            : this.t(
                '可选支援 · 完整观看广告后生效',
                'OPTIONAL · WATCH A FULL AD TO ACTIVATE',
              ),
        px + pw / 2,
        py + 43,
        11,
        pw - 32,
        18,
        choice ? C.mint : C.dim,
      );
      const cw = (pw - 56) / 4,
        cy = py + 58,
        ch = ph - 138,
        icons = ch >= 136;
      for (const [i, [id, title, detail]] of offers.entries()) {
        const x = px + 16 + i * (cw + 8),
          active = id === this.supplySelection;
        this.plate(
          g,
          x,
          cy,
          cw,
          ch,
          active ? '#393528' : '#1c2a2e',
          active ? C.amber : C.line,
          3,
        );
        if (icons) this.supplyIcon(g, id, x + cw / 2, cy + 22);
        const label = this.button(
          'reward:' + id,
          (active ? '✓ ' : '') + title,
          x,
          cy,
          cw,
          ch,
          n,
        );
        label.node.setPosition(
          x + cw / 2 - this.w / 2,
          this.h / 2 - cy - (icons ? 50 : 15),
        );
        label.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(cw - 8, 22);
        label.fontSize = pw < 600 ? 12 : 15;
        label.color = col(active ? C.amber : C.white);
        label.isBold = true;
        this.label(
          n,
          detail,
          x + cw / 2,
          cy + (icons ? 84 : 43),
          icons ? 14 : 11,
          cw - 10,
          icons ? 42 : 32,
          C.white,
        );
        if (icons)
          this.label(
            n,
            active
              ? this.t('已选择', 'SELECTED')
              : this.t('点击选择', 'TAP TO SELECT'),
            x + cw / 2,
            cy + ch - 15,
            11,
            cw - 12,
            20,
            active ? C.amber : C.dim,
          );
      }
      this.label(
        n,
        this.supplyMessage ||
          this.t(
            `已选：${selected[1]} · 启用后立即返回战斗`,
            `${selected[1]} · RESUME IMMEDIATELY`,
          ),
        px + pw / 2,
        py + ph - 66,
        11,
        pw - 32,
        18,
        this.supplyMessage ? C.red : C.dim,
      );
      const bw = (pw - 40) * 0.42,
        y = py + ph - 52;
      this.plate(g, px + 16, y, bw, 44, '#1c2a2e', C.line, 3);
      const skip = this.button(
        'supplyLater',
        choice
          ? this.t('留到下次，继续战斗', 'SAVE FOR LATER · RESUME')
          : this.t('不使用，继续战斗', 'SKIP · RESUME BATTLE'),
        px + 16,
        y,
        bw,
        44,
        n,
      );
      skip.fontSize = pw < 600 ? 11 : 13;
      const bx = px + 24 + bw,
        ww = pw - 40 - bw;
      this.plate(
        g,
        bx,
        y,
        ww,
        44,
        s.buff ? '#1c2a2e' : C.amber,
        s.buff ? C.line : undefined,
        3,
      );
      const watch = this.button(
        'supplyWatch',
        s.buff
          ? this.t('支援生效中', 'SUPPORT ACTIVE')
          : choice
            ? this.t('启用并返回战斗', 'ACTIVATE & RESUME')
            : this.t('看广告并启用', 'WATCH AD & ACTIVATE'),
        bx,
        y,
        ww,
        44,
        n,
      );
      watch.fontSize = pw < 600 ? 13 : 16;
      watch.color = col(s.buff ? C.dim : C.action);
      watch.isBold = !s.buff;
      return;
    }
    if (key === 'exit') {
      const pw = Math.min(440, this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - 32),
        ph = 178;
      const px =
        this.safe.left + (this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - pw) / 2;
      const py =
        this.safe.top +
        58 +
        (this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - 58 - ph) / 2;
      this.panelFrame(g, px, py, pw, ph, C.red);
      this.label(
        n,
        this.t('返回基地？', 'RETURN TO BASE?'),
        px + pw / 2,
        py + 30,
        22,
        pw - 32,
        34,
      ).isBold = true;
      this.label(
        n,
        s.duelMode
          ? this.t(
              '离开后30秒内可重回，超时将按失败结算。',
              'REJOIN WITHIN 30 SECONDS OR THE MATCH COUNTS AS A LOSS.',
            )
          : this.t(
              '将会中断本次游戏并退出。',
              'This will end the current battle and exit.',
            ),
        px + pw / 2,
        py + 76,
        14,
        pw - 32,
        40,
        C.dim,
      );
      const bw = (pw - 40) / 2;
      for (const [i, id] of ['exitCancel', 'exitConfirm'].entries()) {
        const x = px + 16 + i * (bw + 8),
          y = py + ph - 56;
        this.plate(
          g,
          x,
          y,
          bw,
          44,
          i ? '#382730' : C.amber,
          i ? '#8f4853' : undefined,
          6,
        );
        this.button(
          id,
          i
            ? this.t('确认退出', 'CONFIRM EXIT')
            : this.t('继续战斗', 'CONTINUE'),
          x,
          y,
          bw,
          44,
          n,
        ).color = col(i ? '#ff9b9f' : C.action);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (key === 'pause' || key.startsWith('settings')) {
      this.renderSettings(n, g, s, world, key);
      return;
    }
    if (key.startsWith('advert:')) {
      const pw = Math.min(420, this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - 32);
      const ph = Math.min(244, this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - 74);
      const px =
        this.safe.left + (this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - pw) / 2;
      const py =
        this.safe.top +
        58 +
        (this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - 58 - ph) / 2;
      this.rect(
        this.modal?.getChildByName('Backdrop')?.getComponent(Graphics) || g,
        0,
        0,
        this.w,
        this.h,
        '#05090cb8',
      );
      this.plate(g, px, py, pw, ph, C.ink, C.mint, 10);
      this.label(
        n,
        this.advert?.mock
          ? this.t('模拟广告', 'SIMULATED AD')
          : this.t('广告加载中', 'LOADING AD'),
        px + pw / 2,
        py + 34,
        24,
        pw - 32,
        42,
        C.white,
      ).isBold = true;
      this.label(
        n,
        this.advert?.kind === 'supply'
          ? this.t(
              '完整观看后启用所选支援，并返回战斗',
              'COMPLETE TO ACTIVATE SUPPORT AND RESUME',
            )
          : this.t(
              `完成后解锁 ${nextZoomLimit(this.zoomLimit)}× 放大上限`,
              `COMPLETE TO UNLOCK ${nextZoomLimit(this.zoomLimit)}× ZOOM`,
            ),
        px + pw / 2,
        py + 80,
        14,
        pw - 32,
        36,
        C.mint,
      );
      if (this.advert?.mock) {
        const bw = (pw - 56) / 2,
          y = py + ph - 76;
        const b = this.button(
          'adClose',
          this.t('完成模拟广告', 'COMPLETE MOCK AD'),
          px + 24,
          y,
          bw,
          44,
          n,
        );
        this.plate(g, px + 24, y, bw, 44, C.mint, undefined, 5);
        b.color = col(C.action);
        b.isBold = true;
        this.plate(g, px + 32 + bw, y, bw, 44, '#1b3038', C.line, 5);
        this.button(
          'adCancel',
          this.t('取消 · 返回选择', 'CANCEL · GO BACK'),
          px + 32 + bw,
          y,
          bw,
          44,
          n,
        );
      } else
        this.button(
          'adCancel',
          this.t('取消领取', 'CANCEL'),
          px + 24,
          py + ph - 76,
          pw - 48,
          44,
          n,
        );
      this.label(
        n,
        this.advert?.mock
          ? this.t('本次为网页模拟 · 无需等待', 'WEB SIMULATION · NO WAIT')
          : this.t('完成广告后领取奖励', 'COMPLETE THE AD TO CLAIM'),
        px + pw / 2,
        py + ph - 17,
        11,
        pw - 32,
        22,
        C.dim,
      );
      return;
    }
    const allies = s.units.filter((u) => u.friendly).length;
    const training = s.mission.mode === 'training';
    this.rect(
      this.modal?.getChildByName('Backdrop')?.getComponent(Graphics) || g,
      0,
      0,
      this.w,
      this.h,
      '#06111655',
    );
    const result = key === 'success' || key === 'failure';
    const pw = Math.min(
        result ? 720 : 880,
        this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - 32,
      ),
      ph = Math.min(
        result ? 340 : 540,
        this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - 72,
      );
    const px =
        this.safe.left + (this.w - this.safe.left - this.safe.right - pw) / 2,
      py =
        this.safe.top +
        58 +
        (this.h - this.safe.top - this.safe.bottom - 58 - ph) / 2;
    this.panelFrame(
      g,
      px,
      py,
      pw,
      ph,
      key === 'failure' ? C.red : C.mint,
      '#050f1d00',
    );
    const small = result || this.compact || this.w < 600,
      low = ph < 280;
    const heading =
      key === 'briefing'
        ? training
          ? this.t('火控热身', 'FIRE CONTROL')
          : this.t('夜航守望', 'NIGHT OVERWATCH')
        : key === 'success'
          ? training
            ? this.t('三靶清除，热身完成', 'THREE TARGETS CLEARED')
            : this.t('车队安全抵达', 'CONVOY EXTRACTED')
          : key === 'failure'
            ? training
              ? this.t('热身结束，再试一次', 'WARMUP ENDED · TRY AGAIN')
              : this.t('护送中断', 'ESCORT INTERRUPTED')
            : key === 'orientation'
              ? this.t('横屏，进入火控席', 'ROTATE TO LANDSCAPE')
              : key === 'mission'
                ? this.t('山谷公路撤离', 'VALLEY EVACUATION')
                : this.t('暂停', 'PAUSED');
    const headingLabel = this.label(
      n,
      heading,
      px + pw / 2,
      py + (small ? 29 : 48),
      small ? 23 : 36,
      pw - 56,
      small ? 46 : 60,
      C.white,
    );
    headingLabel.isBold = true;
    if (result)
      this.button('resultClose', '×', px + pw - 52, py + 4, 44, 44, n);
    const bottom = py + ph - 58;
    if (key === 'briefing' || key === 'mission') {
      const split = !small,
        contentTop = py + (low ? 52 : small ? 65 : 118);
      if (split) {
        this.label(
          n,
          'NIGHT 07  /  ' + text(s.mission.name, this.lang),
          px + pw / 2,
          py + 86,
          12,
          pw - 48,
          24,
          C.mint,
        );
        if (training) {
          for (const [i, target] of [
            this.t('◇ 静止目标 → 榴弹炮', '◇ STATIC → HE'),
            this.t('◇ 巡逻目标 → 弹着提前量', '◇ ROVER → LEAD THE SHOT'),
            this.t('◇ 重甲目标 → 重型榴弹炮', '◇ ARMOR → HEAVY'),
          ].entries())
            this.label(
              n,
              target,
              px + pw * 0.72,
              contentTop + 52 + i * 56,
              17,
              pw * 0.43,
              44,
              C.amber,
            );
        } else {
          this.routeGraphic(
            g,
            px + pw * 0.48,
            contentTop + 28,
            pw * 0.46,
            ph - 235,
            s.progress,
            s.mission.map,
            'night',
            s.mission.events,
          );
          this.label(
            n,
            this.t(
              '西岭 → 河谷桥 → 东岭营地',
              'WEST RIDGE → RIVER → EXTRACTION',
            ),
            px + pw * 0.71,
            py + ph - 104,
            13,
            pw * 0.5,
            32,
            C.dim,
          );
        }
      }
      const cx = split ? px + pw * 0.25 : px + pw / 2,
        cw = split ? pw * 0.42 : pw - 36;
      this.label(
        n,
        training
          ? this.t('60 秒内清除 3 种目标', 'CLEAR THREE TARGET TYPES IN 60s')
          : text(s.mission.name, this.lang) +
              ' · ' +
              this.t('护送清敌', 'ESCORT & CLEAR'),
        cx,
        contentTop + 14,
        small ? 16 : 21,
        cw,
        42,
        C.white,
      ).isBold = true;
      for (const [i, message] of [
        training
          ? this.t(
              '练瞄准，友军仍不能误伤',
              'Practice aiming; do not hit allies',
            )
          : this.t(
              `□  保护救援车与 ${FRIENDLY_POSTS.length} 处分散据点`,
              `□  Protect rescue & all ${FRIENDLY_POSTS.length} outposts`,
            ),
        text(s.mission.description, this.lang).replace(
          /^(白天|DAY) · /,
          this.t('夜航 · ', 'NIGHT OPS · '),
        ),
      ].entries())
        this.label(
          n,
          message,
          cx,
          contentTop + (low ? 52 : small ? 61 : 90) + (i - 0.5) * 24,
          small ? 14 : 17,
          cw,
          30,
          i === 0 ? C.mint : C.amber,
        );
      this.label(
        n,
        this.touch
          ? this.t(
              '左手拖动瞄准 · 右手按住开火',

          : this.t(
              '鼠标瞄准 · 按住左键开火 · 1 / 2 / 3 切枪',
              'Mouse aims · Hold left button · 1 / 2 / 3 weapons',

        cx,
        contentTop + (low ? 95 : small ? 116 : 159),
        small ? 12 : 14,
        cw,
        low ? 28 : 44,
        C.dim,
      );
      const primaryW =
        key === 'briefing'
          ? (pw - (training ? 56 : 64)) / (training ? 2 : 3)
          : Math.min(260, pw - 48);
      if (key === 'briefing') {
        this.button(
          training ? 'missionReturn' : 'missionNext',
          training
            ? this.t('返回护送', 'BACK TO ESCORT')
            : this.t('切换任务 ↻', 'CHANGE TASK ↻'),
          px + 24,
          bottom,
          primaryW,
          44,
          n,
        );
        if (!training)
          this.button(
            'training',
            this.t('火控热身', 'FIRE CONTROL'),
            px + 32 + primaryW,
            bottom,
            primaryW,
            44,
            n,
          );
      }
      this.button(
        key === 'briefing' ? 'start' : 'resume',
        key === 'briefing'
          ? training
            ? this.t('开始60秒 →', 'START 60s →')
            : this.t('护送开跑 →', 'BEGIN ESCORT →')
          : this.t('返回任务', 'RESUME'),
        key === 'briefing'
          ? px + (training ? 32 + primaryW : 40 + 2 * primaryW)
          : px + pw / 2 - primaryW / 2,
        bottom,
        primaryW,
        44,
        n,
      );
    } else if (key === 'success' || key === 'failure') {
      const success = key === 'success';
      this.emblem(
        g,
        px + 66,
        py + (low ? 73 : 78),
        low ? 15 : 19,
        success ? C.amber : '#c99580',
      );
      const lostOutpost = s.failedGroup !== undefined && s.failedGroup > 0;
      const mainThreat = Object.entries(s.damageByThreat).sort(
        (a, b) => b[1] - a[1],
      )[0];
      const threatAdvice =
        mainThreat &&
        {
          light: [
            `轻车造成 ${Math.round(mainThreat[1])} 伤害。下次用速射追踪，并瞄准前方。`,
            `Light patrol: ${Math.round(mainThreat[1])} damage. Track with Rapid; lead its movement.`,
          ],
          turret: [
            `炮台造成 ${Math.round(mainThreat[1])} 伤害。下次优先用榴弹炮清除固定威胁。`,
            `Turrets: ${Math.round(mainThreat[1])} damage. Clear fixed threats with Burst first.`,
          ],
          heavy: [
            `重甲造成 ${Math.round(mainThreat[1])} 伤害。下次用重炮，预留弹着提前量。`,
            `Armor: ${Math.round(mainThreat[1])} damage. Use Heavy and allow for flight time.`,
          ],
        }[mainThreat[0]];
      this.label(
        n,
        success
          ? this.t(
              `${training ? '热身' : '护送'}评价  ${s.rating}`,
              `${training ? 'WARMUP' : 'ESCORT'} RATING  ${s.rating}`,
            )
          : this.t(
              training
                ? '热身中断，请整备后再战'
                : s.failure === 'vehicle'
                  ? lostOutpost
                    ? `第 ${s.failedGroup} 据点全灭`
                    : '救援车被毁'
                  : '行动结束',
              training
                ? 'WARMUP ENDED · REGROUP'
                : s.failure === 'vehicle'
                  ? lostOutpost
                    ? `OUTPOST ${s.failedGroup} LOST`
                    : 'Rescue vehicle lost'
                  : 'Operation ended',
            ),
        px + pw / 2,
        py + (low ? 73 : small ? 75 : 120),
        small ? 24 : 44,
        pw - 40,
        low ? 36 : 60,
        success ? C.mint : C.red,
      ).isBold = true;
      const rankState: Record<string, [string, string]> = {
        finished: ['已同步', 'SYNCED'],
        assisted: ['辅助通关', 'ASSISTED'],
        unavailable: ['未同步', 'NOT SYNCED'],
        syncing: ['上传中', 'SYNCING'],
        recording: ['计时中', 'RECORDING'],
        practice: ['练习', 'PRACTICE'],
        'update-required': ['需更新', 'UPDATE'],
        'pending-sync': ['待同步', 'PENDING SYNC'],
        'needs-attention': ['待核对', 'CHECK REQUIRED'],
        'storage-unavailable': ['缓存失败', 'CACHE FAILED'],
      };
      const rank = rankState[this.online?.rankStatus || 'local'] || [
        '仅本地',
        'LOCAL',
      ];
      const statY = py + (low ? 110 : small ? 123 : 212),
        statW = (pw - 48) / 3,
        cleared = s.kills + s.friendlyKills;
      for (const [i, [value, caption]] of [
        [
          training
            ? `${s.time.toFixed(1)}s`
            : formatTime(Math.round((s.completionTime ?? s.time) * 1000)),

            ? this.trainingBest
              ? this.t(
                  `用时 · 最佳${this.trainingBest.time.toFixed(1)}s`,
                  `TIME · BEST ${this.trainingBest.time.toFixed(1)}s`,

              : this.t('热身用时', 'WARMUP TIME')
            : this.t(`本次用时 · ${rank[0]}`, `TIME · ${rank[1]}`),

        [
          training
            ? `${s.hitShots} / ${s.fired}`
            : `${cleared} / ${cleared + s.threatsRemaining}`,
          training
            ? this.t(
                `命中弹 / 发射弹 · 耗弹${s.ammoSpent}`,
                `HIT / FIRED · AMMO ${s.ammoSpent}`,
              )
            : this.t('清除威胁', 'THREATS'),
        ],
        [
          `${Math.round(s.friendlyDamage)}`,
          this.t('友方损伤', 'FRIENDLY DAMAGE'),
        ],
      ].entries()) {
        const x = px + 24 + statW * (i + 0.5);
        this.inset(
          g,
          x - statW / 2 + 4,
          statY - 20,
          statW - 8,
          low ? 63 : 68,
          i === 0 ? '#a98c4a80' : '#49657b99',
        );
        if (statW >= 170)
          this.icon(
            g,
            i === 0 ? 'clock' : i === 1 ? 'target' : 'battle',

            i === 0 ? C.amber : C.mint,

        this.label(
          n,

          statY,

          statW - 8,
          low ? 36 : 44,

      }
      if (ph >= 310)
        this.label(
          n,
          training
            ? this.t(
                `清靶 ${s.kills} / 3${this.trainingBest ? ` · 零友伤最佳 ${this.trainingBest.time.toFixed(1)}s / 耗弹${this.trainingBest.ammoSpent}` : ''}`,
                `CLEARED ${s.kills}/3${this.trainingBest ? ` · CLEAN BEST ${this.trainingBest.time.toFixed(1)}s / AMMO ${this.trainingBest.ammoSpent}` : ''}`,
              )
            : this.t(
                `空中 ${s.kills} · 地面 ${s.friendlyKills}`,
                `AIR ${s.kills} · GROUND ${s.friendlyKills}`,
              ),
          px + pw / 2,
          statY + 51,
          11,
          training ? pw - 48 : statW - 8,
          20,
          C.dim,
        );
      const comparison = !training && success ? this.timeComparison : undefined;
      const advice =
        !training && success && this.online?.rankStatus === 'assisted'
          ? this.t(
              '辅助通关已同步，不计入关卡最快榜',
              'ASSISTED CLEAR SYNCED · EXCLUDED FROM FASTEST TIMES',
            )
          : comparison
            ? this.t(
                `${comparison.newRecord ? (comparison.previousMs === undefined ? '首次完成 · 个人最佳' : '新纪录 · 个人最佳') : '个人最佳'} ${formatTime(comparison.bestMs)}${comparison.previousMs === undefined ? '' : ` · ${comparison.currentMs < comparison.previousMs ? '快' : comparison.currentMs > comparison.previousMs ? '慢' : '持平'}${comparison.currentMs === comparison.previousMs ? '' : ' ' + (Math.abs(comparison.currentMs - comparison.previousMs) / 1000).toFixed(2) + '秒'}`}`,
                `${comparison.newRecord ? 'NEW PERSONAL BEST' : 'PERSONAL BEST'} ${formatTime(comparison.bestMs)}${comparison.previousMs === undefined ? '' : ` · ${(comparison.currentMs - comparison.previousMs) / 1000}s VS PREVIOUS`}`,
              )
            : training
              ? text(trainingNextGoal(s), this.lang)
              : success
                ? s.rating === 'S'
                  ? this.t(
                      `${text(s.mission.name, this.lang)} · ${Math.floor(s.time)}s · 耗弹${s.ammoSpent}发。再战，减少耗弹。`,
                      `${text(s.mission.name, this.lang)} · ${Math.floor(s.time)}s · ${s.ammoSpent} rounds used. Try fewer rounds.`,
                    )
                  : this.t(
                      '下次目标：保护救援车，零友伤、零友军损失，争取 S。',
                      'Next: protect rescue, avoid friendly damage and losses to aim for S.',
                    )
                : s.failure === 'timeout'
                  ? this.t(
                      s.threatsRemaining > 0
                        ? `仍有 ${s.threatsRemaining} 个威胁未清除。巡视小地图，保护各组友军并清除全部敌人。`
                        : '待命期间倒计时继续。清路后尽快点击「车队继续」。',
                      s.threatsRemaining > 0
                        ? `${s.threatsRemaining} threats remain. Scan the minimap, protect every group and clear all enemies.`
                        : 'HOLD does not stop the clock. Clear the road and GO.',
                    )
                  : lostOutpost
                    ? this.t(
                        s.failureCause === 'friendly'
                          ? `第 ${s.failedGroup} 据点因己方火力全灭。开火前检查友军与爆炸范围。`
                          : `第 ${s.failedGroup} 据点遭敌袭全灭。每组友军至少保住一辆，救援车存活也不能遗漏据点。`,
                        s.failureCause === 'friendly'
                          ? `Friendly fire eliminated outpost ${s.failedGroup}. Check allies and blast radius before firing.`
                          : `Enemy fire eliminated outpost ${s.failedGroup}. Keep a survivor in every group; rescue survival alone is not enough.`,
                      )
                    : s.failureCause === 'friendly'
                      ? this.t(
                          '致命伤来自己方火力。下次开炮前检查范围圈。',
                          'Lost to friendly fire. Check the blast circle before shooting.',
                        )
                      : threatAdvice
                        ? text(threatAdvice, this.lang)
                        : this.t(
                            '敌袭摧毁救援车。清除沿线威胁，必要时让车队待命。',
                            'Lost to hostile fire. Clear nearby threats; HOLD if needed.',
                          );
      this.label(
        n,
        advice,
        px + pw / 2,
        py + ph - (low ? 74 : small ? 86 : 126),
        small ? 13 : 16,
        pw - 42,
        low ? 24 : small ? 44 : 62,
        C.dim,
      );
      const resultW = (pw - (training ? 56 : 64)) / (training ? 2 : 3);
      this.button(
        'home',

        resultW,
        44,
        n,
      );

          'retry',
