          px + 32 + resultW,
          bottom,
          resultW,
          44,
          n,
        );
      this.button(
        training
          ? 'retry'
          : success && s.mission.id !== MISSIONS.at(-1)!.id
            ? 'missionNext'
            : 'training',
        training
          ? this.t('再练一轮 ↻', 'TRY AGAIN ↻')
          : success && s.mission.id !== MISSIONS.at(-1)!.id
            ? this.t('下一任务 →', 'NEXT OPERATION →')
            : this.t('火控热身', 'FIRE CONTROL'),
        px + (training ? 32 + resultW : 40 + 2 * resultW),
        bottom,
        resultW,
        44,
        n,
      );
    } else if (key === 'orientation') {
      this.label(n, '↻', px + pw / 2, py + ph * 0.32, 60, pw - 30, 90, C.mint);
      this.label(
        n,
        this.t(
          '双手操作，更容易看清前路。\n任务已暂停，横屏后继续。',
          'Two thumbs. A clear view of the road.\nMission paused until landscape.',
        ),
        px + pw / 2,
        py + ph * 0.53,
        15,
        pw - 30,
        110,
        C.white,
      );
    }
    for (const b of this.buttons.filter((b) => b.label.node.parent === n)) {
      const primary = ['start', 'retry', 'resume'].includes(b.id);
      this.plate(
        g,
        b.x,
        b.y,
        b.w,
        b.h,
        primary ? C.amber : '#1c2a2e',
        primary ? C.amber : C.line,
        3,
      );
      b.label.color = col(
        b.id === 'supply' && s.buff && !this.pendingSupply
          ? C.dim
          : primary
            ? C.action
            : C.white,
      );
      b.label.isBold = primary;
    }
  }
  supplyLabel(s: Simulation) {
    return this.pendingSupply
      ? this.t('战斗补给 · 待领取', 'SUPPLY · CLAIM')
      : s.buff
        ? this.t('战斗补给\n增益生效中', 'COMBAT SUPPLY\nBUFF ACTIVE')
        : this.t('战斗补给', 'COMBAT SUPPLY');
  }
  update(s: Simulation, world: World) {
    if (this.quartermaster && this.progressionArt.homeReady)
      this.progressionArt.warm();
    this.quartermaster?.tickPresentation(this.reducedEffects);
    if (this.quartermaster) {
      this.quartermaster.syncIdentity();
      const revision = `${this.quartermaster.revision}:${this.online?.revision}:${this.lang}`;
      if (revision !== this.progressionRevision && !this.editing) {
        this.progressionRevision = revision;
        this.modalKey = 'rebuild';
      }
      const page = this.quartermaster.page;
      const frame = this.progressionArt.get(
        page === 'loadout' ||
          page === 'warehouse' ||
          page === 'checkin' ||
          page === 'tasks'
          ? 'hangar'
          : page
            ? 'base'
            : 'home',
      );
      if (frame) this.homeArtwork.spriteFrame = frame;
    } else if (!this.homeArtwork.spriteFrame)
      this.homeArtwork.spriteFrame = this.progressionArt.get('home') || null;
    this.lockedTargets = [];
    this.effects = { impacts: [], projectiles: [] };
    const w = this.w,
      h = this.h,
      g = this.g,
      top = this.safe.top,
      left = 12 + this.safe.left,
      right = w - this.safe.right - 12,
      by = h - this.footer - this.safe.bottom;
    this.homeBackground.active =
      s.phase === 'briefing' || !!this.quartermaster?.page;
    if (this.homeBackground.active) {
      const background = this.homeBackground.getComponent(Graphics)!;
      background.clear();
      this.rect(background, 0, 0, w, h, '#071522');
      const frame = this.homeArtwork.spriteFrame;
      if (frame) {
        const size = frame.originalSize,
          scale = Math.max(w / size.width, h / size.height);
        this.homeArtwork.node
          .getComponent(UITransform)!
          .setContentSize(size.width * scale, size.height * scale);
      }
    }
    let key =
      s.phase === 'briefing'
        ? this.missionsOpen
          ? 'missions'
          : 'home'
        : s.phase === 'playing'
          ? ''
          : s.phase;
    if (this.quartermaster?.page)
      key = 'progression:' + this.quartermaster.page;
    if (this.online?.panel)
      key = `online:${this.online.panel}:${this.online.revision}:${this.lang}`;
    else if (
      s.duelMode &&
      this.online?.view?.finished &&
      !this.quartermaster?.page
    )
      key = 'duelResult';
    else if (this.online?.entering) key = 'entering';
    if (this.advert)
      key = `advert:${this.advert.kind}:${this.advert.mock}:${this.lang}`;
    else if (this.exitConfirm) key = 'exit';
    else if (s.pauses.has('help'))
      key = 'help:' + this.helpGroup + this.helpTouch + this.lang;
    else if (s.pauses.has('mission')) key = 'mission';
    else if (s.pauses.has('settings'))
      key = this.toolsOpen
        ? 'settings:flight'
        : this.zoomOpen
          ? 'settings:zoom'
          : 'settings';
    else if (s.pauses.has('orientation')) key = 'orientation';
    else if (this.supplyOpen)
      key = this.pendingSupply ? 'supply:choice' : 'supply:offer';
    else if (this.reportOpen && s.phase === 'playing')
      key = `report:${this.equipmentOpen ? 'equipment' : 'overview'}:${Math.floor(s.time * 2)}`;
    else if (s.resumeCountdown > 0)
      key = `countdown:${Math.ceil(s.resumeCountdown)}`;
    else if (s.paused && s.phase !== 'briefing') key = 'pause';
    this.renderModal(key, s, world);
    g.clear();
    this.marks.clear();
    this.globalGraphics.clear();
    this.globalControls.setSiblingIndex(this.root.children.length - 1);
    const tracking = s.buff?.kind === 'tracking' && s.buff.remaining > 0;
    for (const l of this.labels.values()) l.node.active = !key;
    for (const l of this.unitLabels.values()) l.node.active = false;
    for (const b of this.buttons) {
      if (
        this.online?.spectator &&
        (b.id === 'fire' ||
          b.id.startsWith('weapon') ||
          b.id === 'homing' ||
          b.id === 'skillUse')
      ) {
        b.label.node.active = false;
        continue;
      }
      if (
        (b.id.startsWith('weapon') && !s.hasWeapon(Number(b.id.slice(6)))) ||
        (b.id === 'skillUse' && !s.skillKind)
      ) {
        b.label.node.active = false;
        continue;
      }
      if (
        s.duelMode &&
        ['homing', 'convoy', 'supply', 'mission'].includes(b.id)
      ) {
        b.label.node.active = false;
        continue;
      }
      if (
        b.id === 'homing' &&
        !tracking &&
        !s.homingAmmo &&
        !this.rewardsAvailable &&
        !this.pendingSupply
      ) {
        b.label.node.active = false;
        continue;
      }
      if (
        ['supply', 'homingReward', 'supplyWatch', 'zoomUpgrade'].includes(
          b.id,
        ) &&
        !this.rewardsAvailable &&
        !(this.pendingSupply && ['supply', 'supplyWatch'].includes(b.id))
      ) {
        b.label.node.active = false;
        continue;
      }
      if (b.label.node.parent === this.root)
        b.label.node.active =
          !key &&
          b.id !== 'priority' &&
          (b.id !== 'fire' || this.touch || s.homingSelected);
      if (this.isGlobalAction(b.id)) {
        b.label.node.active = !key || key === 'home';
        if (!b.label.node.active) continue;
        const gx = b.x + b.w / 2 - w / 2,
          gy = h / 2 - b.y - b.h / 2,
          gear = this.globalGraphics;
        gear.strokeColor = col(
          !key && world.viewMode === 'enhanced' ? '#123844' : C.white,
        );
        gear.lineWidth = 2;
        if (b.id === 'screen') {
          for (const dx of [-1, 1])
            for (const dy of [-1, 1]) {
              const r = this.screenActive ? 5 : 12,
                direction = this.screenActive ? 1 : -1;
              gear.moveTo(gx + dx * (r + direction * 7), gy + dy * r);
              gear.lineTo(gx + dx * r, gy + dy * r);
              gear.lineTo(gx + dx * r, gy + dy * (r + direction * 7));
            }
          gear.stroke();
          continue;
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) {
          const angle = ((i + 0.5) * Math.PI) / 16,
            radius = i % 4 === 1 || i % 4 === 2 ? 14 : 10.5;
          const x = gx + Math.cos(angle) * radius,
            y = gy + Math.sin(angle) * radius;
          if (i) gear.lineTo(x, y);
          else gear.moveTo(x, y);
        }
        gear.close();
        gear.fillColor = gear.strokeColor;
        gear.fill();
        gear.fillColor = col(
          !key && world.viewMode === 'enhanced' ? '#b9d7dd' : '#122433',
        );
        gear.circle(gx, gy, 5.5);
        gear.fill();
      }
    }
    if (!key) this.layoutCombatControls();
    this.buttonFeedback();
    const connection = this.labels.get('connectionNotice')!;
    connection.string = this.online?.connectionLost
      ? this.t('网络异常 · 正在重连', 'NETWORK LOST · RECONNECTING')
      : this.online?.offlineNotice || '';
    connection.node.active = s.duelMode && !!connection.string;
    if (connection.node.active)
      this.plate(
        this.globalGraphics,
        left,
        top + 54,
        Math.min(300, right - left - 140),
        28,
        '#2f1118ee',
        C.red,
        4,
      );
    const toast = this.labels.get('globalToast')!;
    toast.node.active =
      ['missions', 'settings:zoom'].includes(key) &&
      !!this.toast &&
      Date.now() < this.toastUntil;
    toast.string = this.toast;
    if (toast.node.active) {
      const tw = toast.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.contentSize.width;
      this.rect(
        this.globalGraphics,
        toast.node.position.x + w / 2 - tw / 2,
        top + 8,
        tw,
        36,
        C.ink,
      );
    }
    const buff = this.labels.get('buff')!;
    buff.node.active = !key && s.phase === 'playing' && s.buff?.kind === 'rate';
    if (s.buff && buff.node.active) {
      const size = buff.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.contentSize,
        p = buff.node.position,
        name =
          s.buff.kind === 'tracking'
            ? this.t('追踪模式', 'TRACKING')
            : this.t('射速提升', 'RATE ×1.3'),
        remaining = Math.ceil(s.buff.remaining),
        tint = s.buff.remaining <= 5 ? C.amber : C.mint;
      buff.string =
        name +
        (size.width < 170 ? '\n' : ' · ') +
        this.t(`${remaining}秒`, `${remaining}s`);
      buff.color = col(tint);
    }
    if (key) {
      if (['playing', 'success', 'failure'].includes(s.phase))
        this.effects = drawEffects(
          this.marks,
          s,
          world,
          this.w,
          this.h,
          this.reducedEffects,
        );
      return;
    }
    const gun = s.guns[s.selected],
      weapon = WEAPONS[s.selected],
      reason = s.reason(),
      danger = s.friendlyRisk || s.time - s.friendHitAt < 2;
    const plane = s.aircraft;
    const flightTime = s.flightTime(),
      flightText = Number.isFinite(flightTime)
        ? flightTime.toFixed(1) + 's'
        : '—';
    const allies = s.units.filter((u) => u.friendly).length;
    const bright = world.viewMode === 'enhanced';
    const counts = s.duelMode
      ? this.t(
          `友军 ${allies - s.friendlyLosses} · 敌军 ${s.units.filter((u) => !u.friendly && u.hp > 0).length}`,
          `ALLY ${allies - s.friendlyLosses} · HOST ${s.units.filter((u) => !u.friendly && u.hp > 0).length}`,
        )
      : this.t(
          `友军 ${allies - s.friendlyLosses}/${allies} · 撤离 ${Math.floor(s.ratio * 100)}% · 威胁 ${s.threatsRemaining}`,
          `ALLY ${allies - s.friendlyLosses}/${allies} · EXIT ${Math.floor(s.ratio * 100)}% · HOST ${s.threatsRemaining}`,
        );
    this.set('health', counts);
    this.labels.get('health')!.color = col(
      !s.duelMode && s.rescue.hp / s.rescue.maxHp < 0.3
        ? bright
          ? '#982536'
          : C.red
        : bright
          ? '#123844'
          : C.white,
    );
    this.labels.get('progress')!.color = col(bright ? '#243c42' : C.dim);
    this.labels.get('status')!.color = col(bright ? '#123844' : C.white);
    this.set(
      'progress',
      s.mission.mode === 'training'
        ? this.t(
            `热身清靶 ${s.kills}/3 · 友伤 ${Math.round(s.friendlyDamage)}`,
            `WARMUP ${s.kills}/3 · FRIENDLY ${Math.round(s.friendlyDamage)}`,
          )
        : '',
    );
    for (const side of [0, 1]) {
      const label = this.labels.get('spectator:' + side)!;
      label.node.active = s.duelMode && !!this.online;
      if (label.node.active) {
        const cw = Math.min(
            220,
            (right - left - this.minimapLayout.w - 24) / 2,
          ),
          x = left + side * (cw + 8),
          tint = side ? '#efb366' : '#7cd4f1',
          name = playerLabel(this.online!.combatants[side]).split(' #')[0];
        this.rect(g, x, top + 49, cw, 28, '#07182788', tint, 4);
        label.node.setPosition(x + cw / 2 - w / 2, h / 2 - top - 63);
        label.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(cw - 14, 24);
        label.fontSize = this.w < 900 ? 12 : 14;
        label.lineHeight = 18;
        label.enableWrapText = false;
        label.overflow = Label.Overflow.CLAMP;
        label.isBold = true;
        label.color = col(tint);
        label.string = this.fitBattleName(label, name, cw - 20);
        label.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(cw - 14, 24);
      }
    }
    if (this.online?.spectator)
      this.labels.get('progress')!.node.active = false;
    const clock = Math.floor(s.time);
    const level =
      s.duelMode || s.mission.mode === 'training'
        ? 0
        : MISSIONS.findIndex((m) => m.id === s.mission.id) + 1;
    this.set(
      'status',
      `${level ? this.t(`${level}关`, `L${level}`) + ' · ' : ''}${Math.floor(clock / 60)}:${String(clock % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`,
    );
    this.set(
      'weaponState',
      this.t(
        `${flightText} 弹着\n${gun.overheated ? '过热 · 冷却至40' : '热量 ' + Math.round(gun.heat) + '%'}`,
        `${flightText} IMPACT\n${gun.overheated ? 'OVERHEATED' : 'HEAT ' + Math.round(gun.heat) + '%'}`,
      ),
    );
    if (s.homingSelected)
      this.set(
        'weaponState',
        s.homingAmmo <= 0
          ? this.t('点战斗补给', 'OPEN SUPPLY')
          : s.homingTarget
            ? this.t('已锁定', 'LOCKED')
            : this.t('点选敌人', 'TAP AN ENEMY'),
      );
    const reasons: Record<string, string[]> = {
      extracting: [
        s.extractionRemaining
          ? `停止开火 · 快速撤离 ${Math.ceil(s.extractionRemaining)}秒`
          : s.shots.length
            ? '车队已抵达 · 正在确认炮弹落点'
            : '车队已抵达 · 等待电台播报结束',
        s.extractionRemaining
          ? `CEASE FIRE · EXTRACTING ${Math.ceil(s.extractionRemaining)}s`
          : s.shots.length
            ? 'CONVOY ARRIVED · RESOLVING SHOTS'
            : 'CONVOY ARRIVED · RADIO REPORT',
      ],
      target: ['点选敌人，再按发射', 'TAP AN ENEMY, THEN LAUNCH'],
      protected: ['保护区 · 禁止开火', 'PROTECTED · FIRE BLOCKED'],
      overheated: ['武器过热 · 等待降温或切枪', 'OVERHEATED · COOL OR SWITCH'],
      empty: ['弹药耗尽 · 切换武器', 'EMPTY · SWITCH WEAPON'],
      outside: ['准星超出任务区域', 'OUTSIDE MISSION AREA'],
    };
    let notice = reasons[reason]
      ? text(reasons[reason], this.lang)
      : s.rescue.hp / s.rescue.maxHp < 0.3
        ? this.t('救援车危急 · 清除附近威胁', 'RESCUE CRITICAL · CLEAR THREATS')
        : s.time - s.waveAt < 4
          ? text(s.mission.events[s.lastWave]?.direction || ['', ''], this.lang)
          : s.convoy === 'holding'
            ? this.t('清路后，点击「车队继续」', 'CLEAR THE ROAD, THEN GO')
            : s.warning === 'armor'
              ? this.t('重甲抗速射 · 切换重炮', 'ARMOR · SWITCH TO HEAVY')
              : s.warning === 'lead'
                ? this.t('预留弹着提前量', 'LEAD THE TARGET')
                : '';
    if (s.duelMode)
      notice =
        this.online?.message ||
        this.t(
          '消灭对方全部地面单位 · 菜单不暂停对战',
          'ELIMINATE ALL GROUND UNITS · MENUS DO NOT PAUSE',
        );
    if (s.extractionRemaining === undefined && Date.now() < this.toastUntil)
      notice = this.toast;
    this.set('notice', notice || this.radioText);
    this.set(
      'friendWarning',
      danger && s.extractionRemaining === undefined
        ? s.time - s.friendHitAt < 2
          ? this.t('！正在误伤友方 · 停止射击', '! FRIENDLY HIT · CEASE FIRE')
          : this.t('！范围内有友方 · 当心误伤', '! FRIENDLY IN BLAST RADIUS')
        : '',
    );
    const step = TUTORIAL.find((e) => !s.completed.has(e));
    this.set(
      'tutorial',
      s.duelMode || s.extractionRemaining !== undefined
        ? ''
        : s.mission.mode === 'training'
          ? this.t('清除 3 个目标', 'CLEAR 3 TARGETS')
          : this.tutorial && step && s.time < 12
            ? `${TUTORIAL.indexOf(step) + 1}/${TUTORIAL.length}  ${tutorialText(step, this.lang, this.touch)}`
            : '',
    );
    {
      const messages = ['friendWarning', 'notice', 'tutorial'];
      const active = messages.find((id) => this.labels.get(id)!.string);
      for (const id of messages)
        this.labels.get(id)!.node.active = id === active;
    }
    for (const id of ['notice', 'friendWarning', 'tutorial']) {
      const label = this.labels.get(id)!;
      const state = this.labels.get('weaponState')!,
        stateSize = state.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.contentSize;
      const stateY = h / 2 - state.node.position.y,
        messageY = by - 24;
      const messageRight =
        Math.abs(stateY - messageY) < stateSize.height / 2 + 14
          ? Math.min(
              right - 76,
              state.node.position.x + w / 2 - stateSize.width / 2 - 8,
            )
          : right - 76;
      label.node.setPosition(
        (left + messageRight) / 2 - w / 2,
        h / 2 - messageY,
      );
      label.node
        .getComponent(UITransform)!
        .setContentSize(
          Math.min(
            messageRight - left,
            Math.max(180, label.string.length * label.fontSize + 24),
          ),
          28,
        );
      label.overflow = Label.Overflow.SHRINK;
      if (!label.node.active || !label.string) continue;
      const size = label.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.contentSize,
        p = label.node.position;
      this.rect(
        g,
        p.x + w / 2 - size.width / 2,
        h / 2 - p.y - size.height / 2,
        size.width,
        size.height,
        id === 'friendWarning' ? '#381c26dd' : '#0c171da0',
      );
    }
    for (const b of this.buttons.filter(
      (b) => b.label.node.parent === this.root && b.label.node.active,
    )) {
      if (b.id === 'priority') continue;
      if (b.id === 'battleReport' || b.id === 'sensor') {
        if (b.id === 'sensor')
          b.label.string = this.t(
            { night: '夜景', enhanced: '增强', thermal: '热像' }[
              world.viewMode || 'night'
            ],
            { night: 'NIGHT', enhanced: 'ENH', thermal: 'IR' }[
              world.viewMode || 'night'
            ],
          );
        b.visual = { x: b.x + 2, y: b.y + 8, w: b.w - 4, h: 28 };
        this.rect(
          g,
          b.visual.x,
          b.visual.y,
          b.visual.w,
          b.visual.h,
          '#0b192950',
          '#b4e5dd99',
          4,
        );
        b.label.color = col(C.white);
        continue;
      }
      if (b.id === 'homing')
        b.label.string =
          this.t('追踪', 'LOCK') +
          ' ' +
          (tracking
            ? this.t(
                `${Math.ceil(s.buff!.remaining)}秒`,
                `${Math.ceil(s.buff!.remaining)}s`,
              )
            : s.homingAmmo || '+');
      const selected =
        (!s.homingSelected && b.id === 'weapon' + s.selected) ||
        ((s.homingSelected || tracking) && b.id === 'homing') ||
        b.id === (plane.direction < 0 ? 'orbitLeft' : 'orbitRight');
      if (b.id.startsWith('weapon')) {
        const i = Number(b.id.slice(-1)),
          a = s.guns[i],
          ready = s.weaponReadiness(i);
        const compactNames = [
          ['速射', 'AUTO'],
          ['榴弹', 'HE'],
          ['重炮', 'HVY'],
          ['燃烧', 'FIRE'],
        ];
        const name =
            b.w < 100 && i < 4
              ? this.t(...(compactNames[i] as [string, string]))
              : text(WEAPONS[i].name, this.lang),
          ammo = a.ammo === Infinity ? '∞' : String(a.ammo),
          status = ready.empty
            ? this.t('弹尽', 'EMPTY')
            : ready.remaining > 0
              ? ready.displaySeconds.toFixed(1) + 's'
              : '';
        b.label.string =
          i >= 4 || b.w < 60
            ? name + '\n' + ammo + (status ? '·' + status : '')
            : name + ' ' + ammo + (status ? '\n' + status : '');
      }
      if (b.id === 'skillUse') {
        b.label.string =
          (s.skillKind === 'tracking'
            ? this.t('追踪', 'TRACK')
            : this.t('电磁干扰', 'EMP')) +
          '\n' +
          (s.skillRemaining > 0
            ? this.t('生效 ', 'ACTIVE ') + Math.ceil(s.skillRemaining) + 's'
            : s.skillCooldown > 0
              ? this.t('冷却 ', 'COOLDOWN ') + Math.ceil(s.skillCooldown) + 's'
              : this.t('就绪', 'READY'));
        b.disabled =
          s.skillCooldown > 0 ||
          s.paused ||
          s.extractionRemaining !== undefined;
      }
      if (b.id === 'fire')
        b.label.string =
          reason === 'extracting'
            ? this.t('停火', 'CEASE FIRE')
            : s.homingSelected
              ? s.homingAmmo <= 0
                ? this.t('无弹药', 'EMPTY')
                : s.homingTarget
                  ? this.t('发射', 'LAUNCH')
                  : this.t('待锁定', 'LOCK')
              : s.weaponReadiness().empty
                ? this.t('弹尽', 'EMPTY')
                : reason === 'protected'
                  ? this.t('禁射', 'BLOCKED')
                  : reason === 'overheated'
                    ? this.t('冷却', 'COOL')
                    : reason === 'empty'
                      ? this.t('无弹药', 'EMPTY')
                      : this.t('开火', 'FIRE');
      const fire = b.id === 'fire';
      if (
        fire &&
        !s.homingSelected &&
        !s.weaponReadiness().empty &&
        s.weaponReadiness().remaining > 0
      )
        b.label.string +=
          '\n' + s.weaponReadiness().displaySeconds.toFixed(1) + 's';
      b.visual = { x: b.x + 2, y: b.y + 5, w: b.w - 4, h: b.h - 10 };
      if (fire) {
        const radius = 29,
          x = b.x + b.w / 2 - w / 2,
          y = h / 2 - b.y - b.h / 2;
        b.visual = {
          x: b.x + b.w / 2 - radius,
          y: b.y + b.h / 2 - radius,
          w: radius * 2,
          h: radius * 2,
        };
        g.fillColor = col(danger ? '#48283266' : '#0b192944');
        g.strokeColor = col(danger ? C.red : C.mint);
        g.lineWidth = 2;
        g.circle(x, y, radius);
        g.fill();
        g.stroke();
        g.lineWidth = 1;
      } else {
        const v = b.visual;
        this.rect(
          g,
          v.x,
          v.y,
          v.w,
          v.h,
          selected ? '#173b3b70' : '#0b192950',
          selected ? C.mint : '#b4c9cd77',
          4,
        );
        if (selected) this.rect(g, v.x + 8, v.y + v.h - 2, v.w - 16, 2, C.mint);
      }
      if (b.id.startsWith('weapon'))
        this.cooldownBorder(
          g,
          b,
          s.weaponReadiness(Number(b.id.slice(-1))).ratio,
        );
      if (fire && !s.homingSelected)
        this.cooldownBorder(g, b, s.weaponReadiness().ratio, true);
      if (b.id === 'skillUse' && s.skillKind)
        this.cooldownBorder(
          g,
          b,
          Math.min(
            1,
            s.skillRemaining > 0
              ? s.skillRemaining / SKILLS[s.skillKind].duration
              : s.skillCooldown / SKILLS[s.skillKind].cooldown,
          ),
          false,
          s.skillRemaining > 0 ? C.mint : C.amber,
        );
      b.label.color = col(fire && danger ? C.red : selected ? C.mint : C.white);
      b.label.fontSize = fire
        ? 14
        : b.id === 'skillUse' || b.w < 60
          ? 11
          : this.compact
            ? 12
            : 13;
      b.label.lineHeight = 15;
      if (b.id === 'skillUse')
        b.label.color = col(
          s.skillRemaining > 0 ? C.mint : s.skillCooldown > 0 ? C.dim : C.white,
        );
      if (b.id === 'homing' && tracking && s.buff!.remaining <= 5)
        b.label.color = col(C.amber);
      b.label.isBold = selected || fire;
      if (b.id.startsWith('weapon')) {
        const a = s.guns[Number(b.id.slice(-1))];
        this.rect(
          g,
          b.x + 5,
          b.y + b.h - 5,
          ((b.w - 10) * a.heat) / 100,
          2,
          a.overheated ? C.red : C.amber,
        );
      }
    }
    this.labels.get('priorityMarker')!.node.active = false;
    this.drawWorld(s, world);
    this.drawMinimap(s);
  }
  private fitBattleName(label: Label, name: string, width: number) {
    const key = `${name}:${width}:${label.fontSize}`;
    let fitted = this.nameFits.get(key);
    if (fitted !== undefined) return fitted;
    const measure =
      typeof label.updateRenderData === 'function'
        ? (value: string) => {
            label.overflow = Label.Overflow.NONE;
            label.string = value;
            label.updateRenderData(true);
            return label.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.contentSize.width;
          }
        : undefined;
    fitted = fitName(name, width, label.fontSize, measure);
    label.overflow = Label.Overflow.CLAMP;
    this.nameFits.set(key, fitted);
    return fitted;
  }
  private cooldownBorder(
    g: Graphics,
    b: ButtonRect,
    ratio: number,
    circle = false,
    tint = C.amber,
  ) {
    if (ratio <= 0) return;
    const v = b.visual || b,
      steps = 48;
    g.strokeColor = col(tint);
    g.lineWidth = 2.5;
    for (let i = 0; i <= Math.ceil(steps * ratio); i++) {
      const t = Math.min(ratio, i / steps);
      let x: number, y: number;
      if (circle) {
        const angle = -Math.PI / 2 + t * Math.PI * 2;
        x = v.x + v.w / 2 + (Math.cos(angle) * v.w) / 2;
        y = v.y + v.h / 2 + (Math.sin(angle) * v.h) / 2;
      } else {
        const d = t * (v.w + v.h) * 2;
        if (d <= v.w) {
          x = v.x + d;
          y = v.y;
        } else if (d <= v.w + v.h) {
          x = v.x + v.w;
          y = v.y + d - v.w;
        } else if (d <= v.w * 2 + v.h) {
          x = v.x + v.w - (d - v.w - v.h);
          y = v.y + v.h;
        } else {
          x = v.x;
          y = v.y + v.h - (d - v.w * 2 - v.h);
        }
      }
      if (i) g.lineTo(x - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y);
      else g.moveTo(x - this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y);
    }
    g.stroke();
    g.lineWidth = 1;
  }
  drawMinimap(s: Simulation) {
    const g = this.marks,
      map = this.minimapLayout,
      priorityId = this.autoPriority ? s.priorityThreat?.unit.id : undefined;
    this.rect(g, map.x, map.y, map.w, map.h, '#0c171d66', '#91e6cb55');
    this.set('mapLegend', this.t('北 ↑', 'N ↑'));
    const point = (p: Point) => ({
      x:
        map.x +
        8 +
        ((p.x + MAP.halfWidth) / (2 * MAP.halfWidth)) * (map.w - 16) -
        this.w / 2,
      y:
        this.h / 2 -
        map.y -
        25 -
        ((p.z + MAP.halfDepth) / (2 * MAP.halfDepth)) * (map.h - 33),
    });
    g.strokeColor = col(C.line);
    g.lineWidth = 1;
    mapRoute(s.mission.map).forEach((p, i) => {
      const q = point(p);
      if (i) g.lineTo(q.x, q.y);
      else g.moveTo(q.x, q.y);
    });
    g.stroke();
    for (const u of s.units) {
      if (u.hp <= 0) continue;
      const q = point(u);
      if (priorityId === u.id) {
        g.strokeColor = col(C.white);
        g.lineWidth = 2;
        g.circle(q.x, q.y, 6);
        g.stroke();
        g.lineWidth = 1;
      }
      g.strokeColor = col(u.friendly ? C.mint : C.red);
      g.fillColor = col(u.friendly ? C.mint : C.red);
      if (u.friendly) {
        g.rect(q.x - 3, q.y - 3, 6, 6);
        g.stroke();
      } else {
        g.moveTo(q.x, q.y + 2.5);
        g.lineTo(q.x + 2.5, q.y);
        g.lineTo(q.x, q.y - 2.5);
        g.lineTo(q.x - 2.5, q.y);
        g.close();
        g.fill();
      }
    }
    const aim = point(s.aim);
    if (
      Math.abs(s.aim.x) <= MAP.halfWidth &&
      Math.abs(s.aim.z) <= MAP.halfDepth
    ) {
      g.strokeColor = col(C.white);
      g.circle(aim.x, aim.y, 4);
      g.stroke();
    }
  }
  drawWorld(s: Simulation, world: World) {
    const g = this.marks;
    this.effects = drawEffects(
      g,
      s,
      world,
      this.w,
      this.h,
      this.reducedEffects,
    );
    const project = (p: Point, y = 0) => {
      const q = world.project(p, y);
      return {
        x: q.x / view.getScaleX() - this.w / 2,
        y: q.y / view.getScaleY() - this.h / 2,
        visible: [q.x, q.y, q.z].every(Number.isFinite) && q.z >= 0 && q.z <= 1,
      };
    };
    const onScreen = (p: ReturnType<typeof project>, pad = 0) =>
      p.visible &&
      Math.abs(p.x) <= this.w / 2 - pad &&
      Math.abs(p.y) <= this.h / 2 - pad;
    const priorityLabel = this.labels.get('priorityMarker')!;
    priorityLabel.node.active = false;
    const threat = s.priorityThreat;
    if (threat && this.autoPriority) {
      const p = project(threat.unit, 1.5);
      if (p.visible) {
        let x = Math.max(
          -this.w / 2 + this.safe.left + 48,
          Math.min(this.w / 2 - this.safe.right - 48, p.x),
        );
        const y = Math.max(
          -this.h / 2 + this.safe.bottom + this.footer + 52,
          Math.min(this.h / 2 - this.safe.top - 82, p.y),
        );
        const map = this.minimapLayout;
        if (x + this.w / 2 + 48 > map.x && this.h / 2 - y - 24 < map.y + map.h)
          x = map.x - 56 - this.w / 2;
        const marker = this.buttons.find((b) => b.id === 'priority')!;
        marker.x = x + this.w / 2 - 48;
        marker.y = this.h / 2 - y - 24;
        const clipped = x !== p.x || y !== p.y;
        g.strokeColor = col(C.red);
        g.lineWidth = 2;
        for (const sx of [-1, 1])
          for (const sy of [-1, 1]) {
            g.moveTo(x + sx * 10, y + sy * 19);
            g.lineTo(x + sx * 19, y + sy * 19);
            g.lineTo(x + sx * 19, y + sy * 10);
          }
        g.stroke();
        if (clipped) {
          const angle = Math.atan2(p.y - y, p.x - x);
          g.moveTo(x, y);
          g.lineTo(x + Math.cos(angle) * 28, y + Math.sin(angle) * 28);
          g.stroke();
        }
        priorityLabel.string = this.t(
          `优先 ${Math.ceil(threat.dueIn)}s${clipped ? ' ↗' : ''}`,
          `PRIORITY ${Math.ceil(threat.dueIn)}s${clipped ? ' ↗' : ''}`,
        );
        priorityLabel.node.setPosition(x, y - 33);
        priorityLabel.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(96, 24);
        priorityLabel.node.active = true;
      }
    }
    const groundRing = (p: Point, radius: number) => {
      const points = Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, i) =>
        project({
          x: p.x + Math.cos((i * Math.PI) / 12) * radius,
          z: p.z + Math.sin((i * Math.PI) / 12) * radius,
        }),
      );
      if (!points.every((p) => onScreen(p, -64))) return;
      points.forEach((p, i) => (i ? g.lineTo(p.x, p.y) : g.moveTo(p.x, p.y)));
      g.close();
      g.stroke();
    };
    for (const [id, l] of Array.from(this.unitLabels.entries()))
      if (!s.units.some((u) => u.id === id)) {
        l.node.destroy();
        this.unitLabels.delete(id);
      }
    for (const p of s.duelMode ? [] : PROTECTED) {
      g.strokeColor = col('#729e9180');
      g.lineWidth = 1;
      groundRing(p, p.radius);
    }
    for (const d of s.duelMode ? [] : s.holdPoints) {
      const q = project(s.routePoint(d));
      if (!onScreen(q, 6)) continue;
      g.strokeColor = col(C.amber);
      g.lineWidth = 1;
      g.circle(q.x, q.y, 6);
      g.stroke();
    }
    const placed: { x: number; y: number }[] = [],
      bottom = -this.h / 2 + this.footer + this.safe.bottom + 44,
      top = this.h / 2 - 64 - this.safe.top;
    const banners = ['notice', 'friendWarning', 'tutorial']
      .map((id) => this.labels.get(id)!)
      .filter((l) => l.node.active && l.string);
    const overBanner = (x: number, y: number, halfWidth: number) =>
      banners.some((l) => {
        const p = l.node.position,
          size = l.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.contentSize;
        return (
          Math.abs(x - p.x) < size.width / 2 + halfWidth &&
          Math.abs(y - p.y) < size.height / 2 + 12
        );
      });
    const aim = project(s.aim);
    const focus = this.touch
      ? onScreen(aim)
        ? { x: aim.x + this.w / 2, y: this.h / 2 - aim.y }
        : undefined
      : this.mousePointer;
    const pointer =
      focus && !this.blocksBattlefield(focus.x, focus.y) ? focus : undefined;
    const contacts: {
      unit: Simulation['units'][number];
      point: ReturnType<typeof project>;
    }[] = [];
    let hovered: (typeof contacts)[number] | undefined,
      hoverDistance = Infinity;
    for (const u of s.units) {
      if (u.hp <= 0) continue;
      const q = project(u, 0.8);
      // Behind-camera projections mirror direction; those contacts remain on the north-up minimap.
      if (!q.visible) continue;
      // The minimap covers off-screen contacts without crowding the battlefield edges.
      if (
        q.x < -this.w / 2 + 16 + this.safe.left ||
        q.x > this.w / 2 - 16 - this.safe.right ||
        q.y > top ||
        q.y < bottom - 30
      )
        continue;
      if (this.blocksBattlefield(q.x + this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - q.y, true))
        continue;
      const contact = { unit: u, point: q };
      contacts.push(contact);
      if (!pointer) continue;
      // Keep the forgiving body-to-marker hover region even when distant markers shrink.
      const body = project(u);
      const dx = body.visible ? body.x - q.x : 0,
        dy = body.visible ? body.y - q.y : 0;
      const px = pointer.x - this.w / 2 - q.x,
        py = this.h / 2 - pointer.y - q.y;
      const t = Math.max(
        0,
        Math.min(1, (px * dx + py * dy) / (dx * dx + dy * dy || 1)),
      );
      const distance = Math.hypot(px - t * dx, py - t * dy);
      if (
        distance <= (u.kind === 'heavy' ? 15 : 12) &&
        distance < hoverDistance
      ) {
        hovered = contact;
        hoverDistance = distance;
      }
    }
    const unitLabel = (id: number, width: number, height: number) => {
      let l = this.unitLabels.get(id);
      if (!l) {
        l = this.label(this.root, '', 0, 0, 12, width, height);
        l.node.setSiblingIndex(this.root.children.length - 2);
        this.unitLabels.set(id, l);
      }
      l.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(width, height);
      l.fontSize = this.compact ? 12 : 13;
      l.lineHeight = 18;
      l.isBold = true;
      return l;
    };
    const groups = new Map<number, typeof contacts>();
    for (const contact of contacts) {
      const u = contact.unit;
      if (!u.friendly || u.group === undefined) continue;
      const group = groups.get(u.group) || [];
      group.push(contact);
      groups.set(u.group, group);
    }
    const counts = new Map<number, number>(),
      hidden = new Set<number>();
    for (const [id, members] of groups) {
      if (
        members.length < 2 ||
        (hovered?.unit.friendly && hovered.unit.group === id)
      )
        continue;
      // ponytail: pairwise checks suit the current three-unit groups; use spatial bins for large formations.
      if (
        !members.every((a) =>
          members.every(
            (b) =>
              Math.hypot(a.point.x - b.point.x, a.point.y - b.point.y) < 12,
          ),
        )
      )
        continue;
      const q = members[0].point;
      if (
        q.x + 36 > this.w / 2 - this.safe.right ||
        overBanner(q.x + 22, q.y, 14) ||
        Math.hypot(
          Math.max(0, Math.abs(q.x + 22 - aim.x) - 14),
          Math.max(0, Math.abs(q.y - aim.y) - 9),
        ) < 40 ||
        [8, 36].some((dx) =>
          this.blocksBattlefield(q.x + dx + this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - q.y),
        )
      )
        continue;
      counts.set(members[0].unit.id, members.length);
      for (const member of members.slice(1)) hidden.add(member.unit.id);
    }
    const ordered = hovered
      ? [...contacts.filter((c) => c !== hovered), hovered]
      : contacts;
    for (const contact of ordered) {
      const { unit: u, point: q } = contact;
      if (hidden.has(u.id)) continue;
      const r = contact === hovered ? 9 : u.kind === 'heavy' ? 7 : 5;
      // Dark outline keeps team colors and shapes legible against bright thermal contacts.
      for (const outline of [true, false]) {
        g.lineWidth = outline ? (contact === hovered ? 4 : 3) : 2;
        g.strokeColor = col(outline ? '#061116' : u.friendly ? C.mint : C.red);
        if (u.friendly) g.rect(q.x - r, q.y - r, r * 2, r * 2);
        else {
          g.moveTo(q.x, q.y + r);
          g.lineTo(q.x + r, q.y);
          g.lineTo(q.x, q.y - r);
          g.lineTo(q.x - r, q.y);
          g.close();
        }
        g.stroke();
      }
      const count = counts.get(u.id);
      if (count) {
        const l = unitLabel(u.id, 28, 18);
        l.string = `×${count}`;
        l.color = col(C.mint);
        l.node.setPosition(q.x + 22, q.y);
        l.node.active = true;
        placed.push({ x: q.x + 22, y: q.y });
        this.rect(
          g,
          q.x + this.w / 2 + 8,
          this.h / 2 - q.y - 9,
          28,
          18,
          '#0c191fe8',
        );
      }
    }
    for (const id of new Set([
      ...s.shots
        .filter((shot) => shot.guidance)
        .map((shot) => shot.guidance!.target),
      ...(s.homingSelected && s.homingTarget ? [s.homingTarget.id] : []),
    ])) {
      const unit = s.units.find((u) => u.id === id && u.hp > 0);
      if (!unit) continue;
      const q = project(unit, 0.3);
      if (!onScreen(q, 24)) continue;
      const r = 18;
      for (const outline of [true, false]) {
        g.lineWidth = outline ? 6 : 3;
        g.strokeColor = col(outline ? '#061116' : C.amber);
        for (const x of [-1, 1])
          for (const y of [-1, 1]) {
            g.moveTo(q.x + x * (r - 9), q.y + y * r);
            g.lineTo(q.x + x * r, q.y + y * r);
            g.lineTo(q.x + x * r, q.y + y * (r - 9));
          }
        g.stroke();
      }
      const l = unitLabel(unit.id, 110, 24);
      l.string = s.shots.some((shot) => shot.guidance?.target === id)
        ? this.t('追踪中', 'TRACKING')
        : this.t('已锁定', 'LOCKED');
      l.color = col(C.amber);
      l.node.setPosition(q.x, q.y + 36);
      l.node.active = true;
      this.lockedTargets.push({ id, x: q.x + this.w / 2, y: this.h / 2 - q.y });
    }
    if (
      hovered &&
      !this.lockedTargets.some((lock) => lock.id === hovered.unit.id)
    ) {
      const { unit: u, point: q } = hovered;
      const width = this.compact ? 120 : 176,
        half = width / 2;
      const l = unitLabel(u.id, width, 24);
      l.color = col(u.friendly ? C.mint : C.red);
      l.string = u.friendly
        ? u.kind === 'escort'
          ? this.t('护卫', 'ESCORT')
          : this.t('救援车', 'RESCUE')
        : this.t(
            u.kind === 'heavy' ? '重甲' : u.kind === 'turret' ? '炮台' : '轻车',
            u.kind.toUpperCase(),
          );
      if (u.hp < u.maxHp)
        l.string +=
          u.hp <= u.maxHp * 0.3
            ? this.t(' · 重创', ' · CRITICAL')
            : this.t(' · 受损', ' · DAMAGED');
      const tag = [
        [0, 56],
        [0, -56],
        [half + 44, 0],
        [-half - 44, 0],
        [half + 44, -24],
        [-half - 44, -24],
      ]
        .map(([dx, dy]) => ({ x: q.x + dx, y: q.y + dy }))
        .find(
          (p) =>
            p.y <= top - 12 &&
            p.y >= bottom &&
            p.x >= -this.w / 2 + this.safe.left + half &&
            p.x <= this.w / 2 - this.safe.right - half &&
            Math.hypot(
              Math.max(0, Math.abs(p.x - aim.x) - half),
              Math.max(0, Math.abs(p.y - aim.y) - 12),
            ) >= 40 &&
            !overBanner(p.x, p.y, half) &&
            [-half, 0, half].every((dx) =>
              [-12, 12].every(
                (dy) =>
                  !this.blocksBattlefield(
                    p.x + dx + this.w / 2,
                    this.h / 2 - p.y + dy,
                  ),
              ),
            ),
        );
      l.node.active = !!tag;
      if (tag) {
        l.node.setPosition(tag.x, tag.y);
        placed.push(tag);
        this.rect(
          g,
          tag.x + this.w / 2 - half,
          this.h / 2 - tag.y - 12,
          width,
          24,
          '#0c191fe8',
        );
      }
    }
    const q = project(s.aim);
    if (!onScreen(q, 18)) {
      this.labels.get('target')!.node.active = false;
      this.labels.get('flight')!.node.active = false;
      return;
    }
    g.strokeColor = col(
      s.reason() === 'protected' || s.friendlyRisk ? C.red : C.mint,
    );
    g.lineWidth = 1.5;
    groundRing(s.aim, s.effectiveRadius);
    for (const outline of [true, false]) {
      g.strokeColor = col(
        outline
          ? '#061116'
          : s.reason() === 'protected' || s.friendlyRisk
            ? C.red
            : C.white,
      );
      g.lineWidth = outline ? 6 : 3;
      for (const sign of [-1, 1]) {
        g.moveTo(q.x + sign * 11, q.y);
        g.lineTo(q.x + sign * 32, q.y);
        g.moveTo(q.x, q.y + sign * 11);
        g.lineTo(q.x, q.y + sign * 32);
      }
      g.stroke();
      g.circle(q.x, q.y, 3);
      g.stroke();
    }
    const target = s.aimedUnit;
    const recentImpacts = [...s.events]
      .reverse()
      .filter(
        (e) =>
          e.type === 'impact' &&
          (!s.duelMode || e.side === s.pilotSide) &&
          s.time - e.time < 1.1,
      );
    // A following round landing on a wreck must not erase the destruction confirmation.
    const impact =
      recentImpacts.find((e) => e.outcome === 'friendly') ||
      recentImpacts.find((e) => e.outcome === 'destroyed') ||
      recentImpacts[0];
    const outcome = impact?.outcome;
    const feedback =
      outcome === 'destroyed'
        ? this.t('◆ 威胁已清除', '◆ THREAT ELIMINATED')
        : outcome === 'friendly'
          ? this.t('！误伤友军', '! FRIENDLY HIT')
          : outcome === 'armor'
            ? this.t('装甲低伤 · 换重炮', 'ARMOR · USE HEAVY')
            : outcome === 'hit'
              ? this.t('命中目标', 'TARGET HIT')
              : outcome === 'miss'
                ? this.t('未命中', 'MISS')
                : '';
    this.set(
      'target',
      target?.friendly
        ? this.t('友方目标 · 请勿射击', 'FRIENDLY · DO NOT FIRE')
        : feedback,
    );
    this.labels.get('target')!.color = col(
      outcome === 'friendly' || target?.friendly
        ? C.red
        : outcome === 'miss'
          ? C.dim
          : C.amber,
    );
    // Both transient labels use the same avoidance rule; never clamp text onto the reticle.
    const placeFeedback = (label: Label, p: Point) => {
      const cursor = project(s.aim);
      for (const [dx, dy] of [
        [100, 0],
        [-100, 0],
        [0, -48],
        [0, 48],
        [100, -44],
        [-100, 44],
      ]) {
        const x = p.x + dx,
          y = p.z + dy;
        if (
          x < -this.w / 2 + 84 ||
          x > this.w / 2 - 84 ||
          y < bottom + 12 ||
          y > top - 24
        )
          continue;
        if (Math.abs(x - cursor.x) < 100 && Math.abs(y - cursor.y) < 34)
          continue;
        if (overBanner(x, y, 78)) continue;
        if (
          placed.some((q) => Math.abs(x - q.x) < 122 && Math.abs(y - q.y) < 26)
        )
          continue;
        if (
          s.units.some((u) => {
            const q = project(u, 0.8);
            return (
              u.hp > 0 &&
              q.visible &&
              Math.abs(x - q.x) < 96 &&
              Math.abs(y - q.y) < 30
            );
          })
        )
          continue;
        if (this.blocksBattlefield(x + this.w / 2, this.h / 2 - y)) continue;
        label.node.setPosition(x, y);
        placed.push({ x, y });
        return true;
      }
      return false;
    };
    if (impact || target?.friendly) {
      const p = project(target?.friendly ? s.aim : impact!);
      const label = this.labels.get('target')!;
      label.node.active =
        onScreen(p) && placeFeedback(label, { x: p.x, z: p.y });
    }
    if (impact && outcome !== 'miss' && s.time - impact.time < 0.22) {
      const p = project(impact);
      if (!onScreen(p, 12)) return;
      g.strokeColor = col(outcome === 'friendly' ? C.red : C.white);
      g.lineWidth = 2;
      for (const x of [-1, 1])
        for (const y of [-1, 1]) {
          g.moveTo(p.x + x * 5, p.y + y * 5);
          g.lineTo(p.x + x * 10, p.y + y * 10);
        }
      g.stroke();
    }
    const next = s.selected > 0 ? s.shots.find((a) => a.weapon > 0) : undefined,
      flight = this.labels.get('flight')!;
    flight.string = next
      ? this.t(
          `弹着 ${(next.due - s.time).toFixed(1)}s`,
          `IMPACT ${(next.due - s.time).toFixed(1)}s`,
        )
      : '';
    if (next) {
      const p = project(next);
      flight.node.active =
        onScreen(p) && placeFeedback(flight, { x: p.x, z: p.y });
    }
  }
}

export const playerId = (player: Player) =>
  player.playerId || player.userId || player.id || '';
export const playerLabel = (player: Player) =>
  `${player.nickname || player.name || '飞行员'} #${playerId(player).replace(/-/g, '').slice(-8).toUpperCase()}`;

export type ChatMessage = {
  id: string;
  playerId: string;
  nickname: string;
  text: string;
  at: number;
