};
export type ChatSnapshot = {
  messages: ChatMessage[];
  cursor: string;
  reset: boolean;
  serverNow: number;
};
type Metadata = {
  identity: Player;
  capabilities: { multiplayer: boolean; leaderboards: boolean; chat?: boolean };
};
type Bridge = {
  ready: Promise<Metadata | undefined>;
  refresh?: () => Promise<Metadata | undefined>;
  services: {
    createRoom(input: {
      mode: string;
      visibility?: string;
      password?: string;
    }): Promise<Room>;
    match(): Promise<Room>;
    joinRoom(input: { code: string; password?: string }): Promise<Room>;
    listRooms?(): Promise<{ rooms: RoomSummary[] }>;
    history?(input: { cursor?: string }): Promise<BattleHistory>;
    updateProfile?(input: { nickname: string }): Promise<Player>;
    connectRoom?(input: { code: string }): Promise<Room>;
    spectateRoom?(input: { code: string; password?: string }): Promise<Room>;
    getRoom(input: { code: string }): Promise<Room>;
    roomAction(input: {
      code: string;
      operation: string;
      body?: object;
    }): Promise<Room>;
    nightAssets?(expectedPlayerId?: string): Promise<AssetSnapshot>;
    nightAssetCommand?(
      input: AssetCommand,
      expectedPlayerId?: string,
    ): Promise<AssetSnapshot>;
    nightChat?(
      input: { cursor?: string; waitMs?: number },
      expectedPlayerId?: string,
    ): Promise<ChatSnapshot>;
    sendNightChat?(
      input: { requestId: string; text: string; roomCode?: string },
      expectedPlayerId?: string,
    ): Promise<ChatSnapshot>;
    reportNightChat?(
      input: { id: string; reason: string },
      expectedPlayerId?: string,
    ): Promise<unknown>;
    leaderboard(input: {
      boardId: string;
      ruleVersion?: string;
    }): Promise<Ranking>;
  };
  createRecorder(
    version: string,
    options: {
      onStatus: (status: string) => void;
      startTimeoutMs?: number;
      durable?: { storage: Pick<Storage, 'getItem' | 'setItem'> };
    },
  ): Recorder;
};
const randomBytes = () => {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(16);
  if (globalThis.crypto?.getRandomValues)
    globalThis.crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
  else
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++)
      bytes[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
  return bytes; // Local display identity only; authenticated IDs are assigned by the service.
};
const controls = new Set([
  'setAim',
  'choose',
  'setFire',
  'clearInput',
  'fire',
  'adjustAltitude',
  'adjustRadius',
  'setOrbitDirection',
  'useSkill',
]);
const validCode = (code: string) => /^(?:[0-9]{6}|[A-F0-9]{12})$/.test(code);
export function invitation(search: string) {
  const codes = new URLSearchParams(search).getAll('room');
  return codes.length === 1 && validCode(codes[0]) ? codes[0] : undefined;
}
export function roomLink(href: string, code: string) {
  if (!validCode(code)) throw Error('Invalid room');
  const url = new URL(href);
  url.search = new URLSearchParams({ room: code, fullscreen: '1' }).toString();
  url.hash = url.username = url.password = '';
  return url.href;
}
/** Transport stays in the shared services bridge; this owns only Night's screen and input state. */
export class Online {
  chatMessages: ChatMessage[] = [];
  chatDraft = '';
  chatStatus = '';
  chatRevision = 0;
  chatSending = false;
  chatAvailable = false;
  chatMenu = '';
  blockedPlayers = new Set<string>();
  private chatOwner = '';
  private chatEpoch = 0;
  private chatPoll = false;
  private chatNext = 0;
  private chatCursor = '';
  private foreground = true;
  private destroyed = false;
  private chatIntent?: { requestId: string; text: string; roomCode?: string };
  panelHistory: Online['panel'][] = [];
  get visibleChat() {
    return this.chatMessages.filter(
      (m) => !this.blockedPlayers.has(m.playerId),
    );
  }
  setForeground(value: boolean) {
    if (this.foreground === value) return;
    this.foreground = value;
    this.chatEpoch++;
    this.chatNext = 0;
  }
  private chatAcceptedAt = 0;
  private acceptChat(result: ChatSnapshot) {
    if (result.serverNow < this.chatAcceptedAt) return;
    this.chatAcceptedAt = result.serverNow;
    if (JSON.stringify(this.chatMessages) !== JSON.stringify(result.messages)) {
      this.chatMessages = result.messages;
      this.chatRevision++;
    }
    this.chatCursor = result.cursor;
  }
  private async pollChat(now: number) {
    if (
      this.destroyed ||
      !this.foreground ||
      this.panel !== 'menu' ||
      !this.chatAvailable ||
      !this.bridge?.services.nightChat ||
      this.chatPoll ||
      now < this.chatNext
    )
      return;
    const epoch = this.chatEpoch,
      owner = this.authenticatedId;
    this.chatPoll = true;
    try {
      const result = await this.bridge.services.nightChat(
        {
          cursor: this.chatCursor || undefined,
          waitMs: this.chatCursor ? 5000 : 0,
        },
        owner,
      );
      if (
        epoch !== this.chatEpoch ||
        owner !== this.authenticatedId ||
        this.destroyed
      )
        return;
      this.acceptChat(result);
      this.chatStatus = '';
      this.chatNext = performance.now() + 200;
    } catch {
      if (epoch === this.chatEpoch) {
        this.chatStatus = '交流暂不可用，正在重连';
        this.chatNext = performance.now() + 4000;
        this.chatRevision++;
      }
    } finally {
      this.chatPoll = false;
    }
  }
  async sendChat(invite = false) {
    if (
      this.chatSending ||
      !this.chatAvailable ||
      !this.bridge?.services.sendNightChat
    )
      return;
    const text = this.chatDraft.normalize('NFKC').trim();
    if (
      !this.chatIntent &&
      ((!text && !invite) || Array.from(text).length > 120)
    ) {
      this.chatStatus = '请输入1–120个字';
      this.chatRevision++;
      return;
    }
    const room = invite ? this.room : undefined;
    if (
      invite &&
      (!room ||
        room.visibility !== 'public' ||
        room.status !== 'waiting' ||
        (room.players[0] && playerId(room.players[0]) !== this.authenticatedId))
    ) {
      this.chatStatus = '只有自己创建的公开等待房间可以邀请';
      this.chatRevision++;
      return;
    }
    if (!this.chatIntent) {
      const bytes = randomBytes();
      bytes[6] = (bytes[6] & 15) | 64;
      bytes[8] = (bytes[8] & 63) | 128;
      const hex = Array.from(bytes, (b) =>
        b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'),
      ).join('');
      this.chatIntent = {
        requestId: `${hex.slice(0, 8)}-${hex.slice(8, 12)}-${hex.slice(12, 16)}-${hex.slice(16, 20)}-${hex.slice(20)}`,
        text: text || (invite ? '来我的房间，一起出动！' : ''),
        ...(room ? { roomCode: room.code } : {}),
      };
    }
    const owner = this.authenticatedId,
      intent = this.chatIntent;
    this.chatSending = true;
    this.chatRevision++;
    try {
      const result = await this.bridge.services.sendNightChat(intent, owner);
      if (owner !== this.authenticatedId || this.destroyed) return;
      this.chatEpoch++;
      this.chatNext = 0;
      this.acceptChat(result);
      this.chatIntent = undefined;
      if (this.chatDraft.normalize('NFKC').trim() === intent.text)
        this.chatDraft = '';
      this.chatStatus = '';
    } catch (error) {
      if (owner !== this.authenticatedId || this.destroyed) return;
      const code = (error as { code?: string }).code || '';
      const known = [
        'CHAT_RATE_LIMIT',
        'CHAT_MUTED',
        'INVALID_INPUT',
        'INVITATION_UNAVAILABLE',
        'REQUEST_ID_CONFLICT',
      ].includes(code);
      if (known) this.chatIntent = undefined;
      this.chatStatus =
        code === 'CHAT_RATE_LIMIT'
          ? '发言间隔3秒，请稍后再发'
          : code === 'CHAT_MUTED'
            ? '当前账号暂时无法发言'
            : code === 'INVITATION_UNAVAILABLE'
              ? '房间已不再接受邀请'
              : known
                ? '消息未发送，请检查内容'
                : '发送结果待确认，点击发送可安全重试';
    } finally {
      if (owner === this.authenticatedId) {
        this.chatSending = false;
        this.chatRevision++;
      }
    }
  }
  toggleBlock(id: string) {
    if (id === this.authenticatedId) return;
    this.blockedPlayers.has(id)
      ? this.blockedPlayers.delete(id)
      : this.blockedPlayers.add(id);
    try {
      this.storage.setItem(
        'night-chat-blocks:' + this.authenticatedId,
        JSON.stringify(Array.from(this.blockedPlayers)),
      );
    } catch {}
    this.chatMenu = '';
    this.chatRevision++;
  }
  async reportChat(id: string) {
    const owner = this.authenticatedId;
    try {
      await this.bridge?.services.reportNightChat?.(
        { id, reason: '玩家举报：不适当或骚扰内容' },
        owner,
      );
      if (owner === this.authenticatedId) this.chatStatus = '举报已提交';
    } catch {
      if (owner === this.authenticatedId)
        this.chatStatus = '举报失败，请稍后重试';
    }
    if (owner === this.authenticatedId) {
      this.chatMenu = '';
      this.chatRevision++;
    }
  }
  back() {
    this.show(this.panelHistory.pop() || '', false);
  }
  dispose() {
    this.destroyed = true;
    this.chatEpoch++;
    this.chatMessages = [];
    this.chatDraft = '';
  }
  identity: Player;
  panel:
    | ''
    | 'menu'
    | 'room'
    | 'ranking'
    | 'profile'
    | 'create'
    | 'join'
    | 'history' = '';
  rooms: RoomSummary[] = [];
  roomPage = 0;
  pageSize = 3;
  history?: BattleHistory;
  historyPage = 0;
  board: 'clears' | 'level' | 'points' | 'win-rate' = 'clears';
  draftName = '';
  draftCode = '';
  draftPassword = '';
  privateRoom = false;
  resumeCode = '';
  enteringUntil = 0;
  connectionLost = false;
  private listAt = 0;
  private syncAt = 0;
  private hiddenRooms = new Map<string, number>();
  private roomKey = '';
  revision = 0;
  busy = false;
  available = false;
  ranked = false;
  message = '';
  rankStatus = 'local';
  assets?: AssetSnapshot;
  battleReward?: Settlement;
  rankingVersion = 'night-campaign-v2';
  authenticatedId = '';
  level = 0;
  ranking?: Ranking;
  room?: Room;
  view?: DuelView;
  bot?: DuelState;
  recorder?: Recorder;
  private generation = 0;
  private rankingRequest = 0;
  private restoreRoomOnConnect = true;
  private commands: DuelCommand[] = [];
  private pending?: {
    seq: number;
    action: { commands: DuelCommand[]; keepAlive: true };
  };
  private inflight = false;
  private roomRequests: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve();
  private releasing?: Room;
  private replaced = false;
  private nextPoll = 0;
  private botStarted = 0;
  private bridge?: Bridge;
  // Serialize room mutations with polls; a cancelled creation must leave before the next join.
  private requestRoom<T>(request: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    const pending = this.roomRequests.catch(() => {}).then(request);
    this.roomRequests = pending;
    return pending;
  }
  private async releaseRoom(room: Room) {
    this.hideRoom(room.code);
    this.releasing = room;
    try {
      await this.bridge!.services.roomAction({
        code: room.code,
        operation: 'leave',
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (
        ![
          'INVITATION_NOT_FOUND',
          'ROOM_NOT_FOUND',
          'NOT_A_MEMBER',
          'STALE_LEASE',
        ].includes((error as { code?: string }).code || '')
      )
        throw error;
    }
    if (this.releasing === room) this.releasing = undefined;
  }
  private hideRoom(code: string) {
    for (const [id, until] of this.hiddenRooms)
      if (until <= Date.now()) this.hiddenRooms.delete(id);
    this.hiddenRooms.set(code, Date.now() + 2000);
    this.rooms = this.rooms.filter((room) => room.code !== code);
    this.revision++;
  }
  readonly changed: (view: DuelView, start: boolean) => void;
  private readonly storage: Pick<Storage, 'getItem' | 'setItem'>;
  constructor(
    storage: Pick<Storage, 'getItem' | 'setItem'>,
    changed: (view: DuelView, start: boolean) => void,
  ) {
    this.storage = storage;
    this.changed = changed;
    let saved: Player | undefined;
    try {
      saved = JSON.parse(storage.getItem('night-overwatch-pilot-v1') || 'null');
    } catch {
      /* Fresh local identity. */
    }
    if (
      !saved ||
      !/^[a-f0-9-]{36}$/.test(playerId(saved)) ||
      typeof saved.name !== 'string'
    ) {
      const bytes = randomBytes();
      bytes[6] = (bytes[6] & 15) | 64;
      bytes[8] = (bytes[8] & 63) | 128;
      const hex = Array.from(bytes)
        .map((n) => n.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
        .join('');
      saved = {
        id: `${hex.slice(0, 8)}-${hex.slice(8, 12)}-${hex.slice(12, 16)}-${hex.slice(16, 20)}-${hex.slice(20)}`,
        name:
          ['星夜', '远山', '银翼', '晨风', '逐月', '寒霜', '流光', '静海'][
            bytes[0] % 8
          ] +
          ['猎鹰', '海燕', '云雀', '白鹭', '苍鹭', '信天翁', '游隼', '雨燕'][
            bytes[1] % 8
          ],
      };
      try {
        storage.setItem('night-overwatch-pilot-v1', JSON.stringify(saved));
      } catch {
        /* Memory-only when storage is unavailable. */
      }
    }
    this.identity = saved;
    this.draftName = saved.name || '';
  }
  async connect(bridge: Bridge | undefined, search = '') {
    if (!bridge || this.bridge === bridge) return;
    const generation = this.generation;
    this.bridge = bridge;
    if (bridge) {
      this.recorder = bridge.createRecorder(RANKED_RULE_VERSION, {
        durable: { storage: this.storage },
        startTimeoutMs: 8000,
        onStatus: (status) => {
          this.rankStatus = status === 'expired' ? 'unavailable' : status;
          if (status === 'expired')
            this.message = '本次行动已结束或超过 24 小时，请重新开始';
          this.revision++;
        },
      });
      const metadata = await bridge.ready;
      this.acceptMetadata(metadata);
      void this.recorder.recover?.();
    }
    this.revision++;
    const code = search ? invitation(search) : undefined;
    if (generation !== this.generation || !this.restoreRoomOnConnect) return;
    try {
      const saved = JSON.parse(
        this.storage.getItem('night-overwatch-room-v1') || 'null',
      );
      if (
        saved &&
        saved.playerId === playerId(this.identity) &&
        validCode(saved.code) &&
        Date.now() - saved.at < 30000
      )
        this.resumeCode = saved.code;
    } catch {
      /* Invalid local room memory cannot join a different identity. */
    }
    if (code) {
      this.draftCode = code;
      this.panel = 'join';
      await this.open('join', code);
    } else if (this.resumeCode) await this.open('resume', this.resumeCode);
  }
  async startRanking(options: Parameters<Recorder['start']>[0]) {
    const generation = this.generation;
    if (!this.ranked) {
      try {
        this.acceptMetadata(await this.bridge?.ready);
        if (!this.ranked) this.acceptMetadata(await this.bridge?.refresh?.());
      } catch {
        /* A failed identity lookup must not start a run under an unconfirmed pilot. */
      }
    }
    if (generation !== this.generation) return;
    if (!this.ranked && !this.recorder?.recover) {
      this.rankStatus = options.practice ? 'practice' : 'unavailable';
      this.revision++;
      return;
    }
    return this.recorder?.start(options);
  }
  private acceptMetadata(metadata: Metadata | undefined) {
    if (!metadata) return;
    const owner = playerId(metadata.identity);
    if (owner !== this.authenticatedId) {
      this.assets = undefined;
      this.battleReward = undefined;
      this.authenticatedId = owner;
      this.chatEpoch++;
      this.chatMessages = [];
      this.chatDraft = '';
      this.chatIntent = undefined;
      this.chatSending = false;
      this.chatMenu = '';
      this.chatCursor = '';
      this.chatAcceptedAt = 0;
      this.chatStatus = '';
      this.chatOwner = owner;
      this.chatRevision++;
      this.blockedPlayers = new Set();
      try {
        this.blockedPlayers = new Set(
          JSON.parse(
            this.storage.getItem('night-chat-blocks:' + owner) || '[]',
          ),
        );
      } catch {}
    }
    this.identity = metadata.identity;
    this.draftName = this.identity.nickname || this.identity.name || '';
    this.available = metadata.capabilities.multiplayer;
    this.ranked = metadata.capabilities.leaderboards;
    this.chatAvailable =
      !!metadata.capabilities.chat && !!this.bridge?.services.nightChat;
    this.revision++;
  }
  assetsReceivedAt = 0;
  async refreshAssets() {
    if (!this.bridge?.services.nightAssets) throw Error('Assets unavailable');
    this.acceptMetadata(await (this.bridge.refresh?.() ?? this.bridge.ready));
    const owner = this.authenticatedId,
      result = await this.bridge.services.nightAssets(owner);
    if (owner !== this.authenticatedId || result.playerId !== owner)
      throw Object.assign(Error('Identity changed'), {
        code: 'IDENTITY_CHANGED',
      });
    this.assets = result;
    this.assetsReceivedAt = performance.now();
    this.revision++;
    return result;
  }
  async assetCommand(command: AssetCommand) {
    if (!this.bridge?.services.nightAssetCommand)
      throw Error('Assets unavailable');
    const owner = this.authenticatedId,
      result = await this.bridge.services.nightAssetCommand(command, owner);
    if (owner !== this.authenticatedId || result.playerId !== owner)
      throw Object.assign(Error('Identity changed'), {
        code: 'IDENTITY_CHANGED',
      });
    this.assets = result;
    this.assetsReceivedAt = performance.now();
    this.revision++;
    return result;
  }
  get active() {
    return !!this.view;
  }
  get spectator() {
    return !!this.room?.spectator || this.room?.you === -1;
  }
  get entering() {
    return this.active && performance.now() < this.enteringUntil;
  }
  get combatants(): Player[] {
    const seat = this.view?.you ?? (this.room?.you === 1 ? 1 : 0);
    return [
      this.room?.players[seat] || this.identity,
      this.room?.players[1 - seat] || this.opponent,
    ];
  }
  get opponent(): Player {
    return (
      this.room?.players[this.room.you === 1 ? 0 : 1] ||
      (this.view?.bot
        ? { id: 'bot-' + this.view.seed, name: '训练机器人' }
        : { name: '等待对手' })
    );
  }
  get offlineNotice() {
    const player = this.room?.players.find(
      (p, i) => p.disconnected && (this.spectator || i !== this.room?.you),
    );
    return player ? (player.nickname || player.name || '对方') + ' 已下线' : '';
  }
  show(panel: Online['panel'], push = true) {
    if (push && panel && panel !== this.panel)
      this.panelHistory.push(this.panel);
    if (panel !== this.panel) {
      this.chatEpoch++;
      this.chatNext = 0;
    }
    if (panel === 'profile')
      this.draftName = this.identity.nickname || this.identity.name || '';
    this.panel = panel;
    this.message = '';
    this.revision++;
  }
  private rememberRoom(room?: Room) {
    try {
      this.storage.setItem(
        'night-overwatch-room-v1',
        room && !room.spectator && room.you >= 0 && room.status === 'playing'
          ? JSON.stringify({
              code: room.code,
              playerId: playerId(this.identity),
              at: Date.now(),
            })
          : 'null',
      );
    } catch {
      /* Session remains playable without storage. */
    }
  }
  async loadRooms() {
    if (this.busy || this.room || this.active) return;
    this.busy = true;
    const generation = this.generation;
    try {
      if (!this.available) this.acceptMetadata(await this.bridge?.refresh?.());
      if (!this.available || !this.bridge?.services.listRooms) {
        this.message = '联机暂不可用，可选择本地人机练习';
        return;
      }
      const { rooms } = await this.bridge.services.listRooms();
      if (generation === this.generation) {
        this.rooms = rooms.filter(
          (room) => (this.hiddenRooms.get(room.code) || 0) <= Date.now(),
        );
        this.message = '';
      }
    } catch {
      if (generation === this.generation)
        this.message = '网络异常，正在重新读取房间';
    } finally {
      if (generation === this.generation) {
        this.busy = false;
        this.listAt = performance.now() + 3000;
        this.revision++;
      }
    }
  }
  async saveName() {
    if (this.busy) return;
    const nickname = this.draftName
      .normalize('NFKC')
      .trim()
      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
    if (
      this.draftName.length > 100 ||
      Array.from(nickname).length < 2 ||
      Array.from(nickname).length > 16 ||
      !/^[\p{L}\p{N} ·_-]+$/u.test(nickname)
    ) {
      this.message = '昵称需为 2–16 个字，可用文字、数字、空格、·、_、-';
      this.revision++;
      return;
    }
    this.busy = true;
    try {
      if (this.bridge?.services.updateProfile)
        this.identity = await this.bridge.services.updateProfile({ nickname });
      else {
        this.identity = { ...this.identity, name: nickname, nickname };
        this.storage.setItem(
          'night-overwatch-pilot-v1',
          JSON.stringify(this.identity),
        );
      }
      this.message = this.available ? '昵称已保存' : '昵称已保存在本机';
      this.draftName = this.identity.nickname || this.identity.name || nickname;
      if (this.panel === 'profile') this.back();
    } catch {
      this.message = '昵称保存失败，请重试';
    } finally {
      this.busy = false;
      this.revision++;
    }
  }
  async loadHistory(cursor?: string) {
    if (this.busy) return;
    this.show('history');
    this.busy = true;
    const generation = this.generation;
    try {
      if (!this.available) this.acceptMetadata(await this.bridge?.refresh?.());
      if (!this.bridge?.services.history || !this.available) {
        this.message = '对战记录暂不可用';
        return;
      }
      const result = await this.bridge.services.history({ cursor });
      if (generation !== this.generation || this.panel !== 'history') return;
      this.history =
        cursor && this.history
          ? { ...result, matches: [...this.history.matches, ...result.matches] }
          : result;
      if (!cursor) this.historyPage = 0;
    } catch {
      if (generation === this.generation)
        this.message = '对战记录读取失败，请重试';
    } finally {
      if (generation === this.generation) {
        this.busy = false;
        this.revision++;
      }
    }
  }
  async open(
    kind: 'friend' | 'match' | 'join' | 'bot' | 'watch' | 'resume',
    code?: string,
    createdAt?: number,
  ) {
    if (this.busy || this.active || this.room) return;
    if (
      kind === 'friend' &&
      this.privateRoom &&
      (this.draftPassword.length < 4 || this.draftPassword.length > 64)
    ) {
      this.message = '私密房密码需要 4–64 个字符';
      this.revision++;
      return;
    }
    if (
      ['join', 'watch', 'resume'].includes(kind) &&
      !validCode(code || this.draftCode.trim().toUpperCase())
    ) {
      this.message = '请输入正确的房间号';
      this.revision++;
      return;
    }
    this.message = '';
    const generation = ++this.generation;
    this.busy = true;
    this.revision++;
    try {
      if (!this.available) this.acceptMetadata(await this.bridge?.refresh?.());
      if (generation !== this.generation) return;
      if (!this.available || !this.bridge) {
        if (kind === 'bot') {
          this.busy = false;
          this.startBot(performance.now());
          return;
        }
        this.message = '联机服务暂不可用，可选择本地人机练习';
        return;
      }
      const services = this.bridge.services,
        input = {
          code: code || this.draftCode.trim().toUpperCase(),
          password: this.draftPassword || undefined,
          createdAt,
        };
      const room = await this.requestRoom(async () => {
        if (generation !== this.generation) return;
        if (this.releasing) await this.releaseRoom(this.releasing);
        if (generation !== this.generation) return;
        const room =
          kind === 'friend' || kind === 'bot'
            ? await services.createRoom({
                mode: kind,
                visibility:
                  this.privateRoom && kind === 'friend' ? 'private' : 'public',
                ...(this.privateRoom && kind === 'friend'
                  ? { password: this.draftPassword }
                  : {}),
              })
            : kind === 'match'
              ? await services.match()
              : kind === 'watch'
                ? await services.spectateRoom!(input)
                : kind === 'resume'
                  ? await services.connectRoom!({ code: input.code })
                  : await services.joinRoom(input);
        if (generation !== this.generation) {
          await this.releaseRoom(room);
          return;
        }
        return room;
      });
      if (!room || generation !== this.generation) return;
      this.panel = 'room';
      this.draftPassword = '';
      this.accept(room);
      this.nextPoll = 0;
      if (kind === 'bot' && room.status === 'waiting') {
        const started = await this.requestRoom(async () =>
          generation === this.generation
            ? services.roomAction({
                code: room.code,
                operation: 'ready',
                body: { ready: true },
              })
            : undefined,
        );
        if (started && generation === this.generation) this.accept(started);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (generation === this.generation) {
        const code = (error as { code?: string }).code || '';
        this.message = /PASSWORD/.test(code)
          ? '房间密码不正确，请重新输入'
          : /FULL|STARTED/.test(code)
            ? '房间已满或已经开战，请选择其他房间'
            : /STALE_LEASE/.test(code)
              ? '此对局已在另一个页面恢复'
              : '暂时无法进入房间，请检查房号与网络后重试';
        if (
          kind === 'resume' &&
          [
            'PLAYER_DISCONNECTED',
            'NOT_A_MEMBER',
            'ROOM_NOT_FOUND',
            'INVITATION_NOT_FOUND',
            'MATCH_CLOSED',
            'STALE_LEASE',
          ].includes(code)
        ) {
          this.resumeCode = '';
          this.rememberRoom();
        }
      }
    } finally {
      if (generation === this.generation) {
        this.busy = false;
        this.revision++;
      }
    }
  }
  startBot(now: number) {
    if (this.busy || this.active || this.room) return;
    this.generation++;
    this.bot = createDuel(
      new DataView(randomBytes().buffer).getUint32(0) & 0x7fffffff,
      true,
    );
    configureDuel(this.bot, [
      practiceLoadout(
        this.assets,
        'duel',
        performance.now() - this.assetsReceivedAt,
      ),
    ]);
    this.botStarted = now + 1200;
    this.nextPoll = 0;
    this.panel = '';
    this.message = '本地练习 · 不计公开积分';
    this.enteringUntil = this.botStarted;
    this.acceptView(viewDuel(this.bot));
  }
  async ready() {
    if (
      !this.room ||
      this.room.status !== 'waiting' ||
      !this.bridge ||
      this.busy ||
      this.active ||
      this.spectator
    )
      return;
    const generation = this.generation,
      code = this.room.code,
      ready = !this.room.players[this.room.you]?.ready;
    this.busy = true;
    this.message = '';
    this.revision++;
    try {
      const room = await this.requestRoom(async () =>
        generation === this.generation &&
        this.room?.code === code &&
        this.room.status === 'waiting' &&
        !this.active &&
        !this.spectator
          ? this.bridge!.services.roomAction({
              code,
              operation: 'ready',
              body: { ready },
            })
          : undefined,
      );
      if (room && generation === this.generation) this.accept(room);
    } catch {
      if (generation === this.generation)
        this.message = '准备状态更新失败，请重试';
    } finally {
      if (generation === this.generation) {
        this.busy = false;
        this.revision++;
      }
    }
  }
  leave() {
    this.restoreRoomOnConnect = false;
    const room = this.room;
    this.generation++;
    this.busy = false;
    this.inflight = false;
    if (room) this.hideRoom(room.code);
    this.resumeCode =
      room?.status === 'playing' &&
      room.mode !== 'bot' &&
      !this.view?.finished &&
      !this.spectator
        ? room.code
        : '';
    this.rememberRoom(this.resumeCode ? room : undefined);
    this.connectionLost = false;
    this.replaced = false;
    this.roomKey = '';
    this.enteringUntil = 0;
    this.room = this.view = this.bot = undefined;
    this.panel = '';
    this.message = '';
    this.commands = [];
    this.pending = undefined;
    this.nextPoll = 0;
    this.botStarted = 0;
    this.revision++;
    if (room && this.bridge)
      void this.requestRoom(() => this.releaseRoom(room)).catch(() => {
        this.message = '离开房间未确认，再次进入时会重试';
        this.revision++;
      });
  }
  control(input: Record<string, unknown>) {
    if (
      !this.view ||
      this.view.finished ||
      this.spectator ||
      this.entering ||
      !controls.has(String(input.command))
    )
      return;
    const command = input as DuelCommand;
    if (this.bot) {
      commandDuel(this.bot, 0, [command]);
      return;
    }
    if (
      command.command === 'setAim' &&
      this.commands.at(-1)?.command === 'setAim'
    )
      this.commands.pop();
    if (this.commands.length >= 60) {
      this.commands = [{ command: 'clearInput', args: [] }];
      this.message = '网络拥堵，已停止开火';
    }
    this.commands.push(command);
  }
  bind(sim: Simulation, blocked = () => sim.paused) {
    observeNightInputs(sim, (input) => {
      const attack =
        input.command === 'fire' ||
        (input.command === 'setFire' && (input.args as unknown[])[1] === true);
      if (!attack || !blocked()) this.control(input);
    });
  }
  async pump(now: number) {
    void this.pollChat(now);
    if (now >= this.syncAt) {
      this.syncAt = now + 10000;
      void this.recorder?.recover?.();
    }
    if (this.replaced) return;
    if (this.bot) {
      if (now < this.nextPoll) return;
      this.nextPoll = now + 50;
      if (!this.bot.finished && now >= this.botStarted) {
        advanceDuel(this.bot, now - this.botStarted);
        this.acceptView(viewDuel(this.bot));
      }
      return;
    }
    if (this.entering) return;
    if (this.panel === 'menu' && !this.room && now >= this.listAt) {
      await this.loadRooms();
      return;
    }
    if (
      !this.room ||
      !['waiting', 'playing'].includes(this.room.status) ||
      !this.bridge ||
      this.busy ||
      this.inflight ||
      now < this.nextPoll ||
      this.view?.finished
    )
      return;
    const generation = this.generation,
      code = this.room.code;
    this.inflight = true;
    this.nextPoll = now + (this.room.status === 'playing' ? 100 : 1000);
    try {
      const room = await this.requestRoom(async () => {
        if (generation !== this.generation || !this.room || !this.bridge)
          return;
        let room: Room;
        if (this.room.status === 'playing' && !this.spectator) {
          this.pending ||= {
            seq: this.room.seq + 1,
            action: { commands: this.commands.splice(0, 64), keepAlive: true },
          };
          try {
            room = await this.bridge.services.roomAction({
              code,
              operation: 'actions',
              body: this.pending,
            });
          } catch (error) {
            if (
              ![
                'MATCH_CLOSED',
                'PLAYER_FINISHED',
                'PLAYER_DISCONNECTED',
              ].includes((error as { code?: string }).code || '')
            )
              throw error;
            room = await this.bridge.services.getRoom({ code });
          }
        } else room = await this.bridge!.services.getRoom({ code });
        return room;
      });
      if (!room || generation !== this.generation) return;
      this.pending = undefined;
      this.connectionLost = false;
      this.message = '';
      this.accept(room);
    } catch (error) {
      if (generation === this.generation) {
        this.connectionLost = true;
        this.replaced = [
          'STALE_LEASE',
          'RULE_VERSION_CHANGED',
          'NOT_A_MEMBER',
          'INVITATION_NOT_FOUND',
        ].includes((error as { code?: string }).code || '');
        this.message =
          (error as { code?: string }).code === 'STALE_LEASE'
            ? '对局已在另一页面恢复，请关闭本页面'
            : (error as { code?: string }).code === 'RULE_VERSION_CHANGED'
              ? '游戏已更新，请返回大厅重新进入'
              : (error as { code?: string }).code === 'NOT_A_MEMBER'
                ? '观战连接已过期，请返回大厅重新观战'
                : (error as { code?: string }).code === 'INVITATION_NOT_FOUND'
                  ? '房间已关闭，请返回大厅'
                  : '网络异常，正在重连 · 30秒内恢复可继续对战';
        this.nextPoll = now + 1000;
        this.revision++;
      }
    } finally {
      if (generation === this.generation) this.inflight = false;
    }
  }
  private accept(room: Room) {
    const start = !this.view && !!room.state;
    this.room = room;
    const key = JSON.stringify([
      room.code,
      room.status,
      room.players,
      room.settlement,
      room.you,
    ]);
    if (key !== this.roomKey) {
      this.roomKey = key;
      this.revision++;
    }
    if (room.status === 'playing') {
      this.resumeCode = room.code;
      this.rememberRoom(room);
    }
    if (room.state?.finished) {
      this.resumeCode = '';
      this.rememberRoom();
    }
    if (start)
      this.enteringUntil =
        performance.now() +
        (room.startsAt === undefined
          ? 0
          : Math.max(900, room.startsAt - (room.serverNow || Date.now())));
    if (room.state) {
      if (
        room.you >= 0 &&
        room.players[room.you]?.takenOver &&
        room.settlement
      ) {
        const state = {
          ...room.state,
          simulation: { ...room.state.simulation },
        };
        state.finished = true;
        state.winner = room.you === 0 ? 1 : 0;
        state.simulation.phase = 'failure';
        this.resumeCode = '';
        this.rememberRoom();
        this.message = '重连时间已过，本局已按离线失败结算';
        this.acceptView(state);
        return;
      }
      if (
        !['waiting', 'playing', 'finished'].includes(room.status) &&
        !room.state.finished
      ) {
        const state = {
          ...room.state,
          finished: true,
          winner: null,
          simulation: { ...room.state.simulation, phase: 'failure' as const },
        };
        this.message = '房间已结束';
        this.acceptView(state);
        return;
      }
      this.acceptView(room.state);
    } else if (!['waiting', 'playing'].includes(room.status))
      this.message = '房间已结束或对手已离开，请返回';
  }
  private acceptView(view: DuelView) {
    const start = !this.view;
    this.view = view;
    if (start) this.panel = '';
    this.changed(view, start);
  }
  async cycleRankingVersion() {
    this.rankingVersion =
      this.rankingVersion === 'night-campaign-v2'
        ? 'night-growth-v1'
        : this.rankingVersion === 'night-growth-v1'
          ? 'night-records-v1'
          : 'night-campaign-v2';
    await this.loadRanking();
  }
  async loadRanking(level = this.level, board = this.board) {
    const request = ++this.rankingRequest;
    this.board = board;
    this.level = Math.max(0, Math.min(MISSIONS.length - 1, level));
    if (this.panel !== 'ranking') this.show('ranking');
    this.ranking = undefined;
    this.message = '';
    this.revision++;
    const ownLevel = this.level,
      ownBoard = this.board,
      ownVersion = this.rankingVersion;
    try {
      if (!this.ranked) this.acceptMetadata(await this.bridge?.refresh?.());
      if (
        request !== this.rankingRequest ||
        this.level !== ownLevel ||
        this.board !== ownBoard ||
        this.panel !== 'ranking'
      )
        return;
      if (!this.ranked || !this.bridge) {
        this.message = '全站排行榜暂不可用；个人最佳仍保存在本机';
        this.revision++;
        return;
      }
      const ranking = await this.bridge.services.leaderboard({
        boardId:
          ownBoard === 'level' ? 'level-' + MISSIONS[ownLevel].id : ownBoard,
        ...(ownBoard === 'level' ? { ruleVersion: ownVersion } : {}),
      });
      if (
        request === this.rankingRequest &&
        this.level === ownLevel &&
        this.board === ownBoard &&
        this.rankingVersion === ownVersion &&
        this.panel === 'ranking'
      ) {
        this.ranking = ranking;
        this.revision++;
      }
    } catch {
      if (request === this.rankingRequest && this.panel === 'ranking') {
        this.message = '排行榜读取失败，点击刷新重试';
        this.revision++;
      }
    }
  }
  async share(href: string) {
    if (!this.room || this.room.status !== 'waiting' || this.spectator) return;
    const url = roomLink(href, this.room.code);
    try {
      if (navigator.share)
        await navigator.share({ title: '夜航守望 · 好友对战', url });
      else {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
        this.message = '房间链接已复制';
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if ((error as Error).name !== 'AbortError')
        this.message = '请复制房间链接：' + url;
    }
    this.revision++;
  }
}
import {
  _decorator,
  Component,
  input,
  Input,
  EventMouse,
  EventTouch,
  EventKeyboard,
  game,
  Game,
  view,
  sys,
  macro,
  director,
} from 'cc';
import { Simulation, type PauseReason } from './core/Simulation';
import { ACTIONS, TUTORIAL } from './core/Actions';
import { World } from './World';
import { HUD } from './HUD';
import { Platform, RADIO } from './Platform';
import { Online } from './Online';
import { DuelPresentation } from './Presentation';
import { shareScreenControl } from './ShareScreen';
import { observeNightInputs } from './core/Replay';
import { MAP, WEAPONS, type Point } from './core/Data';

  MISSIONS,
  nextMission,
  readMissionSearch,
  type MissionId,
} from './core/MissionCatalog';
import { completeMission, missionUnlocked } from './core/MissionProgress';

import { recordMissionTime } from './core/BattleRecords';

  nextZoomLimit,
  type RewardKind,
  type SupplyReward,
} from './core/Rewards';

  Quartermaster,
  type ProgressionPage,
  type LoadoutSlot,
} from './Quartermaster';

import { installDeveloperTools } from './DeveloperTools';
const { ccclass } = _decorator;
type TouchRole = {
  role: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  originX?: number;
  originY?: number;
  button?: string;
  moved?: boolean;
};
@ccclass('Overwatch')
export class Overwatch extends Component {
  sim = new Simulation();
  world!: World;
  hud!: HUD;
  platform!: Platform;
  online?: Online;
  quartermaster?: Quartermaster;
  private stopDeveloperTools?: () => void;
  private starting?: Simulation;
  private servicesReady?: Promise<void>;
  touches = new Map<number, TouchRole>();
  keys = new Set<number>();
  mouseButton = '';
  private infoHold?: { id: string; at: number };
  private listGesture?: {
    pointer: number | 'mouse';
    key: string;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    lastX: number;
    lastY: number;
    axis: 'x' | 'y';
    button?: string;
    moved: boolean;
    started: number;
  };
  private loadoutGesture?: {
    pointer: number | 'mouse';
    item?: string;
    source?: LoadoutSlot;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    lastY: number;
    mode: 'pending' | 'scroll' | 'drag';
  };
  private controlGesture?: {
    pointer: number | 'mouse';
    button: string;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    origin: { x: number; y: number };
    position?: { x: number; y: number };
    moved: boolean;
  };
  accumulator = 0;
  lastEvent = 0;
  frameCount = 0;
  frameSeconds = 0;
  measuredFps = 0;
  resizeKey = '';
  lastAimInput = 0;
  selectedMission: MissionId = 'corridor-01';
  private radioMission?: Simulation;
  private radioTarget?: number;
  private radioAt = -Infinity;
  operationPractice = false;
  private rewardRequest?: {
    kind: RewardKind;
    supply?: SupplyReward;
    sim: Simulation;
  };
  private deferredSupply?: { sim: Simulation; reward: SupplyReward };
  private disposed = false;
  private startupSignalled = false;
  private pauseControl?: HTMLButtonElement;
  private screenControl?: ReturnType<typeof shareScreenControl>;
  private presentation = new DuelPresentation();
  private presentedTime = 0;
  private completionSpoken = false;
  start() {
    view.enableAutoFullScreen(false);
    view.resizeWithBrowserSize(true);
    view.setOrientation(macro.ORIENTATION_LANDSCAPE);
    this.world = new World(this.node);
    this.hud = new HUD(this.node);
    this.world.camera.visibility = 1 << 30;
    this.platform = new Platform(this.node, this.pause, this.clear);
    this.prepareMission(sys.isBrowser ? location.search : '');
    this.online = new Online(sys.localStorage, (state, start) => {
      const previous = this.sim,
        pauses = new Set(previous.pauses);
      this.sim = Simulation.restore(state.simulation);
      this.sim.pauses = pauses;
      if (state.finished) {
        if (previous.phase === 'playing') void this.quartermaster?.refresh();
        for (const reason of [
          'settings',
          'help',
          'manual',
          'countdown',
        ] as const)
          this.sim.pauses.delete(reason);
        this.hud.exitConfirm =
          this.hud.reportOpen =
          this.hud.equipmentOpen =
          this.hud.toolsOpen =
          this.hud.zoomOpen =
            false;
      }
      if (!start) this.sim.aim = { ...previous.aim };
      this.online!.bind(this.sim, () => this.sim.paused || !!this.hud.modal);
      if (start) {
        this.presentation = new DuelPresentation();
        this.completionSpoken = false;
        this.clear();
        this.lastEvent = 0;
        this.accumulator = 0;
        this.hud.controlPositions = {};
        this.hud.reportOpen = this.hud.equipmentOpen = false;
        this.hud.timeComparison = undefined;
        this.hud.missionsOpen = false;
        this.hud.tutorial = false;
        this.syncWorld();
        this.world.follow = false;
        this.world.center = { x: 0, z: 0 };
        this.world.updateCamera();
      }
      this.presentation.push(this.sim, performance.now());
      this.hud.modalKey = state.finished ? 'rebuild' : this.hud.modalKey;
    });
    this.hud.online = this.online;
    this.quartermaster = new Quartermaster(this.online, sys.localStorage);
    this.hud.quartermaster = this.quartermaster;
    this.stopDeveloperTools = installDeveloperTools(this);
    this.servicesReady = this.connectServices();
    this.syncWorld();
    this.hud.tutorial = this.platform.readCoach();
    this.hud.muted = this.platform.muted;
    this.hud.voicePrompts = this.platform.voicePrompts;
    this.hud.autoPriority = this.platform.autoPriority;
    this.hud.reducedEffects = this.platform.reducedEffects;
    if (sys.isBrowser) {
      this.screenControl = shareScreenControl(location.search, (active) => {
        this.hud.screenActive = active;
      });
      this.hud.screenControl = !!this.screenControl;
      this.hud.resize();
      const button = document.createElement('button');
      button.setAttribute('aria-label', '设置');
      button.style.cssText =
        'position:absolute;width:1px;height:1px;overflow:hidden;clip-path:inset(50%);';
      button.addEventListener('click', () => this.action('settings'));
      document.body.appendChild(button);
      this.pauseControl = button;
    }
    input.on(Input.EventType.MOUSE_MOVE, this.mouseMove, this);
    input.on(Input.EventType.MOUSE_DOWN, this.mouseDown, this);
    input.on(Input.EventType.MOUSE_UP, this.mouseUp, this);
    input.on(Input.EventType.MOUSE_WHEEL, this.wheel, this);
    input.on(Input.EventType.TOUCH_START, this.touchStart, this);
    input.on(Input.EventType.TOUCH_MOVE, this.touchMove, this);
    input.on(Input.EventType.TOUCH_END, this.touchEnd, this);
    input.on(Input.EventType.TOUCH_CANCEL, this.touchCancel, this);
    input.on(Input.EventType.KEY_DOWN, this.keyDown, this);
    input.on(Input.EventType.KEY_UP, this.keyUp, this);
    game.on(Game.EVENT_HIDE, this.hide, this);
    game.on(Game.EVENT_SHOW, this.show, this);
    if (sys.isBrowser) window.addEventListener('pagehide', this.persistRanking);
    if (
      sys.isBrowser &&
      (location.hostname === 'localhost' || location.hostname === '127.0.0.1')
    )
      (globalThis as any).__night = {
        snapshot: () => this.snapshot(),
        // Read-only observability. Tests send real keyboard/mouse/touch inputs.
        screenPoint: (p: Point) => {
          const q = this.world.project(p);
          return {
            x: q.x / view.getScaleX(),
            y: this.hud.h - q.y / view.getScaleY(),
          };
        },
        flightTime: (p: Point, weapon: number) =>
          this.sim.flightTime(weapon, p),
      };
  }
  pause = (reason: PauseReason, on: boolean) => {
    if (on) this.clear();
    this.sim.pause(reason, on);
    this.accumulator = 0;
    if (on)
      this.platform?.stop(
        this.sim.extractionRemaining !== undefined &&
          this.sim.extractionRadioPending,
      );
    else this.activateDeferredSupply();
  };
  prepareMission(search: string) {
    this.hud.missionProgress = this.platform.readOperationProgress();
    this.hud.developer = this.platform.developer;
    this.operationPractice = this.hud.developer;
    this.selectedMission = this.platform.readMission();
    if (
      !this.hud.developer &&
      !missionUnlocked(this.hud.missionProgress, this.selectedMission)
    )
      this.selectedMission = 'corridor-01';
    let launch = readMissionSearch(search);
    if (
      launch &&
      launch !== 'training-60' &&
      !this.hud.developer &&
      !missionUnlocked(this.hud.missionProgress, launch)
    )
      launch = undefined;
    if (launch && launch !== 'training-60') this.selectedMission = launch;
    this.sim = new Simulation(launch || this.selectedMission);
    this.hud.trainingBest = this.platform.readWarmupRecord();
  }
  clear = () => {
    if (this.controlGesture)
      this.hud.placeControl(
        this.controlGesture.button,
        this.controlGesture.position,
      );
    this.controlGesture = undefined;
    if (this.hud) this.hud.mousePointer = undefined;
    this.infoHold = undefined;
    this.listGesture = undefined;
    this.loadoutGesture = undefined;
    if (this.quartermaster?.drag) {
      this.quartermaster.drag = undefined;
      this.quartermaster.revision++;
    }
    this.sim.clearInput();
    this.touches.clear();
    this.keys.clear();
    this.mouseButton = '';
    if (this.world) {
      this.world.temporary = false;
      this.world.updateCamera();
    }
  };
  private persistRanking = () => this.online?.recorder?.persist?.();
  hide() {
    this.online?.setForeground(false);
    this.pause('background', true);
    this.online?.recorder?.persist?.();
  }
  show() {
    this.resizeKey = '';
    this.platform?.restoreFocus();
    this.online?.setForeground(true);
    this.pause('background', false);
    void this.online?.recorder?.recover?.();
  }
  retry() {
    this.deferredSupply = undefined;
    const pauses = new Set(this.sim.pauses);
    this.clear();
    this.sim = new Simulation(this.sim.mission.id);
    for (const r of Array.from(pauses))
      if (['background', 'orientation', 'focus'].includes(r))
        this.sim.pauses.add(r);
    this.beginCampaign();
    this.accumulator = 0;
    this.lastEvent = 0;
    this.world.reset();
    this.world.setView(this.platform.viewMode);
    this.syncRewards();
    this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
    this.hud.equipmentOpen =
      this.hud.supplyOpen =
      this.hud.reportOpen =
      this.hud.equipmentOpen =
        false;
    this.hud.timeComparison = undefined;
    this.hud.exitConfirm = this.hud.toolsOpen = this.hud.zoomOpen = false;
  }
  selectMission(id: MissionId, persist = false) {
    if (this.sim.phase === 'playing') return;
    this.deferredSupply = undefined;
    this.online?.recorder?.invalidate();
    this.hud.developer = this.platform.developer;
    if (
      id !== 'training-60' &&
      !this.hud.developer &&
      !missionUnlocked(this.hud.missionProgress || this.platform.missions, id)
    ) {
      this.hud.toast = this.hud.t(
        '完成上一任务后解锁',
        'COMPLETE THE PREVIOUS OPERATION TO UNLOCK',
      );
      this.hud.toastUntil = Date.now() + 3000;
      return;
    }
    this.operationPractice = this.hud.developer;
    const pauses = new Set(this.sim.pauses);
    this.clear();
    this.sim = new Simulation(id);
    if (this.sim.mission.mode === 'escort') {
      this.selectedMission = this.sim.mission.id;
      if (persist) this.platform.saveMission(this.selectedMission);
    }
    this.syncMissionAddress();
    for (const reason of pauses)
      if (['background', 'orientation', 'focus'].includes(reason))
        this.sim.pauses.add(reason);
    this.hud.trainingBest = this.platform.readWarmupRecord();
    this.syncWorld();
    this.lastEvent = this.accumulator = 0;
    this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
    this.hud.equipmentOpen =
      this.hud.supplyOpen =
      this.hud.reportOpen =
      this.hud.equipmentOpen =
        false;
    this.hud.timeComparison = undefined;
    this.hud.exitConfirm = this.hud.toolsOpen = this.hud.zoomOpen = false;
  }
  syncWorld() {
    if (this.world.map !== this.sim.mission.map) {
      this.world.root.active = false;
      this.world.root.destroy();
      this.world = new World(this.node, this.sim.mission.map);
      this.world.camera.visibility = 1 << 30;
    }
    this.world.reset();
    this.world.setView(this.platform.viewMode);
    this.syncRewards();
  }
  async connectServices() {
    const globals = globalThis as any,
      deadline = Date.now() + 8000;
    while (
      !globals.SmallGamesServices &&
      globals.__COMPETITION_CONFIG__?.competitionEnabled &&
      !this.disposed &&
      Date.now() < deadline
    )
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
    if (this.disposed) return;
    await this.online?.connect(
      globals.SmallGamesServices,
      sys.isBrowser ? location.search : '',
    );
    if (!this.disposed) await this.quartermaster?.refresh();
    if (!this.disposed) this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
  }
  beginCampaign() {
    if (this.starting === this.sim) return;
    this.online?.leave();
    if (this.quartermaster) this.quartermaster.page = '';
    if (this.online) this.online.battleReward = undefined;
    const sim = this.sim,
      online = this.online;
    const begin = () => {
      if (this.sim !== sim || this.disposed) return;
      if (sim.phase === 'briefing') this.hud.controlPositions = {};
      sim.start();
      this.completionSpoken = false;
      const recorder = online?.recorder;
      if (online?.rankStatus === 'recording') this.hud.toastUntil = 0;
      if (
        sim.mission.mode === 'escort' &&
        online?.rankStatus === 'pending-sync'
      ) {
        this.hud.toast = this.hud.t(
          '进度暂存本机，联网后自动同步',
          'PROGRESS SAVED · SYNC WHEN ONLINE',
        );
        this.hud.toastUntil = Date.now() + 5000;
      } else if (
        sim.mission.mode === 'escort' &&
        online?.rankStatus !== 'recording'
      ) {
        this.hud.toast = this.hud.t(
          '本局成绩仅保存在本机，不计入全站榜',
          'LOCAL RESULT · NOT RANKED',
        );
        this.hud.toastUntil = Date.now() + 5000;
      }
      if (recorder)
        observeNightInputs(sim, (input, ticks) =>
          recorder.record(input, ticks),
        );
      if (recorder && sim.homingAmmo > 0)
        recorder.record(
          { command: 'grantHomingAmmo', args: [sim.homingAmmo] },
          0,
        );
      if (recorder)
        for (const reason of sim.pauses)
          recorder.record({ command: 'pause', args: [reason, true] }, 0);
    };
    if (!online || sim.mission.mode !== 'escort') {
      if (!sim.loadout.effects.length)
        sim.applyLoadout(
          practiceLoadout(
            online?.assets,
            'solo',
            online ? performance.now() - online.assetsReceivedAt : 0,
          ),
        );
      begin();
      return;
    }
    this.starting = sim;
    this.hud.toast = this.hud.t('正在准备计时…', 'PREPARING TIMER…');
    this.hud.toastUntil = Date.now() + 16000;
    const start = async () => {
      await this.servicesReady;
      if (this.sim !== sim || this.disposed) return;
      const run = await online.startRanking({
        mode: 'level-' + sim.mission.id,
        levelId: sim.mission.id,
        practice: this.operationPractice || this.platform.developer,
      });
      if (this.sim !== sim || this.disposed) return;
      if (run?.loadout) {
        sim.applyLoadout(run.loadout);
        if (run.loadout.skipped.length) {
          this.hud.toast = this.hud.t(
            '部分军需不足或已过期，本场使用其余方案',
            'Some gear is unavailable. Remaining loadout applied.',
          );
          this.hud.toastUntil = Date.now() + 5000;
        }
      } else if (this.operationPractice || this.platform.developer)
        sim.applyLoadout(
          practiceLoadout(
            online.assets,
            'solo',
            performance.now() - online.assetsReceivedAt,
          ),
        );
      else online.recorder?.invalidate('local');
    };
    void start()
      .then(begin, begin)
      .finally(() => {
        if (this.starting === sim) this.starting = undefined;
      });
  }
  syncRewards() {
    if (this.sim.duelMode) {
      this.hud.rewardsAvailable = false;
      this.hud.pendingSupply = false;
      this.sim.homingAmmo = 0;
      return;
    }
    this.hud.rewardsAvailable = this.platform.rewardsAvailable;
    const additionalAmmo = this.platform.rewards.ammo - this.sim.homingAmmo;
    if (additionalAmmo > 0) this.sim.grantHomingAmmo(additionalAmmo);
    this.world.zoomLimit = this.platform.rewards.zoomLimit;
    this.hud.zoomLimit = this.platform.rewards.zoomLimit;
    this.hud.pendingSupply = this.platform.rewards.pendingSupply;
    this.world.updateCamera();
  }
  async requestReward(kind: RewardKind, supply?: SupplyReward) {
    if (this.sim.duelMode || !this.platform.rewardsAvailable) return;
    if (
      this.rewardRequest ||
      this.sim.phase !== 'playing' ||
      this.sim.buff ||
      this.sim.resumeCountdown > 0 ||
      ['background', 'focus', 'orientation', 'help', 'mission'].some((reason) =>
        this.sim.pauses.has(reason as PauseReason),
      ) ||
      (kind === 'zoom' && !nextZoomLimit(this.platform.rewards.zoomLimit))
    )
      return;
    if (this.platform.rewards.pendingSupply) {
      this.openSupply();
      return;
    }
    const request = { kind, supply, sim: this.sim };
    this.rewardRequest = request;
    this.hud.toast = '';
    this.hud.toastUntil = 0;
    this.hud.supplyMessage = '';
    this.hud.supplyOpen = false;
    this.pause('manual', true);
    this.pause('settings', false);
    this.hud.toolsOpen = this.hud.zoomOpen = false;
    this.pause('supply', false);
    this.hud.advert = { kind, mock: false };
    this.pause('advert', true);
    const outcome = await this.platform.offerReward(kind);
    if (this.rewardRequest !== request) return;
    if (outcome === 'mock') {
      this.hud.advert = { kind, mock: true };
      return;
    }
    this.finishReward(outcome === 'completed');
    if (outcome !== 'completed' && !this.disposed) {
      this.hud.toast =
        outcome === 'dismissed'
          ? this.hud.t('观看已中断，未获得奖励', 'INTERRUPTED · NO REWARD')
          : this.hud.t('未获得奖励，请稍后再试', 'UNAVAILABLE · NO REWARD');
      this.hud.toastUntil = Date.now() + 4000;
      if (kind === 'supply') {
        this.hud.supplyMessage = this.hud.toast;
        this.hud.toast = '';
        this.hud.toastUntil = 0;
        this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
      }
    }
  }
  finishReward(completed: boolean) {
    if (!this.rewardRequest) return;
    const { kind, supply, sim } = this.rewardRequest;
    this.rewardRequest = undefined;
    if (completed) this.platform.grantReward(kind);
    if (this.disposed) return;
    this.syncRewards();
    this.hud.advert = undefined;
    this.platform.restoreFocus();
    this.pause('advert', false);
    this.pause('manual', true);
    if (kind === 'supply') {
      this.openSupply();
      if (completed && supply && sim === this.sim) {
        this.deferredSupply = { sim, reward: supply };
        this.activateDeferredSupply();
      } else if (!completed)
        this.hud.supplyMessage = this.hud.t(
          '未领取支援，可重试或继续战斗',
          'NO REWARD · RETRY OR RESUME',
        );
    } else if (completed) this.resumeBattle(false);
    this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
  }
  openSupply() {
    if (!this.platform.rewardsAvailable && !this.platform.rewards.pendingSupply)
      return;
    if (
      this.sim.phase !== 'playing' ||
      this.rewardRequest ||
      this.sim.resumeCountdown > 0
    )
      return;
    this.hud.reportOpen =
      this.hud.equipmentOpen =
      this.hud.toolsOpen =
      this.hud.zoomOpen =
        false;
    this.pause('supply', true);
    this.pause('settings', false);
    this.hud.supplyOpen = true;
    this.hud.supplyMessage = '';
    this.hud.pendingSupply = this.platform.rewards.pendingSupply;
    this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
  }
  closeSupply() {
    this.deferredSupply = undefined;
    this.hud.supplyOpen = false;
    this.clear();
    this.hud.toast = '';
    this.hud.toastUntil = 0;
    this.pause('countdown', false);
    this.pause('supply', false);
    this.pause('manual', false);
    this.accumulator = 0;
  }
  private activateDeferredSupply() {
    const pending = this.deferredSupply;
    if (!pending) return;
    if (
      this.disposed ||
      pending.sim !== this.sim ||
      this.sim.phase !== 'playing' ||
      !this.hud.supplyOpen ||
      !this.platform.rewards.pendingSupply
    ) {
      this.deferredSupply = undefined;
      return;
    }
    if (
      ['background', 'focus', 'orientation'].some((reason) =>
        this.sim.pauses.has(reason as PauseReason),
      )
    )
      return;
    this.deferredSupply = undefined;
    this.claimSupply(pending.reward);
  }
  claimSupply(reward: SupplyReward) {
    if (
      this.sim.phase !== 'playing' ||
      !this.hud.supplyOpen ||
      this.sim.buff ||
      !['tracking', 'ammo', 'rate', 'restock'].includes(reward) ||
      !this.platform.claimSupply(reward)
    )
      return;
    if (reward === 'restock') this.sim.grantAmmoSupply();
    else if (reward !== 'ammo') this.sim.grantBuff(reward);
    this.syncRewards();
    if (reward === 'ammo') this.sim.selectHoming();
    this.hud.toast = this.hud.t(
      reward === 'ammo'
        ? '追踪弹 +2 · 点选敌人，再按开火'
        : reward === 'tracking'
          ? '自动追踪已启用 · 持续60秒'
          : '射速提升30% · 持续60秒',
      reward === 'ammo'
        ? '+2 HOMING · TAP ENEMY, THEN FIRE'
        : reward === 'tracking'
          ? 'AUTO TRACKING · 60 SECONDS'
          : '+30% FIRE RATE · 60 SECONDS',
    );
    this.hud.toastUntil = Date.now() + 7500;
    if (reward === 'restock')
      this.hud.toast = this.hud.t(
        '弹药已补充：榴弹 +60 · 重炮 +24 · 燃烧 +6',
        'RESTOCKED: HE +60 · HEAVY +24 · FIRE +6',
      );
    this.hud.supplyOpen = false;
    this.pause('supply', false);
    this.resumeBattle(false);
  }
  resumeBattle(_countdown = false) {
    this.clear();
    this.accumulator = 0;
    this.pause('countdown', false);
    this.pause('manual', false);
  }
  syncMissionAddress() {
    if (!sys.isBrowser) return;
    try {
      const url = new URL(location.href);
      if (!url.searchParams.has('mission')) return;
      const dev = url.searchParams.get('dev');
      url.search = new URLSearchParams({
        mission: this.sim.mission.id,
      }).toString();
      if (dev !== null) url.searchParams.set('dev', dev);
      url.hash = url.username = url.password = '';
      history.replaceState(null, '', url.href);
    } catch {
      /* A public shortcut stays optional when browser history is unavailable. */
    }
  }
  saveMissionResult() {
    if (this.sim.duelMode) return;
    const sim = this.sim,
      online = this.online;
    void online?.recorder?.finish().then((result) => {
      if (this.sim === sim && result && typeof result.coins === 'number') {
        online.battleReward = result;
        online.revision++;
        void this.quartermaster?.refresh();
      }
      if (
        this.sim === sim &&
        result?.accepted &&
        result.fastestEligible === false
      ) {
        online.rankStatus = 'assisted';
        online.revision++;
      }
    });
    this.hud.timeComparison = recordMissionTime(
      this.platform.times,
      this.sim,
      this.operationPractice || this.platform.developer,
    );
    if (this.hud.timeComparison) this.platform.saveTimes();
    const previous = this.platform.missions;
    const next = completeMission(
      previous,
      this.sim.mission.id,
      this.sim.mission.mode === 'escort' &&
        this.sim.phase === 'success' &&
        this.sim.threatsRemaining === 0,
      this.operationPractice || this.platform.developer,
    );
    if (next !== previous) this.platform.saveOperationProgress(next);
    this.hud.missionProgress = next;
  }
  saveTrainingResult() {
    if (this.sim.mission.mode !== 'training') return;
    const previous = this.platform.readWarmupRecord();
    const best = bestTrainingRecord(previous, this.sim);
    this.hud.trainingBest = best;
    if (best && best !== previous) this.platform.saveWarmupRecord(best);
  }
  private progressionAction(id: string) {
    if (id === 'qm:warehouseGo') id = 'qm:warehouse';
    const q = this.quartermaster;
    if (!q) return false;
    if (id === 'skillUse') {
      if (!this.hud.modal && !this.online?.spectator) this.sim.useSkill();
      return true;
    }
    if (
      !/^(qm:|mode:|term:|product:|loadout:|slot:|gear:|equip:|package:|pool:|growth:|skill:|tasks:|task:|taskalt:|achievement:|inbox:|mail:|inventory:|info:)/.test(
        id,
      )
    )
      return false;
    this.clear();
    this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
    const [group, ...rest] = id.split(':'),
      value = rest.join(':');
    if (group === 'qm') {
      if (value === 'artRetry') {
        this.hud.retryArtwork();
        return true;
      }
      const pages = [
        'modes',
        'loadout',
        'shop',
        'supply',
        'growth',
        'tasks',
        'inbox',
        'warehouse',
        'receipts',
        'checkin',
        'probability',
      ];
      if (pages.includes(value)) {
        if (value === 'loadout' && q.page === 'modes')
          q.mode = q.actionMode === 'duel' ? 'duel' : 'solo';
        if (value === 'loadout' && q.page === 'warehouse')
          q.selectInventorySlot();
        this.online?.show('');
        this.hud.missionsOpen = false;
        q.requestLeave(value as ProgressionPage);
        return true;
      }
      if (value === 'home') {
        q.requestLeave('');
        return true;
      }
      if (value === 'back') {
        if (q.info) q.info = '';
        else if (q.confirmation) q.confirmation = undefined;
        else if (q.leaveTo !== undefined) q.cancelLeave();
        else if (q.gift) {
          if (!q.busy) q.gift = undefined;
        } else q.back();
        q.revision++;
        return true;
      }
      if (value === 'announcements') {
        q.show('inbox');
        q.section = 'news';
        return true;
      }
      if (value === 'refresh') {
        void q.refresh();
        return true;
      }
      if (value === 'retry') {
        void q.execute();
        return true;
      }
      if (value === 'prev' || value === 'choosePrev')
        q.index = Math.max(0, q.index - 1);
      if (value === 'next' || value === 'chooseNext') q.index++;
      if (q.page === 'inbox' && (value === 'prev' || value === 'next'))
        q.selectedMail = '';
      if (value === 'list') q.detail = false;
      if (value.startsWith('choose:')) {
        q.index = 0;
        q.message = [
          '从右侧选择已有军需；缺少时可前往商店',
          'Select owned gear on the right, or visit the armory.',
        ];
      }
      if (value === 'buy')
        void q.execute({ kind: 'buy', item: q.selected, quantity: 1 });
      if (value === 'equip') q.equip(q.selected);
      if (value === 'save') void q.save();
      if (value === 'unequip') q.unequip();
      if (value === 'keepEditing') q.cancelLeave();
      if (value === 'discard') q.discard();
      if (value === 'leavePending') q.leavePending();
      if (value === 'giftOpen') void q.openGifts();
      if (value === 'giftClose') q.gift = undefined;
      if (value === 'giftSkip' && q.gift) q.gift.started = undefined;
      if (value === 'infoClose') q.info = '';
      if (value === 'wheelSkip' && q.wheel) {
        q.wheel.started = 0;
        q.tickPresentation(true);
      }
      if (value === 'wheelSpin' && (!q.wheel || q.wheel.shown))
        void q.execute({ kind: 'wheel-spin' });
      if (value === 'wheelWatch')
        void q.watch(() => this.platform.offerSupplyVideo(), 'wheel');
      if (value === 'giftWarehouse') {
        q.gift = undefined;
        q.show('warehouse');
      }
      if (value === 'buyPacks')
        void q.execute({
          kind: 'buy-packs',
          quantity: q.quantity,
          pool: 'general',
        });
      if (value === 'watch')
        void q.watch(() => this.platform.offerSupplyVideo());
      if (value === 'checkinClaim') void q.execute({ kind: 'checkin' });
      if (value === 'claimAll') void q.execute({ kind: 'claim-all' });
      if (value === 'claimMail' && q.selectedMail)
        void q.execute({ kind: 'claim-mail', mailId: q.selectedMail });
      if (value === 'minus') q.amount = Math.max(1, q.amount - 1);
      if (value === 'plus') q.amount = Math.min(100, q.amount + 1);
      if (value === 'open' && q.item)
        void q.execute({ kind: 'open', item: q.item, quantity: q.amount });
      if (value === 'sell' && q.item)
        q.confirmation = { kind: 'sell', item: q.item, quantity: q.amount };
      if (value === 'activate' && q.item)
        q.confirmation = { kind: 'activate', item: q.item };
      if (value === 'confirm' && q.confirmation) {
        const intent = q.confirmation;
        q.confirmation = undefined;
        void q.execute(intent);
      }
      if (value === 'cancel') q.confirmation = undefined;
      if (value === 'trial') {
        const p = q.catalog.find((p) => p.id === q.selected);
        if (p) {
          q.page = '';
          this.selectMission('training-60');
          this.sim.applyLoadout(trainingLoadout(p.effect));
          this.beginCampaign();
        }
      }
    } else if (group === 'mode') {
      if (value === 'enter') {
        q.externalReturn = 'modes';
        q.page = '';
        if (q.actionMode === 'solo') this.action('missions');
        else if (q.actionMode === 'duel') this.action('online:menu');
        else {
          this.selectMission('training-60');
          this.beginCampaign();
        }
      } else if (['solo', 'duel', 'training'].includes(value))
        q.actionMode = value as typeof q.actionMode;
    } else if (group === 'term') {
      q.term = value as typeof q.term;
      q.selected = 'burst-' + value;
      q.index = 0;
      q.detail = false;
    } else if (group === 'product') {
      q.selected = value;
      q.detail = true;
    } else if (group === 'loadout') {
      q.mode = value as typeof q.mode;
      q.index = 0;
      q.loadoutScroll = 0;
    } else if (group === 'slot') q.selectSlot(value as LoadoutSlot);
    else if (group === 'equip' || group === 'gear') q.equip(value);
    else if (group === 'package') q.quantity = Number(value);
    else if (group === 'pool') q.pool = value as typeof q.pool;
    else if (group === 'growth' || group === 'tasks') {
      q.section = value;
      q.index = 0;
    } else if (group === 'skill')
      q.selectedSkill = value as typeof q.selectedSkill;
    else if (group === 'task') {
      const task = q.assets?.tasks.find((t) => t.id === value);
      if (task) {
        if (task.progress >= task.target)
          void q.execute({ kind: 'claim-task', taskId: task.id });
        else if (task.event === 'checkin') q.show('checkin');
        else if (task.event === 'open') q.show('warehouse');
        else if (['video', 'exchange'].includes(task.event)) q.show('supply');
        else {
          q.show('modes');
          q.actionMode = task.event === 'duel' ? 'duel' : 'solo';
        }
      }
    } else if (group === 'taskalt')
      void q.execute({ kind: 'alternate-task', taskId: value });
    else if (group === 'achievement') {
      const task = q.assets?.achievements.find((t) => t.id === value);
      if (task && task.progress >= task.target)
        void q.execute({ kind: 'claim-achievement', achievementId: value });
      else q.show('modes');
    } else if (group === 'inbox') {
      q.section = value === 'news' ? 'news' : '';
      q.index = 0;
      q.selectedMail = '';
    } else if (group === 'mail') {
      q.selectedMail = value;
      const mail = q.assets?.mail.find((m) => m.id === value);
      if (mail && !mail.read)
        void q.execute({ kind: 'read-mail', mailId: value });
    } else if (group === 'inventory') {
      q.item = value;
      q.amount = 1;
    } else if (group === 'info') {
      q.info = value;
    }
    q.revision++;
    return true;
  }
  action(id: string) {
    if (id === 'resultClose') id = 'home';
    const online = this.online;
    this.platform.activate();
    if (this.progressionAction(id)) return;
    if (id === 'screen') {
      void this.screenControl?.toggle().catch(() => {
        this.hud.toast = this.hud.t(
          '浏览器暂不支持此操作',
          'SCREEN MODE UNAVAILABLE',
        );
        this.hud.toastUntil = Date.now() + 3000;
      });
      return;
    }
    if (online && id.startsWith('online:')) {
      if (this.quartermaster) this.quartermaster.page = '';
      const action = id.slice(7);
      if (action === 'dismiss') {
        this.action(
          online.room || online.active ? 'online:backLobby' : 'online:back',
        );
        return;
      }
      this.clear();
      this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
      if (action === 'chatBlocks') {
        online.chatMenu = online.chatMenu === 'blocks' ? '' : 'blocks';
        online.chatRevision++;
        return;
      }
      if (action === 'chatSend') {
        void online.sendChat();
        return;
      }
      if (action === 'roomReturn') {
        online.show('room');
        return;
      }
      if (action === 'chatInvite') {
        if (!online.room) {
          online.show('create');
          online.chatStatus = '先创建公开房间，即可邀请大厅玩家';
        } else {
          void online.sendChat(true);
          online.show('menu');
        }
        return;
      }
      if (action === 'chatLatest') {
        this.hud.listScroll.chat = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
        this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
        return;
      }
      if (action.startsWith('chatMenu:')) {
        online.chatMenu =
          online.chatMenu === action.slice(9) ? '' : action.slice(9);
        online.chatRevision++;
        return;
      }
      if (action.startsWith('chatBlock:')) {
        online.toggleBlock(action.slice(10));
        return;
      }
      if (action.startsWith('chatReport:')) {
        void online.reportChat(action.slice(11));
        return;
      }
      if (action.startsWith('chatJoin:')) {
        const m = online.chatMessages.find((m) => m.id === action.slice(9));
        if (m?.room?.canJoin)
          void online.open('join', m.room.code, m.room.createdAt);
        return;
      }
      if (action === 'menu') {
        online.show('menu');
        void online.loadRooms();
      }
      if (action === 'profile') {
        this.pause('settings', false);
        online.show('profile');
      }
      if (action === 'saveName') void online.saveName();
      if (action === 'friend') {
        online.draftPassword = '';
        online.show('create');
      }
      if (action === 'join') {
        online.draftPassword = '';
        online.show('join');
      }
      if (action === 'public' || action === 'private') {
        online.privateRoom = action === 'private';
        online.revision++;
      }
      if (action === 'createConfirm') void online.open('friend');
      if (action === 'joinConfirm') void online.open('join');
      if (action.startsWith('joinCode:'))
        void online.open('join', action.slice(9));
      if (action.startsWith('watchCode:'))
        void online.open('watch', action.slice(10));
      if (action === 'resumeRoom')
        void online.open('resume', online.resumeCode);
      if (action === 'roomsRefresh') void online.loadRooms();
      if (action === 'roomPrev' || action === 'roomNext') {
        online.roomPage = Math.max(
          0,
          Math.min(
            Math.ceil(online.rooms.length / online.pageSize) - 1,
            online.roomPage + (action === 'roomNext' ? 1 : -1),
          ),
        );
        online.revision++;
      }
      if (action === 'historyMore' && online.history?.nextCursor) {
        void online.loadHistory(online.history.nextCursor);
        return;
      }
      if (action === 'history') void online.loadHistory();
      if (action === 'historyPrev') {
        online.historyPage = Math.max(0, online.historyPage - 1);
        online.revision++;
      }
      if (action === 'historyNext' && online.history) {
        if (
          (online.historyPage + 1) * online.pageSize <
          online.history.matches.length
        )
          online.historyPage++;
        else if (online.history.nextCursor) {
          void online.loadHistory(online.history.nextCursor).then(() => {
            online.historyPage = Math.min(
              online.historyPage + 1,
              Math.max(
                0,
                Math.ceil(
                  (online.history?.matches.length || 0) / online.pageSize,
                ) - 1,
              ),
            );
            online.revision++;
          });
        }
        online.revision++;
      }
      if (action === 'ranking')
        void online.loadRanking(
          Math.max(
            0,
            MISSIONS.findIndex((m) => m.id === this.selectedMission),
          ),
        );
      if (
        action === 'category:solo' &&
        !['clears', 'level'].includes(online.board)
      )
        void online.loadRanking(online.level, 'clears');
      if (
        action === 'category:duel' &&
        !['points', 'win-rate'].includes(online.board)
      )
        void online.loadRanking(online.level, 'points');
      if (
        action.startsWith('board:') &&
        ['clears', 'level', 'points', 'win-rate'].includes(action.slice(6))
      )
        void online.loadRanking(
          online.level,
          action.slice(6) as typeof online.board,
        );
      if (action === 'previous') void online.loadRanking(online.level - 1);
      if (action === 'next') void online.loadRanking(online.level + 1);
      if (action === 'refresh') void online.loadRanking();
      if (action === 'ruleVersion') void online.cycleRankingVersion();
      if (action === 'match' || action === 'bot') void online.open(action);
      if (action === 'ready') void online.ready();
      if (action === 'share' && sys.isBrowser) void online.share(location.href);
      if (action === 'back' && online.panel !== 'room') {
        online.back();
        if (
          !online.panel &&
          !online.room &&
          !online.active &&
          this.quartermaster?.externalReturn === 'modes'
        ) {
          this.quartermaster.show('modes', false);
          this.quartermaster.externalReturn = '';
        }
        return;
      }
      if (action === 'back' || action === 'backLobby') {
        this.platform.stopRadio();
        online.leave();
        this.sim.phase = 'briefing';
        this.selectMission(this.selectedMission);
        this.hud.missionsOpen = false;
        if (sys.isBrowser) {
          const url = new URL(location.href);
          url.searchParams.delete('room');
          history.replaceState(null, '', url.href);
        }
        if (action === 'backLobby') {
          online.show('menu');
          void online.loadRooms();
        }
      }
      return;
    }
    if (
      online?.panel &&
      !this.sim.pauses.has('settings') &&
      !this.sim.pauses.has('help') &&
      ['close', 'home', 'resume'].includes(id)
    ) {
      this.action('online:back');
      return;
    }
    if (
      this.sim.duelMode &&
      [
        'supply',
        'homingReward',
        'homing',
        'zoomUpgrade',
        'convoy',
        'mission',
        'retry',
        'missionNext',
      ].includes(id)
    )
      return;
    if (
      this.sim.duelMode &&
      (id === 'exitConfirm' || (id === 'home' && this.sim.phase !== 'playing'))
    ) {
      this.hud.exitConfirm = false;
      this.action(id === 'home' ? 'online:back' : 'online:backLobby');
      return;
    }
    if (
      ['equipment', 'reportOverview'].includes(id) &&
      this.sim.phase === 'playing'
    ) {
      this.clear();
      this.hud.reportOpen = true;
      this.hud.equipmentOpen = id === 'equipment';
      this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'battleReport' && this.sim.phase === 'playing') {
      this.clear();
      this.hud.reportOpen = !this.hud.reportOpen;
      this.hud.equipmentOpen = false;
      this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
      return;
    }
    if (
      (this.hud.reportOpen || this.hud.equipmentOpen) &&
      ['close', 'equipmentClose', 'resume', 'pause'].includes(id)
    ) {
      this.hud.reportOpen = this.hud.equipmentOpen = false;
      this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'lensIn') id = 'zoomIn';
    if (id === 'lensOut') id = 'zoomOut';
    this.platform.activate();
    if (this.rewardRequest) {
      if (
        this.hud.advert?.mock &&
        ['adClose', 'adCancel', 'close'].includes(id)
      )
        this.finishReward(id === 'adClose');
      else if (['adCancel', 'close'].includes(id)) this.platform.cancelReward();
      return;
    }
    if (this.hud.exitConfirm) {
      if (id === 'exitConfirm') {
        this.hud.exitConfirm = false;
        this.clear();
        this.sim.phase = 'briefing';
        this.selectMission(this.selectedMission);
        this.hud.missionsOpen = false;
      } else if (
        ['exitCancel', 'close', 'resume', 'pause', 'settings'].includes(id)
      ) {
        this.hud.exitConfirm = false;
        this.action('resume');
      }
      return;
    }
    if (this.sim.resumeCountdown > 0) {
      if (id === 'pause') this.pause('manual', true);
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'supply' || id === 'homingReward') {
      this.openSupply();
      return;
    }
    if (this.hud.supplyOpen) {
      if (id === 'supplyWatch') {
        if (this.platform.rewards.pendingSupply)
          this.claimSupply(this.hud.supplySelection);
        else void this.requestReward('supply', this.hud.supplySelection);
      } else if (
        id.startsWith('reward:') &&
        ['tracking', 'ammo', 'rate', 'restock'].includes(id.slice(7))
      ) {
        this.hud.supplySelection = id.slice(7) as SupplyReward;
        this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
      } else if (['supplyLater', 'close', 'resume', 'pause'].includes(id))
        this.closeSupply();
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'zoomUpgrade') {
      void this.requestReward('zoom');
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'zoomControls') {
      this.pause('settings', true);
      this.hud.zoomOpen = !this.hud.zoomOpen;
      this.hud.toolsOpen = false;
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'home') {
      if (this.quartermaster) this.quartermaster.page = '';
      if (this.sim.phase === 'playing') {
        this.hud.exitConfirm = true;
        this.hud.toolsOpen = this.hud.zoomOpen = false;
        this.pause('settings', true);
        return;
      }
      this.clear();
      this.sim.phase = 'briefing';
      this.selectMission(this.selectedMission);
      this.hud.missionsOpen = false;
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'missions' || id === 'homeMenu') {
      if (this.quartermaster) {
        this.quartermaster.page = '';
        if (
