          id === 'homeMenu' &&
          this.quartermaster.externalReturn === 'modes'
        ) {
          this.quartermaster.show('modes', false);
          this.quartermaster.externalReturn = '';
        }
      }
      this.online?.leave();
      this.clear();
      this.hud.missionsOpen = id === 'missions';
      if (id === 'missions') {
        this.hud.missionPage = Math.floor(
          Math.max(
            0,
            MISSIONS.findIndex(
              (mission) => mission.id === this.selectedMission,
            ),
          ) / 3,
        );
        this.hud.missionFocus = Math.max(
          0,
          MISSIONS.findIndex((mission) => mission.id === this.selectedMission),
        );
      }
      this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'missionsPrevious' || id === 'missionsNext') {
      if (!this.hud.missionsOpen || this.sim.phase !== 'briefing') return;
      this.clear();
      this.hud.missionPage = Math.max(
        0,
        Math.min(
          Math.ceil(MISSIONS.length / 3) - 1,
          this.hud.missionPage + (id === 'missionsNext' ? 1 : -1),
        ),
      );
      this.hud.missionFocus = this.hud.missionPage * 3;
      this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
      return;
    }
    if (id.startsWith('mission:')) {
      const mission = MISSIONS.find((m) => m.id === id.slice(8));
      if (mission) this.selectMission(mission.id, true);
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'training' || id === 'missionReturn') {
      this.selectMission(
        id === 'training' ? 'training-60' : this.selectedMission,
      );
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'missionNext') {
      const startNext = this.sim.phase === 'success';
      const id = nextMission(
        this.sim.mission.mode === 'training'
          ? this.selectedMission
          : this.sim.mission.id,
      );
      this.selectMission(id, true);
      if (
        startNext &&
        this.sim.mission.id === id &&
        this.sim.phase === 'briefing'
      )
        this.retry();
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'tools' || id === 'flightControls') {
      this.pause('settings', true);
      this.hud.zoomOpen = false;
      this.hud.toolsOpen = !this.hud.toolsOpen;
      return;
    }
    const flightActions = [
      'rotateLeft',
      'rotateRight',
      'orbitLeft',
      'orbitRight',
      'altitudeUp',
      'altitudeDown',
      'radiusIn',
      'radiusOut',
    ];
    if (id === 'close' || id === 'resume') {
      if (this.sim.pauses.has('help')) this.pause('help', false);
      else if (this.sim.pauses.has('mission')) this.pause('mission', false);
      else if (id === 'close' && (this.hud.toolsOpen || this.hud.zoomOpen)) {
        this.hud.toolsOpen = this.hud.zoomOpen = false;
        this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
      } else {
        this.hud.toolsOpen = this.hud.zoomOpen = false;
        this.pause('settings', false);
        if (this.sim.resumeRequired) this.resumeBattle();
        else this.pause('manual', false);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'settings') {
      if (this.sim.pauses.has('settings') || this.sim.pauses.has('manual')) {
        this.action('resume');
        return;
      }
      this.hud.toolsOpen = this.hud.zoomOpen = false;
      const wasHelp = this.sim.pauses.has('help');
      if (wasHelp) this.pause('help', false);
      this.pause('settings', wasHelp || !this.sim.pauses.has('settings'));
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'help') {
      this.hud.helpTouch = this.hud.touch;
      this.pause('help', !this.sim.pauses.has('help'));
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'pause') {
      this.action('settings');
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'mission') {
      this.pause('mission', !this.sim.pauses.has('mission'));
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'language') {
      this.hud.lang = this.hud.lang === 'zh' ? 'en' : 'zh';
      this.hud.resize();
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'input') {
      this.hud.helpTouch = !this.hud.helpTouch;
      return;
    }
    if (id.startsWith('tab:')) {
      this.hud.helpGroup = id.slice(4) as HUD['helpGroup'];
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'tutorial') {
      this.hud.tutorial = true;
      this.sim.completed.clear();
      this.pause('help', false);
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'sound') {
      this.platform.muted = !this.platform.muted;
      this.hud.muted = this.platform.muted;
      this.platform.savePreferences();
      this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
      if (this.platform.muted) this.platform.stop();
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'voicePrompts' || id === 'autoPriority') {
      this.platform[id] = !this.platform[id];
      this.hud[id] = this.platform[id];
      if (
        id === 'autoPriority' &&
        !this.platform.autoPriority &&
        ['priority', 'night'].some(
          (cue) =>
            this.platform.radio.clip ===
            this.platform.clips.get(`radio-${cue}-${this.hud.lang}`),
        )
      ) {
        this.platform.stopRadio();
        this.hud.radioText = '';
      }
      this.platform.savePreferences();
      if (id === 'voicePrompts' && !this.platform.voicePrompts) {
        this.platform.stopRadio();
        if (this.sim.extractionRemaining !== undefined)
          this.sim.setExtractionRadioPending(false);
      }
      this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
      return;
    }
    if (
      id === 'sensor' ||
      ['view:night', 'view:enhanced', 'view:thermal'].includes(id)
    ) {
      if (
        this.sim.paused &&
        Array.from(this.sim.pauses).some(
          (reason) => !['settings', 'manual'].includes(reason),
        )
      )
        return;
      if (id === 'sensor') this.world.sensor();
      else this.world.setView(id.slice(5) as Platform['viewMode']);
      this.platform.viewMode = this.world.viewMode;
      this.platform.savePreferences();
      this.sim.completed.add('sensor');
      if (this.hud.modal) this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'effects') {
      this.platform.reducedEffects = !this.platform.reducedEffects;
      this.hud.reducedEffects = this.platform.reducedEffects;
      this.platform.savePreferences();
      this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'retry') {
      this.retry();
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'start') {
      this.world.reset();
      this.beginCampaign();
      return;
    }
    const menuAction = [
      ...flightActions,
      'sensor',
      'convoy',
      'locate',
      'zoomIn',
      'zoomOut',
    ].includes(id);
    const menuPaused =
      this.sim.paused &&
      Array.from(this.sim.pauses).every((reason) =>
        ['settings', 'manual'].includes(reason),
      );
    if (
      this.online?.entering ||
      this.online?.spectator ||
      this.sim.phase !== 'playing' ||
      (this.sim.paused && !(menuPaused && menuAction))
    )
      return;
    if (menuPaused) {
      this.pause('settings', true);
      this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
    }
    if (id === 'priority') {
      const threat = this.sim.priorityThreat;
      if (threat && this.platform.autoPriority) this.locateMap(threat.unit);
      return;
    }
    if (flightActions.includes(id)) {
      this.sim.clearInput();
      if (id === 'rotateLeft' || id === 'rotateRight')
        this.world.rotate(id === 'rotateLeft' ? -12 : 12);
      if (id === 'orbitLeft' || id === 'orbitRight')
        this.sim.setOrbitDirection(id === 'orbitLeft' ? -1 : 1);
      if (id === 'altitudeUp' || id === 'altitudeDown')
        this.sim.adjustAltitude(id === 'altitudeUp' ? 20 : -20);
      if (id === 'radiusIn' || id === 'radiusOut')
        this.sim.adjustRadius(id === 'radiusOut' ? 20 : -20);
      return;
    }
    if (id.startsWith('weapon')) this.sim.choose(Number(id.slice(-1)));
    if (id === 'homing') {
      if (this.sim.homingAmmo <= 0) {
        this.openSupply();
        return;
      }
      this.clear();
      this.sim.selectHoming();
    }
    if (id === 'previous')
      this.sim.choose(
        (this.sim.selected + WEAPONS.length - 1) % WEAPONS.length,
      );
    if (id === 'next')
      this.sim.choose((this.sim.selected + 1) % WEAPONS.length);
    if (id === 'convoy') this.sim.command();
    if (id === 'locate') {
      this.world.locate(this.sim.rescue);
      this.sim.setAim({ x: this.sim.rescue.x + 8, z: this.sim.rescue.z - 6 });
      this.sim.completed.add('locate');
    }
    if (id === 'zoomIn' || id === 'zoomOut') {
      this.world.adjustZoom(id === 'zoomIn' ? 1.5 : 1 / 1.5);
      this.sim.completed.add('zoom');
    }
  }
  mousePosition(e: EventMouse) {
    const p = e.getUILocation();
    return { x: p.x, y: this.hud.h - p.y };
  }
  lockAt(x: number, y: number) {
    const target = this.sim.units
      .filter((u) => u.hp > 0)
      .map((u) => {
        const p = this.world.project(u);
        return {
          id: u.id,
          distance: Math.hypot(
            p.x / view.getScaleX() - x,
            this.hud.h - p.y / view.getScaleY() - y,
          ),
        };
      })
      .filter((u) => u.distance <= 28)
      .sort((a, b) => a.distance - b.distance)[0];
    if (target) this.sim.lockHoming(target.id);
    else {
      const aim = this.world.aimAt(
        x * view.getScaleX(),
        (this.hud.h - y) * view.getScaleY(),
      );
      if (aim) this.sim.setAim(aim);
    }
  }
  locateMap(p: Point) {
    if (this.sim.paused || this.sim.phase !== 'playing') return;
    this.clear();
    this.world.center = { ...p };
    this.world.follow = false;
    this.world.updateCamera();
    this.sim.setAim(p);
  }
  private beginListGesture(pointer: number | 'mouse', x: number, y: number) {
    const area = this.hud.listAt(x, y),
      button = this.hud.hit(x, y);
    if (!area || this.listGesture) return false;
    if (button?.id.startsWith('info:'))
      this.infoHold = { id: button.id, at: Date.now() };
    this.listGesture = {
      pointer,
      key: area.key,
      x,
      y,
      lastX: x,
      lastY: y,
      axis: area.axis || 'y',
      button: button?.id,
      moved: false,
      started: Date.now(),
    };
    return true;
  }
  private beginControlGesture(pointer: number | 'mouse', x: number, y: number) {
    if (
      this.controlGesture ||
      this.hud.modal ||
      this.sim.paused ||
      this.sim.phase !== 'playing' ||
      this.online?.spectator ||
      this.online?.entering
    )
      return false;
    const button = this.hud.controlAt(x, y);
    if (!button) return false;
    const bounds = this.hud.controlBounds(button.id),
      saved = this.hud.controlPositions[button.id];
    this.controlGesture = {
      pointer,
      button: button.id,
      x,
      y,
      position: saved && { ...saved },
      origin: {
        x: (button.x - bounds.x) / Math.max(1, bounds.w),
        y: (button.y - bounds.y) / Math.max(1, bounds.h),
      },
      moved: false,
    };
    return true;
  }
  private moveControlGesture(pointer: number | 'mouse', x: number, y: number) {
    const gesture = this.controlGesture;
    if (!gesture || gesture.pointer !== pointer) return false;
    if (Math.hypot(x - gesture.x, y - gesture.y) > 8) gesture.moved = true;
    if (gesture.moved) {
      const bounds = this.hud.controlBounds(gesture.button);
      this.hud.placeControl(gesture.button, {
        x: gesture.origin.x + (x - gesture.x) / Math.max(1, bounds.w),
        y: gesture.origin.y + (y - gesture.y) / Math.max(1, bounds.h),
      });
    }
    return true;
  }
  private endControlGesture(pointer: number | 'mouse', x: number, y: number) {
    const gesture = this.controlGesture;
    if (!gesture || gesture.pointer !== pointer) return false;
    this.moveControlGesture(pointer, x, y);
    this.controlGesture = undefined;
    if (!gesture.moved && this.hud.hit(x, y)?.id === gesture.button)
      this.action(gesture.button);
    return true;
  }
  private moveListGesture(pointer: number | 'mouse', x: number, y: number) {
    const a = this.listGesture;
    if (!a || a.pointer !== pointer) return false;
    if (Math.hypot(x - a.x, y - a.y) > 8) {
      a.moved = true;
      this.infoHold = undefined;
    }
    if (a.moved)
      this.hud.moveList(a.key, a.axis === 'x' ? a.lastX - x : a.lastY - y);
    a.lastX = x;
    a.lastY = y;
    return true;
  }
  private endListGesture(pointer: number | 'mouse', x: number, y: number) {
    const a = this.listGesture;
    if (!a || a.pointer !== pointer) return false;
    this.listGesture = undefined;
    this.infoHold = undefined;
    if (
      !a.moved &&
      Math.hypot(x - a.x, y - a.y) <= 8 &&
      a.button &&
      this.hud.hit(x, y)?.id === a.button
    )
      this.action(a.button);
    return true;
  }
  private beginLoadoutGesture(pointer: number | 'mouse', x: number, y: number) {
    const q = this.quartermaster;
    if (
      !q ||
      q.page !== 'loadout' ||
      q.gift ||
      q.info ||
      q.leaveTo !== undefined ||
      q.confirmation ||
      q.busy ||
      q.pending ||
      this.loadoutGesture
    )
      return false;
    const button = this.hud.hit(x, y);
    if (button?.id.startsWith('info:')) return false;
    const source = button?.id.startsWith('slot:')
        ? (button.id.slice(5) as LoadoutSlot)
        : undefined,
      a = q.loadoutArea;
    if (!source && (x < a.x || x > a.x + a.w || y < a.y || y > a.y + a.h))
      return false;
    const item = source
      ? (source.startsWith('mod:')
          ? q.modSlots[Number(source.slice(-1))]
          : q.loadout[source as 'weapon' | 'skill']) || undefined
      : button && /^(gear|equip):/.test(button.id)
        ? button.id.slice(button.id.indexOf(':') + 1)
        : undefined;
    this.loadoutGesture = {
      pointer,
      item,
      source,
      x,
      y,
      lastY: y,
      mode: 'pending',
    };
    return true;
  }
  private moveLoadoutGesture(pointer: number | 'mouse', x: number, y: number) {
    const gesture = this.loadoutGesture,
      q = this.quartermaster;
    if (!gesture || gesture.pointer !== pointer || !q) return false;
    const dx = x - gesture.x,
      dy = y - gesture.y;
    if (gesture.mode === 'pending' && Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) > 8)
      gesture.mode =
        gesture.item && (gesture.source || Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy))
          ? 'drag'
          : 'scroll';
    if (gesture.mode === 'scroll' && !gesture.source)
      q.scrollLoadout(gesture.lastY - y);
    if (gesture.mode === 'drag' && gesture.item) {
      const hit = this.hud.hit(x, y),
        slot = hit?.id.startsWith('slot:')
          ? (hit.id.slice(5) as LoadoutSlot)
          : undefined,
        category = q.catalog.find((p) => p.id === gesture.item)?.category;
      const target =
        slot && (slot.startsWith('mod:') ? 'mod' : slot) === category
          ? slot
          : undefined;
      q.drag = { item: gesture.item, x, y, target };
      q.revision++;
    }
    gesture.lastY = y;
    return true;
  }
  private endLoadoutGesture(pointer: number | 'mouse', x: number, y: number) {
    const gesture = this.loadoutGesture,
      q = this.quartermaster;
    if (!gesture || gesture.pointer !== pointer || !q) return false;
    this.loadoutGesture = undefined;
    q.drag = undefined;
    const target = this.hud.hit(x, y);
    if (
      gesture.item &&
      gesture.mode === 'drag' &&
      target?.id.startsWith('slot:')
    ) {
      const slot = target.id.slice(5) as LoadoutSlot;
      if (gesture.source) q.moveMod(gesture.source, slot);
      else q.equip(gesture.item, slot);
    } else if (
      gesture.source &&
      gesture.mode === 'pending' &&
      target?.id === 'slot:' + gesture.source
    )
      q.selectSlot(gesture.source);
    else if (
      gesture.item &&
      gesture.mode === 'pending' &&
      target?.id.endsWith(':' + gesture.item)
    )
      q.equip(gesture.item);
    q.revision++;
    return true;
  }
  mouseMove(e: EventMouse) {
    if (this.platform.touchInput) return;
    this.hud.touch = false;
    const p = this.mousePosition(e);
    this.hud.mousePointer = p;
    if (
      this.moveControlGesture('mouse', p.x, p.y) ||
      this.moveLoadoutGesture('mouse', p.x, p.y) ||
      this.moveListGesture('mouse', p.x, p.y)
    )
      return;
    if (this.mouseButton && this.hud.hit(p.x, p.y)?.id !== this.mouseButton) {
      this.sim.setFire('mouse', false);
      this.mouseButton = '';
      this.infoHold = undefined;
    }
    if (this.hud.blocksBattlefield(p.x, p.y) && this.mouseButton !== 'fire')
      this.sim.setFire('mouse', false);
    if (
      !this.sim.homingSelected &&
      !this.hud.modal &&
      !this.hud.blocksBattlefield(p.x, p.y)
    ) {
      const q = e.getLocation();
      const aim = this.world.aimAt(q.x, q.y);
      if (aim) this.sim.setAim(aim);
      else this.sim.setFire('mouse', false);
      this.lastAimInput = Date.now();
    }
  }
  mouseDown(e: EventMouse) {
    if (this.platform.touchInput) return;
    this.hud.touch = false;
    this.platform.activate();
    const p = this.mousePosition(e),
      b = this.hud.hit(p.x, p.y);
    this.hud.mousePointer = p;
    if (
      e.getButton() === 0 &&
      (this.beginControlGesture('mouse', p.x, p.y) ||
        this.beginLoadoutGesture('mouse', p.x, p.y) ||
        this.beginListGesture('mouse', p.x, p.y))
    )
      return;
    if (b) {
      if (e.getButton() !== 0) return;
      if (
        this.hud.modal &&
        !this.hud.inModal(b.label.node) &&
        !this.hud.isGlobalAction(b.id)
      )
        return;
      this.mouseButton = b.id;
      if (b.id.startsWith('info:'))
        this.infoHold = { id: b.id, at: Date.now() };
      if (
        b.id === 'fire' &&
        e.getButton() === 0 &&
        !this.online?.spectator &&
        !this.online?.entering
      )
        this.sim.setFire('mouse', true);
      return;
    }
    const mapPoint = this.hud.minimapPoint(p.x, p.y);
    if (mapPoint && e.getButton() === 0) {
      this.locateMap(mapPoint);
      return;
    }
    if (
      this.hud.modal ||
      this.online?.spectator ||
      this.online?.entering ||
      this.hud.blocksBattlefield(p.x, p.y)
    )
      return;
    if (e.getButton() === 2) {
      this.world.temporary = true;
      this.world.updateCamera();
      this.sim.completed.add('zoom');
      return;
    }
    if (e.getButton() === 0) {
      if (this.sim.homingSelected) {
        this.lockAt(p.x, p.y);
        return;
      }
      const q = e.getLocation();
      const aim = this.world.aimAt(q.x, q.y);
      if (!aim) return;
      this.sim.setAim(aim);
      this.sim.setFire('mouse', true);
    }
  }
  mouseUp(e: EventMouse) {
    if (this.platform.touchInput) return;
    if (e.getButton() === 2) {
      this.world.temporary = false;
      this.world.updateCamera();
    }
    if (e.getButton() !== 0) return;
    this.sim.setFire('mouse', false);
    const p = this.mousePosition(e),
      b = this.hud.hit(p.x, p.y);
    if (
      this.endControlGesture('mouse', p.x, p.y) ||
      this.endLoadoutGesture('mouse', p.x, p.y) ||
      this.endListGesture('mouse', p.x, p.y)
    )
      return;
    this.infoHold = undefined;
    const id = this.mouseButton;
    this.mouseButton = '';
    if (id && id !== 'fire' && b?.id === id) this.action(id);
  }
  wheel(e: EventMouse) {
    if (this.hud.modal) {
      const point = this.mousePosition(e),
        area = this.hud.listAt(point.x, point.y);
      if (area) {
        this.hud.moveList(
          area.key,
          (area.axis === 'x' && e.getScrollX?.()
            ? e.getScrollX()
            : -e.getScrollY()) * 0.2,
        );
        return;
      }
      const q = this.quartermaster;
      if (
        q?.page === 'loadout' &&
        !q.gift &&
        !q.confirmation &&
        !q.info &&
        q.leaveTo === undefined
      ) {
        const p = this.mousePosition(e),
          a = q.loadoutArea;
        if (p.x >= a.x && p.x <= a.x + a.w && p.y >= a.y && p.y <= a.y + a.h) {
          q.scrollLoadout(-e.getScrollY() * 0.2);
          return;
        }
      }
      if (this.hud.scrollNode) this.hud.scrollBy(-e.getScrollY() * 0.2);
      return;
    }
    const p = this.mousePosition(e);
    if (
      this.sim.phase !== 'playing' ||
      this.sim.paused ||
      this.hud.blocksBattlefield(p.x, p.y)
    )
      return;
    const q = e.getLocation();
    this.world.adjustZoom(
      Math.exp(Math.max(-0.35, Math.min(0.35, e.getScrollY() * 0.0015))),
      q.x,
      q.y,
    );
    this.sim.completed.add('zoom');
  }
  touchStart(e: EventTouch) {
    // Creator 3.8.8 leaves simulate=false on mouse-generated touches; browser pointer type is authoritative.
    if (e.simulate || (sys.isBrowser && !this.platform.touchInput)) return;
    this.platform.useTouchInput(true);
    this.hud.mousePointer = undefined;
    this.hud.touch = true;
    this.platform.activate();
    for (const t of e.getTouches()) {
      const p = t.getUILocation(),
        x = p.x,
        y = this.hud.h - p.y,
        b = this.hud.hit(x, y),
        id = t.getID();
      if (this.touches.has(id)) continue;
      if (this.controlGesture && this.controlGesture.pointer !== id) {
        this.touches.set(id, { role: 'cancelled', x, y });
        continue;
      }
      if (this.beginControlGesture(id, x, y)) {
        this.touches.set(id, { role: 'control', x, y });
        continue;
      }
      if (this.loadoutGesture && this.loadoutGesture.pointer !== id) {
        this.touches.set(id, { role: 'cancelled', x, y });
        continue;
      }
      if (this.beginLoadoutGesture(id, x, y)) {
        this.touches.set(id, { role: 'loadout', x, y });
        continue;
      }
      if (this.listGesture && this.listGesture.pointer !== id) {
        this.touches.set(id, { role: 'cancelled', x, y });
        continue;
      }
      if (this.beginListGesture(id, x, y)) {
        this.touches.set(id, { role: 'list', x, y });
        continue;
      }
      const mapPoint = this.hud.minimapPoint(x, y);
      if (!b && mapPoint) {
        this.locateMap(mapPoint);
        this.touches.set(id, { role: 'map', x, y });
        continue;
      }
      if (
        b &&
        (!this.hud.modal ||
          this.hud.inModal(b.label.node) ||
          this.hud.isGlobalAction(b.id))
      ) {
        if (
          b.id === 'fire' &&
          Array.from(this.touches.values()).some((t) => t.role === 'pinch')
        ) {
          this.touches.set(id, { role: 'cancelled', x, y });
          continue;
        }
        if (b.id.startsWith('info:'))
          this.infoHold = { id: b.id, at: Date.now() };
        this.touches.set(id, {
          role: b.id === 'fire' ? 'fire' : 'button',
          x,
          y,
          button: b.id,
        });
        if (
          b.id === 'fire' &&
          !this.online?.spectator &&
          !this.online?.entering
        )
          this.sim.setFire('touch:' + id, true);
      } else if (this.hud.modal) {
        const area = this.hud.scrollArea;
        if (
          this.hud.scrollNode &&
          x >= area.x &&
          x <= area.x + area.w &&
          y >= area.y &&
          y <= area.y + area.h
        )
          this.touches.set(id, { role: 'scroll', x, y });
      } else if (
        !this.hud.blocksBattlefield(x, y) &&
        this.sim.phase === 'playing' &&
        !this.sim.paused
      )
        this.touches.set(id, { role: 'aim', x, y, originX: x, originY: y });
    }
    const fingers = Array.from(this.touches.values()).filter(
      (t) => t.role === 'aim' || t.role === 'pinch',
    );
    if (fingers.length >= 2) {
      this.sim.clearInput();
      fingers.forEach((t, i) => {
        t.role = i < 2 ? 'pinch' : 'cancelled';
      });
      for (const t of this.touches.values())
        if (t.role === 'fire') t.role = 'cancelled';
    }
  }
  touchMove(e: EventTouch) {
    if (e.simulate || (sys.isBrowser && !this.platform.touchInput)) return;
    for (const t of e.getTouches()) {
      const role = this.touches.get(t.getID()),
        p = t.getUILocation();
      if (role?.role === 'control') {
        this.moveControlGesture(t.getID(), p.x, this.hud.h - p.y);
        continue;
      }
      if (role?.role === 'list') {
        this.moveListGesture(t.getID(), p.x, this.hud.h - p.y);
        continue;
      }
      if (role?.role === 'loadout') {
        this.moveLoadoutGesture(t.getID(), p.x, this.hud.h - p.y);
        continue;
      }
      if (
        role?.role === 'button' &&
        this.hud.hit(p.x, this.hud.h - p.y)?.id !== role.button
      ) {
        role.role = 'cancelled';
        this.infoHold = undefined;
      }
    }
    const fingers = Array.from(this.touches.values()).filter(
      (t) => t.role === 'pinch',
    );
    if (fingers.length === 2) {
      const before = Math.hypot(
        fingers[0].x - fingers[1].x,
        fingers[0].y - fingers[1].y,
      );
      const previousX = ((fingers[0].x + fingers[1].x) / 2) * view.getScaleX();
      const previousY =
        (this.hud.h - (fingers[0].y + fingers[1].y) / 2) * view.getScaleY();
      for (const t of e.getTouches()) {
        const role = this.touches.get(t.getID());
        if (role?.role !== 'pinch') continue;
        const p = t.getUILocation();
        role.x = p.x;
        role.y = this.hud.h - p.y;
      }
      const after = Math.hypot(
        fingers[0].x - fingers[1].x,
        fingers[0].y - fingers[1].y,
      );
      if (before > 8 && after > 8 && !this.sim.paused) {
        this.world.adjustZoom(
          after / before,
          ((fingers[0].x + fingers[1].x) / 2) * view.getScaleX(),
          (this.hud.h - (fingers[0].y + fingers[1].y) / 2) * view.getScaleY(),
          previousX,
          previousY,
        );
        this.sim.completed.add('zoom');
      }
      return;
    }
    for (const t of e.getTouches()) {
      const role = this.touches.get(t.getID());
      if (!role) continue;
      const p = t.getUILocation(),
        x = p.x,
        y = this.hud.h - p.y;
      if (role.role === 'scroll') this.hud.scrollBy(role.y - y);
      if (role.role === 'fire' && this.hud.hit(x, y)?.id !== 'fire') {
        this.sim.setFire('touch:' + t.getID(), false);
        role.role = 'cancelled';
      }
      if (role.role === 'aim' && !this.sim.paused) {
        if (
          Math.hypot(
            x - (role.originX ?? role.x),
            y - (role.originY ?? role.y),
          ) > 2
        )
          role.moved = true;
        // Move from the reticle's depth, preserving the finger offset and excluding visual recoil.
        const reticle = this.world.project(this.sim.aim, 0, true);
        const aim = this.world.aimAt(
          reticle.x + (x - role.x) * view.getScaleX(),
          reticle.y - (y - role.y) * view.getScaleY(),
        );
        if (aim) this.sim.setAim(aim);
      }
      role.x = x;
      role.y = y;
    }
  }
  touchEnd(e: EventTouch) {
    if (e.simulate || (sys.isBrowser && !this.platform.touchInput)) return;
    for (const t of e.getTouches()) {
      const id = t.getID(),
        role = this.touches.get(id);
      this.touches.delete(id);
      this.infoHold = undefined;
      this.sim.setFire('touch:' + id, false);
      if (role?.role === 'control') {
        const p = t.getUILocation();
        this.endControlGesture(id, p.x, this.hud.h - p.y);
        continue;
      }
      if (role?.role === 'list') {
        const p = t.getUILocation();
        this.endListGesture(id, p.x, this.hud.h - p.y);
        continue;
      }
      if (role?.role === 'loadout') {
        const p = t.getUILocation();
        this.endLoadoutGesture(id, p.x, this.hud.h - p.y);
        continue;
      }
      if (role?.role === 'pinch')
        for (const finger of this.touches.values())
          if (finger.role === 'pinch') finger.role = 'cancelled';
      if (role?.role === 'button') {
        const p = t.getUILocation(),
          b = this.hud.hit(p.x, this.hud.h - p.y);
        if (b && b.id === role.button) this.action(b.id);
      }
      if (role?.role === 'aim' && !this.sim.paused) {
        const p = t.getUILocation(),
          x = p.x,
          y = this.hud.h - p.y;
        const tap =
          !role.moved &&
          Math.hypot(
            x - (role.originX ?? role.x),
            y - (role.originY ?? role.y),
          ) <= 2;
        if (tap && !this.hud.blocksBattlefield(x, y)) {
          if (this.sim.homingSelected) this.lockAt(x, y);
          else {
            const aim = this.world.aimAt(
              x * view.getScaleX(),
              (this.hud.h - y) * view.getScaleY(),
            );
            if (aim) this.sim.setAim(aim);
          }
        } else if (this.sim.homingSelected && this.sim.homingTarget)
          this.sim.lockHoming(this.sim.homingTarget.id);
      }
    }
  }
  touchCancel(e: EventTouch) {
    if (e.simulate || (sys.isBrowser && !this.platform.touchInput)) return;
    this.clear();
  }
  keyDown(e: EventKeyboard) {
    if (!this.platform.focused || this.keys.has(e.keyCode)) return;
    this.keys.add(e.keyCode);
    this.hud.touch = false;
    this.platform.activate();
    if (
      this.quartermaster?.page &&
      (['help', 'settings', 'mission'] as const).some((reason) =>
        this.sim.pauses.has(reason),
      )
    ) {
      if (e.keyCode === 27) this.action('close');
      this.keys.add(e.keyCode);
      return;
    }
    if ((this.hud.reportOpen || this.hud.equipmentOpen) && e.keyCode === 27) {
      this.action('equipmentClose');
      return;
    }
    if (this.quartermaster?.page) {
      if (e.keyCode === 27) this.action('qm:back');
      else if (e.keyCode === 13 && this.quartermaster.page === 'modes')
        this.action('mode:enter');
      this.keys.add(e.keyCode);
      return;
    }
    if (this.online?.panel || this.online?.entering) {
      if (e.keyCode === 27) this.action('online:back');
      return;
    }
    if (
      e.keyCode === 27 &&
      ['help', 'settings', 'mission', 'advert', 'supply'].some((reason) =>
        this.sim.pauses.has(reason as PauseReason),
      )
    ) {
      this.action('close');
      return;
    }
    if (e.keyCode === 13 && this.sim.phase !== 'playing') {
      this.action(
        this.sim.phase === 'briefing'
          ? this.hud.missionsOpen
            ? 'start'
            : 'qm:modes'
          : 'retry',
      );
      return;
    }
    const a = ACTIONS.find((a) =>
      (a.keys as readonly number[]).includes(e.keyCode),
    );
    if (!a) return;
    if (a.id === 'fire') {
      if (!this.hud.modal && !this.sim.paused && !this.online?.spectator)
        this.sim.setFire('space', true);
    } else this.action(a.id);
  }
  keyUp(e: EventKeyboard) {
    this.keys.delete(e.keyCode);
    if (e.keyCode === 32) this.sim.setFire('space', false);
  }
  updateRadio(dt = 0) {
    if (this.sim.phase === 'success') {
      if (!this.completionSpoken) {
        this.completionSpoken = true;
        this.platform.speak('complete', this.hud.lang);
      }
      this.platform.advanceRadio(dt);
      return;
    }
    if (this.sim.duelMode || this.sim.phase !== 'playing') {
      this.platform.stopRadio();
      this.hud.radioText = '';
      return;
    }
    if (this.sim.paused) {
      this.platform.stop(
        this.sim.extractionRemaining !== undefined &&
          this.sim.extractionRadioPending,
      );
      return;
    }
    let cue: keyof typeof RADIO | undefined;
    const threat = this.sim.priorityThreat;
    const newMission = this.radioMission !== this.sim;
    if (newMission) {
      this.radioMission = this.sim;
      this.radioTarget = undefined;
      this.radioAt = -Infinity;
    }
    if (this.sim.extractionRemaining !== undefined && this.radioTarget !== -1) {
      cue = 'clear';
      this.radioTarget = -1;
    } else if (newMission) {
      cue = this.platform.autoPriority ? 'night' : 'day';
    } else if (this.sim.time - this.radioAt >= 10) {
      if (
        threat &&
        (threat.unit.id !== this.radioTarget ||
          this.sim.time - this.radioAt >= 24)
      ) {
        cue = threat.critical
          ? 'critical'
          : this.platform.autoPriority
            ? 'priority'
            : undefined;
        if (!cue) {
          this.hud.radioText = this.hud.t(
            '敌方即将开火 · 掩护友军',
            'ENEMY PREPARING TO FIRE · COVER OUR UNITS',
          );
          this.radioAt = this.sim.time;
        }
        this.radioTarget = threat.unit.id;
      } else if (
        !threat &&
        this.radioTarget !== undefined &&
        this.sim.units.find((u) => u.id === this.radioTarget)?.hp === 0
      ) {
        cue = 'clear';
        this.radioTarget = undefined;
      }
    }
    if (cue) {
      this.platform.speak(cue, this.hud.lang);
      this.hud.radioText = RADIO[cue][this.hud.lang === 'zh' ? 0 : 1];
      this.radioAt = this.sim.time;
    } else if (this.sim.time - this.radioAt > 8) this.hud.radioText = '';
    this.platform.advanceRadio(dt);
    if (
      this.sim.extractionRemaining !== undefined &&
      this.sim.extractionRadioPending !== this.platform.radioPending
    )
      this.sim.setExtractionRadioPending(this.platform.radioPending);
  }
  update(dt: number) {
    if (!this.hud) return;
    if (
      this.online &&
      !this.online.recorder &&
      (globalThis as any).SmallGamesServices
    )
      this.servicesReady = this.connectServices();
    const frame = view.getFrameSize(),
      key =
        frame.width +
        'x' +
        frame.height +
        ':' +
        this.hud.touch +
        ':' +
        this.hud.safeRevision;
    // Keyboard resize and input-mode changes must not destroy an active EditBox.
    if (key !== this.resizeKey && !this.hud.editing) {
      this.resizeKey = key;
      if (this.controlGesture) this.clear();
      this.hud.resize();
      this.world.updateCamera();
      this.pause('orientation', frame.width < frame.height);
    }
    if (this.infoHold && Date.now() - this.infoHold.at >= 450) {
      const id = this.infoHold.id;
      this.infoHold = undefined;
      this.action(id);
    }
    if (this.online) void this.online.pump(performance.now());
    const before = this.sim.phase;
    const wasExtracting = this.sim.extractionRemaining !== undefined;
    // Arm before stepping: a convoy already at its destination can clear on this very frame.
    if (
      !this.sim.duelMode &&
      before === 'playing' &&
      this.sim.mission.mode === 'escort' &&
      !wasExtracting &&
      !this.sim.extractionRadioPending
    )
      this.sim.setExtractionRadioPending(true);
    const buffBefore = this.sim.buff?.kind;
    if (this.sim.duelMode) {
      this.accumulator = 0;
    } else if (this.sim.resumeCountdown > 0) {
      this.sim.stepCountdown(Math.min(dt, 0.1));
      this.accumulator = 0;
    } else if (this.sim.phase === 'playing' && !this.sim.paused) {
      this.accumulator += Math.min(dt, 0.1);
      let steps = 0;
      while (this.accumulator >= 1 / 60 && steps++ < 6) {
        this.sim.step(1 / 60);
        this.accumulator -= 1 / 60;
      }
    } else this.accumulator = 0;
    if (!wasExtracting && this.sim.extractionRemaining !== undefined) {
      this.world.zoom = 1;
      this.world.locate(this.sim.rescue);
    }
    if (buffBefore && !this.sim.buff) {
      this.hud.toast =
        buffBefore === 'tracking'
          ? this.hud.t('追踪模式已结束', 'TRACKING ENDED')
          : this.hud.t('射速已恢复', 'FIRE RATE RESTORED');
      this.hud.toastUntil = Date.now() + 3000;
    }
    if (
      !this.sim.duelMode &&
      before === 'playing' &&
      this.sim.phase !== 'playing'
    ) {
      this.saveTrainingResult();
      this.saveMissionResult();
      const finished = this.sim;
      (
        globalThis as typeof globalThis & {
          SmallGamesRewardAds?: {
            atBreak(id: object, current: () => boolean): void;
          };
        }
      ).SmallGamesRewardAds?.atBreak?.(
        finished,
        () =>
          this.sim === finished &&
          ['success', 'failure'].includes(this.sim.phase),
      );
    }
    // Pan in screen space: the ground axes rotate with the aircraft and sensor.
    if (
      !this.sim.paused &&
      !this.hud.modal &&
      this.sim.phase === 'playing' &&
      this.world.zoom > 1.05 &&
      (Array.from(this.touches.values()).some((t) => t.role === 'aim') ||
        (!this.platform.touchInput && Date.now() - this.lastAimInput < 500))
    ) {
      const p = this.world.project(this.sim.aim),
        x = p.x / view.getScaleX(),
        y = this.hud.h - p.y / view.getScaleY();
      const dx = x < 55 ? -1 : x > this.hud.w - 68 ? 1 : 0;
      const dy = y < 78 ? 1 : y > this.hud.h - 105 ? -1 : 0;
      if (dx || dy) {
        const a = this.world.aimAt(
          (this.hud.w * view.getScaleX()) / 2,
          (this.hud.h * view.getScaleY()) / 2,
        );
        const b = this.world.aimAt(
          (this.hud.w / 2 + dx * dt * 70) * view.getScaleX(),
          (this.hud.h / 2 + dy * dt * 70) * view.getScaleY(),
        );
        if (
          a &&
          b &&
          Number.isFinite(a.x) &&
          Number.isFinite(a.z) &&
          Number.isFinite(b.x) &&
          Number.isFinite(b.z)
        ) {
          this.world.center.x = Math.max(
            -MAP.halfWidth,
            Math.min(MAP.halfWidth, this.world.center.x + b.x - a.x),
          );
          this.world.center.z = Math.max(
            -MAP.halfDepth,
            Math.min(MAP.halfDepth, this.world.center.z + b.z - a.z),
          );
        }
        this.world.follow = false;
        this.world.updateCamera();
      }
    }
    for (const e of this.sim.events)
      if (e.id > this.lastEvent) {
        if (
          e.type === 'shot' &&
          (!this.sim.duelMode || e.side === this.sim.pilotSide)
        )
          this.platform.play(WEAPONS[e.weapon].id);
        else if (e.type === 'impact') {
          if (e.weapon > 0) this.platform.play('impact' + e.weapon);
          if (e.outcome === 'hit' || e.outcome === 'destroyed')
            this.platform.play('hit');
        } else if (e.type === 'attack')
          this.platform.play(e.friendly ? 'rapid' : 'alert', 0.22);
        else if (e.type === 'wave') this.platform.play('alert');
        this.lastEvent = e.id;
      }
    this.world.root.active =
      this.sim.phase !== 'briefing' || this.hud.missionsOpen;
    const presented = this.sim.duelMode
      ? this.presentation.sample(this.sim, performance.now())
      : this.sim;
    this.presentedTime = presented.time;
    this.world.update(presented, this.platform.reducedEffects);
    if (presented !== this.sim && !this.online?.spectator)
      this.sim.aim = { ...presented.aim };
    if (
      !this.sim.duelMode &&
      this.platform.rewards.ammo !== this.sim.homingAmmo
    ) {
      this.platform.rewards.ammo = this.sim.homingAmmo;
      this.platform.saveRewards();
    }
    this.updateRadio(Math.min(dt, 0.1));
    this.hud.pressedButton =
      this.controlGesture && !this.controlGesture.moved
        ? this.controlGesture.button
        : this.mouseButton ||
          Array.from(this.touches.values()).find(
            (t) => t.role === 'button' || t.role === 'fire',
          )?.button ||
          '';
    this.hud.update(presented, this.world);
    if (this.pauseControl) {
      this.pauseControl.hidden = !this.hud.buttons.find(
        (button) => button.id === 'settings',
      )?.label.node.activeInHierarchy;
      this.pauseControl.disabled =
        !!this.rewardRequest ||
        this.hud.supplyOpen ||
        this.sim.resumeCountdown > 0 ||
        this.hud.exitConfirm;
      this.pauseControl.setAttribute(
        'aria-pressed',
        String(
          this.sim.pauses.has('settings') || this.sim.pauses.has('manual'),
        ),
      );
    }
    if (sys.isBrowser && !this.startupSignalled) {
      const startupError =
        this.world.aircraftModel.status === 'error'
          ? '机舱资源加载失败，请重试。'
          : this.hud.progressionArt.homeFailed
            ? '首页画面加载失败，请检查网络后重试。'
            : '';
      if (
        startupError ||
        (this.world.aircraftModel.status !== 'loading' &&
          this.world.modelImport !== 'loading' &&
          this.hud.interfaceArtworkReady &&
          this.hud.homeArtwork.spriteFrame)
      ) {
        this.startupSignalled = true;
        if (startupError)
          window.dispatchEvent(
            new CustomEvent('night-overwatch:error', {
              detail: new Error(startupError),
            }),
          );
        else window.dispatchEvent(new Event('night-overwatch:ready'));
      }
    }
    this.platform.ambience(this.sim.phase === 'playing' && !this.sim.paused);
    if (TUTORIAL.every((e) => this.sim.completed.has(e)) && this.hud.tutorial) {
      this.hud.tutorial = false;
      this.platform.saveCoach();
    }
    this.frameCount++;
    this.frameSeconds += dt;
    if (this.frameSeconds >= 2) {
      this.measuredFps = this.frameCount / this.frameSeconds;
      this.frameCount = 0;
      this.frameSeconds = 0;
    }
  }
  snapshot() {
    return {
      progression: this.quartermaster && {
        draft: this.quartermaster.loadout,
        slot: this.quartermaster.slot,
        dirty: this.quartermaster.dirty,
        scroll: this.quartermaster.loadoutScroll,
        gift: this.quartermaster.gift,
        leaveTo: this.quartermaster.leaveTo,
        drag: this.quartermaster.drag,
        page: this.quartermaster.page,
        mode: this.quartermaster.mode,
        actionMode: this.quartermaster.actionMode,
        busy: this.quartermaster.busy,
        assets: this.online?.assets,
        message: this.quartermaster.message,
        pending: !!this.quartermaster.pending,
      },
      skill: {
        kind: this.sim.skillKind,
        remaining: this.sim.skillRemaining,
        cooldown: this.sim.skillCooldown,
      },
      online: this.online && {
        identity: this.online.identity,
        panel: this.online.panel,
        room: this.online.room,
        active: this.online.active,
        message: this.online.message,
        rankStatus: this.online.rankStatus,
        rooms: this.online.rooms,
        history: this.online.history,
        ranking: this.online.ranking,
        board: this.online.board,
      },
      fireZones: this.sim.fireZones,
      battleReport: this.hud.reportOpen,
      equipmentOpen: this.hud.equipmentOpen,
      loadout: this.sim.loadout,
      attackCount: this.sim.attackCount,
      enemyEquipment: this.online?.view?.enemyEquipment,
      timeComparison: this.hud.timeComparison,
      homingAmmo: this.sim.homingAmmo,
      homingSelected: this.sim.homingSelected,
      homingTarget: this.sim.homingSelected
        ? this.sim.homingTarget?.id
        : undefined,
      zoomLimit: this.world.zoomLimit,
      advert: this.hud.advert,
      pendingSupply: this.platform.rewards.pendingSupply,
      supplySelection: this.hud.supplySelection,
      supplyMessage: this.hud.supplyMessage,
      extractionRemaining: this.sim.extractionRemaining,
      extractionRadioPending: this.sim.extractionRadioPending,
      buff: this.sim.buff ? { ...this.sim.buff } : null,
      resumeCountdown: this.sim.resumeCountdown,
      locks: this.hud.lockedTargets,
      phase: this.sim.phase,
      mission: {
        id: this.sim.mission.id,
        mode: this.sim.mission.mode,
        map: this.sim.mission.map,
        name: this.sim.mission.name,
        spawned: this.sim.spawned.size,
        total: this.sim.mission.events.length,
      },
      trainingBest: this.hud.trainingBest,
      operationProgress: this.hud.missionProgress,
      operationPractice: this.operationPractice,
      missionPage: this.hud.missionPage,
      scrollLists: this.hud.listAreas.map((a) => ({
        ...a,
        offset: a.key.startsWith('qm:')
          ? this.quartermaster?.scrolls[a.key] || 0
          : this.hud.listScroll[a.key] || 0,
      })),
      chat: this.online
        ? {
            messages: this.online.visibleChat,
            draft: this.online.chatDraft,
            status: this.online.chatStatus,
            blocked: Array.from(this.online.blockedPlayers),
            sending: this.online.chatSending,
          }
        : undefined,
      time: this.sim.time,
      presentedTime: this.presentedTime,
      completionTime: this.sim.completionTime,
      remaining: this.sim.remaining,
      pauses: Array.from(this.sim.pauses),
      convoy: this.sim.convoy,
      progress: this.sim.ratio,
      aim: this.sim.aim,
      aircraft: { ...this.sim.aircraft },
      aircraftModel: {
        status: this.world.aircraftModel.status,
        error: this.world.aircraftModel.error,
        triangles: this.world.aircraftModel.triangleCount,
        muzzle: this.world.aircraftModel.worldMuzzle,
      },
      flightTime: this.sim.flightTime(),
      threatsRemaining: this.sim.threatsRemaining,
      camera: {
        position: {
          x: this.world.cameraNode.position.x,
          y: this.world.cameraNode.position.y,
          z: this.world.cameraNode.position.z,
        },
        rotation: {
          x: this.world.cameraNode.eulerAngles.x,
          y: this.world.cameraNode.eulerAngles.y,
          z: this.world.cameraNode.eulerAngles.z,
        },
        center: { ...this.world.center },
        fov: this.world.camera.fov,
        projection: this.world.camera.projection,
      },
      shotPositions: this.sim.shots.map((shot) => ({
        id: shot.id,
        ...this.sim.shotPosition(shot),
      })),
      unitLabels: Array.from(this.hud.unitLabels, ([id, label]) => ({
        id,
        text: label.string,
        active: label.node.activeInHierarchy,
        x: label.node.position.x,
        y: label.node.position.y,
      })),
      effects: this.hud.effects,
      recoil: this.world.recoil,
      lastSound: this.platform.lastSound,
      mousePointer: this.hud.mousePointer,
      selected: this.sim.selected,
      weaponReadiness: this.sim.guns.map((_, i) => this.sim.weaponReadiness(i)),
      lasers: this.sim.lasers,
      drones: this.sim.drones,
      guns: this.sim.guns.map((g) => ({
        ...g,
        ammo: g.ammo === Infinity ? 'infinite' : g.ammo,
      })),
      shots: this.sim.shots,
      units: this.sim.units.map((u) => ({ ...u })),
      kills: this.sim.kills,
      friendlyKills: this.sim.friendlyKills,
      groundAttacks: this.sim.events
        .filter((e) => e.type === 'attack')
        .slice(-12),
      fired: this.sim.fired,
      ammoSpent: this.sim.ammoSpent,
      hits: this.sim.hits,
      friendlyDamage: this.sim.friendlyDamage,
      rescueDamage: this.sim.rescueDamage,
      damageByThreat: this.sim.damageByThreat,
      failureCause: this.sim.failureCause,
      impacts: this.sim.events.filter((e) => e.type === 'impact').slice(-5),
      rating: this.sim.rating,
      failure: this.sim.failure,
      failedGroup: this.sim.failedGroup,
      reason: this.sim.reason(),
      friendlyRisk: this.sim.friendlyRisk,
      aimedUnit: this.sim.aimedUnit?.id,
      ui: {
        homeArtworkReady: !!this.hud.homeArtwork.spriteFrame,
        interfaceArtworkReady: this.hud.interfaceArtworkReady,
        artwork: {
          loading: this.hud.progressionArt.loadingAssets,
          failed: Array.from(this.hud.progressionArt.failed),
          ready: Array.from(this.hud.progressionArt.frames.keys()),
        },
        pressedButton: this.hud.pressedButton,
        worldVisible: this.world.root.active,
        width: this.hud.w,
        height: this.hud.h,
        minimap: this.hud.minimapLayout,
        notice: this.hud.labels.get('notice')?.string,
        warning: this.hud.labels.get('friendWarning')?.string,
        warningColor: this.hud.labels.get('friendWarning')?.color.toHEX(),
        fire: this.hud.buttons.find((b) => b.id === 'fire')?.label.string,
        radio: this.hud.radioText,
        tutorial: this.hud.labels.get('tutorial')?.string,
        feedback: this.hud.labels.get('target')?.string,
        muted: this.platform.muted,
        voicePrompts: this.platform.voicePrompts,
        autoPriority: this.platform.autoPriority,
        viewMode: this.world.viewMode,
        battleStatus: this.hud.labels.get('health')?.string,
        combatantNames: [0, 1].map((side) => {
          const label = this.hud.labels.get('spectator:' + side);
          return {
            text: label?.string,
            active: label?.node.activeInHierarchy,
            fontSize: label?.fontSize,
            wrap: label?.enableWrapText,
          };
        }),
        reducedEffects: this.platform.reducedEffects,
      },
      held: Array.from(this.sim.held),
      completed: Array.from(this.sim.completed),
      modal: this.hud.modalKey,
      scroll: this.hud.scroll,
      scrollMax: this.hud.scrollMax,
      lastHelpLine: this.hud.lastHelpLine,
      buttons: this.hud.buttons
        .filter(
          (b) =>
            b.label.node.activeInHierarchy &&
            (!this.hud.modal ||
              this.hud.inModal(b.label.node) ||
              this.hud.isGlobalAction(b.id)),
        )
        .map(({ id, x, y, w, h, label, visual, disabled, clip }) => {
          const right = clip ? Math.min(x + w, clip.x + clip.w) : x + w,
            bottom = clip ? Math.min(y + h, clip.y + clip.h) : y + h;
          if (clip) {
            x = Math.max(x, clip.x);
            y = Math.max(y, clip.y);
          }
          w = Math.max(0, right - x);
          h = Math.max(0, bottom - y);
          return {
            id,
            x,
            y,
            w,
            h,
            visual,
            clip,
            disabled: !!disabled,
            text: label.string,
            bold: label.isBold,
            color: label.color.toHEX(),
          };
        })
        .filter((b) => b.w > 0 && b.h > 0),
      zoom: this.world.zoom,
      follow: this.world.follow,
      safe: this.hud.safe,
      temporary: this.world.temporary,
      thermal: this.world.thermal,
      viewMode: this.world.viewMode,
      modelImport: this.world.modelImport,
      visualMaterialStatus: this.world.visualMaterialStatus,
      visualMaterialError: this.world.visualMaterialError,
      audio: this.platform.audioStatus,
      enginePlaying: this.platform.engine.playing,
      radioPlaying: this.platform.radio.playing,
      radioPending: this.platform.radioPending,
      radioTime: this.platform.radio.currentTime,
      radioDuration: this.platform.radio.duration,
      light: this.sim.mission.light,
      priority: this.sim.priorityThreat && {
        id: this.sim.priorityThreat.unit.id,
        targetId: this.sim.priorityThreat.target.id,
        dueIn: this.sim.priorityThreat.dueIn,
        critical: this.sim.priorityThreat.critical,
      },
      assets: Array.from(this.world.assets),
      renderNodes: this.world.views.size,
      wrecks: Array.from(this.world.views, ([id, v]) => ({
        id,
        wreck: v.wreck,
        scale: v.node.scale.x,
      })),
      markerLabels: this.hud.unitLabels.size,
      frameRate: this.measuredFps,
      drawCalls: (director.root as any)?.device?.numDrawCalls,
      triangles: (director.root as any)?.device?.numTris,
      visibleEffects: this.sim.events.filter(
        (e) => this.sim.time - e.time < 0.7,
      ).length,
    };
  }
  onDestroy() {
    this.disposed = true;
    this.stopDeveloperTools?.();
    this.quartermaster?.dispose();
    this.online?.dispose();
    this.online?.recorder?.persist?.();
    this.online?.leave();
    this.online?.recorder?.invalidate();
    this.pauseControl?.remove();
    this.screenControl?.dispose();
    input.off(Input.EventType.MOUSE_MOVE, this.mouseMove, this);
    input.off(Input.EventType.MOUSE_DOWN, this.mouseDown, this);
    input.off(Input.EventType.MOUSE_UP, this.mouseUp, this);
    input.off(Input.EventType.MOUSE_WHEEL, this.wheel, this);
    input.off(Input.EventType.TOUCH_START, this.touchStart, this);
    input.off(Input.EventType.TOUCH_MOVE, this.touchMove, this);
    input.off(Input.EventType.TOUCH_END, this.touchEnd, this);
    input.off(Input.EventType.TOUCH_CANCEL, this.touchCancel, this);
    input.off(Input.EventType.KEY_DOWN, this.keyDown, this);
    input.off(Input.EventType.KEY_UP, this.keyUp, this);
    game.targetOff(this);
    if (sys.isBrowser)
      window.removeEventListener('pagehide', this.persistRanking);
    this.platform?.dispose();
  }
}
import { AudioClip, AudioSource, Node, resources, sys } from 'cc';
import type { ViewMode } from './World';
import type { PauseReason } from './core/Simulation';
import { missionDefinition, type MissionId } from './core/MissionCatalog';
import {
  newMissionProgress,
  readMissionProgress,
  type MissionProgress,
} from './core/MissionProgress';
import {
  readTrainingRecord as parseTrainingRecord,
  type TrainingRecord,
} from './core/TrainingRecords';
import {
  readRewards,
  nextZoomLimit,
  type RewardKind,
  type RewardSave,
  type RewardProvider,
  type SupplyReward,
} from './core/Rewards';
import { readMissionTimes, type MissionTimes } from './core/BattleRecords';
export const RADIO = {
  complete: [
    '敌方威胁已全部清除。任务完成。',
    'All hostile threats eliminated. Mission complete.',
  ],
  day: [
    '指挥部：视野良好。拖动准星，清除友军附近的炮位。',
    'Command: visibility clear. Drag the sight. Clear guns near our convoy.',
  ],
  night: [
    '指挥部：进入夜间战区。切换热成像，优先处理红色标记。',
    'Command: night operation. Use thermal vision. Prioritize the red marker.',
  ],
  priority: [
    '车队呼叫！敌方即将开火，优先压制红色标记！',
    'Convoy calling! Enemy preparing to fire. Suppress the red marker!',
  ],
  critical: [
    '紧急呼叫！友军撑不住了，立即处理红色目标！',
    'Urgent! Our position is critical. Engage the red target now!',
  ],
  clear: [
    '收到，威胁已解除。继续掩护友军。',
    'Confirmed. Threat eliminated. Keep covering our units.',
  ],
} as const;
export class Platform {
  touchInput =
    sys.isMobile ||
    (sys.isBrowser && window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches);
  muted = false;
  reducedEffects = false;
  voicePrompts = true;
  autoPriority = true;
  viewMode: ViewMode = 'night';
  activated = false;
  voices = new Map<string, AudioSource>();
  engine: AudioSource;
  radio: AudioSource;
  radioPending = false;
  private radioPaused = false;
  private radioStartWait = 0;
  private radioLastTime = 0;
  private disposed = false;
  clips = new Map<string, AudioClip>();
  audioStatus = 'loading';
  cleanup: (() => void)[] = [];
  rewards: RewardSave = readRewards(null);
  missions: MissionProgress = newMissionProgress();
  times: MissionTimes = {};
  // Install before launch; this may delegate directly to an authorized GameHost.ads.offer.
  rewardProvider?: RewardProvider;
  private readonly rewardPractice = this.developer;
  private readonly rewardStorageKey = this.rewardPractice
    ? 'night-overwatch-rewards-dev-v1'
    : 'night-overwatch-rewards-v1';
  private clear: () => void;
  private pause: (reason: PauseReason, on: boolean) => void;
  constructor(
    parent: Node,
    pause: (reason: PauseReason, on: boolean) => void,
    clear: () => void,
  ) {
    this.clear = clear;
    this.pause = pause;
    try {
      this.rewards = readRewards(
        sys.localStorage.getItem(this.rewardStorageKey),
      );
      this.times = readMissionTimes(
        sys.localStorage.getItem('night-overwatch-times-v1'),
      );
      this.muted = sys.localStorage.getItem('night-overwatch-muted') === 'true';
      this.voicePrompts =
        sys.localStorage.getItem('night-overwatch-voice-prompts') !== 'false';
      this.autoPriority =
        sys.localStorage.getItem('night-overwatch-auto-priority') !== 'false';
      const preference = sys.localStorage.getItem(
        'night-overwatch-reduced-effects',
      );
      this.reducedEffects =
        preference === 'true' ||
        (preference === null &&
          sys.isBrowser &&
          window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches);
    } catch {
      /* Preferences never block the mission. */
    }
    const engineNode = new Node('AircraftEngine');
    parent.addChild(engineNode);
    this.engine = engineNode.addComponent(AudioSource);
    this.engine.playOnAwake = false;
    this.engine.loop = true;
    this.engine.volume = 0.13;
    const radioNode = new Node('CommandRadio');
    parent.addChild(radioNode);
    this.radio = radioNode.addComponent(AudioSource);
    this.radio.playOnAwake = false;
    this.radio.volume = 0.85;
    radioNode.on(AudioSource.EventType.ENDED, () => {
      this.radioPending = false;
      this.radioPaused = false;
    });
    const names = [
      'rapid',
      'blast',
      'heavy',
      'hit',
      'alert',
      'impact1',
      'impact2',
      'engine',
      ...Object.keys(RADIO).flatMap((id) =>
        ['zh', 'en'].map((lang) => `radio-${id}-${lang}`),
      ),
    ];
    let pending = names.length;
    for (const name of names) {
      if (name.startsWith('radio-')) {
        resources.load('audio/' + name, AudioClip, (error, clip) => {
          if (this.disposed) return;
          if (error) this.audioStatus = 'unavailable';
          else this.clips.set(name, clip);
          if (--pending === 0 && this.clips.size === names.length)
            this.audioStatus = 'ready';
        });
        continue;
      }
      let source = this.engine;
      if (name !== 'engine') {
        const n = new Node('Audio:' + name);
        parent.addChild(n);
        source = n.addComponent(AudioSource);
      }
      source.playOnAwake = false;
      if (name !== 'engine') source.volume = 0.3;
      this.voices.set(name, source);
      resources.load('audio/' + name, AudioClip, (error, clip) => {
        if (this.disposed) return;
        if (error) this.audioStatus = 'unavailable';
        else {
          this.clips.set(name, clip);
          source.clip = clip;
        }
        if (--pending === 0 && this.clips.size === names.length)
          this.audioStatus = 'ready';
      });
    }
    if (sys.isBrowser) {
      const listen = (target: EventTarget, type: string, fn: EventListener) => {
        target.addEventListener(type, fn);
        this.cleanup.push(() => target.removeEventListener(type, fn));
      };
      listen(window, 'blur', () => {
        clear();
        pause('focus', true);
      });
      listen(window, 'focus', () => this.restoreFocus());
      listen(document, 'visibilitychange', () => {
        clear();
        pause('background', document.hidden);
        this.restoreFocus();
      });
      const canvas = document.querySelector('canvas');
      if (canvas) {
        canvas.tabIndex = 0;
        canvas.setAttribute('aria-label', '夜航守望：点击进入，H 查看操作帮助');
        listen(canvas, 'pointerdown', (e) => {
          this.useTouchInput((e as PointerEvent).pointerType !== 'mouse');
          canvas.focus({ preventScroll: true });
          this.restoreFocus();
          this.activate();
        });
        listen(canvas, 'pointermove', (e) => {
          this.useTouchInput((e as PointerEvent).pointerType !== 'mouse');
        });
        listen(canvas, 'pointerleave', (e) => {
          if ((e as PointerEvent).pointerType === 'mouse') clear();
        });
        listen(canvas, 'pointercancel', () => clear());
        listen(canvas, 'contextmenu', (e) => e.preventDefault());
      }
    }
  }
  useTouchInput(touch: boolean) {
    if (touch === this.touchInput) return;
    this.clear();
    this.touchInput = touch;
  }
  restoreFocus() {
    // Some ad WebViews restore focus without delivering a matching window.focus event.
    if (sys.isBrowser && typeof document !== 'undefined' && this.focused)
      this.pause('focus', false);
  }
  get focused() {
    return (
      !sys.isBrowser ||
      (!document.hidden &&
        document.hasFocus() &&
        !['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(
          document.activeElement?.tagName || '',
        ))
    );
  }
  activate() {
    this.activated = true;
  }
  savePreferences() {
    try {
      sys.localStorage.setItem('night-overwatch-muted', String(this.muted));
      sys.localStorage.setItem(
        'night-overwatch-voice-prompts',
        String(this.voicePrompts),
      );
      sys.localStorage.setItem(
        'night-overwatch-auto-priority',
        String(this.autoPriority),
      );
      sys.localStorage.setItem(
        'night-overwatch-reduced-effects',
        String(this.reducedEffects),
      );
    } catch {
      /* Optional local preferences. */
    }
  }
  lastSound = '';
  play(id: string, gain = 1) {
    if (id === 'incendiary') id = 'heavy';
    if (id === 'impact3') id = 'impact2';
    const source = this.voices.get(id);
    if (source?.clip && this.activated && !this.muted) {
      source.volume =
        (id === 'heavy'
          ? 0.7
          : id === 'blast'
            ? 0.5
            : id === 'rapid'
              ? 0.32
              : 0.3) * gain;
      this.lastSound = id;
      source.stop();
      source.play();
    }
  }
  stop(preserveRadio = false) {
    if (preserveRadio && this.radioPending) {
      if (!this.radioPaused) this.radio.pause();
      this.radioPaused = true;
    } else this.stopRadio();
    for (const source of Array.from(this.voices.values())) source.stop();
  }
  stopRadio() {
    this.radio.stop();
    this.radioPending = false;
    this.radioPaused = false;
  }
  speak(id: keyof typeof RADIO, lang: 'zh' | 'en') {
    this.stopRadio();
    const clip = this.clips.get(`radio-${id}-${lang}`);
    if (
      !clip ||
      this.muted ||
      !this.voicePrompts ||
      !this.activated ||
      this.disposed
    )
      return;
    this.radio.clip = clip;
    this.radioPending = true;
    this.radioStartWait = 0;
    this.radioLastTime = 0;
    this.radio.play();
  }
  advanceRadio(dt: number) {
    if (!this.radioPending) return;
    if (this.muted || !this.voicePrompts || this.disposed) {
      this.stopRadio();
      return;
    }
    if (this.radioPaused) {
      this.radioPaused = false;
      this.radioStartWait = 0;
      this.radioLastTime = this.radio.currentTime;
      this.radio.play();
    }
    const time = this.radio.currentTime;
    this.radioStartWait =
      time > this.radioLastTime + 0.0001 ? 0 : this.radioStartWait + dt;
    this.radioLastTime = time;
    // A blocked/stalled audio device must not strand the mission; advancing playback waits for ENDED.
    if (this.radioStartWait >= 2) this.stopRadio();
  }
  ambience(playing: boolean) {
    this.engine.volume = this.radio.playing ? 0.04 : 0.13;
    const clip = this.clips.get('engine');
    if (clip && playing && this.activated && !this.muted) {
      if (!this.engine.playing) {
        this.engine.play();
      }
    } else if (this.engine.playing) this.engine.stop();
  }
  readCoach() {
    try {
      return sys.localStorage.getItem('night-overwatch-coach-v2') !== 'done';
    } catch {
      return true;
    }
  }
  get rewardsAvailable() {
    const bridge = globalThis as typeof globalThis & {
      SmallGamesRewardAds?: { available?: boolean; offer?: RewardProvider };
      SmallGamesPublishingConfig?: { adsEnabled?: boolean };
    };
    if (bridge.SmallGamesPublishingConfig?.adsEnabled === false) return false;
    return Boolean(
      this.rewardProvider ||
        (bridge.SmallGamesRewardAds?.available !== false &&
          bridge.SmallGamesRewardAds?.offer) ||
        (!bridge.SmallGamesPublishingConfig &&
          this.rewardPractice &&
          this.developer),
    );
  }
  async offerReward(kind: RewardKind) {
    if (!this.rewardsAvailable) return 'unavailable' as const;
    const next = nextZoomLimit(this.rewards.zoomLimit);
    if (kind === 'zoom' && !next) return 'unavailable' as const;
    const bridge = (
      globalThis as typeof globalThis & {
        SmallGamesRewardAds?: { offer: RewardProvider };
      }
    ).SmallGamesRewardAds;
    const provider = this.rewardProvider ?? bridge?.offer.bind(bridge);
    if (!provider)
      return this.rewardPractice && this.developer
        ? ('mock' as const)
        : ('unavailable' as const);
    try {
      const outcome = await provider({
        id: 'night-overwatch:' + kind,
        reward: kind === 'supply' ? { supplyChoice: 1 } : { zoomLimit: next! },
      });
      return ['completed', 'dismissed', 'unavailable', 'failed'].includes(
        outcome?.status,
      )
        ? outcome.status
        : ('failed' as const);
    } catch {
      return 'failed' as const;
    }
  }
  async offerSupplyVideo() {
    const bridge = (
      globalThis as typeof globalThis & {
        SmallGamesRewardAds?: { offer: RewardProvider };
      }
    ).SmallGamesRewardAds;
    const provider = this.rewardProvider ?? bridge?.offer.bind(bridge);
    if (!provider) return 'unavailable';
    try {
      return (
        await provider({
          id: 'night-overwatch:supply-package',
          reward: { supplyPackageSegment: 1 },
        })
      ).status;
    } catch {
      return 'failed';
    }
  }
  grantReward(kind: RewardKind) {
    if (kind === 'supply') this.rewards.pendingSupply = true;
    else
      this.rewards.zoomLimit =
        nextZoomLimit(this.rewards.zoomLimit) ?? this.rewards.zoomLimit;
    this.saveRewards();
  }
  claimSupply(reward: SupplyReward) {
    if (
      !this.rewards.pendingSupply ||
      !['tracking', 'ammo', 'rate', 'restock'].includes(reward)
    )
      return false;
    this.rewards.pendingSupply = false;
    if (reward === 'ammo')
      this.rewards.ammo = Math.min(
        Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER,
        this.rewards.ammo + 2,
      );
    this.saveRewards();
    return true;
  }
  saveRewards() {
    try {
      sys.localStorage.setItem(
        this.rewardStorageKey,
        JSON.stringify(this.rewards),
      );
    } catch {
      /* The current session remains playable without storage. */
    }
  }
  cancelReward() {
    (
      globalThis as typeof globalThis & {
        SmallGamesRewardAds?: { cancel(): void };
      }
    ).SmallGamesRewardAds?.cancel();
  }
  saveCoach() {
    try {
      sys.localStorage.setItem('night-overwatch-coach-v2', 'done');
    } catch {
      /* Optional preference; storage denial never blocks play. */
    }
  }
  get developer() {
    if (!sys.isBrowser) return false;
    try {
      return !!(
        globalThis as typeof globalThis & {
          SmallGamesDev?: { isEnabled(): boolean };
        }
      ).SmallGamesDev?.isEnabled();
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }
  readOperationProgress() {
    try {
      this.missions = readMissionProgress(
        sys.localStorage.getItem('night-overwatch-progress-v1'),
      );
    } catch {
      /* Retain current-session progress if optional storage is denied. */
    }
    return this.missions;
  }
  saveOperationProgress(progress: MissionProgress) {
    this.missions = progress;
    try {
      sys.localStorage.setItem(
        'night-overwatch-progress-v1',
        JSON.stringify(progress),
      );
    } catch {
      /* All remaining operations can still unlock in the current session. */
    }
  }
  saveTimes() {
    try {
      sys.localStorage.setItem(
        'night-overwatch-times-v1',
        JSON.stringify(this.times),
      );
    } catch {
      /* Keep this session's personal best when optional storage is denied. */
    }
  }
  readMission(): MissionId {
    try {
      const mission = missionDefinition(
        sys.localStorage.getItem('night-overwatch-mission-v1'),
      );
      return mission.mode === 'escort' ? mission.id : 'corridor-01';
    } catch {
      return 'corridor-01';
    }
  }
  saveMission(id: MissionId) {
    if (missionDefinition(id).mode !== 'escort') return;
    try {
      sys.localStorage.setItem('night-overwatch-mission-v1', id);
    } catch {
      /* Choosing a mission works even when storage is denied. */
    }
  }
  readWarmupRecord(): TrainingRecord | undefined {
    try {
      return parseTrainingRecord(
        sys.localStorage.getItem('night-overwatch-training-v1'),
      );
    } catch {
      return;
    }
  }
  saveWarmupRecord(record: TrainingRecord) {
    try {
      sys.localStorage.setItem(
        'night-overwatch-training-v1',
        JSON.stringify(record),
      );
    } catch {
      /* A denied optional record never blocks another attempt. */
    }
  }
  dispose() {
    this.disposed = true;
    for (const fn of this.cleanup) fn();
    this.stop();
  }
}
import { Simulation } from './core/Simulation.ts';
const blend = (from: number, to: number, amount: number) =>
  from + (to - from) * amount;
const angle = (from: number, to: number, amount: number, turn = Math.PI * 2) =>
  from + (((to - from + turn * 1.5) % turn) - turn / 2) * amount;
/** Presentation only: no prediction of damage, ammunition, collisions or match results. */
export class DuelPresentation {
  private rendered?: Simulation;
  private sourceTime = 0;
  private sourceAircraft?: Simulation['aircraft'];
  private sourceUnits = new Map<
    number,
    { x: number; z: number; heading: number }
  >();
  private received = 0;
  private duration = 100;
  push(authority: Simulation, now: number) {
    const previous = this.rendered;
    this.sourceTime = previous?.time ?? authority.time;
    this.sourceAircraft = { ...(previous?.aircraft ?? authority.aircraft) };
    this.sourceUnits.clear();
    for (const unit of previous?.units ?? authority.units)
      this.sourceUnits.set(unit.id, {
        x: unit.x,
        z: unit.z,
        heading: unit.heading,
      });
    this.duration = Math.min(
      250,
      Math.max(50, (authority.time - this.sourceTime) * 1000),
    );
    this.received = now;
    this.rendered = Simulation.restore(authority.checkpoint());
  }
  sample(authority: Simulation, now: number) {
    const rendered = this.rendered,
      aircraft = this.sourceAircraft;
    if (!rendered || !aircraft || authority.phase !== 'playing')
      return authority;
    const amount = Math.max(
      0,
      Math.min(1, (now - this.received) / this.duration),
    );
    rendered.time = blend(this.sourceTime, authority.time, amount);
    rendered.phase = authority.phase;
    rendered.pauses = authority.pauses;
    rendered.aim = { ...authority.aim };
    rendered.selected = authority.selected;
    rendered.guns = authority.guns;
    rendered.skill = { ...authority.skill };
    rendered.loadout = authority.loadout;
    rendered.drones = authority.drones;
    rendered.lasers = authority.lasers;
    rendered.buff = authority.buff;
    for (const key of [
      'x',
      'y',
      'z',
      'pitch',
      'bank',
      'radius',
      'altitude',
    ] as const)
      rendered.aircraft[key] = blend(
        aircraft[key],
        authority.aircraft[key],
        amount,
      );
    rendered.aircraft.yaw = angle(aircraft.yaw, authority.aircraft.yaw, amount);
    rendered.aircraft.heading = angle(
      aircraft.heading,
      authority.aircraft.heading,
      amount,
      360,
    );
    for (let i = 0; i < rendered.units.length; i++) {
      const unit = rendered.units[i],
        target = authority.units[i],
        from = this.sourceUnits.get(unit.id);
      if (!from || !target || target.id !== unit.id) continue;
      unit.x = blend(from.x, target.x, amount);
      unit.z = blend(from.z, target.z, amount);
      unit.heading = angle(from.heading, target.heading, amount);
    }
    return rendered;
  }
}
import { resources, Texture2D, SpriteFrame, Rect, TTFFont, isValid } from 'cc';
import type { HUD } from './HUD';
import type { ProgressionPage } from './Quartermaster';
// Rectangles sample only the approved illustrations; prices and UI text are rendered live.
const cuts: Record<string, [string, number, number, number, number]> = {
  closedCrate: ['reward-atlas', 0, 165, 790, 530],
  openCrate: ['reward-atlas', 790, 0, 720, 724],
  wheel: ['reward-atlas', 1510, 30, 662, 662],
  powerGear: ['gear-atlas-v2', 60, 0, 510, 440],
  rateGear: ['gear-atlas-v2', 610, 0, 560, 440],
  radiusGear: ['gear-atlas-v2', 1240, 0, 510, 440],
  droneGear: ['gear-atlas-v2', 0, 440, 720, 440],
  coolingGear: ['gear-atlas-v2', 760, 440, 370, 440],

  homeBadge: ['01-home', 34, 24, 120, 72],
  coin: ['01-home', 1137, 27, 46, 47],
  homeCrate: ['01-home', 90, 404, 94, 94],
  homeCompass: ['01-home', 59, 547, 123, 98],
  calendar: ['01-home', 1390, 454, 53, 52],
  gift: ['01-home', 1390, 551, 60, 59],
  loadoutBurst: ['02-loadout', 146, 283, 206, 86],
  loadoutTwin: ['02-loadout', 418, 284, 217, 88],
  loadoutTracking: ['02-loadout', 1060, 500, 195, 108],
  loadoutEmp: ['02-loadout', 145, 676, 258, 89],
  homeShop: ['01-home', 31, 716, 390, 111],
  homeGrowth: ['01-home', 446, 716, 380, 111],
  homeTasks: ['01-home', 849, 716, 379, 111],
  homeWarehouse: ['01-home', 1253, 716, 388, 111],
  drone: ['02-loadout', 143, 483, 353, 87],
  power: ['02-loadout', 1067, 699, 194, 104],
  combo: ['02-loadout', 1055, 138, 585, 125],

  duel: ['03-mode-selection', 606, 154, 466, 419],

  taskSolo: ['07-tasks', 66, 360, 352, 111],

  mailAircraft: ['08-inbox', 1040, 120, 594, 244],

  rewardSupply: ['08-inbox', 1148, 461, 378, 181],
  resultEmblem: ['09-settlement', 600, 21, 470, 148],

};
const homeAssets = ['home', 'title', 'skins', '01-home', 'night-ui-serif'];
const equipmentAssets = ['gear-atlas-v2', '04-shop', '06-growth-skills'];
const pageAssets: Record<ProgressionPage, string[]> = {
  '': ['home', 'title', '01-home'],
  modes: ['base', '03-mode-selection'],
  shop: ['base', ...equipmentAssets],
  loadout: ['hangar', ...equipmentAssets],
  supply: ['base', 'reward-atlas'],
  growth: ['base', '06-growth-skills', 'gear-atlas-v2'],
  tasks: ['hangar', '07-tasks'],
  inbox: ['base', '08-inbox'],
  warehouse: ['hangar', 'reward-atlas', ...equipmentAssets, '01-home'],
  receipts: ['base', 'reward-atlas'],
  checkin: ['hangar', '05-supply', '08-inbox'],
  probability: ['base'],
};
const warmAssets = [
  'base',
  'hangar',
  '03-mode-selection',
  ...equipmentAssets,
  'reward-atlas',
  '07-tasks',
  '08-inbox',
  '05-supply',
  'resultBadge',
];
export class ProgressionArt {
  frames = new Map<string, SpriteFrame>();
  private loading = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
  private disposed = false;
  private warming = false;
  failed = new Set<string>();
  font?: TTFFont;
  private hud: HUD;
  constructor(hud: HUD) {
    this.hud = hud;
    this.ensure(homeAssets);
  }
  private has(name: string) {
    return name === 'night-ui-serif' ? !!this.font : this.frames.has(name);
  }
  load(name: string): Promise<void> {
    if (this.disposed || this.has(name) || this.failed.has(name))
      return Promise.resolve();
    const pending = this.loading.get(name);
    if (pending) return pending;
    const request = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
      const finish = (error: Error | null) => {
        this.loading.delete(name);
        if (!this.disposed && isValid(this.hud.root)) {
          if (error) this.failed.add(name);
          if (!this.hud.editing) this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
        }
        resolve();
      };
      if (name === 'night-ui-serif') {
        resources.load('ui/progression/' + name, TTFFont, (error, font) => {
          if (!error && !this.disposed && isValid(this.hud.root))
            this.font = font;
          finish(error);
        });
        return;
      }
      resources.load(
        'ui/progression/' + name + '/texture',
        Texture2D,
        (error, texture) => {
          if (!error && !this.disposed && isValid(this.hud.root)) {
            const frame = new SpriteFrame();
            frame.texture = texture;
            this.frames.set(name, frame);
            for (const [key, [source, x, y, w, h]] of Object.entries(cuts))
              if (source === name) {
                const crop = new SpriteFrame();
                crop.texture = texture;
                crop.rect = new Rect(x, y, w, h);
                if (['gold', 'blue', 'panel'].includes(key))
                  crop.insetLeft =
                    crop.insetRight =
                    crop.insetTop =
                    crop.insetBottom =
                      48;
                this.frames.set(key, crop);
              }
          }
          finish(error);
        },
      );
    });
    this.loading.set(name, request);
    void request.then(() => {
      if (this.loading.get(name) === request) this.loading.delete(name);
    });
    return request;
  }
  ensure(names: string[]) {
    for (const name of names) void this.load(name);
    return names.every((name) => this.has(name));
  }
  required(page: ProgressionPage, result = false, gift = false) {
    return [
      'skins',
      'night-ui-serif',
      ...(result ? ['resultBadge'] : pageAssets[page]),
      ...(gift ? ['base', 'reward-atlas', ...equipmentAssets] : []),
    ];
  }
  retry() {
    const names = Array.from(this.failed);
    this.failed.clear();
    this.ensure(names);
    this.hud.modalKey = 'rebuild';
  }
  warm() {
    if (this.warming || !this.homeReady || this.disposed) return;
    this.warming = true;
    const queue = [...warmAssets];
    const next = async () => {
      while (queue.length && !this.disposed) await this.load(queue.shift()!);
    };
    void next();
    void next();
  }
  get(key: string) {
    if (!this.frames.has(key)) this.load(cuts[key]?.[0] || key);
    return this.frames.get(key);
  }
  get homeReady() {
    return homeAssets.every((name) => this.has(name));
  }
  get homeFailed() {
    return homeAssets.some((name) => this.failed.has(name));
  }
  get loadingAssets() {
    return Array.from(this.loading.keys());
  }
  destroy() {
    this.disposed = true;
    for (const frame of this.frames.values()) frame.destroy();
    this.frames.clear();
    this.font = undefined;
  }
}
import {
  Color,
  Label,
  Node,

  Sprite,
  UITransform,
  Mask,
  UIOpacity,
  tween,
} from 'cc';
import type { HUD } from './HUD';
import { playerLabel } from './Online';
import { ProgressionArt } from './ProgressionArt';
import {
  Quartermaster,
  effectEnglish,
  wheelLandingAngle,
} from './Quartermaster';
import {
  CATALOG,
  PACKAGES,
  POOLS,

  attackPreview,

  type AssetReward,
} from './core/Progression';

const ivory = '#f4e9d1',
  dim = '#a7bcc9',
  cyan = '#78e3ff',
  gold = '#e5bd71';
const color = (hex: string) => new Color().fromHEX(hex);
const names: Record<string, [string, string]> = {
  modes: ['开始行动', 'DEPLOY'],
  loadout: ['作战配置', 'LOADOUT'],
  shop: ['军需商店', 'ARMORY'],
  supply: ['礼包·抽奖', 'SUPPLY & WHEEL'],
  growth: ['等级成长', 'GROWTH & SKILLS'],
  tasks: ['任务', 'TASKS'],
  inbox: ['消息中心', 'MESSAGES'],
  warehouse: ['我的仓库', 'WAREHOUSE'],
  receipts: ['开包记录', 'OPENING HISTORY'],
  checkin: ['每日签到', 'DAILY CHECK-IN'],
  probability: ['概率与内容', 'CONTENTS & ODDS'],
};
const rarity = ['普通', '精良', '稀有', '史诗'],
  rarityEn = ['Common', 'Fine', 'Rare', 'Epic'],
  rarityColor = ['#b8c0c7', '#74cf9c', '#69b7ff', '#d08aff'];
/** One responsive Cocos presentation for the approved nine screens, with live text and touch targets. */
export class ProgressionView {
  private h: HUD;
  private q: Quartermaster;
  private a: ProgressionArt;
  private n: Node;
  private g: Graphics;
  private surfaces: Node;
  private w: number;
  private height: number;
  private left: number;
  private right: number;
  private top: number;
  private bottom: number;
  private scale: number;
  constructor(
    h: HUD,
    q: Quartermaster,
    a: ProgressionArt,
    n: Node,
    g: Graphics,
    surfaces: Node,
  ) {
    this.h = h;
    this.q = q;
    this.a = a;
    this.n = n;
    this.g = g;
    this.surfaces = surfaces;
    this.w = h.w;
    this.height = h.h;
    this.left = h.safe.left + Math.max(12, h.w * 0.016);
    this.right = h.w - h.safe.right - Math.max(12, h.w * 0.016);
    this.top = h.safe.top + Math.max(52, h.h * 0.1);
    this.bottom = h.h - h.safe.bottom - 12;
    this.scale = Math.min(h.w / 1672, h.h / 941);
  }
  private t(zh: string, en: string) {
    return this.h.t(zh, en);
  }
  private get narrow() {
    return this.right - this.left < 660;
  }
  private get low() {
    return this.height < 500;
  }
  private font(size: number, min = 12) {
