    return Math.max(min, size * this.scale);
  }
  private label(
    value: string,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
    size = 26,
    tone = ivory,
    serif = false,
    left = false,
  ) {
    const money = /^● [\d—]/.test(value);
    if (money) {
      const d = Math.min(24, h - 4);
      this.coin(x, y + (h - d) / 2, d);
      value = value.slice(2);
      x += d + 5;
      w -= d + 5;
    }
    const l = this.h.label(
      this.n,
      value,
      x + w / 2,
      y + h / 2,
      this.font(size),
      w,
      h,
      tone,
    );
    l.lineHeight = l.fontSize * 1.23;
    l.isBold = serif;
    if (money) {
      l.enableWrapText = false;
      l.overflow = Label.Overflow.SHRINK;
    }
    if (serif && this.h.lang === 'zh' && this.a.font) {
      l.font = this.a.font;
      l.useSystemFont = false;
    }
    if (left) l.horizontalAlign = Label.HorizontalAlign.LEFT;
    return l;
  }
  private coin(x: number, y: number, d: number) {
    const g = this.g,
      cx = x + d / 2 - this.w / 2,
      cy = this.height / 2 - y - d / 2;
    g.fillColor = color('#d59a2d');
    g.strokeColor = color('#ffe8a1');
    g.lineWidth = 1;
    g.circle(cx, cy, d * 0.44);
    g.fill();
    g.stroke();
    g.fillColor = color('#f4c85d');
    g.circle(cx - d * 0.04, cy + d * 0.04, d * 0.32);
    g.fill();
    g.strokeColor = color('#9d6519');
    g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, d * 0.07);
    g.circle(cx, cy, d * 0.3);
    g.stroke();
    g.moveTo(cx, cy + d * 0.17);
    g.lineTo(cx, cy - d * 0.17);
    g.moveTo(cx - d * 0.1, cy + d * 0.08);
    g.lineTo(cx + d * 0.1, cy + d * 0.08);
    g.moveTo(cx - d * 0.1, cy - d * 0.08);
    g.lineTo(cx + d * 0.1, cy - d * 0.08);
    g.stroke();
  }
  private picture(
    key: string,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
    cover = true,
  ) {
    const frame = this.a.get(key);
    if (!frame) return;
    if (
      cover &&
      ![
        'closedCrate',
        'openCrate',
        'powerGear',
        'rateGear',
        'radiusGear',
        'droneGear',
        'coolingGear',
        'burstGear',
        'coinPile',
      ].includes(key)
    )
      this.h.coverImage(
        this.surfaces,
        'Illustration:' + key,
        frame,
        x,
        y,
        w,
        h,
      );
    else {
      const ratio = frame.rect.width / frame.rect.height,
        iw = Math.min(w, h * ratio),
        ih = iw / ratio;
      this.h.image(
        this.surfaces,
        'Illustration:' + key,
        frame,
        x + (w - iw) / 2,
        y + (h - ih) / 2,
        iw,
        ih,
      );
    }
  }
  private skin(key: string, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number) {
    const frame = this.a.get(key);
    if (!frame) {
      this.h.rect(this.g, x, y, w, h, '#07121fec', '#638091', 3);
      return;
    }
    const node = this.h.node('ProgressionSkin:' + key, this.surfaces),
      sprite = node.addComponent(Sprite);
    const scale =
      key === 'panel'
        ? Math.max(0.12, 0.24 * this.scale)
        : Math.max(0.12, Math.min(0.5 * this.scale, h / 230));
    node.setPosition(x + w / 2 - this.w / 2, this.height / 2 - y - h / 2);
    node.setScale(scale, scale, 1);
    node.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(w / scale, h / scale);
    sprite.sizeMode = Sprite.SizeMode.CUSTOM;
    sprite.type = Sprite.Type.SLICED;
    sprite.spriteFrame = frame;
  }
  private panel(x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number, selected = false) {
    this.skin('panel', x, y, w, h);
    if (selected) {
      const outline = this.h
        .node('SelectedOutline', this.n)
        .addComponent(Graphics);
      outline.strokeColor = color(cyan);
      outline.lineWidth = Math.max(2, 3 * this.scale);
      outline.roundRect(
        x + 2 - this.w / 2,
        this.height / 2 - y - h + 2,
        w - 4,
        h - 4,
        3,
      );
      outline.stroke();
    }
  }
  private button(
    id: string,
    caption: string,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h = 44,
    kind: 'gold' | 'blue' | 'panel' = 'panel',
    disabled = false,
  ) {
    h = Math.max(44, h);
    this.skin(kind, x + 2, y + 2, w - 4, h - 4);
    const l = this.h.button(id, caption, x, y, w, h, this.n),
      b = this.h.buttons.at(-1)!;
    const units = Array.from(caption).reduce(
      (n, c) => n + (/[^\x00-\x7f]/.test(c) ? 1 : 0.56),
      0,
    );
    b.disabled = disabled;
    b.visual = { x: x + 2, y: y + 2, w: w - 4, h: h - 4 };
    l.fontSize = Math.max(
      12,
      Math.min(
        this.font(kind === 'gold' ? 48 : 43, 14),
        h * 0.56,
        (w - 22) / Math.max(1, units),
      ),
    );
    l.lineHeight = l.fontSize * 1.15;
    l.color = color(disabled ? '#75828b' : ivory);
    l.isBold = true;
    if (this.h.lang === 'zh' && this.a.font) {
      l.font = this.a.font;
      l.useSystemFont = false;
    }
    if (disabled) this.h.rect(this.g, x + 3, y + 3, w - 6, h - 6, '#08121c77');
    return l;
  }
  private tab(
    id: string,
    caption: string,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    selected: boolean,
  ) {
    this.button(id, caption, x, y, w, 44, selected ? 'blue' : 'panel');
    if (selected)
      this.h.rect(this.g, x + 3, y + 3, w - 6, 38, '#78e3ff18', cyan, 3);
  }
  private bar(
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
    ratio: number,
    tone = cyan,
  ) {
    this.h.rect(this.g, x, y, w, h, '#06121a', '#64808d', h / 2);
    if (ratio > 0)
      this.h.gradient(
        this.g,
        x + 2,
        y + 2,
        Math.max(0, (w - 4) * Math.min(1, ratio)),
        h - 4,
        tone,
        '#4887a7',
        Math.min(4, h / 2),
      );
  }
  private productName(p: Product) {
    return this.t(p.name, effectEnglish[p.effect]);
  }
  private productArt(effect: Effect) {
    return (
      (
        {
          rate: 'rateGear',
          radius: 'radiusGear',
          power: 'powerGear',
          drone: 'droneGear',
          burst: 'burst',
          cooling: 'coolingGear',
          twin: 'twin',
        } as Partial<Record<Effect, string>>
      )[effect] || effect
    );
  }
  private term(term: Product['term']) {
    return this.t(
      term === 'match' ? '单场' : term === '24h' ? '24小时' : '永久',
      term === 'match'
        ? 'One match'
        : term === '24h'
          ? '24 hours'
          : 'Permanent',
    );
  }
  private reward(r: AssetReward) {
    return `${this.t('金币', 'Coins')} +${r.coins}   EXP +${r.xp}${r.packs ? `   ${this.t('礼包', 'Crates')} ×${r.packs}` : ''}`;
  }
  private header(title: string) {
    const y = this.h.safe.top,
      h = this.top - y - 6;
    this.h.rect(this.g, 0, y, this.w, h, '#03101dda', '#566872');
    this.button('qm:back', '‹', this.left, y + 3, 48, Math.max(44, h - 6));
    const q = this.q,
      status =
        (q.page === 'loadout' && !this.low) || q.busy
          ? ''
          : q.message[this.h.lang === 'zh' ? 0 : 1];
    this.label(
      status || title,
      this.left + 60,
      y + 4,
      q.page === 'loadout' && !this.low && !status
        ? this.w * 0.15
        : this.right - this.left - (q.pending ? 160 : 200),
      h - 8,
      status ? 23 : 52,
      status ? cyan : ivory,
      !status,
      true,
    );
    if (q.busy || (q.assets && !q.pending && !q.message[0])) {
      const g = this.h
          .node(q.busy ? 'AssetSync:busy' : 'AssetSync:ready', this.n)
          .addComponent(Graphics),
        x = this.right - 172,
        cy = y + h / 2;
      if (q.busy) this.h.controlIcon(g, 'refresh', x, cy, 16, dim);
      else {
        g.strokeColor = color(dim);
        g.lineWidth = 2;
        g.moveTo(x - 6 - this.w / 2, this.height / 2 - cy);
        g.lineTo(x - 2 - this.w / 2, this.height / 2 - cy - 4);
        g.lineTo(x + 6 - this.w / 2, this.height / 2 - cy + 5);
        g.stroke();
      }
    }
    if (q.pending)
      this.button(
        'qm:retry',
        this.t('重试', 'Retry'),
        this.right - 92,
        y + 3,
        92,
        Math.max(44, h - 6),
        'blue',
        q.busy,
      );
    else
      this.label(
        `● ${q.assets?.coins.toLocaleString() ?? '—'}`,
        this.right - 148,
        y + 6,
        148,
        h - 12,
        34,
        gold,
        false,
      );
  }
  private footer() {
    if (this.q.info) this.infoDialog();
    else if (this.q.confirmation) this.confirm();
    else if (this.q.leaveTo !== undefined) this.leaveDialog();
    else if (this.q.gift) this.giftDialog();
  }
  private brief(p: Product) {
    return this.h.lang === 'zh'
      ? {
          power: '普通武器威力 +15%',
          rate: '攻击间隔 −12%',
          burst: '追加一枚60%威力副弹',
          twin: '追加一枚侧向副弹',
          radius: '爆炸范围 +15%',
          cooling: '普通武器热量 −15%',
          drone: '3架持续攻击 · 12秒后坠毁',
          laser: '持续3秒 · 每秒80伤害',
          tracking: '追踪60秒 · 冷却90秒',
          emp: '干扰12秒 · 冷却45秒',
        }[p.effect]
      : p.description;
  }
  private infoButton(id: string, x: number, y: number) {
    this.h.button('info:' + id, 'ⓘ', x, y, 44, 44, this.n).fontSize = 22;
  }
  private list(
    key: string,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
    total: number,
    draw: (offset: number) => void,
  ) {
    const old = { n: this.n, surfaces: this.surfaces, g: this.g },
      list = this.h.listViewport(this.n, 'qm:' + key, { x, y, w, h }, total);
    this.n = list.n;
    this.surfaces = list.surfaces;
    this.g = list.g;
    draw(list.offset);
    list.finish();
    Object.assign(this, old);
  }
  render(s: Simulation, result = false) {
    if (
      this.loading(
        this.a.required(this.q.page, result, !!this.q.gift),
        result ? 'home' : 'qm:back',
      )
    ) {
      if (this.q.leaveTo !== undefined) this.leaveDialog();
      return;
    }
    if (result) {
      this.settlement(s);
      this.footer();
      return;
    }
    if (!this.q.page) {
      this.home();
      this.footer();
      return;
    }
    const page = this.q.page;
    this.header(this.t(...names[page]));
    if (page === 'modes') this.modes();
    else if (page === 'shop') this.shop();
    else if (page === 'loadout') this.loadout();
    else if (page === 'supply') this.supply();
    else if (page === 'growth') this.growth();
    else if (page === 'tasks') this.tasks();
    else if (page === 'inbox') this.inbox();
    else if (page === 'warehouse') this.warehouse();
    else if (page === 'checkin') this.checkin();
    else if (page === 'receipts') this.receipts();
    else this.probability();
    if (!this.q.assets && !this.q.busy && !this.q.pending && page !== 'modes')
      this.button(
        'qm:refresh',
        this.t('连接', 'Connect'),
        this.right - 104,
        this.h.safe.top + 3,
        104,
        44,
        'blue',
      );
    this.footer();
  }
  loading(
    required: string[],
    back = 'qm:back',
    pending = false,
    failed = false,
  ) {
    const ready = this.a.ensure(required);
    if (ready && !pending && !failed) return false;
    failed = failed || required.some((name) => this.a.failed.has(name));
    this.h.gradient(
      this.g,
      0,
      0,
      this.w,
      this.height,
      '#06121a55',
      '#06121acc',
    );
    const w = Math.min(this.w * 0.65, this.height * 0.9, 580),
      x = (this.w - w) / 2,
      y = this.height * 0.2;
    this.picture('title', x, y, w, w / 3, false);
    this.label(
      this.t(
        failed ? '画面加载未完成' : '正在加载画面…',
        failed ? 'ARTWORK UNAVAILABLE' : 'PREPARING THE VIEW…',
      ),
      x,
      this.height * 0.57,
      w,
      42,
      42,
      ivory,
      true,
    );
    this.label(
      this.t(
        failed ? '请检查网络后重试' : '即将就绪，请稍候',
        failed
          ? 'Check your connection and retry.'
          : 'Almost ready. Please wait.',
      ),
      x,
      this.height * 0.57 + 42,
      w,
      26,
      24,
      dim,
    );
    const by = Math.min(this.height - 56, this.height * 0.57 + 82),
      bw = Math.min(160, (w - 12) / 2);
    this.button(
      back,
      this.t('返回', 'BACK'),
      this.w / 2 - bw - (failed ? 6 : -bw / 2),
      by,
      bw,
      44,
      'panel',
    );
    if (failed)
      this.button(
        'qm:artRetry',
        this.t('重新加载', 'RETRY'),
        this.w / 2 + 6,
        by,
        bw,
        44,
        'gold',
      );
    return true;
  }
  private home() {
    const h = this.height,
      w = this.w,
      l = this.left,
      r = this.right,
      q = this.q,
      top = this.h.safe.top,
      bar = Math.max(48, h * 0.115);
    this.h.rect(this.g, 0, top, w, bar, '#020e19c9', '#61707c');
    const compactHeader = w < 1000,
      profileW = compactHeader ? 160 : w * 0.29;
    if (!compactHeader)
      this.picture('homeBadge', l, top + bar * 0.2, bar * 1.15, bar * 0.69);
    const px = l + (compactHeader ? 0 : bar * 1.3),
      pw = profileW - (px - l);
    this.h.button('online:profile', '', px, top, pw, Math.max(44, bar), this.n);
    this.label(
      playerLabel(this.h.online?.identity || {}).split(' #')[0],
      px + 8,
      top + 3,
      pw - 16,
      bar * 0.48,
      32,
      ivory,
      true,
      true,
    );
    this.label(
      `Lv.${q.assets?.level ?? 1}${compactHeader ? ' EXP' : ''}`,
      px + 8,
      top + bar * 0.53,
      compactHeader ? 70 : 48,
      20,
      compactHeader ? 15 : 20,
      ivory,
    );
    this.bar(
      px + (compactHeader ? 82 : 62),
      top + bar * 0.67,
      Math.max(25, pw - (compactHeader ? 96 : 178)),
      Math.max(5, bar * 0.09),
      (q.assets?.current ?? 0) / (q.assets?.required || 200),
    );
    if (!compactHeader)
      this.label(
        `EXP ${q.assets?.current ?? 0}/${q.assets?.required ?? 200}`,
        px + pw - 112,
        top + bar * 0.53,
        104,
        25,
        18,
        dim,
      );
    const recordX = l + profileW + 8;
    this.h.button(
      'online:ranking',
      this.t('战绩', 'Records'),
      recordX,
      top + 3,
      compactHeader ? 50 : 75,
      Math.max(44, bar - 6),
      this.n,
    ).fontSize = this.font(24, 13);
    if (!compactHeader)
      this.h.controlIcon(
        this.g,
        'ranking',
        recordX + 37,
        top + bar * 0.26,
        25,
        ivory,
      );
    const settingsX =
      this.h.buttons.find((b) => b.id === 'settings')?.x ?? r - 44;
    const inboxW = compactHeader ? 46 : 132,
      coinW = compactHeader ? 112 : 170;
    this.h.button(
      'qm:inbox',
      this.t('信箱', 'Inbox'),
      settingsX - inboxW - 6,
      top + 3,
      inboxW,
      Math.max(44, bar - 6),
      this.n,
    ).fontSize = this.font(26, 13);
    const noticeX = settingsX - inboxW * 2 - 12;
    if (!compactHeader)
      this.h.button(
        'qm:announcements',
        this.t('公告', 'News'),
        noticeX,
        top + 3,
        inboxW,
        Math.max(44, bar - 6),
        this.n,
      ).fontSize = this.font(26, 13);
    const coinX =
      (compactHeader ? settingsX - inboxW - 6 : noticeX) - coinW - 8;
    this.label(
      '● ' + (q.assets?.coins.toLocaleString() ?? '—'),
      coinX,
      top + 8,
      coinW,
      bar - 16,
      30,
      gold,
    );
    if (q.unread) {
      this.g.fillColor = color('#ec4940');
      this.g.circle(settingsX - 11 - w / 2, h / 2 - top - 10, 4);
      this.g.fill();
    }
    const titleW = w * (this.low ? 0.29 : 0.33),
      titleY = top + bar + h * 0.045;
    this.picture('title', l + 8, titleY, titleW, titleW / 3, false);
    const bw = w * 0.3,
      bh = Math.max(46, h * 0.133),
      by = Math.max(titleY + titleW / 3 + 8, h * 0.405);
    for (const [i, id] of ['loadout', 'modes'].entries()) {
      const yy = by + i * (bh + Math.max(5, h * 0.014)),
        label = this.button(
          'qm:' + id,
          this.t(
            i ? '开始行动  ›' : '作战配置  ›',
            i ? 'DEPLOY  ›' : 'LOADOUT  ›',
          ),
          l + 8,
          yy,
          bw,
          bh,
          i ? 'gold' : 'blue',
        );
      if (!this.narrow) {
        this.picture(
          i ? 'homeCompass' : 'homeCrate',
          l + bw * 0.07,
          yy + bh * 0.18,
          bw * 0.22,
          bh * 0.64,
          false,
        );
        label.fontSize = this.font(49, 16);
        label.node.setPosition(l + 8 + bw * 0.64 - w / 2, h / 2 - yy - bh / 2);
        label.node
          .getComponent(UITransform)!
          .setContentSize(bw * 0.65, bh - 10);
      }
    }
    const fw = Math.max(128, w * 0.175),
      fy = h * 0.452,
      fh = Math.max(44, h * 0.11);
    this.panel(r - fw, fy, fw, fh * 2 + 6);
    this.button(
      'qm:checkin',
      this.t('每日签到', 'CHECK IN'),
      r - fw + 5,
      fy + 3,
      fw - 10,
      fh,
    );
    this.button(
      'qm:supply',
      this.t('礼包·抽奖', 'SUPPLY & WHEEL'),
      r - fw + 5,
      fy + fh + 3,
      fw - 10,
      fh,
    );
    const cy = h * 0.755,
      ch = Math.min(h * 0.19, this.bottom - cy),
      gap = Math.max(6, w * 0.009),
      cw = (r - l - gap * 3) / 4;
    for (const [i, [id, key, zh, en]] of [
      ['shop', 'homeShop', '军需商店', 'ARMORY'],
      ['growth', 'homeGrowth', '等级成长', 'GROWTH'],
      ['tasks', 'homeTasks', '任务', 'TASKS'],
      ['warehouse', 'homeWarehouse', '我的仓库', 'WAREHOUSE'],
    ].entries()) {
      const x = l + i * (cw + gap);
      this.panel(x, cy, cw, ch);
      this.picture(key, x + 4, cy + 4, cw - 8, Math.max(12, ch - 24));
      this.h.gradient(
        this.g,
        x + 4,
        cy + ch * 0.55,
        cw - 8,
        ch * 0.42,
        '#020a1200',
        '#020a12ef',
      );
      const label = this.h.button(
        'qm:' + id,
        zh,
        x,
        cy,
        cw,
        Math.max(44, ch),
        this.n,
      );
      label.string = this.t(zh, en);
      label.fontSize = this.font(31, 13);
      label.isBold = true;
      label.node.setPosition(
        x + cw / 2 - w / 2,
        h / 2 - cy - ch + Math.min(21, ch * 0.28),
      );
      label.node
        .getComponent(UITransform)!
        .setContentSize(cw - 8, Math.min(40, ch * 0.45));
      if (this.h.lang === 'zh' && this.a.font) {
        label.font = this.a.font;
        label.useSystemFont = false;
      }
    }
  }
  private modes() {
    const q = this.q,
      l = this.left,
      r = this.right,
      y = this.top + Math.max(12, this.height * 0.04),
      bottom = this.bottom - 58,
      gap = Math.max(8, this.w * 0.016),
      cw = (r - l - gap * 2) / 3,
      ch = bottom - y - 12;
    for (const [i, [id, zh, en, desc, edesc]] of [
      ['solo', '单人任务', 'SOLO', '护送车队 · 推进关卡', 'Escort · Campaign'],
      [
        'duel',
        '双人对战',
        'DUEL',
        '好友房间 · 在线匹配',
        'Friends · Matchmaking',
      ],
      [
        'training',
        '火控训练',
        'TRAINING',
        '试用军需 · 熟悉操作',
        'Try gear · Practice',
      ],
    ].entries()) {
      const x = l + i * (cw + gap),
        selected = q.actionMode === id;
      this.panel(x, y, cw, ch, selected);
      this.picture(id, x + 5, y + 5, cw - 10, ch * 0.73);
      this.h.gradient(
        this.g,
        x + 5,
        y + ch * 0.65,
        cw - 10,
        ch * 0.33,
        '#06111b88',
        '#06111bf5',
      );
      const b = this.h.button('mode:' + id, '', x, y, cw, ch, this.n);
      b.string = this.t(zh, en);
      b.fontSize = this.font(52, 19);
      b.isBold = true;
      b.node.setPosition(
        x + cw / 2 - this.w / 2,
        this.height / 2 - y - ch * 0.82,
      );
      b.node
        .getComponent(UITransform)!
        .setContentSize(cw - 12, Math.max(28, ch * 0.14));
      if (this.h.lang === 'zh' && this.a.font) {
        b.font = this.a.font;
        b.useSystemFont = false;
      }
      this.label(
        this.t(desc, edesc),
        x + 6,
        y + ch * 0.9,
        cw - 12,
        ch * 0.08,
        27,
        dim,
      );
    }
    const summary =
      q.assets?.loadouts[q.actionMode === 'duel' ? 'duel' : 'solo'];
    const equipped = summary
      ? [...summary.mods, summary.weapon, summary.skill]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .map((id) => this.catalogName(id!))
          .join(' · ')
      : this.t('基础方案', 'Basic loadout');
    const bw = Math.max(168, (r - l) * 0.27);
    this.button(
      'qm:loadout',
      this.t('作战配置', 'Combat loadout') + '  ›',
      l,
      this.bottom - 48,
      this.narrow ? 144 : 190,
      44,
    );
    if (!this.narrow)
      this.label(
        equipped,
        l + 198,
        this.bottom - 48,
        r - l - bw - 210,
        44,
        22,
        dim,
      );
    this.button(
      'mode:enter',
      this.t(
        q.actionMode === 'solo'
          ? '进入选关'
          : q.actionMode === 'duel'
            ? '进入大厅'
            : '开始训练',
        q.actionMode === 'solo'
          ? 'MISSION MAP'
          : q.actionMode === 'duel'
            ? 'ENTER LOBBY'
            : 'START TRAINING',
      ),
      r - bw,
      this.bottom - 48,
      bw,
      48,
      'gold',
    );
  }
  private catalogName(id: string) {
    const p = this.q.catalog.find((p) => p.id === id);
    return p ? this.productName(p) : id;
  }
  private productDetail(
    p: Product,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number,
    shop: boolean,
  ) {
    const details =
      (this.q.assets?.level ?? 1) < p.unlockLevel
        ? this.t(
            `Lv.${p.unlockLevel}解锁。${p.description}`,
            `Unlock at Lv.${p.unlockLevel}.`,
          )
        : this.h.lang === 'zh'
          ? p.description
          : {
              power: 'Regular weapon damage +15%.',
              rate: 'Regular attack interval −12%.',
              burst:
                'One ammo and heat charge: primary 100%, delayed secondary 60%. Secondary fire lasts 15s.',
              twin: 'One ammo: primary 100%, side round 60%; with burst, three secondaries at 60% (280% total). Secondary fire lasts 15s.',
              radius: 'HE / incendiary blast radius +15%.',
              cooling: 'Weapon heat −15%. Skill cooldown is unchanged.',
              drone: 'Limited lifetime and damage budget. Slows enemies.',
              laser:
                'Tracks one visible target for 3s, 80 damage/s. 8s cooldown, 6 shots.',
              tracking: 'Tracks for 60s; then 90s cooldown. Repeatable.',
              emp: 'Ground enemies stop for 4s; then 45s cooldown.',
            }[p.effect];
    if (this.low) {
      this.panel(x, y, w, h);
      const iw = w * 0.31,
        tx = x + iw + 12,
        tw = w - iw - 24;
      this.picture(this.productArt(p.effect), x + 6, y + 6, iw - 6, h - 12);
      this.label(
        this.productName(p) + ' · ' + this.term(p.term),
        tx,
        y + 4,
        tw,
        24,
        32,
        ivory,
        true,
        true,
      );
      this.label(
        details,
        tx,
        y + 29,
        tw,
        Math.max(28, h - 108),
        23,
        dim,
        false,
        true,
      );
      this.label(
        this.q.assets?.permanent.includes(p.effect)
          ? this.t('已永久拥有', 'Permanently owned')
          : `● ${p.price.toLocaleString()}`,
        tx,
        y + h - 76,
        tw,
        24,
        29,
        gold,
      );
      const bw = (tw - 8) / 2;
      this.button(
        shop ? 'qm:buy' : 'qm:equip',
        this.t(
          shop ? '购买1件' : '装备 / 取消',
          shop ? 'BUY 1' : 'EQUIP / REMOVE',
        ),
        tx,
        y + h - 48,
        bw,
        44,
        'gold',
        this.q.busy ||
          !this.q.assets ||
          !!(
            shop &&
            (this.q.assets.level < p.unlockLevel ||
              (p.term === 'permanent' &&
                this.q.assets.permanent.includes(p.effect)))
          ),
      );
      this.button(
        'qm:trial',
        this.t('训练试用', 'TRY'),
        tx + bw + 8,
        y + h - 48,
        bw,
        44,
      );
      return;
    }
    this.panel(x, y, w, h);
    this.picture(this.productArt(p.effect), x + 7, y + 7, w - 14, h * 0.47);
    this.h.gradient(
      this.g,
      x + 7,
      y + h * 0.22,
      w - 14,
      h * 0.26,
      '#06131b00',
      '#06131bf2',
    );
    this.label(
      this.productName(p),
      x + 16,
      y + 10,
      w - 32,
      Math.max(30, h * 0.1),
      46,
      ivory,
      true,
      true,
    );
    this.label(
      `${this.term(p.term)} · ${this.t(rarity[p.rarity], rarityEn[p.rarity])}`,
      x + 16,
      y + h * 0.42,
      w - 32,
      26,
      23,
      rarityColor[p.rarity],
      false,
      true,
    );
    this.label(
      details,
      x + 16,
      y + h * 0.51,
      w - 32,
      this.low ? 42 : 70,
      27,
      ivory,
      false,
      true,
    );
    const owned = this.q.assets?.permanent.includes(p.effect),
      price = shop
        ? `● ${p.price.toLocaleString()}`
        : this.t(
            `可用库存 ${this.q.assets?.available[p.id] ?? 0}`,
            `Available ${this.q.assets?.available[p.id] ?? 0}`,
          );
    this.label(
      owned ? this.t('已永久拥有', 'Permanently owned') : price,
      x + 16,
      y + h * 0.7,
      w - 32,
      Math.max(25, h * 0.09),
      38,
      gold,
      true,
    );
    const by = y + h - 54,
      bw = (w - 36) / 2;
    this.button(
      shop ? 'qm:buy' : 'qm:equip',
      this.t(
        shop ? '购买1件' : '装备 / 取消',
        shop ? 'BUY 1' : 'EQUIP / REMOVE',
      ),
      x + 12,
      by,
      bw,
      44,
      'gold',
      this.q.busy ||
        !this.q.assets ||
        !!(
          shop &&
          ((this.q.assets?.level ?? 1) < p.unlockLevel ||
            (p.term === 'permanent' && owned))
        ),
    );
    this.button(
      'qm:trial',
      this.t('训练试用', 'TRY IN TRAINING'),
      x + 24 + bw,
      by,
      bw,
      44,
      'panel',
      this.q.busy,
    );
  }
  private shop() {
    const q = this.q,
      l = this.left,
      r = this.right,
      y = this.top,
      leftW = this.narrow ? r - l : (r - l) * 0.57,
      tabW = leftW / 3;
    (['match', '24h', 'permanent'] as const).forEach((term, i) =>
      this.tab(
        'term:' + term,
        this.term(term),
        l + i * tabW,
        y,
        tabW - 4,
        q.term === term,
      ),
    );
    const products = q.catalog.filter((p) => p.term === q.term),
      current = products.find((p) => p.id === q.selected) || products[0];
    if (!current) return;
    const top = y + 52,
      bottom = this.bottom,
      cols = 2,
      cw = (leftW - 20) / 2,
      ch = this.low ? 138 : 200;
    if (this.narrow && q.detail) {
      this.productDetail(current, l, top, r - l, bottom - top - 50, true);
      this.button(
        'qm:list',
        this.t('商品列表', 'ITEM LIST'),
        l,
        bottom - 46,
        144,
      );
      return;
    }
    this.list(
      'shop:' + q.term,
      l,
      top,
      leftW,
      bottom - top,
      Math.ceil(products.length / cols) * (ch + 10),
      (offset) =>
        products.forEach((p, i) => {
          const x = l + (i % 2) * (cw + 10),
            yy = top + Math.floor(i / 2) * (ch + 10) - offset;
          if (yy + ch < top || yy > bottom) return;
          const locked = (q.assets?.level ?? 1) < p.unlockLevel;
          this.panel(x, yy, cw, ch, p.id === q.selected);
          this.picture(
            this.productArt(p.effect),
            x + 5,
            yy + 5,
            cw - 10,
            ch * 0.6,
          );
          this.h.gradient(
            this.g,
            x + 5,
            yy + ch * 0.45,
            cw - 10,
            ch * 0.51,
            '#06121a22',
            '#06121aff',
          );
          const b = this.h.button('product:' + p.id, '', x, yy, cw, ch, this.n);
          b.string = this.productName(p);
          b.fontSize = this.font(32, 14);
          b.node.setPosition(
            x + cw / 2 - this.w / 2,
            this.height / 2 - yy - ch * 0.64,
          );
          b.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(cw - 14, 26);
          this.label(
            this.brief(p),
            x + 8,
            yy + ch * 0.74,
            cw - 16,
            ch * 0.12,
            22,
            dim,
          );
          this.label(
            locked
              ? this.t(`Lv.${p.unlockLevel} 解锁`, `UNLOCK Lv.${p.unlockLevel}`)
              : `● ${p.price.toLocaleString()}`,
            x + 8,
            yy + ch * 0.87,
            cw - 16,
            ch * 0.12,
            23,
            locked ? dim : gold,
          );
        }),
    );
    if (!this.narrow)
      this.productDetail(
        current,
        l + leftW + 14,
        top,
        r - l - leftW - 14,
        bottom - top,
        true,
      );
  }
  private loadout() {
    const q = this.q,
      l = this.left,
      r = this.right,
      y = this.top,
      load = q.loadout,
      width = r - l;
    const tabW = Math.min(180, width * 0.22);
    this.tab(
      'loadout:solo',
      this.t('单人方案', 'SOLO') + (q.dirtyModes.includes('solo') ? ' •' : ''),
      l,
      y,
      tabW,
      q.mode === 'solo',
    );
    this.tab(
      'loadout:duel',
      this.t('双人方案', 'DUEL') + (q.dirtyModes.includes('duel') ? ' •' : ''),
      l + tabW + 6,
      y,
      tabW,
      q.mode === 'duel',
    );
    this.label(
      this.t(
        q.dirty ? '有未保存的修改' : '当前已保存方案',
        q.dirty ? 'UNSAVED CHANGES' : 'SAVED LOADOUT',
      ),
      l + tabW * 2 + 16,
      y,
      width - tabW * 2 - 16,
      44,
      23,
      q.dirty ? gold : dim,
      false,
      true,
    );
    const top = y + 52,
      bottom = this.bottom - 52,
      lw = width * (this.narrow ? 0.47 : 0.55),
      rx = l + lw + 14,
      rw = r - rx;
    const selected = q.slotCategory,
      selectedName = this.t(
        selected === 'mod'
          ? '武器增益'
          : selected === 'weapon'
            ? '特种武器'
            : '主动技能',
        selected === 'mod'
          ? 'WEAPON MOD'
          : selected === 'weapon'
            ? 'SPECIAL WEAPON'
            : 'ACTIVE SKILL',
      );
    const heading = 20,
      gh = (bottom - top - 8) / 2,
      cw = (lw - 8) / 2;
    const slots = [
      ['mod:0', q.modSlots[0]],
      ['mod:1', q.modSlots[1]],
      ['weapon', load.weapon],
      ['skill', load.skill],
    ] as const;
    slots.forEach(([slot, id], i) => {
      const x = l + (i % 2) * (cw + 8),
        yy = top + Math.floor(i / 2) * (gh + 8),
        p = q.catalog.find((p) => p.id === id),
        hh = gh - heading;
      const type = this.t(
        i < 2 ? `武器增益 ${i + 1}` : i === 2 ? '特种武器' : '主动技能',
        i < 2 ? `MOD ${i + 1}` : i === 2 ? 'WEAPON' : 'SKILL',
      );
      this.label(
        type,
        x,
        yy,
        cw,
        heading,
        25,
        q.slot === slot ? cyan : gold,
        true,
        true,
      );
      this.panel(x, yy + heading, cw, hh, q.slot === slot);
      if (p) {
        this.picture(
          this.productArt(p.effect),
          x + 5,
          yy + heading + 5,
          cw - 10,
          hh - 10,
        );
        this.h.gradient(
          this.g,
          x + 5,
          yy + heading + hh * 0.45,
          cw - 10,
          hh * 0.5,
          '#07131b33',
          '#07131bf2',
        );
      }
      const label = this.h.button(
        'slot:' + slot,
        p ? this.productName(p) : this.t('＋ 选择装配', '＋ Choose'),
        x,
        yy + heading,
        cw,
        Math.max(44, hh),
        this.n,
      );
      label.fontSize = this.font(30, 12);
      label.enableWrapText = false;
      label.overflow = Label.Overflow.SHRINK;
      label.node.setPosition(
        x + cw / 2 - this.w / 2,
        this.height / 2 - yy - heading - hh + 16,
      );
      label.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(cw - 12, 28);
      if (p) this.infoButton(p.id, x + cw - 46, yy + heading + 2);
      if (q.slot === slot)
        this.label('✓', x + cw - 28, yy + heading + 4, 22, 22, 25, cyan, true);
      if (q.drag?.target === slot)
        this.h.rect(
          this.g,
          x + 2,
          yy + heading + 2,
          cw - 4,
          hh - 4,
          '#78e3ff22',
          cyan,
          4,
        );
    });
    const preview = attackPreview(
        [...load.mods, load.weapon, load.skill].map(
          (id) => q.catalog.find((p) => p.id === id)?.effect || '',
        ),
      ),
      previewH = this.low ? 48 : 74;
    this.panel(rx, top, rw, previewH);
    const title = this.label(
      this.t(
        `每轮 ${preview.shots} 弹 · 只耗 1 发`,
        `PROJECTILES ${preview.shots} · AMMO 1`,
      ),
      rx + 10,
      top + 5,
      rw - 20,
      previewH * 0.46,
      38,
      ivory,
      true,
    );
    title.enableWrapText = false;
    title.overflow = Label.Overflow.SHRINK;
    const detail = this.label(
      this.t(
        `主弹100%${preview.shots > 1 ? `＋${preview.shots - 1}×${preview.secondaryStrength * 100}%` : ''} · 热量×${preview.heatMultiplier}`,
        `Primary 100%${preview.shots > 1 ? ` + ${preview.shots - 1}×${preview.secondaryStrength * 100}%` : ''} · heat ×${preview.heatMultiplier}`,
      ),
      rx + 10,
      top + previewH * 0.52,
      rw - 20,
      previewH * 0.36,
      23,
      dim,
    );
    detail.enableWrapText = false;
    detail.overflow = Label.Overflow.SHRINK;
    const listTop = top + previewH + 25,
      listH = Math.max(44, bottom - listTop),
      rowH = this.low ? 64 : 104;
    this.label(
      selectedName + this.t(' · 上下滑动选择', ' · SCROLL TO CHOOSE'),
      rx,
      top + previewH + 4,
      rw,
      20,
      23,
      cyan,
      false,
      true,
    );
    q.loadoutArea = { x: rx, y: listTop, w: rw, h: listH };
    q.loadoutScrollMax = Math.max(
      0,
      q.availableGear.length * (rowH + 7) - 7 - listH,
    );
    q.loadoutScroll = Math.min(q.loadoutScroll, q.loadoutScrollMax);
    const parent = this.n,
      surfaces = this.surfaces,
      g = this.g,
      viewport = this.h.node('LoadoutViewport', parent),
      maskX = rx + rw / 2 - this.w / 2,
      maskY = this.height / 2 - listTop - listH / 2;
    viewport.setPosition(maskX, maskY);
    viewport.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(rw, listH);
    viewport.addComponent(Mask).type = Mask.Type.GRAPHICS_RECT;
    const content = this.h.node('LoadoutItems', viewport);
    content.setPosition(-maskX, -maskY);
    this.n = content;
    this.surfaces = this.h.node('ItemSurfaces', content);
    this.g = this.h.node('ItemInk', content).addComponent(Graphics);
    q.availableGear.forEach((p, i) => {
      const py = listTop + i * (rowH + 7) - q.loadoutScroll;
      if (py + rowH < listTop || py > bottom) return;
      const equipped = q.equipped(p.effect);
      this.panel(rx, py, rw - 7, rowH);
      const iw = this.narrow ? 42 : Math.min(100, rw * 0.23);
      this.picture(this.productArt(p.effect), rx + 5, py + 5, iw, rowH - 10);
      const tx = rx + iw + 12,
        tw = rw - iw - 82;
      this.label(
        this.productName(p),
        tx,
        py + 6,
        tw,
        Math.max(21, rowH * 0.34),
        30,
        equipped ? dim : ivory,
        true,
        true,
      ).overflow = Label.Overflow.SHRINK;
      this.label(
        this.brief(p) + ' · ' + this.term(p.term),
        tx,
        py + rowH * 0.44,
        tw,
        rowH * 0.42,
        20,
        gold,
        false,
        true,
      ).overflow = Label.Overflow.SHRINK;
      this.button(
        'equip:' + p.id,
        this.t(equipped ? '已装配' : '装配', equipped ? 'USED' : 'EQUIP'),
        rx + rw - 72,
        py + (rowH - 44) / 2,
        62,
        44,
        equipped ? 'panel' : 'blue',
        q.busy || !!q.pending || equipped,
      );
      const button = this.h.buttons.at(-1)!;
      button.clip = q.loadoutArea;
      // The whole card is draggable; the button remains the explicit tap action.
      const card = this.h.button(
        'gear:' + p.id,
        '',
        rx,
        py,
        rw - 78,
        rowH,
        this.n,
      );
      const hit = this.h.buttons.at(-1)!;
      hit.disabled = equipped || q.busy || !!q.pending;
      hit.clip = q.loadoutArea;
    });
    this.n = parent;
    this.surfaces = surfaces;
    this.g = g;
    if (!q.availableGear.length)
      this.label(
        this.t(
          '此插槽暂无可用装配\n可前往商店或领取礼包',
          'No compatible gear\nVisit the armory or claim a crate',
        ),
        rx + 8,
        listTop + 8,
        rw - 16,
        listH - 16,
        25,
        dim,
      );
    if (q.loadoutScrollMax) {
      this.h.rect(this.g, r - 4, listTop, 3, listH, '#162a38');
      this.h.rect(
        this.g,
        r - 4,
        listTop + ((listH - 28) * q.loadoutScroll) / q.loadoutScrollMax,
        3,
        28,
        cyan,
      );
    }
    const fy = this.bottom - 48,
      unloadW = Math.min(132, lw * 0.42);
    this.button(
      'qm:unequip',
      this.t('卸载所选', 'UNEQUIP'),
      l,
      fy,
      unloadW,
      44,
      'panel',
      !q.slotItem || q.busy || !!q.pending,
    );
    this.button(
      'qm:shop',
      this.t('商店', 'ARMORY'),
      l + unloadW + 8,
      fy,
      Math.min(100, lw - unloadW - 8),
      44,
      'blue',
    );
    this.button(
      'qm:save',
      this.t(
        q.busy ? '保存中…' : '确定保存',
        q.busy ? 'SAVING…' : 'CONFIRM SAVE',
      ),
      rx,
      fy,
      rw,
      44,
      'gold',
      q.busy || !q.dirty || !!q.pending,
    );
    if (q.drag) {
      const p = q.catalog.find((p) => p.id === q.drag!.item);
      this.panel(q.drag.x - 60, q.drag.y - 25, 120, 50, true);
      this.label(
        p ? this.productName(p) : '',
        q.drag.x - 56,
        q.drag.y - 20,
        112,
        40,
        26,
        cyan,
        true,
      );
    }
  }
  private supply() {
    const q = this.q,
      l = this.left,
      r = this.right,
      y = this.top,
      b = this.bottom,
      gap = 12,
      lw = (r - l - gap) * 0.53,
      rx = l + lw + gap,
      rw = r - rx,
      h = b - y;
    this.panel(l, y, lw, h);
    this.panel(rx, y, rw, h);
    this.label(
      this.t('统一补给箱', 'SUPPLY CRATE'),
      l + 12,
      y + 3,
      lw - 65,
      32,
      45,
      ivory,
      true,
      true,
    );
    this.infoButton('pool', l + lw - 50, y);
    this.label(
      this.t('幸运转盘', 'LUCKY WHEEL'),
      rx + 12,
      y + 3,
      rw - 62,
      32,
      42,
      ivory,
      true,
      true,
    );
    this.infoButton('wheel', r - 50, y);
    const by = b - 54,
      packY = by - (this.low ? 80 : 138),
      heroY = y + 35,
      heroH = Math.max(52, packY - heroY - 6);
    this.picture('closedCrate', l + lw * 0.08, heroY, lw * 0.84, heroH, false);
    const pw = (lw - 24) / 5;
    q.packages.forEach((p, i) =>
      this.tab(
        'package:' + p.quantity,
        `${p.quantity}个`,
        l + 10 + i * pw,
        packY,
        pw - 3,
        q.quantity === p.quantity,
      ),
    );
    const quote = q.packages.find((p) => p.quantity === q.quantity)!,
      order = q.pendingOrder,
      cw = (lw - 28) / 2,
      cy = packY + 48;
    if (!this.low) {
      this.panel(l + 8, cy, cw, b - cy - 8);
      this.panel(l + 20 + cw, cy, cw, b - cy - 8);
    }
    this.label(
      `● ${quote.coins}`,
      l + 12,
      cy,
      cw - 4,
      this.low ? 24 : 34,
      37,
      gold,
      true,
    );
    this.label(
      this.t(
        `视频 ${order?.completed ?? 0}/${order?.required ?? quote.videos}`,
        `VIDEO ${order?.completed ?? 0}/${order?.required ?? quote.videos}`,
      ),
      l + 20 + cw,
      cy,
      cw,
      this.low ? 24 : 34,
      30,
      ivory,
      true,
    );
    if (!this.low) {
      this.label(
        this.t('单场物资 · 随等级解锁', 'ONE MATCH · LEVEL UNLOCKS'),
        l + 12,
        cy + 35,
        lw - 24,
        24,
        23,
        dim,
      );
    }
    this.button(
      'qm:buyPacks',
      this.t(`购买${quote.quantity}个`, `BUY ${quote.quantity}`),
      l + 10,
      by,
      cw,
      44,
      'gold',
      q.busy || !q.assets || !!q.pending || (!!q.wheel && !q.wheel.shown),
    );
    this.button(
      'qm:watch',
      q.viewing
        ? this.t('观看中', 'WATCHING')
        : this.t(order ? '继续观看' : '观看视频', order ? 'CONTINUE' : 'WATCH'),
      l + 20 + cw,
      by,
      cw,
      44,
      'blue',
      q.busy ||
        q.viewing ||
        !q.assets ||
        !!q.pending ||
        (!!q.wheel && !q.wheel.shown),
    );
    const diameter = Math.min(rw - 20, by - 37 - (y + 35)),
      cx = rx + rw / 2,
      cyWheel = y + 35 + diameter / 2;
    const wheel = this.h.node('LuckyWheel', this.n);
    wheel.setPosition(cx - this.w / 2, this.height / 2 - cyWheel);
    const frame = this.a.get('wheel');
    if (frame) {
      const sprite = wheel.addComponent(Sprite);
      sprite.spriteFrame = frame;
      sprite.sizeMode = Sprite.SizeMode.CUSTOM;
      wheel.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(diameter, diameter);
    }
    const motion = q.wheel,
      end = motion ? wheelLandingAngle(motion.index, motion.startAngle) : 0,
      elapsed = motion
        ? Math.min(1, (Date.now() - motion.started) / motion.duration)
        : 1;
    wheel.angle = motion
      ? motion.startAngle +
        (end - motion.startAngle) * (1 - Math.pow(1 - elapsed, 4))
      : 0;
    const prizes = q.assets?.wheelPrizes || WHEEL_PRIZES;
    prizes.forEach((p, i) => {
      const angle = ((30 + i * 60) * Math.PI) / 180,
        rr = diameter * 0.32,
        xx = Math.sin(angle) * rr,
        yy = Math.cos(angle) * rr;
      const name = p.coins
        ? this.t(`${p.coins}币`, `${p.coins}`)
        : this.t(rarity[p.tier] + '箱', rarityEn[p.tier]);
      const label = this.h.label(
        wheel,
        `${name}\n${p.weight}%`,
        this.w / 2 + xx,
        this.height / 2 - yy,
        Math.max(10, diameter * 0.052),
        diameter * 0.26,
        diameter * 0.16,
        p.tier >= 0 ? rarityColor[p.tier] : gold,
      );
      label.node.name = 'WheelPrize:' + i;
      label.node.angle = -wheel.angle;
      label.isBold = true;
      label.lineHeight = label.fontSize * 1.08;
      label.overflow = Label.Overflow.SHRINK;
      if (motion && elapsed < 1 && !this.h.reducedEffects)
        tween(label.node)
          .to(
            (motion.duration / 1000) * (1 - elapsed),
            { angle: -end },
            { easing: 'quartOut' },
          )
          .start();
    });
    if (motion && elapsed < 1 && !this.h.reducedEffects)
      tween(wheel)
        .to(
          (motion.duration / 1000) * (1 - elapsed),
          { angle: end },
          { easing: 'quartOut' },
        )
        .start();
    this.label(
      '▼',
      cx - 16,
      cyWheel - diameter / 2 - 7,
      32,
      32,
      42,
      gold,
      true,
    );
    this.label(
      this.t(
        `剩余次数 ${q.assets?.wheelTickets ?? 0} · 今日视频 ${q.assets?.wheelVideosToday ?? 0}/${q.assets?.wheelDailyLimit ?? 5}`,
        `SPINS ${q.assets?.wheelTickets ?? 0} · VIDEOS ${q.assets?.wheelVideosToday ?? 0}/${q.assets?.wheelDailyLimit ?? 5}`,
      ),
      rx + 8,
      by - 34,
      rw - 16,
      30,
      26,
      gold,
      true,
    );
    const spinning = !!motion && !motion.shown,
      bw = (rw - 26) / 2;
    this.button(
      spinning ? 'qm:wheelSkip' : 'qm:wheelSpin',
      this.t(spinning ? '跳过动画' : '抽一次', spinning ? 'SKIP' : 'SPIN'),
      rx + 9,
      by,
      bw,
      44,
      'gold',
      q.busy || (!spinning && !q.assets?.wheelTickets) || !!q.pending,
    );
    this.button(
      'qm:wheelWatch',
      this.t('视频+1次', 'VIDEO +1'),
      rx + 17 + bw,
      by,
      bw,
      44,
      'blue',
      q.busy ||
        q.viewing ||
        spinning ||
        !q.assets ||
        !!q.pending ||
        (q.assets.wheelVideosToday ?? 0) >= (q.assets.wheelDailyLimit ?? 5) ||
        q.assets.videosToday >= q.assets.videoDailyLimit,
    );
  }
  private growth() {
    const q = this.q,
      l = this.left,
      r = this.right,
      y = this.top,
      b = this.bottom,
      lw = (r - l) * 0.27,
      rx = l + lw + 14,
      rw = r - rx,
      h = b - y;
    this.panel(l, y, lw, h);
    this.picture('emblem', l + 8, y + 6, lw - 16, h * 0.28);
    this.label(
      `Lv.${q.assets?.level ?? 1}`,
      l + 8,
      y + h * 0.29,
      lw - 16,
      30,
      48,
      gold,
      true,
    );
    this.bar(
      l + 14,
      y + h * 0.43,
      lw - 28,
      10,
      (q.assets?.current ?? 0) / (q.assets?.required || 200),
    );
    this.label(
      `EXP ${q.assets?.current ?? 0} / ${q.assets?.required ?? 200}`,
      l + 8,
      y + h * 0.48,
      lw - 16,
      25,
      24,
      dim,
    );
    const milestones = [
      [3, '基础强化', 'BASE STRENGTH'],
      [6, '追踪资格', 'TRACKING'],
      [10, '电磁干扰', 'EMP'],
      [15, '火控精修', 'PRECISION'],
    ] as const;
    milestones.forEach(([level, zh, en], i) =>
      this.label(
        `${(q.assets?.level ?? 1) >= level ? '✓' : '○'} Lv.${level} ${this.t(zh, en)}`,
        l + 8,
        y + h * 0.61 + i * h * 0.085,
        lw - 16,
        Math.max(18, h * 0.08),
        25,
        (q.assets?.level ?? 1) >= level ? gold : dim,
        false,
        true,
      ),
    );
    const tw = Math.min(185, (rw - 6) / 2);
    this.tab(
      'growth:base',
      this.t('成长加成', 'GROWTH'),
      rx,
      y,
      tw,
      q.section !== 'skills',
    );
    this.tab(
      'growth:skills',
      this.t('主动技能', 'SKILLS'),
      rx + tw + 6,
      y,
      tw,
      q.section === 'skills',
    );
    const top = y + 52,
      bottom = b - 54;
    if (q.section !== 'skills') {
      const level = q.assets?.level ?? 1,
        next = milestones.find((m) => m[0] > level);
      this.panel(rx, top, rw, 52, true);
      this.label(
        this.t(
          `基础威力 +${level >= 3 ? 5 : 0}%`,
          `BASE DAMAGE +${level >= 3 ? 5 : 0}%`,
        ),
        rx + 12,
        top + 3,
        rw - 24,
        25,
        34,
        cyan,
        true,
        true,
      );
      this.label(
        next
          ? this.t(
              `下一成长 Lv.${next[0]} · ${next[0] === 3 ? '基础威力提升5%' : next[0] === 15 ? '攻击间隔缩短5%' : next[1]}`,
              `NEXT Lv.${next[0]} · ${next[2]}`,
            )
          : this.t(
              '火控精修已生效 · 攻击间隔缩短5%',
              'PRECISION ACTIVE · Attack interval −5%',
            ),
        rx + 12,
        top + 28,
        rw - 24,
        21,
        22,
        dim,
        false,
        true,
      );
      const listTop = top + 60,
        rowH = this.low ? 68 : 90;
      this.list(
        'growth',
        rx,
        listTop,
        rw,
        bottom - listTop,
        rowH * 4,
        (offset) =>
          milestones.forEach(([level, zh, en], i) => {
            const yy = listTop + i * rowH - offset;
            this.panel(
              rx,
              yy,
              rw - 10,
              rowH - 6,
              (q.assets?.level ?? 1) >= level,
            );
            const desc =
              i === 0
                ? this.t('普通武器伤害 +5%', 'Regular weapon damage +5%')
                : i === 3
                  ? this.t('攻击间隔缩短5%', 'Attack interval −5%')
                  : this.t('永久解锁主动技能', 'Permanent active skill');
            this.picture(
              i === 1
                ? 'tracking'
                : i === 2
                  ? 'emp'
                  : i === 0
                    ? 'powerGear'
                    : 'coolingGear',
              rx + 8,
              yy + 8,
              48,
              rowH - 16,
              false,
            );
            this.label(
              `Lv.${level}  ${this.t(zh, en)}`,
              rx + 64,
              yy + 6,
              rw * 0.57 - 52,
              24,
              32,
              ivory,
              true,
              true,
            );
            this.label(
              desc,
              rx + 64,
              yy + 30,
              rw * 0.7 - 52,
              rowH - 36,
              23,
              dim,
              false,
              true,
            );
            this.label(
              (q.assets?.level ?? 1) >= level
                ? this.t('✓ 已生效', '✓ ACTIVE')
                : this.t('未解锁', 'LOCKED'),
              rx + rw * 0.72,
              yy + 6,
              rw * 0.24,
              rowH - 18,
              25,
              (q.assets?.level ?? 1) >= level ? gold : dim,
            );
          }),
      );
    } else {
      const cw = (rw - 10) / 2,
        ch = bottom - top;
      (['tracking', 'emp'] as const).forEach((effect, i) => {
        const x = rx + i * (cw + 10),
          skill = SKILLS[effect],
          owned = q.assets?.permanent.includes(effect);
        this.panel(
          x,
          top,
          cw,
          ch,
          owned && q.loadout.skill?.startsWith(effect),
        );
        this.picture(effect, x + 5, top + 5, cw - 10, ch * 0.58);
        this.h.gradient(
          this.g,
          x + 5,
          top + ch * 0.4,
          cw - 10,
          ch * 0.2,
          '#07131b00',
          '#07131bff',
        );
        this.label(
          this.t(
            effect === 'tracking' ? '追踪' : '电磁干扰',
            effectEnglish[effect],
          ),
          x + 10,
          top + 8,
          cw - 54,
          28,
          39,
          ivory,
          true,
          true,
        );
        this.infoButton(effect + '-permanent', x + cw - 48, top + 3);
        this.label(
          this.t(
            effect === 'tracking'
              ? '锁定目标，持续追踪射击'
              : '释放电磁波，使敌方地面单位定身',
            effect === 'tracking'
              ? 'Lock and track targets'
              : 'Immobilize enemy ground units',
          ),
          x + 10,
          bottom - 85,
          cw - 20,
          36,
          25,
          ivory,
          false,
          true,
        );
        this.label(
          this.t(
            `持续 ${skill.duration}秒 · 冷却 ${skill.cooldown}秒`,
            `ACTIVE ${skill.duration}s · CD ${skill.cooldown}s`,
          ),
          x + 8,
          bottom - 46,
          cw - 16,
          22,
          23,
          gold,
        );
        this.label(
          owned
            ? this.t('永久拥有', 'PERMANENT')
            : this.t(`Lv.${skill.level}解锁`, `UNLOCK Lv.${skill.level}`),
          x + 8,
          bottom - 24,
          cw - 16,
          21,
          22,
          owned ? cyan : dim,
        );
      });
    }
    this.button(
      'qm:loadout',
      this.t('作战配置  ›', 'LOADOUT  ›'),
      r - Math.min(220, rw),
      b - 48,
      Math.min(220, rw),
      44,
      'gold',
    );
  }
  private tasks() {
    const q = this.q,
      l = this.left,
      r = this.right,
      y = this.top;
    this.tab(
      'tasks:daily',
      this.t('每日任务', 'DAILY'),
      l,
      y,
      Math.min(230, (r - l) * 0.3),
      q.section !== 'achievements',
    );
    this.tab(
      'tasks:achievements',
      this.t('长期成就', 'ACHIEVEMENTS'),
      l + Math.min(240, (r - l) * 0.31),
      y,
      Math.min(230, (r - l) * 0.34),
      q.section === 'achievements',
    );
    const achievements = q.section === 'achievements',
      tasks = achievements
        ? q.assets?.achievements || []
        : (q.assets?.tasks || []).filter(
            (t) =>
              t.day === q.assets?.day || (!t.claimed && t.progress >= t.target),
          );
    const top = y + 53,
      bottom = this.bottom - 54,
      size = this.low ? 2 : 4,
      rowH = this.low ? 92 : 130;
    this.list(
      'tasks:' + q.section,
      l,
      top,
      r - l,
      bottom - top,
      tasks.length * rowH,
      (offset) =>
        tasks.forEach((task, i) => {
          const yy = top + i * rowH - offset;
          this.panel(l, yy, r - l, rowH - 7);
          const art =
              task.event === 'checkin'
                ? 'checkin'
                : task.event === 'open'
                  ? 'taskSupply'
                  : task.event === 'duel'
                    ? 'taskDuel'
                    : 'taskSolo',
            iw = this.narrow ? 0 : (r - l) * 0.23;
          if (iw) this.picture(art, l + 5, yy + 5, iw, rowH - 17);
          const alternate =
            !achievements &&
            task.event === 'duel' &&
            !task.claimed &&
            task.progress < task.target;
          const tx = l + iw + 12,
            buttonW = this.narrow ? 66 : Math.min(180, (r - l) * 0.15),
            bx = r - buttonW - 12,
            contentW = bx - tx - 12 - (alternate ? buttonW + 6 : 0);
          const title =
            this.h.lang === 'zh'
              ? task.name
              : task.event === 'checkin'
                ? 'Check in today'
                : task.event === 'open'
                  ? 'Open a supply crate'
                  : task.event === 'solo'
                    ? `Complete ${task.target} solo operations`
                    : task.event === 'duel'
                      ? `Complete ${task.target} duels`
                      : task.event === 'win'
                        ? `Win ${task.target} operations`
                        : `Complete ${task.target} operations`;
          this.label(
            title,
            tx,
            yy + 5,
            contentW,
            rowH * 0.32,
            32,
            ivory,
            true,
            true,
          );
          const barW = contentW * 0.65;
          this.bar(
            tx,
            yy + rowH * 0.45,
            barW,
            Math.max(7, rowH * 0.09),
            task.progress / task.target,
          );
          this.label(
            `${task.progress}/${task.target}`,
            tx + barW + 6,
            yy + rowH * 0.34,
            contentW - barW - 6,
            25,
            24,
            dim,
          );
          this.label(
            this.reward(task.reward),
            tx,
            yy + rowH * 0.63,
            contentW,
            rowH * 0.25,
            24,
            gold,
            false,
            true,
          );
          const ready = task.progress >= task.target && !task.claimed;
          this.button(
            (achievements ? 'achievement:' : 'task:') + task.id,
            this.t(
              task.claimed ? '已领取' : ready ? '领取' : '前往',
              task.claimed ? 'Claimed' : ready ? 'Claim' : 'Go',
            ),
            bx,
            yy + (rowH - 51) / 2,
            buttonW,
            44,
            ready ? 'blue' : 'panel',
            task.claimed || q.busy,
          );
          if (alternate)
            this.button(
              'taskalt:' + task.id,
              this.t('替代', 'Replace'),
              bx - buttonW - 6,
              yy + (rowH - 51) / 2,
              buttonW,
              44,
              'panel',
              q.busy,
            );
        }),
    );
    if (!tasks.length)
      this.label(
        this.t('连接后获取今日目标', 'Connect to load today’s objectives'),
        l,
        top,
        r - l,
        80,
        30,
        dim,
      );
    if (!achievements)
      this.button(
        'qm:claimAll',
        this.t('一键领取', 'CLAIM ALL'),
        r - Math.max(160, (r - l) * 0.24),
        this.bottom - 48,
        Math.max(160, (r - l) * 0.24),
        48,
        'gold',
        q.busy || !q.claimable,
      );
  }
  private inbox() {
    const q = this.q,
      l = this.left,
      r = this.right,
      y = this.top;
    this.tab(
      'inbox:mail',
      this.t('信箱', 'INBOX'),
      l,
      y,
      Math.min(200, (r - l) * 0.25),
      q.section !== 'news',
    );
    this.tab(
      'inbox:news',
      this.t('公告', 'NEWS'),
      l + Math.min(208, (r - l) * 0.26),
      y,
      Math.min(200, (r - l) * 0.25),
      q.section === 'news',
    );
    const news = q.section === 'news',
      entries = news ? q.assets?.announcements || [] : q.assets?.mail || [],
      selected = entries.find((m) => m.id === q.selectedMail);
    if (selected) q.selectedMail = selected.id;
    const top = y + 53,
      bottom = this.bottom - 54,
      lw = this.narrow ? (r - l) * 0.32 : (r - l) * 0.35,
      rx = l + lw + 14,
      rw = r - rx,
      size = this.low ? 2 : 3,
      rowH = Math.min(
        this.low ? 68 : 112,
        (bottom - top - (size - 1) * 8) / size,
      );
    this.list(
      'inbox:' + q.section,
      l,
      top,
      lw,
      bottom - top,
      entries.length * (rowH + 8),
      (offset) =>
        entries.forEach((m, i) => {
          const yy = top + i * (rowH + 8) - offset;
          this.panel(l, yy, lw, rowH, m.id === q.selectedMail);
          if (!this.narrow)
            this.picture('envelope', l + 6, yy + 6, rowH - 12, rowH - 12);
          const tx = l + (this.narrow ? 7 : rowH),
            title =
              this.h.lang === 'zh'
                ? m.title
                : m.id.startsWith('night-return')
                  ? 'Welcome / return compensation'
                  : 'Night Overwatch update';
          const b = this.h.button(
            'mail:' + m.id,
            title,
            l,
            yy,
            lw,
            rowH,
            this.n,
          );
          b.fontSize = this.font(29, 13);
          b.node.setPosition(
            tx + (lw - (tx - l)) / 2 - this.w / 2,
            this.height / 2 - yy - rowH * 0.38,
          );
          b.node
            .getComponent(UITransform)!
            .setContentSize(lw - (tx - l) - 12, rowH * 0.55);
          const mail = !news
            ? q.assets?.mail.find((x) => x.id === m.id)
            : undefined;
          this.label(
            mail
              ? this.t(
                  mail.claimed ? '已领取' : mail.read ? '附件待领取' : '未读',
                  mail.claimed
                    ? 'Claimed'
                    : mail.read
                      ? 'Attachment pending'
                      : 'Unread',
                )
              : this.t('公告', 'News'),
            tx,
            yy + rowH * 0.64,
            lw - (tx - l) - 8,
            rowH * 0.28,
            19,
            mail?.claimed ? dim : cyan,
          );
        }),
    );
    this.panel(rx, top, rw, bottom - top);
    if (!selected) {
      this.label(
        this.t(
          entries.length ? '请选择本页消息' : '暂无消息',
          entries.length ? 'Select a message on this page' : 'No messages',
        ),
        rx + 12,
        top + 30,
        rw - 24,
        60,
        35,
        dim,
      );
      return;
    }
    const title =
      this.h.lang === 'zh'
        ? selected.title
        : news
          ? 'Growth & supplies update'
          : 'Welcome / return compensation';
    this.picture(
      'mailAircraft',
      rx + 5,
      top + 5,
      rw - 10,
      (bottom - top) * 0.33,
    );
    this.h.gradient(
      this.g,
      rx + 5,
      top + 5,
      rw - 10,
      (bottom - top) * 0.33,
      '#03101c88',
      '#03101cb0',
    );
    this.label(
      title,
      rx + 18,
      top + (this.low ? 6 : 15),
      rw - 36,
      this.low ? 30 : Math.max(34, (bottom - top) * 0.14),
      45,
      ivory,
      true,
      true,
    );
    const body =
      this.h.lang === 'zh'
        ? selected.body
        : news
          ? 'Points retain their original rules. Coins and EXP are separate. Save solo and duel loadouts independently. Skills can be reused after cooldown.'
          : 'Your previous points, progress and verified assets are preserved. The attachment can be claimed once.';
    const bodyLabel = this.label(
      body,
      rx + 18,
      top + (this.low ? 38 : (bottom - top) * 0.33),
      rw - 36,
      this.low ? bottom - top - 90 : (bottom - top) * 0.19,
      25,
      ivory,
      false,
      true,
    );
    if (this.low) bodyLabel.overflow = Label.Overflow.SHRINK;
    const mail = !news
      ? q.assets?.mail.find((m) => m.id === selected.id)
      : undefined;
    if (mail && this.low) {
      this.button(
        'qm:claimMail',
        this.t(
          mail.claimed
            ? '附件已领取'
            : `领取 · ${mail.reward.coins}金币 / ${mail.reward.packs || 0}礼包`,
          mail.claimed
            ? 'CLAIMED'
            : `CLAIM · ${mail.reward.coins} COINS / ${mail.reward.packs || 0} CRATES`,
        ),
        rx + 8,
        bottom - 48,
        rw - 16,
        44,
        'gold',
        mail.claimed || q.busy,
      );
    } else if (mail) {
      const cy = top + (bottom - top) * 0.53,
        cw = (rw - 40) / 2,
        ch = (bottom - top) * 0.26;
      this.picture('coins', rx + 14, cy, cw, ch);
      this.picture('rewardSupply', rx + 26 + cw, cy, cw, ch);
      this.label(
        `● ${mail.reward.coins}   EXP ${mail.reward.xp}`,
        rx + 14,
        cy + ch - 26,
        cw,
        24,
        25,
        gold,
      );
      this.label(
        this.t(
          `礼包 ×${mail.reward.packs || 0}`,
          `CRATES ×${mail.reward.packs || 0}`,
        ),
        rx + 26 + cw,
        cy + ch - 26,
        cw,
        24,
        25,
        ivory,
      );
      this.button(
        'qm:claimMail',
        this.t(
          mail.claimed ? '已领取' : '领取附件',
          mail.claimed ? 'CLAIMED' : 'CLAIM ATTACHMENT',
        ),
        rx + rw * 0.28,
        bottom - 54,
        rw * 0.66,
        44,
        'gold',
        mail.claimed || q.busy,
      );
      if (!this.narrow)
        this.label(
          this.t(mail.read ? '已读' : '未读', mail.read ? 'Read' : 'Unread'),
          rx + 14,
          bottom - 52,
          rw * 0.22,
          40,
          21,
          dim,
        );
    }
  }
  private inventoryName(id: string) {
    const p = this.q.catalog.find((p) => p.id === id),
      pack = /^pack:(general|gold):([0-3])$/.exec(id);
    if (pack)
      return this.t(
        '补给箱 · ' + rarity[Number(pack[2])],
        'Crate · ' + rarityEn[Number(pack[2])],
      );
    return p
      ? this.productName(p) + ' · ' + this.term(p.term)
      : this.t('旧版物资', 'Legacy item');
  }
  private warehouse() {
    const q = this.q,
      l = this.left,
      r = this.right,
      top = this.top,
      bottom = this.bottom - 52,
      items = q.inventory,
      lw = (r - l) * 0.62,
      rx = l + lw + 12,
      rw = r - rx;
    if (!items.some(([id]) => id === q.item)) {
      q.item = items[0]?.[0] || '';
      q.amount = 1;
    }
    const cols = this.narrow ? 2 : 3,
      cw = (lw - 26 - (cols - 1) * 8) / cols,
      ch = this.low ? 126 : Math.max(170, (bottom - top - 12) / 2),
      rowH = ch + 8;
    this.panel(l, top, lw, bottom - top);
    this.list(
      'warehouse',
      l + 6,
      top + 6,
      lw - 12,
      bottom - top - 12,
      Math.ceil(items.length / cols) * rowH,
      (offset) =>
        items.forEach(([id, count], i) => {
          const x = l + 6 + (i % cols) * (cw + 8),
            y = top + 6 + Math.floor(i / cols) * rowH - offset;
          if (y + ch < top || y > bottom) return;
          const p = q.catalog.find((p) => p.id === id),
            pack = id.startsWith('pack:'),
            tier = pack ? Number(id.split(':')[2]) : (p?.rarity ?? 0);
          this.panel(x, y, cw, ch, id === q.item);
          this.picture(
            pack
              ? 'closedCrate'
              : p
                ? this.productArt(p.effect)
                : 'homeWarehouse',
            x + 5,
            y + 5,
            cw - 10,
            ch * 0.58,
            pack ? false : true,
          );
          const label = this.h.button(
            'inventory:' + id,
            '',
            x,
            y,
            cw,
            ch,
            this.n,
          );
          label.string =
            (p ? this.productName(p) : this.t('补给箱', 'CRATE')) +
            ` ×${count}`;
          label.fontSize = this.font(29, 13);
          label.node.setPosition(
            x + cw / 2 - this.w / 2,
            this.height / 2 - y - ch * 0.65,
          );
          label.node.getComponent(UITransform)!.setContentSize(cw - 10, 26);
          this.label(
            p
              ? this.brief(p)
              : this.t('打开获得金币或物资', 'Coins or supplies'),
            x + 6,
            y + ch * 0.75,
            cw - 12,
            ch * 0.13,
            20,
            dim,
          );
          this.label(
            this.t(rarity[tier], rarityEn[tier]) +
              (p ? ' · ' + this.term(p.term) : ''),
            x + 6,
            y + ch * 0.88,
            cw - 12,
            ch * 0.1,
            20,
            rarityColor[tier],
          );
        }),
    );
    this.panel(rx, top, rw, bottom - top);
    if (q.item) {
      const id = q.item,
        p = q.catalog.find((p) => p.id === id),
        pack = id.startsWith('pack:'),
        available = q.assets?.available[id] ?? 0;
      q.amount = Math.max(1, Math.min(q.amount, available || 1));
      this.label(
        p ? this.productName(p) : this.t('补给箱', 'CRATE'),
        rx + 8,
        top + 5,
        rw - 50,
        26,
        34,
        ivory,
        true,
        true,
      );
      this.infoButton(id, r - 48, top);
      const bodyH = bottom - top,
        artH = Math.max(0, bodyH - 224);
      if (artH > 30)
        this.picture(
          pack
            ? 'closedCrate'
            : p
              ? this.productArt(p.effect)
              : 'homeWarehouse',
          rx + 10,
          top + 34,
          rw - 20,
          artH,
          false,
        );
      const yy = bottom - 154;
      this.label(
        p
          ? this.brief(p)
          : this.t('金币或单场物资', 'Coins or one-match supplies'),
        rx + 8,
        yy - 32,
        rw - 16,
        28,
        22,
        dim,
      );
      this.label(
        (p
          ? this.term(p.term) + ' · '
          : this.t(
              rarity[Number(id.split(':')[2])],
              rarityEn[Number(id.split(':')[2])],
            ) + ' · ') + this.t(`可用 ${available}`, `${available} available`),
        rx + 8,
        yy - 9,
        rw - 16,
        22,
        21,
        dim,
      );
      this.button(
        'qm:minus',
        '−',
        rx + 8,
        yy + 15,
        44,
        44,
        'panel',
        q.amount <= 1,
      );
      this.label(String(q.amount), rx + 52, yy + 15, rw - 104, 44, 32, ivory);
      this.button(
        'qm:plus',
        '+',
        r - 52,
        yy + 15,
        44,
        44,
        'panel',
        q.amount >= Math.min(100, available),
      );
      const bw = Math.max(44, (rw - 24) * 0.32),
        owned = !!(p?.term === '24h' && q.assets?.permanent.includes(p.effect));
      this.button(
        pack ? 'qm:open' : p?.term === '24h' ? 'qm:activate' : 'qm:loadout',
        this.t(
          pack ? '打开' : p?.term === '24h' ? '激活' : '配装',
          pack ? 'OPEN' : p?.term === '24h' ? 'USE' : 'EQUIP',
        ),
        rx + 8,
        bottom - 49,
        bw,
        44,
        'gold',
        q.busy || available < 1 || owned,
      );
      const proceeds = q.sellPrice(id) * q.amount;
      this.button(
        'qm:sell',
        this.t(`出售可得${proceeds}`, `SELL FOR ${proceeds}`),
        rx + 16 + bw,
        bottom - 49,
        rw - 24 - bw,
        44,
        'blue',
        q.busy || available < 1 || q.sellPrice(id) === 0,
      );
    } else
      this.label(
        this.t('仓库暂无物资', 'No supplies yet'),
        rx + 8,
        top + 30,
        rw - 16,
        65,
        28,
        dim,
      );
    this.button(
      'qm:receipts',
      this.t('开包记录', 'HISTORY'),
      r - 240,
      this.bottom - 46,
      112,
      44,
    );
    this.button(
      'qm:supply',
      this.t('获取礼包', 'SUPPLIES'),
      r - 120,
      this.bottom - 46,
      120,
      44,
      'gold',
    );
  }
  private receipts() {
    const q = this.q,
      l = this.left,
      r = this.right,
      y = this.top,
      bottom = this.bottom,
      entries = q.assets?.openReceipts || [],
      rowH = this.low ? 82 : 112;
    this.panel(l, y, r - l, bottom - y);
    this.list(
      'receipts',
      l + 8,
      y + 8,
      r - l - 16,
      bottom - y - 16,
      entries.length * (rowH + 8),
      (offset) =>
        entries.forEach((entry, i) => {
          const yy = y + 8 + i * (rowH + 8) - offset;
          if (yy + rowH < y || yy > bottom) return;
          const tier = Number(entry.item.split(':')[2]),
            date = new Date(entry.at),
            stamp = `${date.getMonth() + 1}/${date.getDate()} ${String(date.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(date.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
          this.panel(l + 8, yy, r - l - 28, rowH);
          this.picture('closedCrate', l + 16, yy + 8, 70, rowH - 16, false);
          this.label(
            stamp +
              ' · ' +
              this.t(rarity[tier] || '补给', rarityEn[tier] || 'Supply') +
              ` ×${entry.quantity}`,
            l + 94,
            yy + 6,
            r - l - 124,
            27,
            29,
            rarityColor[tier] || gold,
            true,
            true,
          );
          const counts = new Map<string, number>();
          let coins = 0;
          for (const d of entry.drops) {
            if (d.item) counts.set(d.item, (counts.get(d.item) || 0) + 1);
            else coins += d.coins || 0;
          }
          this.label(
            [
              ...(coins ? [this.t(`金币 +${coins}`, `Coins +${coins}`)] : []),
              ...Array.from(
                counts,
                ([id, n]) => `${this.catalogName(id)} ×${n}`,
              ),
            ].join(' · '),
            l + 94,
            yy + 37,
            r - l - 124,
            rowH - 42,
            25,
            ivory,
            false,
            true,
          );
        }),
    );
    if (!entries.length)
      this.label(
        this.t('暂无开包记录', 'No opening history'),
        l + 20,
        y + 40,
        r - l - 40,
        60,
        32,
        dim,
      );
  }
  private checkin() {
    const q = this.q,
      l = this.left,
      r = this.right,
      top = this.top + 8,
      bottom = this.bottom - 58;
    this.panel(l, top, r - l, bottom - top);
    this.label(
      this.t(
        '累计签到，漏签不清零',
        'CUMULATIVE CHECK-IN · MISSING A DAY NEVER RESETS',
      ),
      l + 12,
      top + 8,
      r - l - 24,
      36,
      38,
      gold,
      true,
    );
    const cols = this.narrow ? 4 : 7,
      gap = 8,
      cw = (r - l - 32 - (cols - 1) * gap) / cols,
      rows = this.narrow ? 2 : 1,
      cy = top + 50,
      ch = (bottom - cy - 12 - (rows - 1) * 8) / rows,
      count = q.assets?.signedDays.length ?? 0;
    const currentDay = Math.max(1, count + (q.assets?.signedToday ? 0 : 1)),
      groupStart = Math.floor((currentDay - 1) / 7) * 7;
    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
      const x = l + 16 + (i % cols) * (cw + gap),
        yy = cy + Math.floor(i / cols) * (ch + 8),
        day = groupStart + i + 1,
        done = day <= count;
      this.panel(x, yy, cw, ch, day === currentDay);
      this.picture(
        i === 6 ? 'supply' : 'coins',
        x + 4,
        yy + 25,
        cw - 8,
        Math.max(12, ch - 52),
      );
      this.label(
        this.t(`第 ${day} 天`, `DAY ${day}`),
        x + 4,
        yy + 3,
        cw - 8,
        24,
        28,
        done ? dim : gold,
        true,
      );
      this.label(
        done ? '✓' : `● ${i === 6 ? 200 : 100} + EXP40`,
        x + 3,
        yy + ch - 25,
        cw - 6,
        22,
        20,
        done ? dim : ivory,
      );
    }
    this.button(
      'qm:checkinClaim',
      this.t(
        q.assets?.signedToday ? '今日已签到' : '今日签到',
        q.assets?.signedToday ? 'CHECKED IN' : 'CHECK IN TODAY',
      ),
      r - 220,
      this.bottom - 48,
      220,
      48,
      'gold',
      q.busy || !q.assets || q.assets.signedToday,
    );
    this.label(
      this.t(
        '每日额外获得1个普通礼包',
        'Includes 1 common general crate daily',
      ),
      l,
      this.bottom - 48,
      r - l - 240,
      44,
      22,
      dim,
      false,
      true,
    );
  }
  private probability() {
    this.q.info = 'pool';
  }
  private infoDialog() {
    const q = this.q,
      p = q.catalog.find((p) => p.id === q.info);
    let lines: string[] = [];
    if (p)
      lines = [
        p.description,
        this.t(
          `使用期限：${this.term(p.term)} · Lv.${p.unlockLevel}解锁`,
          `TERM: ${this.term(p.term)} · UNLOCK Lv.${p.unlockLevel}`,
        ),
        this.t(
          '同效果不可重复装配。单场资格在正式出动时消耗；训练不消耗。',
          'Duplicate effects cannot stack. One-match gear is consumed on deployment, not in training.',
        ),
      ];
    else if (q.info === 'wheel')
      lines = (q.assets?.wheelPrizes || WHEEL_PRIZES)
        .map(
          (p) =>
            (p.coins
              ? `${p.coins} ${this.t('金币', 'coins')}`
              : this.t(rarity[p.tier] + '礼包', rarityEn[p.tier] + ' crate')) +
            `：${p.weight}%`,
        )
        .concat(
          this.t(
            '扇区面积相同，中奖概率以标注数值为准。',
            'Sectors are equal in size; the displayed percentages are the actual odds.',
          ),
          this.t(
            '完整观看1段视频获得1次；每日最多5段，与礼包共用每日12段。次数跨天保留。',
            'One completed video grants one saved spin. 5 wheel videos/day, sharing 12 total videos/day.',
          ),
        );
    else {
      const pool = q.pools.general,
        pack = /^pack:(general|gold):([0-3])$/.exec(q.info),
        tier = pack ? Number(pack[2]) : -1;
      lines = [
        this.t(
          pack
            ? '此箱档位已确定，开启不重新抽取档位。'
            : '以下是获取随机礼包时的档位概率。已持有的箱子档位已确定，开启不重新抽档。',
          pack
            ? 'This crate’s tier is fixed. Opening does not reroll its tier.'
            : 'These are random crate acquisition odds. Owned crates keep their existing tier.',
        ),
      ];
      lines.push(
        ...pool.weights.flatMap((weight, i) => {
          if (tier >= 0 && tier !== i) return [];
          const eligible = eligibleDrops(
              q.catalog,
              i,
              q.assets?.level || 1,
              q.assets?.permanent || [],
            ),
            content =
              i === 0
                ? this.t('获得40金币', 'Receive 40 coins')
                : eligible.length
                  ? eligible.map((p) => this.productName(p)).join(' / ') +
                    this.t(
                      `（每种${(100 / eligible.length).toFixed(1)}%，档内等概率）`,
                      ` (equal odds within tier)`,
                    )
                  : this.t(
                      `本档当前无可用装备，获得${pool.coinDrops[i]}金币`,
                      `No eligible item: ${pool.coinDrops[i]} coins`,
                    );
          return [
            this.t(rarity[i], rarityEn[i]) +
              (tier < 0 ? ` ${weight}%` : '') +
              '\n' +
              content,
          ];
        }),
      );
      lines.push(
        this.t(
          '仅抽取已解锁、未永久拥有的单场武器或增益；主动技能在Lv.6/10永久解锁。各数量套餐概率相同。',
          'Only unlocked, unowned one-match gear. Active skills unlock permanently at Lv.6/10. All package sizes share odds.',
        ),
      );
      const locked = q.catalog.filter(
        (p) =>
          p.term === 'match' &&
          p.category !== 'skill' &&
          p.unlockLevel > (q.assets?.level || 1),
      );
      if (locked.length) {
        lines.push(
          this.t(
            '尚未进入当前奖池（达到等级后解锁）：',
            'NOT IN THE CURRENT POOL (unlock by level):',
          ),
        );
        lines.push(
          ...locked.map((p) => `${this.productName(p)} · Lv.${p.unlockLevel}`),
        );
      }
    }
    const pw = Math.min(650, this.right - this.left),
      font = this.font(27),
      width = pw - 52;
    const heights = lines.map((line) =>
      Math.max(
        30,
        line
          .split('\n')
          .reduce(
            (n, part) =>
              n +
              Math.max(
                1,
                Math.ceil(
                  (Array.from(part).reduce(
                    (sum, c) => sum + (/[^\x00-\x7f]/.test(c) ? 1 : 0.6),
                    0,
                  ) *
                    font) /
                    width,
                ),
              ),
            0,
          ) *
          font *
          1.23 +
          8,
      ),
    );
    const content = heights.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0),
      title = p
        ? this.productName(p)
        : this.t(
            q.info === 'wheel' ? '转盘概率' : '补给箱概率与内容',
            q.info === 'wheel' ? 'WHEEL ODDS' : 'CRATE CONTENTS & ODDS',
          );
    const { x, y, ph } = this.dialog(title, content + 118);
    this.list('info', x + 18, y + 53, pw - 36, ph - 118, content, (offset) => {
      let yy = y + 55 - offset;
      lines.forEach((line, i) => {
        this.label(
          line,
          x + 22,
          yy,
          width,
          heights[i] - 8,
          27,
          ivory,
          false,
          true,
        );
        yy += heights[i];
      });
    });
    this.button(
      'qm:infoClose',
      this.t('知道了', 'CLOSE'),
      x + pw / 2 - 70,
      y + ph - 57,
      140,
      44,
      'blue',
    );
  }
  private dialog(title: string, desiredHeight = 390) {
    const pw = Math.min(650, this.right - this.left),
      ph = Math.min(
        desiredHeight,
        this.height - this.h.safe.top - this.h.safe.bottom - 24,
      ),
      x = (this.w - pw) / 2,
      y =
        this.h.safe.top +
        (this.height - this.h.safe.top - this.h.safe.bottom - ph) / 2;
    for (const b of this.h.buttons)
      if (this.h.inModal(b.label.node)) b.disabled = true;
    this.h.listAreas = [];
    const overlay = this.h.node('RewardDialog', this.n);
    this.n = overlay;
    this.g = this.h.node('DialogShade', overlay).addComponent(Graphics);
    this.h.rect(this.g, 0, 0, this.w, this.height, '#01070ca8');
    this.surfaces = this.h.node('DialogPanel', overlay);
    this.panel(x, y, pw, ph);
    this.g = this.h.node('DialogFill', overlay).addComponent(Graphics);
    this.h.gradient(
      this.g,
      x + 6,
      y + 6,
      pw - 12,
      ph - 12,
      '#1a3547f5',
      '#0b1b2af5',
      4,
    );
    this.surfaces = this.h.node('DialogSurfaces', overlay);
    this.g = this.h.node('DialogInk', overlay).addComponent(Graphics);
    if (title) this.label(title, x + 16, y + 10, pw - 32, 34, 38, gold, true);
    return { x, y, pw, ph };
  }
  private leaveDialog() {
    const pending = this.q.pending?.kind === 'loadout',
      { x, y, pw, ph } = this.dialog(
        this.t(
          pending ? '保存结果待确认' : '方案尚未保存',
          pending ? 'SAVE RESULT PENDING' : 'UNSAVED LOADOUT',
        ),
        220,
      );
    const modes = this.q.dirtyModes
      .map((mode) =>
        this.t(
          mode === 'solo' ? '单人方案' : '双人方案',
          mode === 'solo' ? 'Solo' : 'Duel',
        ),
      )
      .join('、');
    this.label(
      pending
        ? this.t(
            '保存请求已发送，结果尚未确认。\n离开不会取消请求；草稿与交易编号会保留，稍后可重试确认。',
            'The save was sent but is not yet confirmed.\nLeaving keeps the request and draft. Retry later to confirm.',
          )
        : this.t(
            `${modes}有未保存的修改。\n返回编辑并确定保存，或放弃修改后离开。`,
            `${modes} has unsaved changes.\nContinue editing to save, or discard and leave.`,
          ),
      x + 22,
      y + 55,
      pw - 44,
      ph - 126,
      29,
      ivory,
    );
    this.button(
      'qm:keepEditing',
      this.t(
        pending ? '继续确认' : '返回编辑',
        pending ? 'CONTINUE' : 'KEEP EDITING',
      ),
      x + 20,
      y + ph - 60,
      (pw - 52) / 2,
      44,
      'blue',
    );
    this.button(
      pending ? 'qm:leavePending' : 'qm:discard',
      this.t(
        pending ? '保留请求并离开' : '放弃修改',
        pending ? 'KEEP REQUEST & LEAVE' : 'DISCARD',
      ),
      x + 32 + (pw - 52) / 2,
      y + ph - 60,
      (pw - 52) / 2,
      44,
      'panel',
    );
  }
  private giftDialog() {
    const q = this.q,
      gift = q.gift!,
      opening = !!gift.started;
    for (const b of this.h.buttons)
      if (this.h.inModal(b.label.node)) b.disabled = true;
    this.h.listAreas = [];
