    const overlay = this.h.node('GiftPresentation', this.n);
    this.n = overlay;
    const background = this.a.get('base');
    if (background)
      this.h.coverImage(
        overlay,
        'GiftAirfield',
        background,
        0,
        0,
        this.w,
        this.height,
      );
    const shade = this.h.node('GiftShade', overlay).addComponent(Graphics);
    this.h.rect(shade, 0, 0, this.w, this.height, '#03101b55');
    this.surfaces = this.h.node('GiftArt', overlay);
    this.g = this.h.node('GiftInk', overlay).addComponent(Graphics);
    const l = this.left,
      r = this.right,
      top = this.h.safe.top + 8,
      b = this.bottom;
    this.label(
      this.t(
        opening ? '礼包开启' : gift.opened ? '获得物资' : '获得礼包',
        opening ? 'OPENING' : gift.opened ? 'REWARDS' : 'SUPPLIES RECEIVED',
      ),
      l + 12,
      top,
      r - l - 140,
      42,
      53,
      gold,
      true,
      true,
    );
    if (opening) {
      const elapsed = (Date.now() - gift.started!) / (gift.duration || 1600),
        key = elapsed < 0.3 ? 'closedCrate' : 'openCrate',
        size = Math.min(this.w * 0.6, this.height * 0.9);
      this.picture(
        key,
        (this.w - size) / 2,
        top + 43,
        size,
        b - top - 50,
        false,
      );
      const glow = this.h.node('CrateGlow', overlay).addComponent(Graphics);
      glow.fillColor = color('#ffc35a22');
      glow.circle(0, 0, size * 0.27);
      glow.fill();
      const opacity = glow.node.addComponent(UIOpacity);
      if (!this.h.reducedEffects)
        tween(opacity)
          .to(0.35, { opacity: 80 })
          .to(0.35, { opacity: 230 })
          .union()
          .repeatForever()
          .start();
      const chest = this.surfaces.children.find((n) => n.name.includes(key));
      if (chest && !this.h.reducedEffects) {
        const fade = chest.addComponent(UIOpacity);
        fade.opacity = elapsed < 0.3 ? 180 : 80;
        tween(fade).to(0.22, { opacity: 255 }).start();
      }
      this.button(
        'qm:giftSkip',
        this.t('跳过 ›', 'SKIP ›'),
        r - 105,
        top,
        105,
        44,
      );
      return;
    }
    const heroW = (r - l) * 0.29,
      rx = l + heroW + 14,
      rw = r - rx,
      cy = top + 52,
      bottom = b - 54;
    this.picture(
      gift.opened ? 'openCrate' : 'closedCrate',
      l,
      cy,
      heroW,
      bottom - cy,
      false,
    );
    this.panel(rx, cy, rw, bottom - cy);
    const counts = new Map<string, number>();
    let coins = 0;
    for (const d of gift.drops) {
      if (d.item) counts.set(d.item, (counts.get(d.item) || 0) + 1);
      else coins += d.coins || 0;
    }
    const entries: Array<{ id: string; n: number }> = gift.opened
      ? [
          ...(coins ? [{ id: 'coins', n: coins }] : []),
          ...Array.from(counts, ([id, n]) => ({ id, n })),
        ]
      : Object.entries(gift.packs).map(([id, n]) => ({ id, n }));
    const cols = Math.min(this.narrow ? 2 : 3, Math.max(1, entries.length)),
      cw = (rw - 26 - (cols - 1) * 9) / cols,
      ch = this.low ? 150 : Math.max(220, bottom - cy - 22);
    this.list(
      'gifts',
      rx + 8,
      cy + 8,
      rw - 16,
      bottom - cy - 16,
      Math.max(0, Math.ceil(entries.length / cols) * (ch + 9) - 9),
      (offset) =>
        entries.forEach(({ id, n }, i) => {
          const x = rx + 10 + (i % cols) * (cw + 9),
            y = cy + 10 + Math.floor(i / cols) * (ch + 9) - offset,
            p = q.catalog.find((p) => p.id === id),
            money = id === 'coins',
            tier =
              p?.rarity ??
              (id.startsWith('pack:') ? Number(id.split(':')[2]) : 0);
          this.panel(x, y, cw, ch);
          this.h.rect(
            this.g,
            x + 2,
            y + 2,
            cw - 4,
            ch - 4,
            '#00000000',
            money ? gold : rarityColor[tier],
            3,
          );
          this.picture(
            money ? 'coinPile' : p ? this.productArt(p.effect) : 'closedCrate',
            x + 5,
            y + 25,
            cw - 10,
            ch * 0.54,
            !id.startsWith('pack:'),
          );
          this.label(
            money
              ? this.t('金币', 'COINS')
              : this.t(rarity[tier], rarityEn[tier]) +
                  (p ? ' · ' + this.term(p.term) : ''),
            x + 8,
            y + 4,
            cw - 16,
            24,
            25,
            money ? gold : rarityColor[tier],
            true,
            true,
          );
          if (p) this.infoButton(id, x + cw - 44, y + 1);
          this.label(
            (money
              ? this.t('金币 +', 'COINS +')
              : p
                ? this.productName(p) + ' ×'
                : this.t('补给箱 ×', 'CRATES ×')) + n,
            x + 8,
            y + ch * 0.66,
            cw - 16,
            36,
            32,
            money ? gold : ivory,
            true,
          );
          this.label(
            p
              ? this.brief(p)
              : money
                ? this.t('已存入金币余额', 'Added to your balance')
                : this.t('点击一键打开查看物资', 'Open to reveal supplies'),
            x + 8,
            y + ch * 0.84,
            cw - 16,
            ch * 0.13,
            22,
            dim,
          );
        }),
    );
    const bw = (rw - 12) / 2;
    this.button(
      'qm:giftClose',
      this.t(gift.opened ? '完成' : '稍后打开', gift.opened ? 'DONE' : 'LATER'),
      rx,
      b - 48,
      bw,
      44,
      'gold',
      q.busy,
    );
    this.button(
      gift.opened ? 'qm:giftWarehouse' : 'qm:giftOpen',
      this.t(
        gift.opened ? '查看仓库' : q.pending ? '重试打开' : '一键打开',
        gift.opened ? 'INVENTORY' : q.pending ? 'RETRY' : 'OPEN ALL',
      ),
      rx + bw + 12,
      b - 48,
      bw,
      44,
      'blue',
      q.busy,
    );
  }
  private confirm() {
    const q = this.q,
      intent = q.confirmation!,
      { x, y, pw, ph } = this.dialog('', 220);
    const text =
      intent.kind === 'sell'
        ? this.t(
            `出售 ${this.inventoryName(intent.item)} ×${intent.quantity}？\n总计 ${q.sellPrice(intent.item) * intent.quantity} 金币`,
            `Sell ${this.inventoryName(intent.item)} ×${intent.quantity}?\nTotal ${q.sellPrice(intent.item) * intent.quantity} coins`,
          )
        : intent.kind === 'activate'
          ? this.t(
              `激活 ${this.inventoryName(intent.item)}？\n连续24小时；再次激活只续期，不叠加强度。`,
              `Activate ${this.inventoryName(intent.item)}?\nLasts 24 continuous hours. Renewal extends time, not strength.`,
            )
          : q.message[this.h.lang === 'zh' ? 0 : 1];
    this.label(text, x + 24, y + 16, pw - 48, ph - 88, 30, ivory, true);
    this.button(
      'qm:cancel',
      this.t('取消', 'CANCEL'),
      x + 20,
      y + ph - 60,
      (pw - 52) / 2,
      44,
      'panel',
    );
    this.button(
      'qm:confirm',
      this.t('确认', 'CONFIRM'),
      x + 32 + (pw - 52) / 2,
      y + ph - 60,
      (pw - 52) / 2,
      44,
      'gold',
    );
  }
  private settlement(s: Simulation) {
    const q = this.q,
      online = this.h.online,
      reward = s.duelMode ? online?.room?.settlement : online?.battleReward,
      training = s.mission.mode === 'training',
      success = s.phase === 'success';
    const duel = s.duelMode,
      outcome =
        reward?.outcome ||
        (online?.view?.winner === null
          ? 'draw'
          : online?.view?.winner === (online?.room?.you ?? 0)
            ? 'win'
            : 'loss');
    const title = duel
      ? online?.spectator
        ? this.t('观战结束', 'MATCH ENDED')
        : this.t(
            outcome === 'win'
              ? '战斗胜利'
              : outcome === 'draw'
                ? '战斗平局'
                : '战斗失败',
            outcome === 'win'
              ? 'VICTORY'
              : outcome === 'draw'
                ? 'DRAW'
                : 'DEFEAT',
          )
      : training
        ? this.t(
            success ? '训练完成' : '训练结束',
            success ? 'TRAINING COMPLETE' : 'TRAINING ENDED',
          )
        : this.t(
            success ? '任务完成' : '行动结束',
            success ? 'MISSION COMPLETE' : 'OPERATION ENDED',
          );
    const reason = success
      ? this.t(
          `评价 ${s.rating} · 友伤 ${Math.round(s.friendlyDamage)}`,
          `RATING ${s.rating} · FRIENDLY DAMAGE ${Math.round(s.friendlyDamage)}`,
        )
      : s.failedGroup
        ? this.t(
            `第 ${s.failedGroup} 据点全灭`,
            `OUTPOST ${s.failedGroup} LOST`,
          )
        : s.failure === 'vehicle'
          ? this.t('救援车被毁', 'RESCUE VEHICLE LOST')
          : this.t('整备后再战', 'REGROUP AND RETRY');
    const subtitle = duel
      ? this.t('对手：', 'OPPONENT: ') + playerLabel(online?.opponent || {})
      : text(s.mission.name, this.h.lang) + ' · ' + reason;
    const pw = Math.min(
        this.w - this.h.safe.left - this.h.safe.right - 24,
        this.low ? this.w * 0.79 + 80 : this.w * 0.78,
      ),
      ph = Math.min(
        this.height - this.h.safe.top - this.h.safe.bottom - 24,
        this.height * 0.84 + (this.low ? 20 : 0),
      ),
      x = (this.w - pw) / 2,
      y = (this.height - ph) / 2;
    this.h.rect(this.g, 0, 0, this.w, this.height, '#030c1766');
    this.panel(x, y, pw, ph);
    const banner = this.low ? 57 : ph * 0.34;
    if (!this.low)
      this.picture(
        'resultBadge',
        x + pw * 0.32,
        y - 52 * this.scale,
        pw * 0.36,
        (pw * 0.36) / 3,
        false,
      );
    this.label(
      title,
      x + 18,
      y + (this.low ? 6 : banner * 0.34),
      pw - 36,
      this.low ? 32 : banner * 0.4,
      82,
      gold,
      true,
    );
    this.label(subtitle, x + 18, y + banner - 22, pw - 36, 22, 26, ivory);
    const sy = y + banner + 8,
      gap = 8,
      cw = (pw - 40 - gap * 3) / 4,
      sh = this.low ? 65 : ph * 0.21;
    const credited = reward?.status === 'credited',
      values = [
        formatTime(Math.round((s.completionTime ?? s.time) * 1000)),
        training
          ? '—'
          : `+${reward?.points ?? (!duel && (online?.rankStatus === 'finished' || online?.rankStatus === 'assisted') ? 100 : 0)}`,
        credited ? `+${reward?.coins ?? 0}` : '—',
        credited ? `+${reward?.xp ?? 0}` : '—',
      ];
    ['作战用时', '本局积分', '获得金币', '获得经验'].forEach((zh, i) => {
      const xx = x + 20 + i * (cw + gap);
      this.panel(xx, sy, cw, sh);
      this.label(
        this.t(zh, ['TIME', 'POINTS', 'COINS', 'EXP'][i]),
        xx + 4,
        sy + 5,
        cw - 8,
        sh * 0.35,
        28,
        dim,
      );
      this.label(
        values[i],
        xx + 4,
        sy + sh * 0.35,
        cw - 8,
        sh * 0.6,
        61,
        i === 2 ? gold : i === 3 ? '#b8e3a5' : ivory,
        true,
      );
    });
    const py = sy + sh + (this.low ? 10 : 20 * this.scale);
    if (!this.low) this.panel(x + 20, py, pw - 40, 76 * this.scale);
    this.label(
      `Lv.${q.assets?.level ?? 1}`,
      x + 20,
      py + (this.low ? 0 : 16 * this.scale),
      this.low ? 75 : 150 * this.scale,
      this.low ? 28 : 42 * this.scale,
      this.low ? 29 : 39,
      gold,
      true,
    );
    this.bar(
      x + (this.low ? 98 : 184 * this.scale),
      py + (this.low ? 8 : 29 * this.scale),
      pw - (this.low ? 220 : 380 * this.scale),
      this.low ? 11 : 18 * this.scale,
      (q.assets?.current ?? 0) / (q.assets?.required || 200),
    );
    this.label(
      `EXP ${q.assets?.current ?? 0}/${q.assets?.required ?? 200}`,
      x + pw - (this.low ? 117 : 185 * this.scale),
      py + (this.low ? 0 : 16 * this.scale),
      this.low ? 98 : 160 * this.scale,
      this.low ? 28 : 42 * this.scale,
      26,
      dim,
    );
    const next =
      !duel && !training && success && s.mission.id !== MISSIONS.at(-1)!.id;
    if (!this.low) {
      const task = q.assets?.tasks.find(
          (t) => t.event === (duel ? 'duel' : 'solo') || t.event === 'complete',
        ),
        ty = py + 90 * this.scale;
      this.panel(x + 20, ty, pw - 40, 72 * this.scale);
      this.label(
        task
          ? `${this.t('每日任务', 'DAILY TASK')}  ${task.progress}/${task.target}`
          : this.t('查看每日任务与奖励', 'VIEW DAILY TASKS & REWARDS'),
        x + 30,
        ty + 7,
        pw * (next ? 0.46 : 0.59),
        44,
        31,
        ivory,
        false,
        true,
      );
      this.button(
        'qm:tasks',
        this.t('查看奖励', 'REWARDS'),
        x + pw * (next ? 0.51 : 0.69),
        ty + 7,
        pw * (next ? 0.22 : 0.27),
        44,
      );
      if (next)
        this.button(
          'missionNext',
          this.t('下一任务 ›', 'NEXT ›'),
          x + pw * 0.75,
          ty + 7,
          pw * 0.21,
          44,
        );
    }
    const pending = ['pending-sync', 'syncing', 'recording'].includes(
      online?.rankStatus || '',
    );
    const status =
      training || online?.bot
        ? this.t(
            '训练不消耗库存，不产出正式资产',
            'Practice: no stock consumed or assets earned',
          )
        : online?.spectator
          ? this.t('观战不计收益', 'SPECTATING · NO REWARDS')
          : credited
            ? this.t('✓ 收益已到账', '✓ REWARDS CREDITED')
            : duel || pending
              ? this.t(
                  '待同步 · 联网后确认收益',
                  'PENDING SYNC · awaiting confirmation',
                )
              : this.t(
                  '本局仅本地记录，不产出正式资产',
                  'LOCAL RESULT · NO ASSET REWARDS',
                );
    const comparison = this.h.timeComparison,
      record = duel
        ? ''
        : training
          ? this.h.trainingBest
            ? this.t(
                `最佳 ${this.h.trainingBest.time.toFixed(1)} 秒`,
                `BEST ${this.h.trainingBest.time.toFixed(1)}s`,
              )
            : ''
          : comparison
            ? this.t(
                `${comparison.newRecord ? '新纪录 · ' : ''}个人最佳 ${formatTime(comparison.bestMs)}`,
                `${comparison.newRecord ? 'NEW RECORD · ' : ''}PERSONAL BEST ${formatTime(comparison.bestMs)}`,
              )
            : !success && s.failureCause === 'friendly'
              ? this.t(
                  '致命伤来自己方火力，开火前检查友军',
                  'FRIENDLY FIRE · CHECK ALLIES BEFORE FIRING',
                )
              : '';
    this.label(
      status + (record ? '\n' + record : ''),
      x + 20,
      y + ph - (this.low ? 104 : 172 * this.scale),
      pw - 40,
      this.low ? 42 : 60 * this.scale,
      23,
      credited ? '#94da90' : dim,
    );
    const bw = this.low ? (pw - (next ? 88 : 76)) / (next ? 3 : 2) : pw * 0.36,
      by = y + ph - (this.low ? 58 : 105 * this.scale),
      bh = this.low ? 48 : 80 * this.scale;
    this.button(
      'home',
      this.t('返回基地', 'RETURN TO BASE'),
      this.low ? x + 24 : x + pw * 0.07,
      by,
      bw,
      bh,
      'blue',
    );
    this.button(
      s.duelMode ? 'online:backLobby' : 'retry',
      this.t(
        s.duelMode ? '返回大厅' : '再次出动',
        s.duelMode ? 'BACK TO LOBBY' : 'DEPLOY AGAIN',
      ),
      this.low ? x + 52 + bw : x + pw * 0.57,
      by,
      bw,
      bh,
      'gold',
    );
    if (this.low && next)
      this.button(
        'missionNext',
        this.t('下一任务', 'NEXT'),
        x + 64 + bw * 2,
        y + ph - 58,
        bw,
        48,
        'gold',
      );
  }
}
import {
  CATALOG,
  PACKAGES,
  POOLS,
  emptyLoadout,
  type AssetCommand,
  type AssetSnapshot,
  type Effect,
  type Loadout,
  type Product,
} from './core/Progression.ts';
import type { Online } from './Online';
export type AssetIntent = AssetCommand extends infer T
  ? T extends AssetCommand
    ? Omit<T, 'requestId'>
    : never
  : never;
export type LoadoutSlot = 'mod:0' | 'mod:1' | 'weapon' | 'skill';
export type ProgressionPage =
  | ''
  | 'modes'
  | 'loadout'
  | 'shop'
  | 'supply'
  | 'growth'
  | 'tasks'
  | 'inbox'
  | 'warehouse'
  | 'receipts'
  | 'checkin'
  | 'probability';
type NavigationState = Pick<
  Quartermaster,
  | 'page'
  | 'section'
  | 'index'
  | 'detail'
  | 'selected'
  | 'item'
  | 'amount'
  | 'term'
  | 'mode'
  | 'slot'
  | 'loadoutScroll'
  | 'scrolls'
  | 'selectedMail'
  | 'selectedSkill'
>;
/** Wheel artwork has boundaries at 12 o'clock. A clockwise sector centre is 30 + i*60 degrees. */
export const wheelLandingAngle = (index: number, start = 0) =>
  start + 360 * 5 + ((30 + index * 60 - (start % 360) + 360) % 360);
export const effectEnglish: Record<Effect, string> = {
  power: 'Power upgrade',
  rate: 'Rapid feed',
  burst: 'Double burst',
  twin: 'Twin trajectory',
  radius: 'Blast radius',
  cooling: 'Cooling system',
  drone: 'Drone swarm',
  laser: 'Laser weapon',
  tracking: 'Tracking',
  emp: 'Electromagnetic pulse',
};
const errors: Record<string, [string, string]> = {
  DEV_TOOLS_DISABLED: [
    '服务器未启用开发调试，请连接已启用开发能力的测试服',

  ],

  ],
  INSUFFICIENT_INVENTORY: [

  ],
  ALREADY_OWNED: ['已永久拥有此资格', 'Already permanently owned.'],
  ALREADY_CLAIMED: [
    '已经领取，请刷新查看',
    'Already claimed. Refresh to see the result.',
  ],
  DAILY_VIDEO_LIMIT: [

  ],

    '观看确认已过期，请重新观看本段',
    'Video confirmation expired. Please watch this segment again.',
  ],
  AD_IN_PROGRESS: [
    '已有视频正在播放，请结束后再继续观看',
    'A video is already playing. Finish or close it before continuing.',
  ],
  DAILY_WHEEL_LIMIT: [
    '今日转盘视频已达上限，明日可继续；已有次数仍可使用',
    'Today’s wheel video limit is reached. Saved spins remain available.',
  ],
  NO_WHEEL_TICKETS: [
    '先完整观看一次视频，即可获得一次抽奖',
    'Complete one video to earn a spin.',
  ],
  LEVEL_LOCKED: [
    '等级尚未解锁这件物资',
    'Reach the required level to unlock this item.',
  ],
  NOT_CLAIMABLE: [
    '条件尚未完成或奖励已领取',
    'Not complete or already claimed.',
  ],
  INVALID_LOADOUT: [
    '方案最多2个改装、1个武器、1个技能，同效果不能重复',
    'Use at most 2 mods, 1 weapon and 1 skill, with no duplicate effects.',
  ],
};
/** Stores only pending intentions; balances always come from the authenticated service. */
export class Quartermaster {
  page: ProgressionPage = '';
  mode: 'solo' | 'duel' = 'solo';
  actionMode: 'solo' | 'duel' | 'training' = 'solo';
  term: Product['term'] = 'match';
  selected = 'burst-match';
  item = '';
  amount = 1;
  quantity = 5;
  pool: 'general' | 'gold' = 'general';
  section = '';
  index = 0;
  detail = false;
  confirmation?: AssetIntent;
  selectedMail = '';
  selectedSkill: Effect = 'tracking';
  scrolls: Record<string, number> = {};
  info = '';
  navigation: NavigationState[] = [];
  externalReturn: '' | 'modes' | 'result' = '';
  private leavingBack = false;
  wheel?: {
    index: number;
    startAngle: number;
    started: number;
    duration: number;
    shown: boolean;
    coins: number;
    packs: Record<string, number>;
  };
  slot: LoadoutSlot = 'mod:0';
  loadoutScroll = 0;
  loadoutScrollMax = 0;
  loadoutArea = { x: 0, y: 0, w: 0, h: 0 };
  drafts: Partial<Record<'solo' | 'duel', Loadout>> = {};
  private modDrafts: Partial<Record<'solo' | 'duel', Array<string | null>>> =
    {};
  leaveTo?: ProgressionPage;
  gift?: {
    packs: Record<string, number>;
    drops: Array<{ item?: string; coins?: number }>;
    opened: boolean;
    started?: number;
    duration?: number;
    illuminated?: boolean;
  };
  drag?: { item: string; x: number; y: number; target?: LoadoutSlot };
  private openingGift = false;
  private adReady?: { orderId: string; attemptId: string; startedAt: number };
  busy = false;
  viewing = false;
  revision = 0;
  message: [string, string] = ['', ''];
  pending?: AssetCommand;
  private owner = '';
  private disposed = false;
  private online: Online;
  private storage: Pick<Storage, 'getItem' | 'setItem' | 'removeItem'>;
  constructor(
    online: Online,
    storage: Pick<Storage, 'getItem' | 'setItem' | 'removeItem'>,
  ) {
    this.online = online;
    this.storage = storage;
  }
  get assets() {
    return this.online.assets;
  }
  get catalog() {
    return this.assets?.catalog || CATALOG;
  }
  get packages() {
    return this.assets?.packages || PACKAGES;
  }
  get pools() {
    return this.assets?.pools || POOLS;
  }
  sellPrice(id: string) {
    const pack = /^pack:(general|gold):[0-3]$/.exec(id);
    return pack
      ? this.pools[pack[1] as 'general' | 'gold'].packSell
      : (this.catalog.find((p) => p.id === id)?.sell ??
          (id.startsWith('legacy') ? 20 : 0));
  }
  get loadout() {
    return (
      this.drafts[this.mode] ||
      this.assets?.loadouts[this.mode] ||
      emptyLoadout()
    );
  }
  get dirty() {
    return this.dirtyModes.includes(this.mode);
  }
  get dirtyModes() {
    return (['solo', 'duel'] as const).filter((mode) => {
      const saved = this.assets?.loadouts[mode] || emptyLoadout(),
        slots = this.modDrafts[mode];
      return (
        this.drafts[mode] &&
        (JSON.stringify(this.drafts[mode]) !== JSON.stringify(saved) ||
          (slots &&
            [0, 1].some((i) => (slots[i] ?? null) !== (saved.mods[i] ?? null))))
      );
    });
  }
  get anyDirty() {
    return this.dirtyModes.length > 0;
  }
  get slotCategory() {
    return this.slot.startsWith('mod:')
      ? 'mod'
      : (this.slot as 'weapon' | 'skill');
  }
  get modSlots() {
    return this.modDrafts[this.mode] || this.loadout.mods;
  }
  get slotItem() {
    return this.slotCategory === 'mod'
      ? this.modSlots[Number(this.slot.slice(-1))]
      : this.loadout[this.slotCategory];
  }
  get availableGear() {
    return this.catalog.filter(
      (p) =>
        p.category === this.slotCategory &&
        (this.assets?.permanent.includes(p.effect)
          ? p.term === 'permanent'
          : (this.assets?.active[p.effect] ?? 0) >
              (this.assets?.serverNow ?? Date.now())
            ? p.term === '24h'
            : p.term === 'match' && (this.assets?.available[p.id] ?? 0) > 0),
    );
  }
  equipped(effect: Effect) {
    return [...this.loadout.mods, this.loadout.weapon, this.loadout.skill].some(
      (id) => this.catalog.find((p) => p.id === id)?.effect === effect,
    );
  }
  selectSlot(slot: LoadoutSlot) {
    this.slot = slot;
    this.loadoutScroll = 0;
    this.revision++;
  }
  selectInventorySlot() {
    const product = this.catalog.find((p) => p.id === this.item);
    if (!product) return;
    const index = this.modSlots.findIndex(
      (id) => this.catalog.find((p) => p.id === id)?.effect === product.effect,
    );
    this.selectSlot(
      product.category === 'mod'
        ? (`mod:${index >= 0 ? index : !this.modSlots[0] ? 0 : !this.modSlots[1] ? 1 : 0}` as LoadoutSlot)
        : product.category,
    );
  }
  moveMod(from: LoadoutSlot, to: LoadoutSlot) {
    this.syncIdentity();
    if (
      this.busy ||
      this.pending ||
      from === to ||
      !from.startsWith('mod:') ||
      !to.startsWith('mod:')
    )
      return;
    const slots = [...this.modSlots],
      a = Number(from.slice(-1)),
      b = Number(to.slice(-1));
    if (!slots[a]) return;
    [slots[a], slots[b]] = [slots[b] ?? null, slots[a]];
    this.modDrafts[this.mode] = slots;
    this.drafts[this.mode] = {
      ...this.loadout,
      mods: slots.filter((id): id is string => !!id),
    };
    this.slot = to;
    this.message = [
      '方案尚未保存，请点击确定保存',
      'Unsaved changes. Confirm to save.',
    ];
    this.revision++;
  }
  scrollLoadout(amount: number) {
    this.loadoutScroll = Math.max(
      0,
      Math.min(this.loadoutScrollMax, this.loadoutScroll + amount),
    );
    this.revision++;
  }
  cancelLeave() {
    this.leaveTo = undefined;
    this.leavingBack = false;
  }
  requestLeave(page: ProgressionPage) {
    this.leavingBack = false;
    if (
      this.page === 'loadout' &&
      (this.anyDirty || this.pending?.kind === 'loadout')
    ) {
      this.leaveTo = page;
      this.revision++;
      return;
    }
    this.show(page);
  }
  private capture(): NavigationState {
    return {
      page: this.page,
      section: this.section,
      index: this.index,
      detail: this.detail,
      selected: this.selected,
      item: this.item,
      amount: this.amount,
      term: this.term,
      mode: this.mode,
      slot: this.slot,
      loadoutScroll: this.loadoutScroll,
      scrolls: { ...this.scrolls },
      selectedMail: this.selectedMail,
      selectedSkill: this.selectedSkill,
    };
  }
  back() {
    if (this.info) {
      this.info = '';
      this.revision++;
      return;
    }
    if (this.detail && this.page === 'shop') {
      this.detail = false;
      this.revision++;
      return;
    }
    if (
      this.page === 'loadout' &&
      (this.anyDirty || this.pending?.kind === 'loadout')
    ) {
      this.leavingBack = true;
      this.leaveTo = this.navigation.at(-1)?.page ?? '';
      this.revision++;
      return;
    }
    this.pop();
  }
  private pop() {
    const previous = this.navigation.pop();
    this.show(previous?.page ?? '', false);
    if (previous) Object.assign(this, previous);
    this.leavingBack = false;
    this.revision++;
  }
  leavePending() {
    if (this.pending?.kind === 'loadout' && this.leaveTo !== undefined) {
      if (this.leavingBack) this.pop();
      else this.show(this.leaveTo);
    }
  }
  discard() {
    if (this.pending?.kind === 'loadout') {
      this.requestLeave(this.leaveTo ?? '');
      return;
    }
    this.drafts = {};
    this.modDrafts = {};
    const page = this.leaveTo;
    this.leaveTo = undefined;
    if (this.leavingBack) this.pop();
    else this.show(page ?? '');
  }
  async save() {
    this.syncIdentity();
    return this.execute({
      kind: 'loadout',
      mode: this.mode,
      loadout: structuredClone(this.loadout),
    });
  }
  async openGifts() {
    this.syncIdentity();
    const gift = this.gift,
      owner = this.owner;
    if (!gift || this.busy || this.openingGift) return;
    if (this.pending && this.pending.kind !== 'open') return;
    this.openingGift = true;
    try {
      for (const [item, quantity] of Object.entries(gift.packs)) {
        if (quantity < 1) continue;
        const result = await this.execute(
          this.pending ? undefined : { kind: 'open', item, quantity },
        );
        if (this.owner !== owner || this.disposed) return;
        if (!result) {
          this.gift = gift;
          return;
        }
        gift.drops.push(
          ...((result.lastReceipt?.drops as Array<{
            item?: string;
            coins?: number;
          }>) || []),
        );
        delete gift.packs[item];
      }
      gift.opened = true;
      gift.started = Date.now();
      gift.duration = gift.drops.length > 1 ? 2200 : 1600;
      this.gift = gift;
    } finally {
      this.openingGift = false;
      this.revision++;
    }
  }
  get unread() {
    return this.assets?.mail.some((m) => !m.read || !m.claimed) || false;
  }
  get claimable() {
    return (
      this.assets?.tasks.some((t) => !t.claimed && t.progress >= t.target) ||
      false
    );
  }
  get pendingOrder() {
    return this.assets?.videoOrders.find(
      (o) =>
        !o.delivered && o.purpose !== 'wheel' && o.quantity === this.quantity,
    );
  }
  get wheelOrder() {
    return this.assets?.videoOrders.find(
      (o) => !o.delivered && o.purpose === 'wheel',
    );
  }
  get inventory() {
    return Object.entries(this.assets?.inventory || {})
      .filter(([, n]) => n > 0)
      .sort(
        ([a], [b]) =>
          Number(!a.startsWith('pack:')) - Number(!b.startsWith('pack:')) ||
          (a.startsWith('pack:') && b.startsWith('pack:')
            ? Number(a.split(':')[2]) - Number(b.split(':')[2])
            : 0),
      );
  }
  private key() {
    return 'night-overwatch-asset-intent:' + this.owner;
  }
  private allowDeveloperIntent() {
    if (
      (globalThis as { __SMALL_GAMES_BUILD__?: { type: string } })
        .__SMALL_GAMES_BUILD__?.type === 'development'
    )
      return true;
    if (this.pending?.kind === 'dev-adjust') {
      this.pending = undefined;
      try {
        this.storage.removeItem(this.key());
      } catch {
        /* A rejected developer intention is never sent from this package. */
      }
      this.message = [
        '已清除开发包遗留的调试操作，当前包不会重试',
        'Removed the saved developer operation; this package will not retry it.',
      ];
      this.revision++;
    }
    return false;
  }
  syncIdentity() {
    const id = this.online.authenticatedId;
    if (id === this.owner) return;
    this.owner = id;
    this.adReady = undefined;
    this.pending = undefined;
    this.drafts = {};
    this.modDrafts = {};
    this.gift = undefined;
    this.wheel = undefined;
    this.leaveTo = undefined;
    this.message = ['', ''];
    this.confirmation = undefined;
    this.selectedMail = '';
    this.info = '';
    this.navigation = [];
    this.scrolls = {};
    this.externalReturn = '';
    this.revision++;
    if (!id) return;
    try {
      const value = JSON.parse(this.storage.getItem(this.key()) || 'null');
      if (value?.requestId && value?.kind) this.pending = value;
    } catch {
      /* Corrupt local intent cannot alter server assets. */
    }
    this.allowDeveloperIntent();
  }
  show(page: ProgressionPage, push = true) {
    if (page !== 'supply' && this.wheel && !this.wheel.shown) {
      this.wheel.shown = true;
      this.wheel.started = 0;
    }
    if (push && page !== this.page) this.navigation.push(this.capture());
    this.page = page;
    this.leaveTo = undefined;
    this.leavingBack = false;
    this.drag = undefined;
    this.section = page === 'growth' ? 'base' : '';
    this.index = 0;
    this.detail = false;
    this.info = '';
    this.scrolls = {};
    this.confirmation = undefined;
    this.message = ['', ''];
    this.revision++;
    if (page && page !== 'modes')
      void this.refresh().then(() => {
        if (this.page === 'inbox' && this.section !== 'news') {
          const mail =
            this.assets?.mail.find((m) => m.id === this.selectedMail) ||
            this.assets?.mail[0];
          if (mail) {
            this.selectedMail = mail.id;
            this.revision++;
            if (!mail.read)
              void this.execute({ kind: 'read-mail', mailId: mail.id });
          }
        }
      });
  }
  tickPresentation(reduced = false) {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (
      this.gift?.started &&
      !this.gift.illuminated &&
      now - this.gift.started > (this.gift.duration || 1600) * 0.3
    ) {
      this.gift.illuminated = true;
      this.revision++;
    }
    if (
      this.gift?.started &&
      (reduced || now - this.gift.started >= (this.gift.duration || 1600))
    ) {
      this.gift.started = undefined;
      this.revision++;
    }
    if (
      this.wheel &&
      !this.wheel.shown &&
      this.page === 'supply' &&
      !this.gift &&
      (reduced || now - this.wheel.started >= this.wheel.duration)
    ) {
      this.wheel.shown = true;
      this.gift = {
        packs: this.wheel.packs,
        drops: this.wheel.coins ? [{ coins: this.wheel.coins }] : [],
        opened: !Object.keys(this.wheel.packs).length,
      };
      this.revision++;
    }
  }
  async refresh() {
    if (this.busy) return;
    this.busy = true;
    this.revision++;
    try {
      await this.online.refreshAssets();
      this.syncIdentity();
      this.message = ['', ''];
    } catch {
      this.message = [
        '军需服务暂不可用，可继续普通行动',
        'Quartermaster unavailable. Normal operations remain playable.',
      ];
    } finally {
      this.busy = false;
      this.revision++;
    }
  }
  async execute(intent?: AssetIntent): Promise<AssetSnapshot | undefined> {
    this.syncIdentity();
    if (this.busy || this.disposed) return;
    if (!this.allowDeveloperIntent() && intent?.kind === 'dev-adjust') {
      this.message = [
        '只有开发测试包可调整资产',
        'Asset adjustment requires a development package.',
      ];
      this.revision++;
      return;
    }
    if (!this.owner || !this.assets) {
      this.message = [
        '请先连接军需服务',
        'Connect to the quartermaster first.',
      ];
      this.revision++;
      return;
    }
    if (
      this.wheel &&
      !this.wheel.shown &&
      intent &&
      intent.kind !== 'read-mail'
    ) {
      this.message = ['抽奖揭晓后可继续操作', 'Wait for the wheel to finish.'];
      this.revision++;
      return;
    }
    if (this.pending && intent) {
      this.message = [
        '上一操作等待确认，请先重试，避免重复扣款',
        'Confirm the pending operation before making another purchase.',
      ];
      this.revision++;
      return;
    }
    if (intent) {
      const bytes = new Uint8Array(16);
      if (globalThis.crypto?.getRandomValues)
        globalThis.crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
      else
        for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) bytes[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
      bytes[6] = (bytes[6] & 15) | 64;
      bytes[8] = (bytes[8] & 63) | 128;
      const hex = Array.from(bytes, (b) =>
        b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'),
      ).join('');
      this.pending = {
        ...intent,
        requestId: `${hex.slice(0, 8)}-${hex.slice(8, 12)}-${hex.slice(12, 16)}-${hex.slice(16, 20)}-${hex.slice(20)}`,
      } as AssetCommand;
      try {
        this.storage.setItem(this.key(), JSON.stringify(this.pending));
      } catch {
        this.pending = undefined;
        this.message = [
          '无法保存交易编号，请允许本地存储后重试',
          'Cannot save the transaction ID. Enable local storage and retry.',
        ];
        this.revision++;
        return;
      }
    }
    if (!this.pending) return;
    const command = this.pending,
      owner = this.owner;
    this.busy = true;
    this.revision++;
    try {
      const snapshot = await this.online.assetCommand(command);
      if (this.owner !== owner || this.disposed) return;
      this.pending = undefined;
      try {
        this.storage.removeItem(this.key());
      } catch {
        /* Replaying this accepted ID is safe. */
      }
      this.message = ['', ''];
      if (command.kind === 'loadout') {
        const selected = this.mode === command.mode ? this.slotItem : undefined;
        delete this.drafts[command.mode];
        delete this.modDrafts[command.mode];
        // The server stores mods in order without empty placeholders; show that same order immediately.
        if (
          selected &&
          this.mode === command.mode &&
          this.slotCategory === 'mod'
        ) {
          const index = snapshot.loadouts[command.mode].mods.indexOf(selected);
          if (index >= 0) this.slot = `mod:${index}` as LoadoutSlot;
        }
      }
      const receipt = snapshot.lastReceipt;
      if (command.kind === 'ad-start' && typeof receipt?.attemptId === 'string')
        this.adReady = {
          orderId: command.orderId,
          attemptId: receipt.attemptId,
          startedAt:
            snapshot.videoOrders.find((o) => o.id === command.orderId)?.attempt
              ?.startedAt ?? snapshot.serverNow,
        };
      if (command.kind === 'ad-complete' || command.kind === 'ad-cancel')
        this.adReady = undefined;
      const reward = receipt?.reward as { packs?: number } | undefined;
      const packs = receipt?.packs as Record<string, number> | undefined;
      if (packs && Object.keys(packs).length)
        this.gift = { packs: { ...packs }, drops: [], opened: false };
      else if (reward?.packs)
        this.gift = {
          packs: { 'pack:general:0': reward.packs },
          drops: [],
          opened: false,
        };
      if (command.kind === 'open') {
        const drops = snapshot.lastReceipt?.drops as
          | Array<{ item?: string; coins?: number }>
          | undefined;
        if (!this.openingGift)
          this.gift = {
            packs: {},
            drops: drops || [],
            opened: true,
            started: Date.now(),
            duration: command.quantity > 1 ? 2200 : 1600,
          };
        const coins = (drops || []).reduce((n, d) => n + (d.coins || 0), 0),
          items = (drops || []).filter((d) => d.item);
        this.message = [
          `已开 ${command.quantity} 个：金币 +${coins}，军需 ${items.length} 件（见仓库）`,
          `Opened ${command.quantity}: +${coins} coins, ${items.length} gear items in warehouse`,
        ];
      }
      if (command.kind === 'wheel-spin') {
        const startAngle = this.wheel
          ? wheelLandingAngle(this.wheel.index, this.wheel.startAngle)
          : 0;
        this.gift = undefined;
        this.wheel = {
          startAngle,
          index: Number(receipt?.prizeIndex) || 0,
          started: Date.now(),
          duration: 2800,
          shown: false,
          coins: Number(receipt?.coins) || 0,
          packs: packs || {},
        };
      }
      return snapshot;
    } catch (error) {
      const code = (error as { code?: string }).code || '';
      if (code === 'IDENTITY_CHANGED') {
        try {
          await this.online.refreshAssets();
          this.syncIdentity();
        } catch {
          /* Keep the original account's intent until identity can be confirmed. */
        }
        return;
      }
      const confirmed =
        !!errors[code] ||
        [
          'INVALID_INPUT',
          'TASK_NOT_FOUND',
          'MAIL_NOT_FOUND',
          'ACHIEVEMENT_NOT_FOUND',
          'INVALID_PACKAGE',
          'INVALID_POOL',
          'ASSET_LIMIT',
          'ASSET_VERSION',
          'ITEM_UNAVAILABLE',
          'ITEM_NOT_PACK',
          'ITEM_NOT_SELLABLE',
          'TASK_NOT_REPLACEABLE',
          'TOO_MANY_ORDERS',
          'ORDER_NOT_FOUND',
          'REQUEST_ID_CONFLICT',
        ].includes(code);
      if (this.owner === owner) {
        if (confirmed) {
          this.pending = undefined;
          try {
            this.storage.removeItem(this.key());
          } catch {
            /* The next successful refresh can retry this rejected ID safely. */
          }
        }
        this.message =
          errors[code] ||
          (confirmed
            ? [
                '操作未完成，请刷新后重试',
                'Operation rejected. Refresh and try again.',
              ]
            : [
                '网络中断，原交易编号已保留，请重试确认',
                'Connection interrupted. Retry the saved transaction.',
              ]);
      }
    } finally {
      this.busy = false;
      this.revision++;
    }
  }
  equip(id: string, slot = this.slot) {
    this.syncIdentity();
    if (this.busy || this.pending) return;
    const p = this.catalog.find((p) => p.id === id),
      category = slot.startsWith('mod:') ? 'mod' : slot;
    if (
      !p ||
      p.category !== category ||
      this.equipped(p.effect) ||
      (!this.availableGear.some((item) => item.id === id) &&
        category === this.slotCategory)
    )
      return;
    const available =
      this.assets?.permanent.includes(p.effect) ||
      (this.assets?.active[p.effect] ?? 0) >
        (this.assets?.serverNow ?? Date.now()) ||
      (p.term === 'match' && (this.assets?.available[p.id] ?? 0) > 0);
    if (!available) return;
    const loadout = structuredClone(this.loadout);
    if (category === 'mod') {
      const slots = [...this.modSlots];
      slots[Number(slot.slice(-1))] = id;
      this.modDrafts[this.mode] = slots;
      loadout.mods = slots.filter((item): item is string => !!item);
    } else loadout[category] = id;
    this.drafts[this.mode] = loadout;
    this.slot = slot;
    this.message = [
      '方案尚未保存，请点击确定保存',
      'Unsaved changes. Confirm to save.',
    ];
    this.revision++;
  }
  unequip() {
    this.syncIdentity();
    if (this.busy || this.pending) return;
    const loadout = structuredClone(this.loadout);
    if (this.slotCategory === 'mod') {
      const slots = [...this.modSlots];
      slots[Number(this.slot.slice(-1))] = null;
      this.modDrafts[this.mode] = slots;
      loadout.mods = slots.filter((item): item is string => !!item);
    } else loadout[this.slotCategory] = null;
    this.drafts[this.mode] = loadout;
    this.message = [
      '方案尚未保存，请点击确定保存',
      'Unsaved changes. Confirm to save.',
    ];
    this.revision++;
  }
  async watch(
    offer: () => Promise<string>,
    purpose: 'packs' | 'wheel' = 'packs',
  ) {
    if (this.viewing || this.busy || this.pending) return;
    this.viewing = true;
    const owner = this.online.authenticatedId;
    this.revision++;
    try {
      let order = purpose === 'wheel' ? this.wheelOrder : this.pendingOrder;
      if (!order) {
        await this.execute({
          kind: 'ad-order',
          quantity: purpose === 'wheel' ? 1 : this.quantity,
          purpose,
        });
        order = purpose === 'wheel' ? this.wheelOrder : this.pendingOrder;
      }
      if (!order || owner !== this.online.authenticatedId) return;
      const now =
        (this.assets?.serverNow ?? Date.now()) +
        Math.max(
          0,
          performance.now() -
            (this.online.assetsReceivedAt ?? performance.now()),
        );
      if (
        this.adReady &&
        (this.adReady.orderId !== order.id ||
          now - this.adReady.startedAt >= 30 * 60000)
      )
        this.adReady = undefined;
      if (
        !this.adReady &&
        order.attempt &&
        now >= order.attempt.startedAt &&
        now - order.attempt.startedAt < 30 * 60000
      )
        this.adReady = {
          orderId: order.id,
          attemptId: order.attempt.id,
          startedAt: order.attempt.startedAt,
        };
      if (this.adReady?.orderId !== order.id)
        await this.execute({ kind: 'ad-start', orderId: order.id });
      const attemptId =
        this.adReady?.orderId === order.id ? this.adReady.attemptId : undefined;
      if (typeof attemptId !== 'string') return;
      let result = 'failed';
      try {
        result = await offer();
      } catch {
        /* Provider failure releases its account-wide playback slot below. */
      }
      if (this.disposed || owner !== this.online.authenticatedId) return;
      if (result === 'completed') {
        const completed = await this.execute({
          kind: 'ad-complete',
          orderId: order.id,
          attemptId,
        });
        if (
          !completed &&
          !this.pending &&
          !this.disposed &&
          owner === this.online.authenticatedId
        ) {
          const message = this.message;
          await this.execute({
            kind: 'ad-cancel',
            orderId: order.id,
            attemptId,
          });
          this.message = message;
        }
      } else {
        await this.execute({ kind: 'ad-cancel', orderId: order.id, attemptId });
        this.message =
          result === 'dismissed'
            ? [
                '未完整观看，本次未计入；已确认进度保留',
                'Video not completed. Confirmed progress is kept.',
              ]
            : [
                '视频暂不可用，已确认进度保留，可稍后继续',
                'Video unavailable. Confirmed progress is kept. Try later.',
              ];
      }
    } finally {
      this.viewing = false;
      this.revision++;
    }
  }
  dispose() {
    if (
      !this.disposed &&
      this.owner === this.online.authenticatedId &&
      this.adReady &&
      !this.busy &&
      !this.pending
    )
      void this.execute({
        kind: 'ad-cancel',
        orderId: this.adReady.orderId,
        attemptId: this.adReady.attemptId,
      });
    this.disposed = true;
  }
}
/** Explicitly requested browser share entry; ordinary and native entries have no control. */
export function shareScreenControl(
  search: string,
  changed: (active: boolean) => void,
) {
  const values = new URLSearchParams(search).getAll('fullscreen');
  if (
    values.length !== 1 ||
    values[0] !== '1' ||
    typeof document === 'undefined' ||
    !document.fullscreenEnabled
  )
    return;
  const update = () => changed(!!document.fullscreenElement);
  document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', update);
  update();
  return {
    async toggle() {
      if (document.fullscreenElement) await document.exitFullscreen();
      else await document.documentElement.requestFullscreen();
    },
    dispose() {
      document.removeEventListener('fullscreenchange', update);
    },
  };
}
