// Legacy display-only helper. Live flight and every shot use Simulation.aircraft instead.
export function aircraft(time: number) {
  const angle = Math.PI / 2 + (time * FLIGHT.speed) / FLIGHT.radius;
  return {
    x: Math.cos(angle) * FLIGHT.radius,
    z: Math.sin(angle) * FLIGHT.radius,
    y: FLIGHT.altitude,
    yaw: -angle,
    pitch: 0,
    bank: Math.atan(FLIGHT.speed ** 2 / (FLIGHT.gravity * FLIGHT.radius)),
    heading: ((((-angle * 180) / Math.PI) % 360) + 360) % 360,
  };
}

export const HIGHLAND_ROUTE: Point[] = [
  { x: -129, z: 67 },
  { x: -95, z: 59 },
  { x: -70, z: 30 },
  { x: -40, z: 45 },
  { x: -12, z: 20 },
  { x: 22, z: 38 },
  { x: 55, z: 3 },
  { x: 85, z: 12 },
  { x: 117, z: -28 },
  { x: 137, z: -42 },
];
export const mapRoute = (map: BattlefieldId = 'valley') =>
  map === 'highland' ? HIGHLAND_ROUTE : ROUTE;
export const routeLength = (map: BattlefieldId = 'valley') =>
  mapRoute(map)
    .slice(1)
    .reduce((n, p, i) => n + distance(mapRoute(map)[i], p), 0);
export const riverX = (z: number) =>
  Math.sin((z - 1.4) * 0.045) * 1.6 +
  Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (Math.abs(z) - 75) / 100)) * Math.sin(z * 0.013) * 32;
export function isWater(
  p: Point,
  map: BattlefieldId = 'valley',
  margin = 0,
): boolean {
  return (
    map === 'valley' &&
    Math.abs(p.x - riverX(p.z)) < 2.9 + margin &&
    !(Math.abs(p.z - 1.4) <= Math.max(0.1, 0.9 - margin) && Math.abs(p.x) < 12)
  );
}
export function landPoint(
  p: Point,
  map: BattlefieldId = 'valley',
  side = p.x - riverX(p.z),
): Point {
  if (map !== 'valley' || (Math.abs(p.z - 1.4) <= 0.4 && Math.abs(p.x) < 12))
    return p;
  const bank = riverX(p.z) + (side < 0 ? -3.4 : 3.4);
  return { x: side < 0 ? Math.min(p.x, bank) : Math.max(p.x, bank), z: p.z };
}
export function routePoint(
  d: number,
  map: BattlefieldId = 'valley',
): Point3 & { heading: number } {
  const route = mapRoute(map),
    length = routeLength(map);
  d = Math.max(0, Math.min(length, d));
  for (let i = 1; i < route.length; i++) {
    const a = route[i - 1],
      b = route[i],
      len = distance(a, b);
    if (d <= len || i === route.length - 1) {
      const x = a.x + ((b.x - a.x) * d) / len,
        z = a.z + ((b.z - a.z) * d) / len;
      return {
        x,
        z,
        y: terrainHeight(x, z, map) + TERRAIN.roadLift,
        heading: Math.atan2(b.x - a.x, b.z - a.z),
      };
    }
    d -= len;
  }
  return {
    ...route[0],
    y: terrainHeight(route[0].x, route[0].z, map) + TERRAIN.roadLift,
    heading: 0,
  };
}
export function patrolPoint(
  origin: Point & { patrolId?: number },
  elapsed: number,
  map: BattlefieldId = 'valley',
  unitId = origin.patrolId ?? 0,
): Point & { heading: number } {
  let seed = 2166136261;
  for (const char of `${unitId}:${origin.x}:${origin.z}`)
    seed = Math.imul(seed ^ char.charCodeAt(0), 16777619) >>> 0;
  const radius =
    seed % 10 === 0
      ? 6 + ((seed >>> 8) % 201) / 100
      : 16 + ((seed >>> 8) % 801) / 100;
  const side = origin.x - riverX(origin.z) < 0 ? -1 : 1;
  // Keep the whole circle on its bank; projecting each frame onto the river creates sharp turns.
  const bank = 5 + Math.max(0, (MAP.halfDepth - 75) / 100) * 32;
  const minX =
    map === 'valley' && side > 0
      ? bank + radius
      : -MAP.halfWidth + radius + 0.5;
  const maxX =
    map === 'valley' && side < 0
      ? -bank - radius
      : MAP.halfWidth - radius - 0.5;
  const x = Math.max(minX, Math.min(maxX, origin.x));
  const z = Math.max(
    -MAP.halfDepth + radius + 0.5,
    Math.min(MAP.halfDepth - radius - 0.5, origin.z - radius),
  );
  const direction = seed & 1 ? 1 : -1;
  const angle =
    Math.atan2(origin.x - x, origin.z - z) +
    (direction * Math.max(0, elapsed) * UNITS.light.speed) / radius;
  return {
    x: x + Math.sin(angle) * radius,
    z: z + Math.cos(angle) * radius,
    heading: Math.atan2(
      direction * Math.cos(angle),
      -direction * Math.sin(angle),
    ),
  };
}
export const text = (pair: readonly string[], language: Language) =>
  pair[language === 'zh' ? 0 : 1];
export function validateData() {
  if (
    ROUTE.length < 2 ||
    !Number.isFinite(ROUTE_LENGTH) ||
    MISSION.duration <= ROUTE_LENGTH / MISSION.speed
  )
    throw Error('Mission route/time invalid');
  if (
    MISSION.events.some(
      (e) =>
        !UNITS[e.kind] ||
        ![e.time, e.progress, e.x, e.z].every(Number.isFinite),
    )
  )
    throw Error('Invalid mission event');
  if (
    MISSION.events.some(
      (e) => Math.abs(e.x) > MAP.halfWidth || Math.abs(e.z) > MAP.halfDepth,
    ) ||
    ROUTE.some(
      (p) => Math.abs(p.x) > MAP.halfWidth || Math.abs(p.z) > MAP.halfDepth,
    )
  )
    throw Error('Mission point outside map');
  if (WEAPONS.some((w) => w.interval <= 0 || w.speed <= 0 || w.radius <= 0))
    throw Error('Invalid weapon configuration');
}
import { Simulation } from './Simulation.ts';
import { WEAPONS, patrolPoint, type Point } from './Data.ts';
import { MISSIONS } from './MissionCatalog.ts';
import type { BattleLoadout } from './Progression.ts';
export const DUEL_VERSION = 'night-duel-v7';
// Retained for older presentation callers; authority has no match time limit.
export const DUEL_SECONDS = 0;
type Pilot = ReturnType<Simulation['pilotState']> & { leaseUntil: number };
export type DuelCommand = { command: string; args: unknown[] };
export type DuelState = {
  simulation: ReturnType<Simulation['checkpoint']>;
  pilots: Pilot[];
  ticks: number;
  seed: number;
  bot: boolean;
  winner: number | null;
  finished: boolean;
  bots: boolean[];
  botAt: number[];
};
const copy = <T,>(value: T): T => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
const check = (value: unknown) => {
  if (!value) throw Error('Invalid duel input');
};
export function createDuel(seed: number, bot = false): DuelState {
  check(Number.isSafeInteger(seed) && seed >= 0 && seed <= 0x7fffffff);
  const map = seed % 2 ? 'highland' : 'valley';
  const sim = new Simulation(
    MISSIONS.find((mission) => mission.map === map)!.id,
  );
  sim.duelMode = true;
  sim.phase = 'playing';
  sim.units = [];
  sim.spawned = new Set(sim.mission.events.map((_, i) => i));
  sim.convoy = 'holding';
  sim.progress = 0;
  let random = seed || 1;
  const next = () => {
    random = (Math.imul(random, 1664525) + 1013904223) >>> 0;
    return random / 4294967296;
  };
  const formation = Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) => ({
    x: 48 + (i % 3) * 22 + next() * 8,
    z: -57 + Math.floor(i / 3) * 38 + next() * 8,
  }));
  for (const side of [0, 1])
    for (let i = 0; i < formation.length; i++) {
      const sign = side === 0 ? -1 : 1;
      const unit = sim.addUnit(
        i < 4 ? 'light' : i < 6 ? 'turret' : 'heavy',
        { x: formation[i].x * sign, z: formation[i].z * sign },
        side === 0,
      );
      unit.group = side;
    }
  const first = copy({ ...sim.pilotState(), leaseUntil: 0 }),
    second = copy(first);
  second.flight.aircraft.x *= -1;
  second.flight.aircraft.z *= -1;
  second.flight.aircraft.yaw += Math.PI;
  second.flight.aircraft.heading = (second.flight.aircraft.heading + 180) % 360;
  first.aim = { ...formation[0] };
  second.aim = { x: -formation[0].x, z: -formation[0].z };
  return {
    simulation: sim.checkpoint(),
    pilots: [first, second],
    ticks: 0,
    seed,
    bot,
    winner: null,
    finished: false,
    bots: [false, bot],
    botAt: [6, 6],
  };
}
function applyControl(sim: Simulation, input: DuelCommand) {
  check(
    input &&
      typeof input === 'object' &&
      Object.keys(input).length === 2 &&
      Array.isArray(input.args),
  );
  const a = input.args;
  switch (input.command) {
    case 'setAim': {
      const p = a[0] as Point;
      check(
        a.length === 1 &&
          p &&
          Object.keys(p).length === 2 &&
          Number.isFinite(p.x) &&
          Number.isFinite(p.z) &&
          Math.abs(p.x) <= 10000 &&
          Math.abs(p.z) <= 10000,
      );
      sim.setAim(p);
      break;
    }
    case 'choose':
      check(
        a.length === 1 &&
          Number.isInteger(a[0]) &&
          Number(a[0]) >= 0 &&
          Number(a[0]) < WEAPONS.length &&
          sim.hasWeapon(Number(a[0])),
      );
      sim.choose(Number(a[0]));
      break;
    case 'setFire':
      check(
        a.length === 2 &&
          typeof a[0] === 'string' &&
          a[0].length > 0 &&
          a[0].length <= 80 &&
          typeof a[1] === 'boolean',
      );
      check(!a[1] || sim.held.has(a[0] as string) || sim.held.size < 10);
      sim.setFire(a[0] as string, a[1] as boolean);
      break;
    case 'fire':
      check(a.length === 0);
      sim.fire();
      break;
    case 'useSkill':
      check(a.length === 0);
      sim.useSkill();
      break;
    case 'clearInput':
      check(a.length === 0);
      sim.clearInput();
      break;
    case 'adjustAltitude':
    case 'adjustRadius':
      check(a.length === 1 && (a[0] === -20 || a[0] === 20));
      sim[input.command](a[0] as number);
      break;
    case 'setOrbitDirection':
      check(a.length === 1 && (a[0] === -1 || a[0] === 1));
      sim.setOrbitDirection(a[0] as -1 | 1);
      break;
    default:
      throw Error('Unsupported duel command');
  }
}
export function configureDuel(state: DuelState, snapshots: BattleLoadout[]) {
  check(state.ticks === 0 && snapshots.length >= 1 && snapshots.length <= 2);
  const sim = Simulation.restore(state.simulation);
  for (const [seat, snapshot] of snapshots.entries()) {
    sim.usePilot(state.pilots[seat], seat);
    sim.applyLoadout(snapshot);
    state.pilots[seat] = copy({
      ...sim.pilotState(),
      leaseUntil: state.pilots[seat].leaseUntil,
    });
  }
  sim.usePilot(state.pilots[0], 0);
  state.simulation = sim.checkpoint();
  return state;
}
export function commandDuel(
  state: DuelState,
  seat: number,
  commands: DuelCommand[],
) {
  check(
    (seat === 0 || seat === 1) &&
      !state.bots[seat] &&
      !state.finished &&
      Array.isArray(commands) &&
      commands.length <= 64,
  );
  const sim = Simulation.restore(state.simulation),
    pilot = state.pilots[seat];
  sim.usePilot(pilot, seat);
  for (const command of commands) applyControl(sim, command);
  state.pilots[seat] = copy({ ...sim.pilotState(), leaseUntil: sim.time + 1 });
  sim.usePilot(state.pilots[0], 0);
  state.simulation = sim.checkpoint();
  return state;
}
export function takeoverDuel(state: DuelState, seat: number) {
  check(seat === 0 || seat === 1);
  state.bots[seat] = true;
  state.pilots[seat].held = [];
  return state;
}
function botControl(state: DuelState, sim: Simulation, side: number) {
  if (sim.time < state.botAt[side]) return;
  state.botAt[side] = sim.time + 8;
  const targets = sim.units.filter(
    (unit) => unit.group !== side && unit.hp > 0,
  );
  const enemy = targets[Math.floor(sim.time / 20) % targets.length];
  if (!enemy) return;
  if (sim.skillKind && sim.skillCooldown <= 0) sim.useSkill();
  sim.choose(enemy.kind !== 'turret' && sim.guns[2].ammo > 0 ? 2 : 1);
  // A single rough lead deliberately underestimates flight; never solve the exact intercept.
  const flight = sim.flightTime(sim.selected, enemy);
  const aim =
    enemy.kind === 'light' && Number.isFinite(flight)
      ? patrolPoint(
          enemy.origin,
          (enemy.patrolElapsed ?? 0) + flight * 0.7,
          sim.mission.map,
        )
      : enemy;
  const spread = 4;
  sim.setAim({
    x: aim.x + Math.sin(sim.time * 2 + state.seed) * spread,
    z: aim.z + Math.cos(sim.time * 1.7 + side) * spread,
  });
  if (!sim.friendlyRisk) sim.fire();
}
export function advanceDuel(state: DuelState, elapsedMs: number): DuelState {
  check(Number.isFinite(elapsedMs) && elapsedMs >= 0);
  if (state.finished) return state;
  const target = Math.floor((elapsedMs * 60) / 1000);
  check(target >= state.ticks);
  const sim = Simulation.restore(state.simulation);
  while (state.ticks < target && !state.finished) {
    for (const side of [0, 1]) {
      const pilot = state.pilots[side];
      sim.usePilot(pilot, side);
      if (sim.time >= pilot.leaseUntil) sim.clearInput();
      sim.stepPilot(1 / 60);
      if (state.bots[side]) botControl(state, sim, side);
      state.pilots[side] = copy({
        ...sim.pilotState(),
        leaseUntil: pilot.leaseUntil,
      });
    }
    sim.usePilot(state.pilots[0], 0);
    sim.step(1 / 60);
    state.ticks++;
    const alive = [0, 1].map((side) =>
      sim.units.some((unit) => unit.group === side && unit.hp > 0),
    );
    if (!alive[0] || !alive[1]) {
      state.finished = true;
      state.winner = alive[0] === alive[1] ? null : alive[0] ? 0 : 1;
    }
  }
  state.simulation = sim.checkpoint();
  return state;
}
export function viewDuel(state: DuelState, seat = 0) {
  check(seat === 0 || seat === 1);
  const sim = Simulation.restore(state.simulation);
  sim.usePilot(state.pilots[seat], seat);
  for (const event of sim.events)
    if (event.friendly !== undefined && event.side !== undefined)
      event.friendly = event.side === seat;
  if (state.finished) sim.phase = state.winner === seat ? 'success' : 'failure';
  const enemy = state.pilots[1 - seat];
  return {
    enemyEquipment: copy({
      loadout: enemy.loadout,
      selected: enemy.selected,
      guns: enemy.guns,
      skill: enemy.skill,
      buff: enemy.buff,
    }),
    simulation: sim.checkpoint(),
    seed: state.seed,
    bot: state.bot,
    bots: [...state.bots],
    finished: state.finished,
    winner: state.winner,
    you: seat,
    enemyAircraft: copy(state.pilots[1 - seat].flight.aircraft),
    elapsed: sim.time,
    remaining: 0,
  };
}
import {
  AIRFRAME,
  FLIGHT,
  WEAPONS,
  terrainHeight,
  type Point,
  type Point3,
} from './Data.ts';
export type Aircraft = Point3 & {
  heading: number; // Display degrees, forward = (sin(yaw), cos(yaw)).
  yaw: number;
  pitch: number;
  bank: number; // Radians, positive lowers the port wing: Ry(yaw) * Rx(-pitch) * Rz(bank).
  radius: number; // Desired orbit radius; transitions fly a continuous path toward this circle.
  altitude: number;
  direction: -1 | 1; // +1 is clockwise when viewed from above with north = -Z.
};
const clamp = (value: number, min: number, max: number) =>
  Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
export class Flight {
  readonly aircraft: Aircraft = {
    x: 0,
    y: FLIGHT.altitude,
    z: FLIGHT.radius,
    heading: 270,
    yaw: -Math.PI / 2,
    pitch: 0,
    bank: Math.atan(FLIGHT.speed ** 2 / (FLIGHT.gravity * FLIGHT.radius)),
    radius: FLIGHT.radius,
    altitude: FLIGHT.altitude,
    direction: 1,
  };
  private altitude: number = FLIGHT.altitude;
  private radius: number = FLIGHT.radius;
  checkpoint() {
    return {
      aircraft: { ...this.aircraft },
      altitude: this.altitude,
      radius: this.radius,
    };
  }
  restore(value: ReturnType<Flight['checkpoint']>) {
    Object.assign(this.aircraft, value.aircraft);
    this.altitude = value.altitude;
    this.radius = value.radius;
  }
  setDirection(direction: -1 | 1, immediate = false) {
    if (direction !== -1 && direction !== 1) return;
    const a = this.aircraft;
    a.direction = direction;
    if (immediate) {
      const angle = Math.atan2(a.z, a.x);
      a.yaw = Math.atan2(
        -Math.sin(angle) * direction,
        Math.cos(angle) * direction,
      );
      a.heading = ((((a.yaw * 180) / Math.PI) % 360) + 360) % 360;
      a.bank = clamp(
        Math.atan(
          (direction * FLIGHT.speed ** 2) /
            (FLIGHT.gravity * Math.max(FLIGHT.minRadius, Math.hypot(a.x, a.z))),
        ),
        -FLIGHT.maxBank,
        FLIGHT.maxBank,
      );
    }
  }
  adjustAltitude(delta: number, immediate = false) {
    if (!Number.isFinite(delta)) return;
    this.altitude = clamp(
      this.altitude + delta,
      FLIGHT.minAltitude,
      FLIGHT.maxAltitude,
    );
    if (immediate) {
      this.aircraft.y = this.aircraft.altitude = this.altitude;
      this.aircraft.pitch = 0;
    }
  }
  adjustRadius(delta: number, immediate = false) {
    if (!Number.isFinite(delta)) return;
    this.radius = clamp(
      this.radius + delta,
      FLIGHT.minRadius,
      FLIGHT.maxRadius,
    );
    if (immediate) {
      const a = this.aircraft,
        angle = Math.atan2(a.z, a.x);
      a.radius = this.radius;
      a.x = Math.cos(angle) * this.radius;
      a.z = Math.sin(angle) * this.radius;
      this.setDirection(a.direction, true);
    }
  }
  step(dt: number) {
    if (!Number.isFinite(dt) || dt <= 0 || dt > 0.1)
      throw Error('Use fixed steps <= 0.1 seconds');
    const a = this.aircraft,
      y = a.y;
    const blend = 1 - Math.exp(-FLIGHT.response * dt);
    a.radius += clamp(
      (this.radius - a.radius) * blend,
      -FLIGHT.radiusRate * dt,
      FLIGHT.radiusRate * dt,
    );
    a.altitude += clamp(
      (this.altitude - a.altitude) * blend,
      -FLIGHT.climbRate * dt,
      FLIGHT.climbRate * dt,
    );
    a.y = a.altitude;
    const climb = (a.y - y) / dt;
    const speed = Math.sqrt(FLIGHT.speed ** 2 - climb ** 2);
    const radius = Math.hypot(a.x, a.z),
      angle = Math.atan2(a.z, a.x);
    // Tangent plus radial correction guides a constant-speed aircraft back to the selected orbit.
    // Reversal changes the desired heading, never the sign of airspeed (no mid-air stop).
    const radial = clamp(((a.radius - radius) / a.radius) * 2, -1, 1);
    const desired = Math.atan2(
      -Math.sin(angle) * a.direction + Math.cos(angle) * radial,
      Math.cos(angle) * a.direction + Math.sin(angle) * radial,
    );
    const error = Math.atan2(
      Math.sin(desired - a.yaw),
      Math.cos(desired - a.yaw),
    );
    const yawRate =
      error * FLIGHT.response -
      (a.direction * speed) / Math.max(radius, FLIGHT.minRadius);
    // Coordinated-turn relation tan(bank) = v²/(gR) = v * turnRate / g (FAA PHAK ch.5).
    // https://www.faa.gov/sites/faa.gov/files/pilots/pilot_handbook_1.pdf
    const bank = clamp(
      Math.atan((-speed * yawRate) / FLIGHT.gravity),
      -FLIGHT.maxBank,
      FLIGHT.maxBank,
    );
    a.bank += clamp(bank - a.bank, -FLIGHT.rollRate * dt, FLIGHT.rollRate * dt);
    const turn = ((-FLIGHT.gravity * Math.tan(a.bank)) / speed) * dt;
    const midpoint = a.yaw + turn / 2;
    const travel =
      speed *
      dt *
      (Math.abs(turn) < 1e-8 ? 1 : Math.sin(turn / 2) / (turn / 2));
    a.x += Math.sin(midpoint) * travel;
    a.z += Math.cos(midpoint) * travel;
    a.yaw += turn;
    a.pitch = Math.atan2(climb, speed);
    a.heading = ((((a.yaw * 180) / Math.PI) % 360) + 360) % 360;
  }
}
export type AircraftPose = Point3 & {
  yaw: number;
  pitch?: number;
  bank?: number;
};
/** Shared quaternion for the rendered model, physical gun stations and sensor mount. */
export function aircraftRotation(
  a: Pick<AircraftPose, 'yaw' | 'pitch' | 'bank'>,
) {
  const cy = Math.cos(a.yaw / 2),
    sy = Math.sin(a.yaw / 2);
  const cp = Math.cos((a.pitch ?? 0) / 2),
    sp = Math.sin((a.pitch ?? 0) / 2);
  const cb = Math.cos((a.bank ?? 0) / 2),
    sb = Math.sin((a.bank ?? 0) / 2);
  return {
    x: -cy * sp * cb + sy * cp * sb,
    y: sy * cp * cb + cy * sp * sb,
    z: cy * cp * sb + sy * sp * cb,
    w: cy * cp * cb - sy * sp * sb,
  };
}
export function aircraftPoint(a: AircraftPose, mount: Point3): Point3 {
  const q = aircraftRotation(a);
  const x = 2 * (q.y * mount.z - q.z * mount.y);
  const y = 2 * (q.z * mount.x - q.x * mount.z);
  const z = 2 * (q.x * mount.y - q.y * mount.x);
  return {
    x: a.x + mount.x + q.w * x + q.y * z - q.z * y,
    y: a.y + mount.y + q.w * y + q.z * x - q.x * z,
    z: a.z + mount.z + q.w * z + q.x * y - q.y * x,
  };
}
export function muzzlePosition(a: AircraftPose, weapon = 0): Point3 {
  return aircraftPoint(a, WEAPONS[weapon]?.muzzle ?? FLIGHT.muzzle);
}
/** Convert the original metre-authored bay mesh into the selected aircraft-local station. */
export function cabinPoint(point: Point3, weapon = 0): Point3 {
  const mount = WEAPONS[weapon]?.muzzle ?? FLIGHT.muzzle;
  return {
    x: mount.x + (point.x - AIRFRAME.cabinMuzzle.x) * AIRFRAME.cabinScale,
    y: mount.y + (point.y - AIRFRAME.cabinMuzzle.y) * AIRFRAME.cabinScale,
    z: mount.z + (point.z - AIRFRAME.cabinMuzzle.z) * AIRFRAME.cabinScale,
  };
}
// Low ballistic arc at the specified muzzle speed. Stable quadratic root in t².
export function ballisticLaunch(
  origin: Point3,
  target: Point,
  speed: number,
  height = terrainHeight,
) {
  if (
    ![origin.x, origin.y, origin.z, target.x, target.z, speed].every(
      Number.isFinite,
    ) ||
    speed <= 0 ||
    origin.y <= height(origin.x, origin.z)
  )
    return;
  const targetY = height(target.x, target.z);
  const dx = target.x - origin.x,
    dy = targetY - origin.y,
    dz = target.z - origin.z;
  const distance2 = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz;
  const k = speed * speed - FLIGHT.gravity * dy;
  const discriminant = k * k - FLIGHT.gravity * FLIGHT.gravity * distance2;
  if (discriminant < 0 || !Number.isFinite(discriminant)) return;
  const duration = Math.sqrt((2 * distance2) / (k + Math.sqrt(discriminant)));
  if (duration <= 0 || !Number.isFinite(duration)) return;
  return {
    duration,
    targetY,
    velocity: {
      x: dx / duration,
      y: dy / duration + 0.5 * FLIGHT.gravity * duration,
      z: dz / duration,
    },
  };
}
export type Trajectory = {
  origin: Point3;
  velocity: Point3;
  born: number;
  due: number;
  timeScale?: number;
};
export function shotPosition(shot: Trajectory, time: number): Point3 {
  const t =
    (clamp(time, shot.born, shot.due) - shot.born) * (shot.timeScale ?? 1);
  return {
    x: shot.origin.x + shot.velocity.x * t,
    y: shot.origin.y + shot.velocity.y * t - 0.5 * FLIGHT.gravity * t * t,
    z: shot.origin.z + shot.velocity.z * t,
  };
}
export function terrainContact(
  shot: Trajectory,
  from: number,
  to: number,
  height = terrainHeight,
) {
  let start = Math.max(shot.born, from);
  const end = Math.min(shot.due, to);
  if (end < start) return;
  // ponytail: 0.5-unit sweeps resolve these broad hills; use mesh CCD if terrain gains cliffs/caves.
  const scale = shot.timeScale ?? 1;
  const count = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil(
      ((end - start) *
        scale *
        Math.hypot(
          shot.velocity.x,
          shot.velocity.y - FLIGHT.gravity * (end - shot.born) * scale,
          shot.velocity.z,
        )) /
        0.5,
    ),
  );
  const begin = start;
  for (let i = 1; i <= count; i++) {
    let stop = begin + ((end - begin) * i) / count;
    const p = shotPosition(shot, stop);
    if (p.y <= height(p.x, p.z) + 1e-8) {
      for (let n = 0; n < 24; n++) {
        const mid = (start + stop) / 2,
          q = shotPosition(shot, mid);
        if (q.y <= height(q.x, q.z)) stop = mid;
        else start = mid;
      }
      const point = shotPosition(shot, stop);
      point.y = height(point.x, point.z);
      return { time: stop, point };
    }
    start = stop;
  }
}
/** Presentation-only recoil in UI pixels; never feeds the physical gun or aim. */
export function recoilOffset(
  events: readonly { type: string; weapon: number; time: number }[],
  time: number,
  reduced = false,
) {
  let x = 0,
    y = 0;
  for (const e of events.slice(-24)) {
    const age = time - e.time,
      life = [0.12, 0.24, 0.42, 0.3][e.weapon] ?? 0;
    if (e.type !== 'shot' || age < 0 || age >= life) continue;
    const strength =
      ([1.1, 3.4, 7, 4][e.weapon] ?? 0) *
      (1 - age / life) ** 2 *
      (reduced ? 0.2 : 1);
    x += Math.sin(age * 105 + e.time * 8) * strength * 0.45;
    y += (Math.cos(age * 72) * 0.35 + 0.65) * strength;
  }
  return { x: Math.max(-8, Math.min(8, x)), y: Math.max(-8, Math.min(8, y)) };
}
import {
  MISSION,
  MAP,
  UNITS,
  routeLength,
  type BattlefieldId,
  type Kind,
} from './Data.ts';
export type MissionId =
  | 'corridor-01'
  | 'ambush-02'
  | 'patrol-03'
  | `operation-${string}`
  | 'training-60';
export const CAMPAIGN_BOARD_VERSION = 'night-campaign-v2';
export type MissionEvent = {
  time: number;
  progress: number;
  kind: Kind;
  x: number;
  z: number;
  direction: readonly string[];
};
export type MissionLight = 'day' | 'dusk' | 'night';
export type MissionDefinition = Omit<typeof MISSION, 'id' | 'events'> & {
  light: MissionLight;
  initialWeapon: number;
  id: MissionId;
  map: BattlefieldId;
  mode: 'escort' | 'training';
  name: readonly string[];
  description: readonly string[];
  events: readonly MissionEvent[];
};
const introductoryContacts = [
  [0, 5, 13, 17],
  [0, 2, 5, 9, 13, 17],
  [0, 2, 5, 9, 13, 17, 21, 23],
];
const profiles: MissionDefinition[] = (
  ['corridor-01', 'ambush-02', 'patrol-03'] as const
).map((id, index) => ({
  ...MISSION,
  id,
  map: index === 1 ? 'highland' : 'valley',
  mode: 'escort',
  speed: routeLength(index === 1 ? 'highland' : 'valley') / 240,
  light: 'day',
  initialWeapon: 1,
  warmup: 18 - index * 3,
  attackInterval: 7,
  name: [
    ['晨光护送', 'FIRST LIGHT'],
    ['高地警戒', 'HIGHLAND WATCH'],
    ['谷地支援', 'VALLEY SUPPORT'],
  ][index],
  description: [
    `白天 · ${introductoryContacts[index].length} 个固定炮位 · 榴弹炮上手`,
    `DAY · ${introductoryContacts[index].length} stationary emplacements · Start with HE`,
  ],
  events: introductoryContacts[index].map((i) => ({
    ...MISSION.events[i],
    kind: 'turret',
    time: 0,
    progress: 0,
    direction: [
      '指挥部：优先清除友军附近的固定炮位。',
      'COMMAND: clear emplacements near our convoy first.',
    ],
  })),
}));
// Stable deterministic content; all 120 entries use the existing escort simulator.
// Existing IDs and unlock progress survive the introductory rebalance.
const chapters = [
  '谷地晨哨',
  '高地警戒',
  '河岸巡航',
  '山路截击',
  '车队守护',
  '据点支援',
  '夜色伏击',
  '机动围剿',
  '长夜突围',
  '终夜守望',
];
for (let number = 4; number <= 120; number++) {
  const chapter = Math.floor((number - 1) / 12),
    task = (number - 1) % 12;
  const map: BattlefieldId = (chapter + task) % 2 ? 'highland' : 'valley';
  const style = task % 3;
  const spans = [
    [6, 12, 20, 26],
    [13, 24, 28, 36],
    [25, 48, 38, 54],
    [49, 72, 56, 70],
    [73, 96, 72, 84],
    [97, 120, 86, 96],
  ];
  const span = spans.find(([from, to]) => number >= from && number <= to);
  const total =
    number === 4
      ? 12
      : number === 5
        ? 16
        : Math.round(
            span![2] +
              ((span![3] - span![2]) * (number - span![0])) /
                (span![1] - span![0]),
          );
  const waveCount = number <= 5 ? 1 : Math.max(2, Math.ceil(total / 18));
  const duration = 420 - chapter * 3;
  const contacts = Array.from(
    { length: total },
    (_, i) => MISSION.events[i % MISSION.events.length],
  );
  const events = contacts.map((event, i): MissionEvent => {
    const wave = Math.floor((i * waveCount) / contacts.length);
    const jitterX = (((number * 37 + i * 29) % 127) - 63) / 32;
    const jitterZ = (((number * 41 + i * 23) % 113) - 56) / 32;
    // Late chapters introduce a few additional armored contacts, without inflating waves.
    const kind: Kind =
      style === 2 && event.kind === 'turret'
        ? 'light'
        : chapter >= 5 && i === 4 + chapter
          ? 'heavy'
          : event.kind;
    return {
      ...event,
      kind,
      x: event.x + jitterX,
      z: event.z + jitterZ,
      time: wave * 36,
      progress: wave / waveCount,
      direction: [
        `第 ${wave + 1} / ${waveCount} 批威胁出现 · 巡视四组友军`,
        `CONTACT WAVE ${wave + 1}/${waveCount} · Check all four friendly groups`,
      ],
    };
  });
  profiles.push({
    ...MISSION,
    id: `operation-${String(number).padStart(3, '0')}`,
    map,
    mode: 'escort',
    light: number <= 5 ? 'dusk' : number % 2 ? 'day' : 'night',
    initialWeapon: number <= 5 ? 1 : 0,
    speed: routeLength(map) / 240,
    duration,
    warmup: 10 - chapter * 0.2,
    attackInterval: 5.8 - chapter * 0.1,
    name: [
      `${chapters[chapter]} ${task + 1}`,
      `NIGHT ${chapter + 1} · ${task + 1}`,
    ],
    description: [
      `${waveCount} 批威胁 · ${map === 'valley' ? '河谷' : '高地'}${style === 2 ? '机动目标' : '火力部署'} · 保护四组友军`,
      `${waveCount} waves · ${map.toUpperCase()} ${style === 2 ? 'ROVERS' : 'DEPLOYMENT'} · Protect four groups`,
    ],
    events,
  });
}
export const TRAINING: MissionDefinition = {
  light: 'day',
  initialWeapon: 1,
  ...MISSION,
  id: 'training-60',
  map: 'valley',
  mode: 'training',
  duration: 60,
  speed: 0,
  name: ['火控热身', 'FIRE CONTROL'],
  description: [
    '静止 / 巡逻 / 重甲 · 练切炮与提前量',
    'Static / rover / armor · Switch & lead',
  ],
  events: [
    {
      time: 0,
      progress: 0,
      kind: 'turret',
      x: -108,
      z: 25,
      direction: [
        '静止目标用榴弹 · 巡逻目标看提前量 · 重甲用重炮',
        'HE for static · Lead rovers · Heavy for armor',
      ],
    },
    {
      time: 0,
      progress: 0,
      kind: 'light',
      x: -109,
      z: 70,
      direction: [
        '巡逻目标：准星放到弹着时的位置',
        'Rover: aim where it will be at impact',
      ],
    },
    {
      time: 0,
      progress: 0,
      kind: 'heavy',
      x: -91,
      z: 49,
      direction: [
        '练瞄准，友军仍不能误伤 · 清除三个目标',
        'Practice aiming. Avoid allies. Clear three targets.',
      ],
    },
  ],
};
if (
  new Set([...profiles, TRAINING].map((mission) => mission.id)).size !==
  profiles.length + 1
)
  throw new Error('Duplicate mission ID.');
for (const profile of [...profiles, TRAINING]) {
  if (
    profile.duration <=
      (profile.speed ? routeLength(profile.map) / profile.speed : 0) ||
    profile.attackInterval <= 0 ||
    profile.warmup < 0
  )
    throw new Error('Invalid mission timing.');
  if (
    !['day', 'dusk', 'night'].includes(profile.light) ||
    ![0, 1, 2].includes(profile.initialWeapon) ||
    profile.events.length < 3 ||
    profile.events.length > 96 ||
    profile.events.some(
      (event) =>
        !UNITS[event.kind] ||
        ![event.time, event.progress, event.x, event.z].every(
          Number.isFinite,
        ) ||
        event.time < 0 ||
        event.time >= profile.duration ||
        event.progress < 0 ||
        event.progress > 1 ||
        Math.abs(event.x) > MAP.halfWidth ||
        Math.abs(event.z) > MAP.halfDepth,
    )
  )
    throw new Error('Invalid mission profile.');
  for (const event of profile.events) {
    Object.freeze(event.direction);
    Object.freeze(event);
  }
  Object.freeze(profile.events);
  Object.freeze(profile.name);
  Object.freeze(profile.description);
  Object.freeze(profile);
}
export const MISSIONS: readonly MissionDefinition[] = Object.freeze(profiles);
export function missionDefinition(id: unknown): MissionDefinition {
  return id === TRAINING.id
    ? TRAINING
    : MISSIONS.find((mission) => mission.id === id) || MISSIONS[0];
}
export function nextMission(id: MissionId): MissionId {
  const index = MISSIONS.findIndex((mission) => mission.id === id);
  return MISSIONS[Math.min(MISSIONS.length - 1, Math.max(0, index + 1))].id;
}
export function readMissionSearch(search: string): MissionId | undefined {
  const values = new URLSearchParams(search).getAll('mission');
  return values.length === 1 &&
    [...MISSIONS, TRAINING].some((mission) => mission.id === values[0])
    ? (values[0] as MissionId)
    : undefined;
}
import { MISSIONS, type MissionId } from './MissionCatalog.ts';
export type MissionProgress = { version: 1; cleared: MissionId[] };
export const newMissionProgress = (): MissionProgress => ({
  version: 1,
  cleared: [],
});
export function readMissionProgress(raw: string | null): MissionProgress {
  try {
    const data = raw && raw.length <= 16000 ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    if (data?.version !== 1 || !Array.isArray(data.cleared))
      return newMissionProgress();
    const candidates = new Set(
      data.cleared.filter((id: unknown) => typeof id === 'string'),
    );
    const cleared: MissionId[] = [];
    for (const [index, mission] of MISSIONS.entries())
      if (
        candidates.has(mission.id) &&
        (index < 3 || cleared.includes(MISSIONS[index - 1].id))
      )
        cleared.push(mission.id);
    return { version: 1, cleared };
  } catch {
    return newMissionProgress();
  }
}
export function missionUnlocked(
  progress: MissionProgress,
  id: MissionId,
): boolean {
  const index = MISSIONS.findIndex((m) => m.id === id);
  // Preserve all three existing public tasks during migration.
  return (
    index >= 0 &&
    (index < 3 ||
      progress.cleared.includes(id) ||
      progress.cleared.includes(MISSIONS[index - 1].id))
  );
}
export function completeMission(
  progress: MissionProgress,
  id: MissionId,
  success: boolean,
  practice = false,
): MissionProgress {
  if (
    !success ||
    practice ||
    !missionUnlocked(progress, id) ||
    progress.cleared.includes(id)
  )
    return progress;
  return { version: 1, cleared: [...progress.cleared, id] };
}

export function eligibleDrops(
  catalog: Product[],
  tier: number,
  level: number,
  permanent: readonly string[],
) {
  return catalog.filter(
    (p) =>
      p.term === 'match' &&
      p.category !== 'skill' &&
      p.rarity === tier &&
      p.unlockLevel <= level &&
      !permanent.includes(p.effect),
  );
}
export function levelProgress(xp: number) {
  const total = Math.max(0, Math.floor(xp));
  let level = 1,
    floor = 0;
  while (level < 50 && total >= floor + level * 200) {
    floor += level * 200;
    level++;
  }
  return { level, current: total - floor, required: level * 200 };
}
export const emptyLoadout = (): Loadout => ({
  mods: [],
  weapon: null,
  skill: null,
});
export function validLoadout(value: unknown): value is Loadout {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return false;
  const v = value as Loadout;
  if (
    Object.keys(v).sort().join(',') !== 'mods,skill,weapon' ||
    !Array.isArray(v.mods) ||
    v.mods.length > 2
  )
    return false;
  const ids = [
    ...v.mods,
    ...[v.weapon, v.skill].filter((id): id is string => id !== null),
  ];
  if (ids.some((id) => typeof id !== 'string')) return false;
  const products = ids.map((id) => CATALOG.find((p) => p.id === id));
  return (
    products.every(Boolean) &&
    new Set(products.map((p) => p!.effect)).size === ids.length &&
    v.mods.every((id) =>
      CATALOG.some((p) => p.id === id && p.category === 'mod'),
    ) &&
    (v.weapon === null ||
      CATALOG.some((p) => p.id === v.weapon && p.category === 'weapon')) &&
    (v.skill === null ||
      CATALOG.some((p) => p.id === v.skill && p.category === 'skill'))
  );
}
export function validBattleLoadout(value: unknown): value is BattleLoadout {
  const v = value as BattleLoadout;
  if (
    !v ||
    v.version !== GROWTH_VERSION ||
    !Number.isInteger(v.level) ||
    v.level < 1 ||
    v.level > 50 ||
    !Array.isArray(v.effects)
  )
    return false;
  return (
    v.effects.every((e) => effects.some(([id]) => id === e)) &&
    new Set(v.effects).size === v.effects.length &&
    v.effects.filter((e) => effects.find(([id]) => id === e)?.[1] === 'mod')
      .length <= 2 &&
    v.effects.filter((e) => effects.find(([id]) => id === e)?.[1] === 'weapon')
      .length <= 1 &&
    v.effects.filter((e) => effects.find(([id]) => id === e)?.[1] === 'skill')
      .length <= 1
  );
}
export const trainingLoadout = (effect: Effect): BattleLoadout => ({
  version: GROWTH_VERSION,
  level: 1,
  effects: [effect],
  skipped: [],
});
export const attackPreview = (effects: readonly string[]) => {
  const shots =
    (effects.includes('burst') ? 2 : 1) * (effects.includes('twin') ? 2 : 1);
  return {
    shots,
    secondaryStrength: shots > 1 ? 0.6 : 0,
    heatMultiplier: effects.includes('cooling') ? 0.85 : 1,
  };
};
export const PROGRESSION = {
  version: GROWTH_VERSION,
  catalog: CATALOG,
  packages: PACKAGES,
  pools: POOLS,
  skills: SKILLS,
  wheelPrizes: WHEEL_PRIZES,
  levelProgress,
  validLoadout,
};
/** Practice uses a server snapshot only; ranked matches still require the authoritative reservation. */
export function practiceLoadout(
  assets: AssetSnapshot | undefined,
  mode: 'solo' | 'duel',
  elapsedMs = 0,
): BattleLoadout {
  const loadout: BattleLoadout = {
    version: GROWTH_VERSION,
    level: assets?.level || 1,
    effects: [],
    skipped: [],
  };
  if (!assets) return loadout;
  const saved = assets.loadouts[mode],
    now = assets.serverNow + Math.max(0, elapsedMs);
  for (const id of [...saved.mods, saved.weapon, saved.skill]) {
    if (!id) continue;
    const product = assets.catalog.find((p) => p.id === id);
    if (
      product &&
      (assets.permanent.includes(product.effect) ||
        (assets.active[product.effect] || 0) > now ||
        (product.term === 'match' && (assets.available[id] || 0) > 0))
    )
      loadout.effects.push(product.effect);
    else loadout.skipped.push(id);
  }
  return loadout;
}
import { Simulation, type PauseReason } from './Simulation.ts';
import { WEAPONS } from './Data.ts';
import { MISSIONS, TRAINING } from './MissionCatalog.ts';
export const RANKED_RULE_VERSION = 'night-input-replay-v14';
export type ReplayFrame = { input: Record<string, unknown>; ticks: number };
export type NightReplay = {
  simulation: ReturnType<Simulation['checkpoint']>;
  assisted: boolean;
};
const commands = [
  'setOrbitDirection',
  'adjustAltitude',
  'adjustRadius',
  'pause',
  'beginResumeCountdown',
  'stepCountdown',
  'clearInput',
  'choose',
  'selectHoming',
  'lockHoming',
  'setAim',
  'setFire',
  'command',
  'fire',
  'useSkill',
  'grantBuff',
  'grantAmmoSupply',
  'grantHomingAmmo',
  'setExtractionRadioPending',
] as const;
const pauseReasons: PauseReason[] = [
  'help',
  'settings',
  'mission',
  'manual',
  'orientation',
  'background',
  'focus',
  'advert',
  'supply',
  'countdown',
];
function requireInput(ok: unknown): asserts ok {
  if (!ok) throw new Error('Invalid night input');
}
const finite = (value: unknown): value is number =>
  typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
export function createNightReplay(
  board: string,
  levelId?: string,
): NightReplay {
  const mission =
    board === 'training' && levelId === undefined
      ? TRAINING
      : board === 'campaign'
        ? MISSIONS.find((item) => item.id === levelId)
        : undefined;
  requireInput(mission);
  const sim = new Simulation(mission.id);
  sim.start();
  return { simulation: sim.checkpoint(), assisted: false };
}
function command(
  sim: Simulation,
  input: Record<string, unknown>,
  state: NightReplay,
) {
  requireInput(
    Object.keys(input).length === 2 &&
      Object.hasOwn(input, 'command') &&
      Object.hasOwn(input, 'args'),
  );
  const args = input.args;
  requireInput(Array.isArray(args));
  const one = () => requireInput(args.length === 1);
  const none = () => requireInput(args.length === 0);
  switch (input.command) {
    case 'setOrbitDirection':
      one();
      requireInput(args[0] === -1 || args[0] === 1);
      sim.setOrbitDirection(args[0]);
      break;
    case 'adjustAltitude':
    case 'adjustRadius':
      one();
      requireInput(args[0] === -20 || args[0] === 20);
      sim[input.command](args[0]);
      break;
    case 'pause':
      requireInput(
        args.length === 2 &&
          pauseReasons.includes(args[0]) &&
          typeof args[1] === 'boolean',
      );
      sim.pause(args[0], args[1]);
      break;
    case 'beginResumeCountdown':
      none();
      sim.beginResumeCountdown();
      break;
    case 'stepCountdown':
      one();
      requireInput(finite(args[0]) && args[0] > 0 && args[0] <= 0.1);
      sim.stepCountdown(args[0]);
      break;
    case 'clearInput':
      none();
      sim.clearInput();
      break;
    case 'choose':
      one();
      requireInput(
        Number.isInteger(args[0]) &&
          args[0] >= 0 &&
          args[0] < WEAPONS.length &&
          sim.hasWeapon(args[0]),
      );
      sim.choose(args[0]);
      break;
    case 'selectHoming':
      none();
      sim.selectHoming();
      break;
    case 'lockHoming':
      one();
      requireInput(
        Number.isSafeInteger(args[0]) && args[0] > 0 && args[0] <= 10000,
      );
      sim.lockHoming(args[0]);
      break;
    case 'setAim': {
      one();
      const point = args[0];
      requireInput(
        point &&
          typeof point === 'object' &&
          !Array.isArray(point) &&
          Object.keys(point).length === 2 &&
          finite(point.x) &&
          finite(point.z) &&
          Math.abs(point.x) <= 10000 &&
          Math.abs(point.z) <= 10000,
      );
      sim.setAim({ x: point.x, z: point.z });
      break;
    }
    case 'setFire':
      requireInput(
        args.length === 2 &&
          typeof args[0] === 'string' &&
          args[0].length > 0 &&
          args[0].length <= 80 &&
          typeof args[1] === 'boolean',
      );
      requireInput(!args[1] || sim.held.has(args[0]) || sim.held.size < 10);
      sim.setFire(args[0], args[1]);
      break;
    case 'command':
      none();
      sim.command();
      break;
    case 'setExtractionRadioPending':
      one();
      requireInput(typeof args[0] === 'boolean');
      sim.setExtractionRadioPending(args[0]);
      break;
    case 'fire':
      none();
      sim.fire();
      break;
    case 'useSkill':
      none();
      sim.useSkill();
      break;
    case 'grantHomingAmmo':
      one();
      requireInput(Number.isSafeInteger(args[0]) && args[0] > 0);
      sim.grantHomingAmmo(args[0]);
      state.assisted = true;
      break;
    case 'grantAmmoSupply':
      none();
      state.assisted = true;
      sim.grantAmmoSupply();
      break;
    case 'grantBuff':
      one();
      requireInput(args[0] === 'tracking' || args[0] === 'rate');
      state.assisted = true;
      sim.grantBuff(args[0]);
      break;
    default:
      throw new Error('Unknown night input');
  }
}
export function applyNightReplay(
  state: NightReplay,
  frames: ReplayFrame[],
): NightReplay {
  const sim = Simulation.restore(state.simulation);
  for (const { input, ticks } of frames) {
    requireInput(
      input &&
        typeof input === 'object' &&
        !Array.isArray(input) &&
        Number.isSafeInteger(ticks) &&
        ticks >= 0 &&
        ticks <= 2400 &&
        ticks % 2 === 0,
    );
    requireInput(sim.phase === 'playing');
    if (Object.keys(input).length) {
      requireInput(ticks === 0);
      command(sim, input, state);
    } else {
      requireInput(ticks > 0 && !sim.paused);
      for (let tick = 0; tick < ticks; tick += 2) {
        requireInput(sim.phase === 'playing');
        sim.step(1 / 60);
      }
    }
  }
  state.simulation = sim.checkpoint();
  return state;
}
export function nightReplayResult(state: NightReplay) {
  const sim = Simulation.restore(state.simulation);
  const complete = sim.phase === 'success' || sim.phase === 'failure';
  const training = sim.mission.mode === 'training';
  const cleared =
    complete &&
    sim.phase === 'success' &&
    !sim.threatsRemaining &&
    !sim.training &&
    (!training || (!sim.friendlyDamage && !sim.friendlyLosses));
  const eligible = cleared && !state.assisted;
  return {
    complete,
    eligible,
    cleared,
    fastestEligible: eligible,
    assisted: state.assisted,
    participated: sim.ammoSpent >= 3 && sim.hitShots > 0,
    score: eligible
      ? training
        ? 1
        : MISSIONS.findIndex((item) => item.id === sim.mission.id) + 1
      : 0,
    secondary: Math.round((sim.completionTime ?? sim.time) * 1000),
  };
}
/** Observe public controls only; nested fire/clear calls remain owned by the simulation. */
export function observeNightInputs(
  sim: Simulation,
  record: (input: Record<string, unknown>, ticks: number) => void,
) {
  let depth = 0;
  for (const name of commands) {
    const original = sim[name];
    Object.defineProperty(sim, name, {
      configurable: true,
      writable: true,
      value: (...args: unknown[]) => {
        if (depth === 0) {
          const point = args[0] as { x: number; z: number };
          const values =
            name === 'setAim' ? [{ x: point.x, z: point.z }] : args;
          record(
            { command: name, args: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(values)) },
            0,
          );
        }
        depth++;
        try {
          return Reflect.apply(original, sim, args);
        } finally {
          depth--;
        }
      },
    });
  }
  const step = sim.step;
  Object.defineProperty(sim, 'step', {
    configurable: true,
    writable: true,
    value: (dt: number) => {
      if (depth === 0 && sim.phase === 'playing' && !sim.paused)
        record(
          dt === 1 / 60 ? {} : { invalidStep: true },
          dt === 1 / 60 ? 2 : 0,
        );
      depth++;
      try {
        return step.call(sim, dt);
      } finally {
        depth--;
      }
    },
  });
}
import { ZOOM_LEVELS } from './Data.ts';
export type RewardKind = 'supply' | 'zoom';
export type SupplyReward = 'tracking' | 'ammo' | 'rate' | 'restock';
export type RewardOutcome = {
  status: 'completed' | 'dismissed' | 'unavailable' | 'failed';
};
// Structurally compatible with GameHost.ads.offer; the SDK bridge owns playback and completion.
export type RewardProvider = (opportunity: {
  id: string;
  reward: Record<string, number>;
}) => Promise<RewardOutcome>;
export type RewardSave = {
  ammo: number;
  zoomLimit: number;
  pendingSupply: boolean;
};
export function readRewards(raw: string | null): RewardSave {
  try {
    const data = JSON.parse(raw || '{}');
    return {
      ammo: Number.isSafeInteger(data?.ammo) && data.ammo >= 0 ? data.ammo : 0,
      zoomLimit: ZOOM_LEVELS.some((value) => value === data?.zoomLimit)
        ? Math.max(10, data.zoomLimit)
        : 10,
      pendingSupply: data?.pendingSupply === true,
    };
  } catch {
    return { ammo: 0, zoomLimit: 10, pendingSupply: false };
  }
}
export const nextZoomLimit = (limit: number) =>
  ZOOM_LEVELS.find((value) => value > limit);
export type Insets = {
  left: number;
  right: number;
  top: number;
  bottom: number;
};
export function nativeHosts(root: any = globalThis): any[] {
  return [root.tap, root.wx, root.bl, root.tt, root.my].filter(Boolean);
}
/** Host values are CSS/window coordinates, never device pixels. */
export function safeArea(
  width: number,
  height: number,
  baseline: Insets,
  hosts = nativeHosts(),
) {
  const result = { ...baseline, capsule: false };
  const valid = (n: unknown, max: number): n is number =>
    typeof n === 'number' && Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && n <= max;
  for (const sdk of hosts)
    try {
      const get =
        sdk.getWindowInfo || sdk.getWindowInfoSync || sdk.getSystemInfoSync;
      const info = get?.call(sdk);
      if (!info || info.windowWidth !== width || info.windowHeight !== height)
        continue;
      const area = info.safeArea;
      if (
        area &&
        valid(area.top, height) &&
        valid(area.bottom, height) &&
        valid(area.left, width) &&
        valid(area.right, width) &&
        area.bottom >= area.top &&
        area.right >= area.left
      ) {
        result.top = Math.max(result.top, area.top);
        result.bottom = Math.max(result.bottom, height - area.bottom);
        result.left = Math.max(result.left, area.left);
        result.right = Math.max(result.right, width - area.right);
      }
      if (valid(info.statusBarHeight, height))
        result.top = Math.max(result.top, info.statusBarHeight);
      const capsule = sdk.getMenuButtonBoundingClientRect?.call(sdk);
      if (
        capsule &&
        valid(capsule.top, height) &&
        valid(capsule.bottom, height) &&
        capsule.bottom > capsule.top
      ) {
        result.top = Math.max(result.top, capsule.bottom);
        result.capsule = true;
      }
    } catch {
      /* Unavailable native APIs retain engine safe area. */
    }
  return result;
}
export function watchSafeArea(changed: () => void, hosts = nativeHosts()) {
  const attached: any[] = [];
  for (const sdk of hosts)
    try {
      if (sdk.onWindowResize) {
        sdk.onWindowResize.call(sdk, changed);
        attached.push(sdk);
      }
    } catch {}
  return () => {
    for (const sdk of attached)
      try {
        sdk.offWindowResize?.call(sdk, changed);
      } catch {}
  };
}
/** Whole code points and a visible ellipsis, without font shrink or wrapping. */
export function fitName(
  name: string,
  width: number,
  font: number,
  measure = (s: string) => Array.from(s).length * font,
) {
  const chars = Array.from(name);
  if (measure(name) <= width) return name;
  while (chars.length && measure(chars.join('') + '…') > width) chars.pop();
  return chars.join('') + '…';
}

export type PauseReason =
  | 'help'
  | 'settings'
  | 'mission'
  | 'manual'
  | 'orientation'
  | 'background'
  | 'focus'
  | 'advert'
  | 'supply'
  | 'countdown';
export type BuffKind = 'tracking' | 'rate';
export type ConvoyState = 'moving' | 'holdRequested' | 'holding' | 'arrived';
export type Unit = Point3 & {
  id: number;
  kind: Kind;
  hp: number;
  maxHp: number;
  friendly: boolean;
  heading: number;
  born: number;
  attack: number;
  hit: number;
  deadAt: number;
  origin: Point & { patrolId?: number };
  patrolElapsed?: number;
  group?: number;
  routeOffset?: number;
  rootedUntil?: number;
  immuneUntil?: number;
  slowedUntil?: number;
};
export type Shot = Point & {
  strength?: number;
  damageMultiplier?: number;
  radiusScale?: number;
  trackingUntil?: number;
  side?: number;
  id: number;
  weapon: number;
  born: number;
  due: number;
  origin: Point & { y: number };
  velocity: Point3;
  targetY: number;
  timeScale?: number;
  guidance?: {
    kind: 'tracking' | 'consumable';
    target: number;
    position: Point3;
    trail: (Point3 & { time: number })[];
  };
};

  id: number;
  radius: number;
  born: number;
  expires: number;
  side?: number;
};

  volley: number;
  hitCounted: boolean;
  side: number;
  expires: number;
  budget: number;
  nextAttack: number;
  target?: number;
};
