  id: number;
  side: number;
  targetId: number;
  origin: Point3;
  remaining: number;
  budget: number;
  hitCounted: boolean;
};
export type BattleEvent = Point & {
  side?: number;
  id: number;
  type: 'shot' | 'impact' | 'kill' | 'attack' | 'wave';
  weapon: number;
  time: number;
  unit?: number;
  outcome?: 'hit' | 'miss' | 'armor' | 'friendly' | 'destroyed';
  damage?: number;
  y?: number;
  shot?: number;
  intercepted?: boolean;
  target?: Point3;
  friendly?: boolean;
};
export type FireReason =
  | 'ready'
  | 'briefing'
  | 'paused'
  | 'finished'
  | 'extracting'
  | 'protected'
  | 'overheated'
  | 'empty'
  | 'cooldown'
  | 'target'
  | 'outside';
export class Simulation {
  readonly mission: MissionDefinition;
  phase: 'briefing' | 'playing' | 'success' | 'failure' = 'briefing';
  pauses = new Set<PauseReason>();
  buff?: { kind: BuffKind; remaining: number };
  loadout: BattleLoadout = {
    version: GROWTH_VERSION,
    level: 1,
    effects: [],
    skipped: [],
  };
  skill = { activeUntil: 0, readyAt: 0 };
  drones: Drone[] = [];
  lasers: Laser[] = [];
  resumeCountdown = 0;
  resumeRequired = false;
  time = 0;
  progress = 0;
  convoy: ConvoyState = 'moving';
  extractionRemaining?: number;
  extractionRadioPending = false;
  completionTime?: number;
  selected = 0;
  homingAmmo = 0;
  homingSelected = false;
  homingTargetId?: number;
  aim: Point = { x: MISSION.events[0].x, z: MISSION.events[0].z };
  guns = WEAPONS.map((w) => ({
    heat: 0,
    ammo: Number(w.ammo),
    cooldown: 0,
    cooldownTotal: 0,
    overheated: false,
  }));
  units: Unit[] = [];
  shots: Shot[] = [];
  private pendingShotEvents: number[] = [];
  fireZones: FireZone[] = [];
  events: BattleEvent[] = [];
  spawned = new Set<number>();
  completed = new Set<string>();
  held = new Set<string>();
  kills = 0;
  friendlyKills = 0;
  fired = 0;
  ammoSpent = 0;
  hits = 0;
  hitShots = 0;
  friendlyDamage = 0;
  rescueDamage = { friendly: 0, enemy: 0 };
  damageByThreat: Partial<Record<Kind, number>> = {};
  failureCause: '' | 'friendly' | 'enemy' = '';
  // 0: critical rescue vehicle; 1–3: the corresponding outpost lost its last survivor.
  failedGroup: number | undefined;
  friendHitAt = -100;
  failure: '' | 'vehicle' | 'timeout' = '';
  lastWave = -1;
  waveAt = -100;
  warning: '' | 'armor' | 'lead' = '';
  misses = 0;
  training = false;
  duelMode = false;
  pilotSide = 0;
  duelStats = [this.combatStats(), this.combatStats()];
  private combatStats() {
    return {
      kills: this.kills,
      fired: this.fired,
      ammoSpent: this.ammoSpent,
      hits: this.hits,
      hitShots: this.hitShots,
      friendlyDamage: this.friendlyDamage,
      friendHitAt: this.friendHitAt,
      misses: this.misses,
      warning: this.warning,
    };
  }
  private useSide(side: number) {
    this.duelStats[this.pilotSide] = this.combatStats();
    Object.assign(this, this.duelStats[side]);
    this.pilotSide = side;
    for (const unit of this.units) unit.friendly = unit.group === side;
  }
  private flight = new Flight();
  readonly aircraft = this.flight.aircraft;
  private serial = 0;
  pilotState() {
    return {
      flight: this.flight.checkpoint(),
      guns: this.guns.map((gun) => ({ ...gun })),
      aim: { ...this.aim },
      selected: this.selected,
      held: Array.from(this.held),
      loadout: {
        ...this.loadout,
        effects: [...this.loadout.effects],
        skipped: [...this.loadout.skipped],
      },
      skill: { ...this.skill },
      buff: this.buff ? { ...this.buff } : undefined,
    };
  }
  usePilot(pilot: ReturnType<Simulation['pilotState']>, side: number) {
    this.useSide(side);
    this.flight.restore(pilot.flight);
    this.guns = pilot.guns.map((gun, i) => ({
      ...gun,
      cooldownTotal: gun.cooldownTotal ?? WEAPONS[i].interval,
      ammo: Number.isFinite(WEAPONS[i].ammo) ? gun.ammo : Infinity,
    }));
    this.aim = { ...pilot.aim };
    this.selected = pilot.selected;
    this.held = new Set(pilot.held);
    this.loadout = JSON.parse(
      JSON.stringify(
        pilot.loadout ?? {
          version: GROWTH_VERSION,
          level: 1,
          effects: [],
          skipped: [],
        },
      ),
    );
    this.skill = { ...(pilot.skill ?? { activeUntil: 0, readyAt: 0 }) };
    this.buff = pilot.buff ? { ...pilot.buff } : undefined;
  }
  stepPilot(dt: number) {
    this.flight.step(dt);
    this.updateLaserOrigin();
    for (const gun of this.guns) {
      gun.cooldown = Math.max(0, gun.cooldown - dt);
      gun.heat = Math.max(0, gun.heat - 18 * dt);
      if (gun.overheated && gun.heat <= 40) gun.overheated = false;
    }
    if (this.held.size && WEAPONS[this.selected].automatic) this.fire();
    if (this.buff) {
      this.buff.remaining = Math.max(0, this.buff.remaining - dt);
      if (this.buff.remaining <= 1e-8) this.buff = undefined;
    }
  }
  applyLoadout(loadout: BattleLoadout) {
    if (this.time !== 0 || this.fired || !validBattleLoadout(loadout))
      throw Error('Invalid battle loadout');
    this.loadout = {
      ...loadout,
      effects: [...loadout.effects],
      skipped: [...loadout.skipped],
    };
    for (const index of [4, 5])
      this.guns[index].ammo = loadout.effects.includes(
        index === 4 ? 'drone' : 'laser',
      )
        ? WEAPONS[index].ammo
        : 0;
  }
  get skillKind() {
    return this.loadout.effects.includes('tracking')
      ? 'tracking'
      : this.loadout.effects.includes('emp')
        ? 'emp'
        : null;
  }
  get skillRemaining() {
    return Math.max(0, this.skill.activeUntil - this.time);
  }
  get skillCooldown() {
    return Math.max(0, this.skill.readyAt - this.time);
  }
  get trackingActive() {
    return (
      this.buff?.kind === 'tracking' ||
      (this.skillKind === 'tracking' && this.skillRemaining > 0)
    );
  }
  useSkill() {
    const kind = this.skillKind;
    if (
      !kind ||
      this.phase !== 'playing' ||
      this.paused ||
      this.time < this.skill.readyAt ||
      this.extractionRemaining !== undefined
    )
      return false;
    if (kind === 'tracking' && this.trackingActive) return false;
    const spec = SKILLS[kind];
    this.skill.activeUntil = this.time + spec.duration;
    this.skill.readyAt = this.skill.activeUntil + spec.cooldown;
    if (kind === 'emp')
      for (const unit of this.units)
        if (this.canTrack(unit, 0) && this.time >= (unit.immuneUntil ?? 0)) {
          unit.rootedUntil = this.skill.activeUntil;
          unit.immuneUntil = this.skill.activeUntil + SKILLS.emp.immunity;
        }
    return true;
  }
  private radiusScale(weapon = this.selected) {
    return weapon >= 1 && weapon <= 3 && this.loadout.effects.includes('radius')
      ? 1.15
      : 1;
  }
  get effectiveRadius() {
    return WEAPONS[this.selected].radius * this.radiusScale();
  }
  get attackCount() {
    return this.selected < 4 ? attackPreview(this.loadout.effects).shots : 1;
  }
  private get powerMultiplier() {
    return (
      1 +
      (this.loadout.level >= 3 ? 0.05 : 0) +
      (this.loadout.effects.includes('power') ? 0.15 : 0)
    );
  }
  checkpoint() {
    const {
      flight,
      height: _height,
      mission,
      aircraft: _aircraft,
      pauses,
      spawned,
      completed,
      held,
      ...state
    } = this;
    const value = {
      ...state,
      missionId: mission.id,
      flight: flight.checkpoint(),
      pauses: Array.from(pauses),
      spawned: Array.from(spawned),
      completed: Array.from(completed),
      held: Array.from(held),
    };
    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value)) as typeof value;
  }
  static restore(value: ReturnType<Simulation['checkpoint']>) {
    const { missionId, flight, pauses, spawned, completed, held, ...state } =
      JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value)) as typeof value;
    const sim = new Simulation(missionId);
    Object.assign(sim, state);
    sim.ammoSpent = state.ammoSpent ?? state.fired;
    for (const stats of sim.duelStats) stats.ammoSpent ??= stats.fired;
    sim.fireZones = state.fireZones ?? [];
    sim.lasers = state.lasers ?? [];
    sim.guns = WEAPONS.map(
      (weapon, index) =>
        sim.guns[index] ?? {
          heat: 0,
          ammo: Number(weapon.ammo),
          cooldown: 0,
          cooldownTotal: 0,
          overheated: false,
        },
    );
    // JSON stores unlimited ammunition as null; its value comes from the weapon rules.
    sim.guns.forEach((gun, index) => {
      gun.cooldownTotal ??= WEAPONS[index].interval;
      if (!Number.isFinite(WEAPONS[index].ammo)) gun.ammo = Infinity;
    });
    sim.flight.restore(flight);
    sim.pauses = new Set(pauses);
    sim.spawned = new Set(spawned);
    sim.completed = new Set(completed);
    sim.held = new Set(held);
    return sim;
  }
  constructor(missionId: MissionId = 'corridor-01') {
    validateData();
    this.mission = missionDefinition(missionId);
    this.selected = this.mission.initialWeapon;
    this.guns[4].ammo = 0;
    this.guns[5].ammo = 0;
    this.aim = { x: this.mission.events[0].x, z: this.mission.events[0].z };
    Object.assign(this.addUnit('rescue', this.routePoint(0), true), {
      group: 0,
      routeOffset: 0,
    });
    Object.assign(this.addUnit('escort', this.routePoint(5), true), {
      group: 0,
      routeOffset: 5,
    });
    Object.assign(this.addUnit('escort', this.routePoint(10), true), {
      group: 0,
      routeOffset: 10,
    });
    FRIENDLY_POSTS.forEach((p, i) => {
      Object.assign(this.addUnit('escort', p, true), { group: i + 1 });
      Object.assign(this.addUnit('escort', { x: p.x + 3, z: p.z + 2 }, true), {
        group: i + 1,
      });
      Object.assign(this.addUnit('escort', { x: p.x - 3, z: p.z + 3 }, true), {
        group: i + 1,
      });
    });
  }
  height = (x: number, z: number) => terrainHeight(x, z, this.mission.map);
  get routeLength() {
    return routeLength(this.mission.map);
  }
  get holdPoints() {
    return [this.routeLength * 0.28, this.routeLength * 0.64];
  }
  routePoint(d: number) {
    return routePoint(d, this.mission.map);
  }
  get paused() {
    return this.pauses.size > 0;
  }
  get rescue() {
    return this.units[0];
  }
  get remaining() {
    return Math.max(0, this.mission.duration - this.time);
  }
  get ratio() {
    return this.progress / this.routeLength;
  }
  get threatsRemaining() {
    return (
      this.mission.events.length -
      this.spawned.size +
      this.units.filter((u) => !u.friendly && u.hp > 0).length
    );
  }
  get friendlyLosses() {
    return this.units.filter((u) => u.friendly && u.hp <= 0).length;
  }
  get canConfigure() {
    return (
      this.phase === 'playing' &&
      (!this.paused ||
        (this.pauses.has('settings') &&
          Array.from(this.pauses).every(
            (reason) => reason === 'settings' || reason === 'manual',
          )))
    );
  }
  setOrbitDirection(direction: -1 | 1) {
    if (this.canConfigure) this.flight.setDirection(direction, this.paused);
  }
  adjustAltitude(delta: number) {
    if (this.canConfigure) this.flight.adjustAltitude(delta, this.paused);
  }
  adjustRadius(delta: number) {
    if (this.canConfigure) this.flight.adjustRadius(delta, this.paused);
  }
  flightTime(weapon = this.selected, p: Point | null = this.aim) {
    if (
      !p ||
      !Number.isInteger(weapon) ||
      !WEAPONS[weapon] ||
      ![p.x, p.z].every(Number.isFinite) ||
      Math.abs(p.x) > MAP.halfWidth ||
      Math.abs(p.z) > MAP.halfDepth
    )
      return Infinity;
    return (
      ballisticLaunch(
        muzzlePosition(this.aircraft, weapon),
        p,
        WEAPONS[weapon].speed,
        this.height,
      )?.duration ?? Infinity
    );
  }
  shotPosition(shot: Shot, time = this.time) {
    if (shot.guidance) {
      const trail = shot.guidance.trail;
      const after =
        trail.find((p) => p.time >= time) ?? trail[trail.length - 1];
      const before = trail[Math.max(0, trail.indexOf(after) - 1)];
      const fraction = Math.max(
        0,
        Math.min(1, (time - before.time) / (after.time - before.time || 1)),
      );
      return {
        x: before.x + (after.x - before.x) * fraction,
        y: before.y + (after.y - before.y) * fraction,
        z: before.z + (after.z - before.z) * fraction,
      };
    }
    return shotPosition(shot, time);
  }
  addUnit(kind: Kind, p: Point, friendly = false) {
    const u: Unit = {
      ...p,
      y: this.height(p.x, p.z),
      id: ++this.serial,
      kind,
      hp: UNITS[kind].hp,
      maxHp: UNITS[kind].hp,
      friendly,
      heading: Math.PI / 2,
      born: this.time,
      attack: this.mission.warmup,
      hit: 0,
      deadAt: -1,
      origin: { x: p.x, z: p.z, patrolId: this.serial },
    };
    if (kind === 'light') {
      Object.assign(u, patrolPoint(u.origin, 0, this.mission.map));
      u.patrolElapsed = 0;
      u.y = this.height(u.x, u.z);
    }
    this.units.push(u);
    return u;
  }
  start() {
    if (this.phase === 'briefing') {
      this.phase = 'playing';
      this.spawn();
    }
  }
  pause(reason: PauseReason, on: boolean) {
    if (on) {
      if (reason !== 'countdown' && this.pauses.has('countdown')) {
        this.resumeCountdown = 0;
        this.pauses.delete('countdown');
        this.pauses.add('manual');
        this.resumeRequired = true;
      }
      this.pauses.add(reason);
      this.clearInput();
    } else {
      this.pauses.delete(reason);
      if (reason === 'countdown') this.resumeCountdown = 0;
      if (reason === 'manual') this.resumeRequired = false;
    }
  }
  grantBuff(kind: BuffKind): boolean {
    if (
      this.buff ||
      (kind === 'tracking' && this.trackingActive) ||
      (kind !== 'tracking' && kind !== 'rate')
    )
      return false;
    this.buff = { kind, remaining: COMBAT_BUFF.duration };
    return true;
  }
  grantHomingAmmo(amount: number) {
    if (
      !Number.isSafeInteger(amount) ||
      amount <= 0 ||
      !Number.isSafeInteger(this.homingAmmo + amount)
    )
      throw new Error('Invalid homing ammo grant');
    this.homingAmmo += amount;
  }
  grantAmmoSupply() {
    this.guns[1].ammo += 60;
    this.guns[2].ammo += 24;
    this.guns[3].ammo += 6;
  }
  beginResumeCountdown() {
    if (this.phase !== 'playing') return;
    if (this.pauses.has('background') || this.pauses.has('focus')) {
      this.pause('countdown', false);
      this.pause('manual', true);
      this.resumeRequired = true;
      return;
    }
    this.pauses.delete('manual');
    this.resumeRequired = false;
    this.resumeCountdown = 3;
    this.pause('countdown', true);
  }
  stepCountdown(dt: number) {
    if (!Number.isFinite(dt) || dt <= 0 || dt > 0.1)
      throw Error('Use fixed steps <= 0.1 seconds');
    if (
      this.phase !== 'playing' ||
      this.pauses.size !== 1 ||
      !this.pauses.has('countdown')
    )
      return;
    this.resumeCountdown = Math.max(0, this.resumeCountdown - dt);
    if (this.resumeCountdown <= 1e-8) this.pause('countdown', false);
  }
  clearInput() {
    this.held.clear();
  }
  choose(index: number) {
    if (!Number.isInteger(index) || index < 0 || index >= WEAPONS.length)
      return;
    if (!this.hasWeapon(index)) return;
    this.clearInput();
    if (index !== this.selected) this.completed.add('weapon');
    this.selected = index;
    this.homingSelected = false;
    this.homingTargetId = undefined;
  }
  hasWeapon(index: number) {
    return (
      (index >= 0 && index < 4) ||
      (index === 4 && this.loadout.effects.includes('drone')) ||
      (index === 5 && this.loadout.effects.includes('laser'))
    );
  }
  selectHoming() {
    this.clearInput();
    this.homingSelected = true;
    this.homingTargetId = undefined;
  }
  get homingTarget() {
    const target =
      this.homingTargetId === undefined
        ? this.aimedUnit
        : this.units.find((u) => u.id === this.homingTargetId && u.hp > 0);
    return target &&
      !target.friendly &&
      !PROTECTED.some(
        (p) => distance(p, target) <= p.radius + UNITS[target.kind].radius,
      )
      ? target
      : undefined;
  }
  lockHoming(id: number) {
    this.homingTargetId = id;
    const target = this.homingTarget;
    if (target) this.aim = { x: target.x, z: target.z };
    return !!target;
  }
  setAim(p: Point) {
    if (!Number.isFinite(p.x) || !Number.isFinite(p.z)) return;
    if (distance(p, this.aim) > 0.2) this.completed.add('aim');
    this.aim = { x: p.x, z: p.z };
    this.homingTargetId = undefined;
  }
  setFire(source: string, on: boolean) {
    if (!on) {
      this.held.delete(source);
      return;
    }
    if (
      this.phase !== 'playing' ||
      this.paused ||
      this.extractionRemaining !== undefined ||
      this.held.has(source)
    )
      return;
    const pressed = this.held.size === 0;
    this.held.add(source);
    if (pressed) this.fire();
  }
  command() {
    if (
      this.mission.mode === 'training' ||
      !this.canConfigure ||
      this.convoy === 'arrived' ||
      this.extractionRemaining !== undefined
    )
      return;
    if (this.convoy === 'moving') {
      if (!this.holdPoints.some((p) => p > this.progress + 0.01)) return;
      this.convoy = 'holdRequested';
      this.completed.add('hold');
    } else {
      if (this.convoy === 'holding') this.completed.add('continue');
      this.convoy = 'moving';
    }
  }
  setExtractionRadioPending(pending: boolean) {
    if (this.phase === 'playing') this.extractionRadioPending = pending;
  }
  private attackTargets(): Point[] {
    if (this.attackCount === 1) return [this.aim];
    const origin = muzzlePosition(this.aircraft, this.selected);
    const angle = Math.atan2(this.aim.z - origin.z, this.aim.x - origin.x);
    const spread = Math.min(
      2.4,
      Math.max(0.35, WEAPONS[this.selected].radius * 0.55),
    );
    const radius = spread * (this.attackCount === 4 ? Math.SQRT2 : 1);
    // Keep the full-strength round on the reticle; alternate the surrounding coverage per attack.
    const phase =
      angle -
      (this.loadout.effects.includes('twin') ? Math.PI / 2 : 0) +
      (this.ammoSpent % 2) * Math.PI;
    return Array.from({ length: this.attackCount }, (_, n) => {
      if (n === 0) return this.aim;
      const direction =
        phase + ((n - 1) * Math.PI * 2) / (this.attackCount - 1);
      return {
        x: this.aim.x + Math.cos(direction) * radius,
        z: this.aim.z + Math.sin(direction) * radius,
      };
    });
  }
  weaponReadiness(index = this.selected) {
    const gun = this.guns[index],
      heatTime = gun.overheated ? Math.max(0, (gun.heat - 40) / 18) : 0;
    const remaining = Math.max(gun.cooldown, heatTime),
      total = Math.max(
        gun.cooldownTotal || WEAPONS[index].interval,
        gun.overheated ? 60 / 18 : 0,
      );
    return {
      remaining,
      displaySeconds: Math.ceil(remaining * 10) / 10,
      total,
      ratio: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, remaining / total)),
      empty: gun.ammo <= 0,
      overheated: gun.overheated,
    };
  }
  reason(): FireReason {
    if (this.phase === 'briefing') return 'briefing';
    if (this.phase !== 'playing') return 'finished';
    if (this.paused) return 'paused';
    if (this.extractionRemaining !== undefined) return 'extracting';
    if (this.homingSelected)
      return this.homingAmmo <= 0
        ? 'empty'
        : this.homingTarget
          ? 'ready'
          : 'target';
    const w = WEAPONS[this.selected],
      g = this.guns[this.selected];
    const targets = this.attackTargets();
    if (
      targets.some(
        (point) =>
          ![point.x, point.z].every(Number.isFinite) ||
          Math.abs(point.x) > MAP.halfWidth ||
          Math.abs(point.z) > MAP.halfDepth,
      )
    )
      return 'outside';
    // The entire blast disk must stay outside a protected area, including its boundary.
    if (
      !this.duelMode &&
      targets.some((point) =>
        PROTECTED.some(
          (p) => distance(p, point) <= p.radius + this.effectiveRadius,
        ),
      )
    )
      return 'protected';
    if (
      this.selected >= 4 &&
      !this.loadout.effects.includes(this.selected === 4 ? 'drone' : 'laser')
    )
      return 'empty';
    if (
      this.selected === 5 &&
      (!this.aimedUnit ||
        !this.canTrack(this.aimedUnit, w.radius) ||
        !this.lineOfSight(this.aimedUnit))
    )
      return 'target';
    if (g.overheated) return 'overheated';
    if (g.ammo < 1) return 'empty';
    if (g.cooldown > 1e-6) return 'cooldown';
    return 'ready';
  }
  get friendlyRisk() {
    if (this.homingSelected) return false;
    const target = this.trackingTarget;
    const points = target ? [target] : this.attackTargets();
    return this.units.some(
      (u) =>
        u.friendly &&
        u.hp > 0 &&
        points.some(
          (point) =>
            distance(u, point) <= this.effectiveRadius + UNITS[u.kind].radius,
        ),
    );
  }
  get aimedUnit() {
    return this.units
      .filter(
        (u) => u.hp > 0 && distance(u, this.aim) <= UNITS[u.kind].radius + 1.2,
      )
      .sort((a, b) => distance(a, this.aim) - distance(b, this.aim))[0];
  }
  private canTrack(target: Unit, radius: number) {
    return (
      target.hp > 0 &&
      !target.friendly &&
      (this.duelMode ||
        !PROTECTED.some(
          (p) =>
            distance(p, target) <=
            p.radius + Math.max(radius, UNITS[target.kind].radius),
        ))
    );
  }
  private get trackingTarget() {
    if (!this.trackingActive || this.selected >= 4) return;
    const radius = this.effectiveRadius,
      aimed = this.aimedUnit;
    if (aimed && this.canTrack(aimed, radius)) return aimed;
    return this.units
      .filter(
        (u) =>
          this.canTrack(u, radius) &&
          distance(u, this.aim) <= COMBAT_BUFF.trackingRadius,
      )
      .sort((a, b) => distance(a, this.aim) - distance(b, this.aim))[0];
  }
  fire() {
    if (this.homingSelected) return this.fireHoming();
    if (this.reason() !== 'ready') return false;
    if (this.selected >= 4) return this.fireSpecial();
    const w = WEAPONS[this.selected],
      g = this.guns[this.selected];
    const preview = attackPreview(this.loadout.effects),
      count = preview.shots;
    const launches = this.attackTargets().map((point, n) => {
      const mount = w.muzzle;
      const origin = Object.freeze(
        n > 0 && this.loadout.effects.includes('twin')
          ? aircraftPoint(this.aircraft, {
              ...mount,
              x: mount.x + (n % 2 === 0 ? -0.6 : 0.6),
            })
          : muzzlePosition(this.aircraft, this.selected),
      );
      return {
        origin,
        point,
        launch: ballisticLaunch(origin, point, w.speed, this.height),
      };
    });
    if (launches.some((item) => !item.launch)) return false;
    g.ammo--;
    this.ammoSpent++;
    g.heat = Math.min(100, g.heat + w.heat * preview.heatMultiplier);
    g.overheated = g.heat >= 100;
    g.cooldown =
      (w.interval *
        (this.loadout.effects.includes('rate') ? 0.88 : 1) *
        (this.loadout.level >= 15 ? 0.95 : 1)) /
      (this.buff?.kind === 'rate' ? COMBAT_BUFF.rateMultiplier : 1);
    g.cooldownTotal = g.cooldown;
    const target = this.trackingTarget;
    for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {
      const { origin, point, launch } = launches[n];
      const delay = this.loadout.effects.includes('burst')
        ? Math.floor(n / (this.loadout.effects.includes('twin') ? 2 : 1)) *
          Math.min(0.1, g.cooldown / 2)
        : 0;
      const strength = n === 0 ? 1 : preview.secondaryStrength;
      const shot: Shot = {
        id: ++this.serial,
        ...(this.duelMode ? { side: this.pilotSide } : {}),
        weapon: this.selected,
        x: point.x,
        z: point.z,
        born: this.time + delay,
        origin,
        velocity: launch!.velocity,
        targetY: launch!.targetY,
        due: this.time + delay + launch!.duration,
        strength,
        damageMultiplier: this.powerMultiplier * strength,
        radiusScale: this.radiusScale(),
        trackingUntil: Math.max(
          this.buff?.kind === 'tracking' ? this.time + this.buff.remaining : 0,
          this.skillKind === 'tracking' ? this.skill.activeUntil : 0,
        ),
      };
      if (target)
        shot.guidance = {
          kind: 'tracking',
          target: target.id,
          position: { ...origin },
          trail: [{ ...origin, time: shot.born }],
        };
      else {
        Object.freeze(shot.velocity);
        Object.freeze(shot);
      }
      this.shots.push(shot);
      if (!delay) this.emit('shot', shot, shot.weapon);
      else this.pendingShotEvents.push(shot.id);
      this.fired++;
    }
    this.completed.add('fire');
    return true;
  }
  private lineOfSight(
    target: Point3,
    origin = muzzlePosition(this.aircraft, this.selected),
    maximum = 650,
  ) {
    const range = Math.hypot(
      target.x - origin.x,
      target.y - origin.y,
      target.z - origin.z,
    );
    if (!Number.isFinite(range) || range > maximum) return false;
    const samples = Math.max(2, Math.ceil(range / 0.5));
    for (let i = 1; i < samples; i++) {
      const t = i / samples,
        x = origin.x + (target.x - origin.x) * t,
        z = origin.z + (target.z - origin.z) * t,
        y = origin.y + (target.y - origin.y) * t;
      if (y <= this.height(x, z) + 0.05) return false;
    }
    return true;
  }
  private fireSpecial() {
    const weapon = this.selected,
      g = this.guns[weapon],
      origin = muzzlePosition(this.aircraft, weapon);
    if (weapon === 4) {
      if (
        this.drones.filter((d) => d.side === this.pilotSide).length >=
        DRONE.count
      )
        return false;
      const volley = ++this.serial;
      for (let i = 0; i < DRONE.count; i++) {
        const angle = (i * Math.PI * 2) / DRONE.count;
        this.drones.push({
          ...origin,
          x: origin.x + Math.cos(angle) * 6,
          z: origin.z + Math.sin(angle) * 6,
          id: ++this.serial,
          volley,
          hitCounted: false,
          side: this.pilotSide,
          expires: this.time + DRONE.duration,
          budget: (DRONE.damage * DRONE.duration) / DRONE.interval,
          nextAttack: this.time + DRONE.interval + i * 0.12,
        });
      }
      this.emit('shot', origin, weapon);
    } else {
      if (this.lasers.some((beam) => beam.side === this.pilotSide))
        return false;
      const target = this.aimedUnit!;
      this.lasers.push({
        id: ++this.serial,
        side: this.pilotSide,
        targetId: target.id,
        origin: { ...origin },
        remaining: LASER.duration,
        budget: WEAPONS[weapon].damage,
        hitCounted: false,
      });
      this.emit('shot', origin, weapon).target = {
        x: target.x,
        y: target.y,
        z: target.z,
      };
    }
    g.ammo--;
    g.cooldown = g.cooldownTotal = WEAPONS[weapon].interval;
    this.fired++;
    this.ammoSpent++;
    this.completed.add('fire');
    return true;
  }
  private directHit(
    target: Unit,
    damage: number,
    weapon: number,
    countShot = true,
    continuous = false,
  ) {
    const actual = Math.min(target.hp, damage);
    target.hp -= actual;
    target.hit = 0.3;
    if (!continuous || countShot) this.hits++;
    if (countShot) this.hitShots++;
    if (!continuous || countShot || target.hp <= 0) {
      const event = this.emit('impact', target, weapon, target.id);
      event.damage = actual;
      event.outcome = target.hp > 0 ? 'hit' : 'destroyed';
    }
    if (target.hp <= 0) {
      target.deadAt = this.time;
      this.kills++;
      this.emit('kill', target, weapon, target.id);
    }
  }
  private updateLaserOrigin() {
    for (const beam of this.lasers)
      if (beam.side === this.pilotSide)
        beam.origin = { ...muzzlePosition(this.aircraft, 5) };
  }
  private stepLasers(dt: number) {
    const side = this.pilotSide;
    for (const beam of this.lasers) {
      if (this.duelMode) this.useSide(beam.side);
      const target = this.units.find((unit) => unit.id === beam.targetId);
      if (
        !target ||
        target.hp <= 0 ||
        !this.canTrack(target, WEAPONS[5].radius) ||
        Math.abs(target.x) > MAP.halfWidth ||
        Math.abs(target.z) > MAP.halfDepth ||
        !this.lineOfSight(target, beam.origin, LASER.range)
      ) {
        beam.remaining = 0;
        continue;
      }
      const duration = Math.min(dt, beam.remaining, beam.budget / LASER.dps),
        damage = duration * LASER.dps;
      if (duration <= 0) continue;
      beam.remaining = Math.max(0, beam.remaining - duration);
      beam.budget = Math.max(0, beam.budget - damage);
      this.directHit(
        target,
        damage * (target.kind === 'heavy' ? WEAPONS[5].armor : 1),
        5,
        !beam.hitCounted,
        true,
      );
      beam.hitCounted = true;
      if (target.hp <= 0) beam.remaining = 0;
    }
    if (this.duelMode) this.useSide(side);
    this.lasers = this.lasers.filter(
      (beam) => beam.remaining > 1e-8 && beam.budget > 1e-8,
    );
  }
  private stepDrones(dt: number) {
    const side = this.pilotSide,
      claimed = new Map<number, Set<number>>();
    for (const drone of this.drones) {
      if (this.duelMode) this.useSide(drone.side);
      const ground = this.height(drone.x, drone.z);
      if (drone.expires <= this.time) {
        drone.target = undefined;
        drone.y = Math.max(ground, drone.y - 48 * dt);
        if (drone.y <= ground) this.emit('impact', drone, 4);
        continue;
      }
      const assigned = claimed.get(drone.side) ?? new Set<number>();
      claimed.set(drone.side, assigned);
      const target = this.units
        .filter((u) => this.canTrack(u, 0))
        .sort(
          (a, b) =>
            Number(assigned.has(a.id)) - Number(assigned.has(b.id)) ||
            distance(a, drone) - distance(b, drone),
        )[0];
      drone.target = target?.id;
      if (target) {
        assigned.add(target.id);
        const angle =
          ((drone.id - drone.volley - 1) * Math.PI * 2) / DRONE.count;
        const hover = {
            x: target.x + Math.cos(angle) * 6,
            z: target.z + Math.sin(angle) * 6,
          },
          length = Math.max(0.1, distance(hover, drone)),
          travel = Math.min(1, (WEAPONS[4].speed * dt) / length);
        drone.x += (hover.x - drone.x) * travel;
        drone.z += (hover.z - drone.z) * travel;
        drone.y = Math.max(
          target.y + 16,
          this.height(drone.x, drone.z) + 8,
          drone.y - 60 * dt,
        );
        if (
          drone.budget > 0 &&
          distance(target, drone) < 30 &&
          this.time >= drone.nextAttack &&
          this.lineOfSight(target, drone, 40)
        ) {
          const damage = Math.min(DRONE.damage, drone.budget);
          drone.budget -= damage;
          drone.nextAttack = this.time + DRONE.interval;
          target.slowedUntil = this.time + 2;
          const countShot = !drone.hitCounted;
          for (const member of this.drones)
            if (member.volley === drone.volley) member.hitCounted = true;
          this.directHit(target, damage, 4, countShot);
        }
      }
    }
    if (this.duelMode) this.useSide(side);
    this.drones = this.drones.filter(
      (d) => d.expires > this.time || d.y > this.height(d.x, d.z),
    );
  }
  fireHoming() {
    const target = this.homingTarget;
    if (
      this.phase !== 'playing' ||
      this.paused ||
      this.extractionRemaining !== undefined ||
      this.homingAmmo <= 0 ||
      !target ||
      target.friendly ||
      PROTECTED.some(
        (p) => distance(p, target) <= p.radius + UNITS[target.kind].radius,
      )
    )
      return false;
    const origin = muzzlePosition(this.aircraft, 2);
    const delta = {
      x: target.x - origin.x,
      y: target.y - origin.y,
      z: target.z - origin.z,
    };
    const range = Math.hypot(delta.x, delta.y, delta.z);
    if (!Number.isFinite(range) || range <= 0) return false;
    this.clearInput();
    this.homingAmmo--;
    this.ammoSpent++;
    const shot: Shot = {
      id: ++this.serial,
      weapon: 2,
      x: target.x,
      z: target.z,
      born: this.time,
      due: this.time + range / HOMING.speed,
      origin,
      velocity: {
        x: (delta.x / range) * HOMING.speed,
        y: (delta.y / range) * HOMING.speed,
        z: (delta.z / range) * HOMING.speed,
      },
      targetY: target.y,
      guidance: {
        kind: 'consumable',
        target: target.id,
        position: { ...origin },
        trail: [{ ...origin, time: this.time }],
      },
    };
    this.shots.push(shot);
    this.emit('shot', shot, shot.weapon).unit = target.id;
    this.fired++;
    this.completed.add('fire');
    return true;
  }
  emit(
    type: BattleEvent['type'],
    p: Point & { y?: number },
    weapon = 0,
    unit?: number,
  ) {
    const event: BattleEvent = {
      id: ++this.serial,
      type,
      x: p.x,
      y: p.y ?? this.height(p.x, p.z),
      z: p.z,
      weapon,
      time: this.time,
      unit,
      ...(this.duelMode ? { side: this.pilotSide } : {}),
    };
    this.events.push(event);
    if (this.events.length > 64) this.events.shift();
    return event;
  }
  private spawn() {
    let active = this.units.filter(
      (unit) => !unit.friendly && unit.hp > 0,
    ).length;
    this.mission.events.forEach((e, i) => {
      if (
        active < 24 &&
        !this.spawned.has(i) &&
        (this.time >= e.time || this.ratio >= e.progress)
      ) {
        active++;
        this.spawned.add(i);
        this.addUnit(e.kind, landPoint(e, this.mission.map));
        this.lastWave = i;
        this.waveAt = this.time;
        this.emit('wave', e);
      }
    });
  }
  private impact(
    s: Shot,
    point: Point3,
    time: number,
    previous: Point3[],
    dt: number,
  ) {
    if (
      s.weapon === 3 &&
      !isWater(point, this.mission.map) &&
      Math.abs(point.x) <= MAP.halfWidth &&
      Math.abs(point.z) <= MAP.halfDepth &&
      (this.duelMode ||
        !PROTECTED.some(
          (zone) =>
            distance(zone, point) <=
            zone.radius + WEAPONS[3].radius * (s.radiusScale ?? 1),
        ))
    ) {
      this.fireZones.push({
        ...point,
        id: s.id,
        radius: WEAPONS[3].radius * (s.radiusScale ?? 1),
        born: time,
        expires: time + FIRE_ZONE.duration * (s.strength ?? 1),
        side: s.side,
      });
    }
    let hit = false,
      friendly = false,
      armor = false,
      destroyed = false,
      total = 0;
    const fraction = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (time - (this.time - dt)) / dt));
    for (let i = 0; i < this.units.length; i++) {
      const u = this.units[i],
        before = previous[i] ?? u;
      const x = before.x + (u.x - before.x) * fraction;
      const z = before.z + (u.z - before.z) * fraction;
      const y = this.height(x, z);
      if (u.hp <= 0) continue;
      if (u.friendly && this.training) continue;
      const damage =
        s.guidance?.kind === 'consumable'
          ? u.id === s.guidance.target && !u.friendly
            ? HOMING.damage
            : 0
          : impactDamage(
              s.weapon,
              u.kind,
              UNITS[u.kind].radius +
                Math.max(
                  0,
                  Math.hypot(x - point.x, y - point.y, z - point.z) -
                    UNITS[u.kind].radius,
                ) /
                  (s.radiusScale ?? 1),
            ) * (s.damageMultiplier ?? 1);
      if (damage <= 0) continue;
      const actual = Math.min(u.hp, damage);
      u.hp = Math.max(0, u.hp - damage);
      total += actual;
      u.hit = 0.18;
      if (u.friendly) {
        friendly = true;
        this.friendlyDamage += actual;
        this.friendHitAt = time;
        if (u.kind === 'rescue') {
          this.rescueDamage.friendly += actual;
        }
      } else {
        hit = true;
        this.hits++;
        this.completed.add('hit');
        if (u.kind === 'heavy' && s.weapon === 0) {
          this.warning = 'armor';
          armor = true;
        } else this.warning = '';
      }
      if (u.hp === 0) {
        u.deadAt = time;
        if (u.friendly) this.recordFriendlyLoss(u, 'friendly');
        else {
          this.kills++;
          destroyed = true;
        }
        this.emit('kill', { x, y, z }, s.weapon, u.id).time = time;
      }
    }
    if (hit) {
      this.hitShots++;
      this.misses = 0;
      if (this.warning === 'lead') this.warning = '';
    } else if ((s.strength ?? 1) === 1 && ++this.misses >= 7)
      this.warning = 'lead';
    const event = this.emit('impact', point, s.weapon);
    event.time = time;
    event.shot = s.id;
    event.intercepted = time < s.due - 1e-5;
    event.outcome = friendly
      ? 'friendly'
      : destroyed
        ? 'destroyed'
        : armor
          ? 'armor'
          : hit
            ? 'hit'
            : 'miss';
    event.damage = total;
  }
  private releaseTracking(shot: Shot, time: number) {
    shot.origin = { ...shot.guidance!.position };
    shot.born = time;
    shot.guidance = undefined;
    // Rebase the ballistic arc at lock loss; the original muzzle is no longer its origin.
    const duration =
      (shot.velocity.y +
        Math.sqrt(
          shot.velocity.y ** 2 +
            2 * FLIGHT.gravity * Math.max(0, shot.origin.y),
        )) /
      FLIGHT.gravity;
    shot.due = time + Math.max(0.1, duration);
    const contact = terrainContact(shot, time, shot.due, this.height);
    if (contact) {
      shot.due = contact.time;
      shot.x = contact.point.x;
      shot.z = contact.point.z;
      shot.targetY = contact.point.y;
    }
  }
  private trackingContact(
    position: Point3,
    velocity: Point3,
    time: number,
    dt: number,
  ) {
    const at = (t: number): Point3 => ({
      x: position.x + velocity.x * t,
      y: position.y + velocity.y * t,
      z: position.z + velocity.z * t,
    });
    // shortcut: half-unit sweeps match the ballistic terrain test; upgrade together for cliffs/caves.
    const count = Math.max(
      1,
      Math.ceil((dt * Math.hypot(velocity.x, velocity.y, velocity.z)) / 0.5),
    );
    let start = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i <= count; i++) {
      let stop = (dt * i) / count;
      const point = at(stop);
      if (point.y <= this.height(point.x, point.z) + 1e-8) {
        for (let n = 0; n < 24; n++) {
          const mid = (start + stop) / 2,
            p = at(mid);
          if (p.y <= this.height(p.x, p.z)) stop = mid;
          else start = mid;
        }
        const hit = at(stop);
        hit.y = this.height(hit.x, hit.z);
        return { point: hit, time: time + stop };
      }
      start = stop;
    }
  }
  private canMove(unit: Unit, point: Point) {
    const dx = point.x - unit.x,
      dz = point.z - unit.z,
      length2 = dx * dx + dz * dz;
    for (const zone of this.fireZones) {
      const x = unit.x - zone.x,
        z = unit.z - zone.z,
        radius = zone.radius + UNITS[unit.kind].radius;
      const dot = x * dx + z * dz;
      // Escape may cross already-overlapping fire; vehicles outside a disk cannot enter it.
      if (x * x + z * z >= radius * radius - 1e-8) {
        const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, -dot / (length2 || 1)));
        if ((x + dx * t) ** 2 + (z + dz * t) ** 2 < radius * radius - 1e-8)
          return false;
      }
    }
    const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.sqrt(length2) / 0.25));
    for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
      const p = { x: unit.x + (dx * i) / steps, z: unit.z + (dz * i) / steps };
      if (
        Math.abs(p.x) > MAP.halfWidth ||
        Math.abs(p.z) > MAP.halfDepth ||
        isWater(p, this.mission.map)
      )
        return false;
    }
    return true;
  }
  private moveUnit(
    unit: Unit,
    target: Point & { heading?: number },
    limit: number,
  ) {
    const range = distance(unit, target),
      fraction = Math.min(1, limit / (range || 1));
    let next = {
      x: unit.x + (target.x - unit.x) * fraction,
      z: unit.z + (target.z - unit.z) * fraction,
    };
    const engulfed = this.fireZones.some(
      (zone) => distance(unit, zone) < zone.radius + UNITS[unit.kind].radius,
    );
    const direct = !engulfed && this.canMove(unit, next);
    if (!direct) {
      if (!engulfed) return false;
      // Search only a single speed-limited step to escape a newly engulfing fire, including overlapping zones.
      const candidates: (Point & { escape: number })[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
        const angle = (i * Math.PI) / 8;
        const p = {
          x: unit.x + Math.sin(angle) * limit,
          z: unit.z + Math.cos(angle) * limit,
        };
        if (this.canMove(unit, p)) {
          const escape = Math.max(
            ...this.fireZones
              .filter(
                (zone) =>
                  distance(unit, zone) < zone.radius + UNITS[unit.kind].radius,
              )
              .map((zone) => {
                const x = unit.x - zone.x,
                  z = unit.z - zone.z,
                  radius = zone.radius + UNITS[unit.kind].radius;
                const dot = x * Math.sin(angle) + z * Math.cos(angle);
                return (
                  -dot + Math.sqrt(dot * dot + radius * radius - x * x - z * z)
                );
              }),
          );
          candidates.push({ ...p, escape });
        }
      }
      candidates.sort(
        (a, b) =>
          a.escape - b.escape || distance(a, target) - distance(b, target),
      );
      if (!candidates.length) return false;
      next = { x: candidates[0].x, z: candidates[0].z };
    }
    const moved = distance(unit, next);
    if (moved > 1e-8)
      unit.heading =
        direct && fraction === 1 && target.heading !== undefined
          ? target.heading
          : Math.atan2(next.x - unit.x, next.z - unit.z);
    Object.assign(unit, next);
    unit.y = this.height(unit.x, unit.z);
    return direct && fraction === 1;
  }
  private burn(dt: number) {
    const side = this.pilotSide;
    for (const unit of this.units) {
      if (
        unit.hp <= 0 ||
        (unit.friendly && this.training) ||
        (!this.duelMode &&
          PROTECTED.some(
            (zone) =>
              distance(unit, zone) <= zone.radius + UNITS[unit.kind].radius,
          ))
      )
        continue;
      const zone = this.fireZones.find(
        (zone) => distance(unit, zone) <= zone.radius + UNITS[unit.kind].radius,
      );
      if (!zone) continue;
      if (this.duelMode) this.useSide(zone.side ?? side);
      const damage = Math.min(
        unit.hp,
        FIRE_ZONE.damagePerSecond *
          Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(this.time, zone.expires) -
              Math.max(this.time - dt, zone.born),
          ),
      );
      unit.hp -= damage;
      if (unit.friendly) {
        this.friendlyDamage += damage;
        this.friendHitAt = this.time;
        if (unit.kind === 'rescue') this.rescueDamage.friendly += damage;
      }
      if (unit.hp <= 0) {
        unit.deadAt = this.time;
        if (unit.friendly) this.recordFriendlyLoss(unit, 'friendly');
        else this.kills++;
        this.emit('kill', unit, 3, unit.id);
      }
    }
    if (this.duelMode) this.useSide(side);
  }
  step(dt: number) {
    if (this.phase !== 'playing' || this.paused) return;
    if (!Number.isFinite(dt) || dt <= 0 || dt > 0.1)
      throw Error('Use fixed steps <= 0.1 seconds');
    if (
      !this.duelMode &&
      this.buff &&
      this.buff.remaining > 1e-8 &&
      this.buff.remaining < dt - 1e-8
    ) {
      const remaining = this.buff.remaining;
      this.step(remaining);
      this.step(dt - remaining);
      return;
    }
    this.time += dt;
    if (!this.duelMode) {
      this.flight.step(dt);
      this.updateLaserOrigin();
    }
    const previous = this.units.map(({ x, y, z }) => ({ x, y, z }));
    for (const g of this.duelMode ? [] : this.guns) {
      g.cooldown = Math.max(0, g.cooldown - dt);
      g.heat = Math.max(0, g.heat - 18 * dt);
      if (g.overheated && g.heat <= 40) g.overheated = false;
    }
    let convoyBlocked = false;
    if (
      !this.duelMode &&
      this.convoy !== 'holding' &&
      this.convoy !== 'arrived'
    ) {
      let next =
        this.extractionRemaining === undefined
          ? this.progress + this.mission.speed * dt
          : this.progress +
            Math.min(
              (this.routeLength / 3) * dt,
              (this.routeLength - this.progress) *
                Math.min(1, dt / Math.max(dt, this.extractionRemaining)),
            );
      if (this.convoy === 'holdRequested') {
        const stop = this.holdPoints.find((p) => p >= this.progress - 0.001);
        if (stop !== undefined && next >= stop) {
          next = stop;
        }
      }
      convoyBlocked = this.units.some(
        (unit) =>
          unit.friendly &&
          unit.hp > 0 &&
          unit.routeOffset !== undefined &&
          !this.canMove(
            unit,
            this.routePoint(
              Math.min(this.routeLength, next + unit.routeOffset),
            ),
          ),
      );
      if (!convoyBlocked) {
        this.progress = Math.min(this.routeLength, next);
        if (
          this.convoy === 'holdRequested' &&
          this.holdPoints.some((stop) => Math.abs(stop - this.progress) < 1e-8)
        )
          this.convoy = 'holding';
      }
    }
    if (this.extractionRemaining !== undefined && !convoyBlocked)
      this.extractionRemaining = Math.max(0, this.extractionRemaining - dt);
    for (const u of this.units) {
      u.hit = Math.max(0, u.hit - dt);
      if (u.hp <= 0) continue;
      u.attack -= dt;
      if (this.time < (u.rootedUntil ?? 0)) continue;
      const moveDt = dt * (this.time < (u.slowedUntil ?? 0) ? 0.55 : 1);
      if (u.friendly && !this.duelMode) {
        if (u.routeOffset !== undefined) {
          const point = this.routePoint(
            Math.min(this.routeLength, this.progress + u.routeOffset),
          );
          const limit =
            this.extractionRemaining === undefined || convoyBlocked
              ? Math.max(this.mission.speed, UNITS[u.kind].speed) * dt
              : (this.routeLength / 3) * dt;
          this.moveUnit(u, point, limit);
          u.y = this.height(u.x, u.z) + TERRAIN.roadLift;
        } else if (
          this.fireZones.some(
            (zone) => distance(u, zone) < zone.radius + UNITS[u.kind].radius,
          )
        ) {
          this.moveUnit(u, u, UNITS[u.kind].speed * dt);
        }
        continue;
      }
      const spec = UNITS[u.kind];
      if (u.kind === 'light') {
        const elapsed = u.patrolElapsed ?? this.time - dt - u.born;
        const point = patrolPoint(
          u.origin,
          elapsed + moveDt,
          this.mission.map,
          u.id,
        );
        if (this.moveUnit(u, point, spec.speed * moveDt))
          u.patrolElapsed = elapsed + moveDt;
        else u.patrolElapsed = elapsed;
      }
      const target = this.nearestOpponent(u);
      if (
        u.kind === 'heavy' &&
        this.fireZones.some(
          (zone) => distance(u, zone) < zone.radius + spec.radius,
        )
      ) {
        this.moveUnit(u, u, spec.speed * moveDt);
      } else if (u.kind === 'heavy' && target && distance(u, target) > 12) {
        const angle = Math.atan2(target.x - u.x, target.z - u.z);
        const move = Math.min(spec.speed * moveDt, distance(u, target) - 12);
        this.moveUnit(
          u,
          {
            ...landPoint(
              {
                x: u.x + Math.sin(angle) * move,
                z: u.z + Math.cos(angle) * move,
              },
              this.mission.map,
              u.x - riverX(u.z),
            ),
            heading: angle,
          },
          move,
        );
      }
      u.y = this.height(u.x, u.z);
    }
    const contacts = this.shots
      .flatMap((shot) => {
        if (this.time < shot.born) return [];
        const from = Math.max(this.time - dt, shot.born),
          aliveDt = this.time - from;
        if (this.pendingShotEvents.includes(shot.id)) {
          this.pendingShotEvents = this.pendingShotEvents.filter(
            (id) => id !== shot.id,
          );
          const event = this.emit('shot', shot, shot.weapon);
          event.time = shot.born;
          if (shot.side !== undefined) event.side = shot.side;
        }
        if (shot.guidance) {
          const ordinary = shot.guidance.kind === 'tracking';
          const target = this.units.find((u) => u.id === shot.guidance!.target);
          const radius = ordinary
            ? WEAPONS[shot.weapon].radius * (shot.radiusScale ?? 1)
            : 0;
          const legal =
            target &&
            target.hp > 0 &&
            (this.duelMode ? target.group !== shot.side : !target.friendly) &&
            (this.duelMode ||
              !PROTECTED.some(
                (p) =>
                  distance(p, target) <=
                  p.radius + Math.max(radius, UNITS[target.kind].radius),
              ));
          if (
            !legal ||
            (ordinary &&
              this.time >
                (shot.trackingUntil ??
                  (this.buff?.kind === 'tracking' ? Infinity : 0)) +
                  1e-8)
          ) {
            if (!ordinary)
              return [
                {
                  shot,
                  point: shot.guidance.position,
                  time: this.time,
                  cancelled: true,
                },
              ];
            this.releaseTracking(shot, from);
            const contact = terrainContact(shot, from, this.time, this.height);
            return contact ? [{ shot, ...contact, cancelled: false }] : [];
          }
          const position = shot.guidance.position;
          const delta = {
            x: target.x - position.x,
            y: target.y - position.y,
            z: target.z - position.z,
          };
          const speed = ordinary ? WEAPONS[shot.weapon].speed : HOMING.speed;
          const range = Math.hypot(delta.x, delta.y, delta.z),
            travel = speed * aliveDt;
          if (range > 0)
            shot.velocity = {
              x: (delta.x / range) * speed,
              y: (delta.y / range) * speed,
              z: (delta.z / range) * speed,
            };
          if (ordinary) {
            const contact = this.trackingContact(
              position,
              shot.velocity,
              from,
              Math.min(aliveDt, range / speed),
            );
            if (contact) return [{ shot, ...contact, cancelled: false }];
          }
          if (range <= travel)
            return [
              {
                shot,
                point: { x: target.x, y: target.y, z: target.z },
                time: from + range / speed,
                cancelled: false,
              },
            ];
          position.x += shot.velocity.x * aliveDt;
          position.z += shot.velocity.z * aliveDt;
          position.y += shot.velocity.y * aliveDt;
          if (!ordinary)
            position.y = Math.max(
              position.y,
              this.height(position.x, position.z) + Math.min(2, range * 0.1),
            );
          shot.x = target.x;
          shot.z = target.z;
          shot.targetY = target.y;
          shot.due = this.time + (range - travel) / speed;
          shot.guidance.trail.push({ ...position, time: this.time });
          // shortcut: 32 fixed-step samples cover the visible trail; retain more only for flight replay.
          if (shot.guidance.trail.length > 32) shot.guidance.trail.shift();
          return [];
        }
        const contact = terrainContact(
          shot,
          this.time - dt,
          this.time,
          this.height,
        );
        return contact ? [{ shot, ...contact, cancelled: false }] : [];
      })
      .sort((a, b) => a.time - b.time);
    for (const contact of contacts)
      if (!contact.cancelled) {
        if (contact.shot.guidance) {
          Object.assign(contact.shot.guidance.position, contact.point);
          contact.shot.guidance.trail.push({
            ...contact.point,
            time: contact.time,
          });
        }
        const side = this.pilotSide;
        if (this.duelMode) this.useSide(contact.shot.side ?? side);
        this.impact(contact.shot, contact.point, contact.time, previous, dt);
        if (this.duelMode) this.useSide(side);
      }
    this.shots = this.shots.filter(
      (shot) => !contacts.some((c) => c.shot === shot),
    );
    this.stepLasers(dt);
    this.stepDrones(dt);
    this.burn(dt);
    this.fireZones = this.fireZones.filter(
      (zone) => zone.expires > this.time + 1e-8,
    );
    if (!this.duelMode && this.buff) {
      this.buff.remaining = Math.max(0, this.buff.remaining - dt);
      if (this.buff.remaining <= 1e-8) {
        this.buff = undefined;
        if (!this.trackingActive)
          for (const shot of this.shots)
            if (shot.guidance?.kind === 'tracking' && shot.born <= this.time)
              this.releaseTracking(shot, this.time);
      }
    }
    for (const u of this.units) {
      if (u.hp <= 0) continue;
      const intent = this.attackIntent(u);
      if (intent && intent.dueIn <= 0) {
        const { target, damage } = intent;
        target.hp = Math.max(0, target.hp - damage);
        if (target === this.rescue) {
          this.rescueDamage.enemy += damage;
          this.damageByThreat[u.kind] =
            (this.damageByThreat[u.kind] || 0) + damage;
        }
        target.hit = 0.3;
        const event = this.emit('attack', u, 0, target.id);
        event.target = { x: target.x, y: target.y, z: target.z };
        event.damage = damage;
        event.friendly = u.friendly;
        if (this.duelMode) event.side = u.group;
        if (target.hp === 0) {
          target.deadAt = this.time;
          if (target.friendly) this.recordFriendlyLoss(target, 'enemy');
          else this.friendlyKills++;
          const killed = this.emit('kill', target, 0, target.id);
          killed.friendly = u.friendly;
          if (this.duelMode) killed.side = u.group;
        }
        u.attack =
          !this.duelMode && u.friendly
            ? this.mission.friendlyAttackInterval
            : this.mission.attackInterval;
      }
    }
    if (!this.duelMode) this.spawn();
    if (this.duelMode) return;
    if (
      this.mission.mode === 'escort' &&
      this.threatsRemaining === 0 &&
      this.extractionRemaining === undefined
    ) {
      this.extractionRemaining = this.progress >= this.routeLength ? 0 : 3;
      this.convoy = 'moving';
      this.clearInput();
      this.shots = this.shots.map((shot) => {
        if (shot.guidance?.kind === 'consumable') return shot;
        if (shot.guidance) this.releaseTracking(shot, this.time);
        // Rebase at the current position; fast-forward the same remaining arc and terrain sweep.
        return Object.freeze({
          ...shot,
          origin: shotPosition(shot, this.time),
          velocity: {
            ...shot.velocity,
            y: shot.velocity.y - FLIGHT.gravity * (this.time - shot.born),
          },
          born: this.time,
          due: this.time + (shot.due - this.time) / 4,
          timeScale: 4,
        });
      });
    }
    if (
      this.progress >= this.routeLength &&
      this.units.every(
        (unit) =>
          !unit.friendly ||
          unit.hp <= 0 ||
          unit.routeOffset === undefined ||
          distance(unit, this.routePoint(this.routeLength)) < 1e-6,
      )
    )
      this.convoy = 'arrived';
    if (
      this.mission.mode === 'escort' &&
      this.convoy === 'arrived' &&
      this.shots.length === 0 &&
      this.threatsRemaining === 0
    )
      this.completionTime ??= this.time; // Audio may delay the result screen, never the ranked clock.
    if (
      this.rescue.hp <= 0 ||
      this.failedGroup !== undefined ||
      (this.mission.mode === 'training' && this.friendlyLosses > 0)
    ) {
      this.failedGroup ??=
        this.mission.mode === 'training'
          ? (this.units.find((unit) => unit.friendly && unit.hp <= 0)?.group ??
            0)
          : 0;
      this.phase = 'failure';
      this.failure = 'vehicle';
    } else if (
      ((this.convoy === 'arrived' &&
        this.shots.length === 0 &&
        !this.extractionRadioPending) ||
        this.mission.mode === 'training') &&
      this.threatsRemaining === 0
    ) {
      this.phase = 'success';
      if (this.mission.mode === 'escort') this.completed.add('arrived');
    }
    if (this.phase !== 'playing') {
      this.clearInput();
      return;
    }
    if (this.homingSelected && this.homingTargetId !== undefined) {
      const target = this.homingTarget;
      if (target) this.aim = { x: target.x, z: target.z };
    }
    if (
      this.held.size &&
      !this.homingSelected &&
      WEAPONS[this.selected].automatic
    )
      this.fire();
  }
  private recordFriendlyLoss(unit: Unit, cause: 'friendly' | 'enemy') {
    if (this.failedGroup !== undefined) return;
    if (
      unit === this.rescue ||
      (unit.group !== undefined &&
        !this.units.some(
          (other) =>
            other.friendly && other.group === unit.group && other.hp > 0,
        ))
    ) {
      this.failedGroup = unit.group ?? 0;
      this.failureCause = cause;
    }
  }
  private nearestOpponent(from: Unit) {
    let nearest: Unit | undefined,
      range = Infinity;
    for (const u of this.units) {
      if (u.friendly === from.friendly || u.hp <= 0) continue;
      const d = distance(from, u);
      if (d < range) {
        nearest = u;
        range = d;
      }
    }
    return nearest;
  }
  attackIntent(unit: Unit) {
    if ((!this.duelMode && this.mission.mode === 'training') || unit.hp <= 0)
      return;
    const spec = UNITS[unit.kind],
      target = this.nearestOpponent(unit);
    if (!target || spec.damage <= 0 || distance(unit, target) >= spec.range)
      return;
    return {
      target,
      damage: Math.min(
        target.hp,
        spec.damage *
          (this.duelMode
            ? target.kind === 'heavy'
              ? 0.25
              : 0.5
            : target.friendly
              ? this.mission.friendlyArmor
              : target.kind === 'heavy'
                ? 0.25
                : 1),
      ),
      dueIn: Math.max(
        0,
        unit.attack,
        this.mission.warmup - (this.time - unit.born),
      ),
    };
  }
  get priorityThreat() {
    if (this.phase !== 'playing') return;
    return this.units
      .filter((unit) => !unit.friendly)
      .flatMap((unit) => {
        const intent = this.attackIntent(unit);
        if (!intent || intent.dueIn > 8) return [];
        const lethal = intent.damage >= intent.target.hp;
        const critical =
          lethal &&
          (intent.target === this.rescue ||
            !this.units.some(
              (other) =>
                other !== intent.target &&
                other.friendly &&
                other.hp > 0 &&
                other.group === intent.target.group,
            ));
        return [{ unit, ...intent, lethal, critical }];
      })
      .sort(
        (a, b) =>
          Number(b.critical) - Number(a.critical) ||
          Number(b.lethal) - Number(a.lethal) ||
          a.dueIn - b.dueIn ||
          b.damage - a.damage ||
          a.unit.id - b.unit.id,
      )[0];
  }
  get rating() {
    if (this.phase !== 'success') return '—';
    const health = this.rescue.hp / this.rescue.maxHp;
    return this.friendlyDamage === 0 &&
      health >= 0.7 &&
      this.friendlyLosses === 0
      ? 'S'
      : health >= 0.4 && this.friendlyDamage < 60 && this.friendlyLosses <= 2
        ? 'A'
        : 'B';
  }
}
import type { Simulation } from './Simulation.ts';
export type TrainingRecord = {
  time: number;
  fired: number;
  hitShots: number;
  ammoSpent: number;
};
function parse(value: unknown): TrainingRecord | undefined {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return;
  const record = value as TrainingRecord;
  const ammoSpent =
    record.ammoSpent === undefined ? record.fired : record.ammoSpent;
  if (
    Number.isFinite(record.time) &&
    record.time > 0 &&
    Number.isInteger(ammoSpent) &&
    ammoSpent > 0 &&
    ammoSpent <= 1000 &&
    Number.isInteger(record.fired) &&
    record.fired >= ammoSpent &&
    record.fired <= ammoSpent * 4 &&
    Number.isInteger(record.hitShots) &&
    record.hitShots > 0 &&
    record.hitShots <= record.fired
  )
    return {
      time: record.time,
      fired: record.fired,
      hitShots: record.hitShots,
      ammoSpent,
    };
}
export function readTrainingRecord(
  raw: string | null,
): TrainingRecord | undefined {
  try {
    return parse(raw && raw.length < 1000 ? JSON.parse(raw) : null);
  } catch {
    return;
  }
}
export function bestTrainingRecord(
  previous: TrainingRecord | undefined,
  sim: Simulation,
): TrainingRecord | undefined {
  if (
    sim.mission.mode !== 'training' ||
    sim.phase !== 'success' ||
    sim.threatsRemaining ||
    sim.friendlyDamage ||
    sim.friendlyLosses
  )
    return previous;
  const current = parse({
    time: sim.time,
    fired: sim.fired,
    hitShots: sim.hitShots,
    ammoSpent: sim.ammoSpent,
  });
  if (!current) return previous;
  return !previous ||
    current.time < previous.time ||
    (current.time === previous.time && current.ammoSpent < previous.ammoSpent)
    ? current
    : previous;
}
export function trainingNextGoal(sim: Simulation): readonly string[] {
  if (sim.friendlyDamage > 0)
    return [
      '下一轮先看友军与爆炸圈，零友伤才能留下热身纪录。',
      'Next: check allies and blast circles. Only clean runs set a record.',
    ];
  if (sim.threatsRemaining)
    return [
      '固定用榴弹、巡逻留提前量、重甲用重炮；再练一轮。',
      'HE for static, lead the rover, Heavy for armor. Try again.',
    ];
  if (
    sim.ammoSpent > 10 ||
    (!sim.loadout.effects.some(
      (effect) => effect === 'burst' || effect === 'twin',
    ) &&
      sim.hitShots < sim.fired * 0.8)
  )
    return [
      '零友伤完成！下一轮少用一发，或更早预判巡逻车。',
      'Clean clear! Try one fewer round, or lead the rover earlier.',
    ];
  return [
    '精准完成！挑战更快清靶，或回护送保护四组友军。',
    'Precise clear! Beat your time, or escort all four friendly groups.',
  ];
}

  duel: true,
  unrated: true,
  lobby: true,
  noTimeLimit: true,
  durationMs: 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
  takeover: (state, seat) => takeoverDuel(copy(state), seat),
  pollMs: 100,
  modes: [
    { id: 'friend', title: '好友对战' },
    { id: 'match', title: '随机匹配' },
    { id: 'bot', title: '服务器陪练' },
  ],
  roomSize: (mode) => (mode === 'bot' ? 1 : 2),
  initial: (seed, mode) => createDuel(seed, mode === 'bot'),
  advance: (state, elapsedMs) => advanceDuel(copy(state), elapsedMs),
  view: (state, seat) => viewDuel(copy(state), seat),
  action(state, action, elapsedMs, seat) {
    if (
      !action ||
      typeof action !== 'object' ||
      Array.isArray(action) ||
      Object.keys(action).some(
        (key) => !['commands', 'keepAlive'].includes(key),
      ) ||
      !Array.isArray(action.commands) ||
      action.commands.length > 64 ||
      (action.keepAlive !== undefined && action.keepAlive !== true)
    )
      throw Error('Invalid duel action');
    return commandDuel(
      advanceDuel(copy(state), elapsedMs),
      seat,
      action.commands,
    );
  },
  result(state, seat) {
    const sim = viewDuel(state, seat).simulation;
    return {
      participated: sim.ammoSpent >= 3 && sim.hitShots > 0,
      finished: state.finished,
      eligible: state.finished,
      outcome:
        state.winner === null ? 'draw' : state.winner === seat ? 'win' : 'loss',
      score: state.finished && state.winner === seat ? 1 : 0,
      secondary: Math.round((state.ticks * 1000) / 60),
    };
  },
};

export const nightDay = (at: number) =>
  new Date(at + 8 * 3600000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
const cutoff = Date.parse('2026-11-01T00:00:00+08:00');
const fail = (code: string): never => {
  throw new CompetitionError(code, 422);
};
const emptyLoadout = (): Loadout => ({ mods: [], weapon: null, skill: null });
const integer = (value: unknown) =>
  typeof value === 'number' && Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0
    ? value
    : 0;
const canonical = (v: unknown): unknown =>
  Array.isArray(v)
    ? v.map(canonical)
    : v && typeof v === 'object'
      ? Object.fromEntries(
          Object.entries(v)
            .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
            .map(([k, x]) => [k, canonical(x)]),
        )
      : v;
const signature = (v: unknown) =>
  createHash('sha256')
    .update(JSON.stringify(canonical(v)))
    .digest('hex');
