function prepare(
  w: NightAssets,
  owner: string,
  at: number,
  rules: ProgressionRules,
) {
  const day = nightDay(at),
    oldest = nightDay(at - 7 * 86400000);
  w.tasks = w.tasks.filter((t) => t.day >= oldest);
  if (!w.tasks.some((t) => t.day === day)) {
    const seed = createHash('sha256')
      .update(`${owner}:${day}`)
      .digest()
      .readUInt32LE();
    const selected = [
      templates[0]!,
      templates[1]!,
      templates[3]!,
      templates[2 + (seed % 3 === 0 ? 0 : (seed % 3) + 1)]!,
    ];
    w.tasks.push(
      ...selected.map((t) => ({
        ...structuredClone(t),
        id: `${day}:${t.id}`,
        day,
        progress: 0,
        claimed: false,
      })),
    );
  }
  for (const a of ACHIEVEMENTS)
    w.achievements[a.id] ??= { progress: 0, claimed: false };
  const level = rules.levelProgress(w.xp).level;
  for (const [effect, skill] of Object.entries(rules.skills))
    if (level >= skill.level && !w.permanent.includes(effect))
      w.permanent.push(effect);
  for (const reservation of Object.values(w.reservations))
    if (reservation.status === 'reserved' && reservation.expiresAt <= at)
      reservation.status = 'released';
}
function add(w: NightAssets, reward: Reward) {
  w.coins += reward.coins;
  w.xp += reward.xp;
  if (reward.packs) stock(w, 'pack:general:0', reward.packs);
  if (reward.permanent && !w.permanent.includes(reward.permanent))
    w.permanent.push(reward.permanent);
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(w.coins) || !Number.isSafeInteger(w.xp))
    fail('ASSET_LIMIT');
}
function stock(w: NightAssets, id: string, n: number) {
  const value = (w.inventory[id] ?? 0) + n;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 0) fail('INSUFFICIENT_INVENTORY');
  w.inventory[id] = value;
}
function reserved(w: NightAssets, id: string) {
  return Object.values(w.reservations)
    .filter((r) => r.status === 'reserved')
    .reduce((n, r) => n + r.items.filter((i) => i === id).length, 0);
}
function available(w: NightAssets, id: string) {
  return (w.inventory[id] ?? 0) - reserved(w, id);
}
function progress(w: NightAssets, day: string, events: string[], count = 1) {
  for (const task of w.tasks)
    if (task.day === day && events.includes(task.event))
      task.progress = Math.min(task.target, task.progress + count);
  for (const a of ACHIEVEMENTS)
    if (events.includes(a.event))
      w.achievements[a.id]!.progress = Math.min(
        a.target,
        w.achievements[a.id]!.progress + count,
      );
}
/** Read-only legacy compensation preview; legacy JSON and its ledger are never deleted. */
export async function previewNightMigration(tx: Tx | Database, owner: string) {
  const old = await tx.execute(
    sql`select data from runtime.competition_matches where id=${`night-wallet:${owner}`}`,
  );
  const legacy = old[0]?.data as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  const result =
    await tx.execute(sql`select count(distinct match_id)::int as count from runtime.competition_results
    where player_id=${owner} and board like 'night-overwatch:%' and score>0 and created_at<${cutoff}`);
  const players = await tx.execute(
    sql`select created_at from runtime.competition_players where id=${owner}`,
  );
  const historical = Number(result[0]?.count ?? 0),
    veteran = !!legacy || Number(players[0]?.created_at ?? Infinity) < cutoff;
  const legacyPrices: Record<string, number[]> =
    legacy?.version === 2
      ? {
          power: [0, 900],
          velocity: [0, 600],
          duration: [0, 600],
          hePower: [300, 600, 1200],
          heVelocity: [300, 600, 1200],
          heavyPower: [300, 600, 1200],
          heavyVelocity: [300, 600, 1200],
          spread: [300, 600, 1200],
          emp: [7900, 10000, 14000],
        }
      : {
          power: [0, 900],
          velocity: [0, 600],
          duration: [0, 600],
          spread: [300, 750],
          emp: [1200],
        };
  const checkins = await tx.execute(
    sql`select created_at from runtime.competition_matches where board='night-overwatch:private-wallet-v1' and id like ${`night-wallet-event:${owner}:request:%`} and data->>'signature'='checkin'`,
  );
  const signedDays = [
    ...new Set(
      [
        ...(Array.isArray(legacy?.signedDays) ? legacy.signedDays : []),
        legacy?.signed,
        ...checkins.map((row) => nightDay(Number(row.created_at))),
      ].filter(
        (value): value is string =>
          typeof value === 'string' && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value),
      ),
    ),
  ].sort();
  let conversionCoins = 0;
  for (const [id, tier] of Object.entries(
    (legacy?.owned as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {},
  ))
    conversionCoins += (legacyPrices[id] ?? [])
      .slice(0, Math.min(3, integer(tier)))
      .reduce((n, p) => n + p, 0);
  for (const [key, count] of Object.entries(
    (legacy?.trials as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {},
  )) {
    const [id, tier] = key.split(':');
    conversionCoins +=
      (legacyPrices[id!]?.[Number(tier) - 1] ?? 0) * integer(count);
  }
  return {
    version: 'night-return-2026-11-01',
    cutoff,
    legacy: legacy ? structuredClone(legacy) : undefined,
    preservedCoins: integer(legacy?.coins),
    veteran,
    historical,
    conversionCoins,
    signedDays,
    reward: {
      coins:
        (veteran ? 200 + Math.min(10000, historical * 60) : 200) +
        conversionCoins,
      xp: veteran ? Math.min(6000, historical * 40) : 0,
      packs: veteran ? 3 : 1,
    },
  };
}
async function locked(
  tx: Tx,
  owner: string,
  at: number,
  rules: ProgressionRules,
) {
  // Asset lock never acquires another player's identity lock, including two-player settlement.
  await tx.execute(
    sql`select pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(${walletId(owner)},0))`,
  );
  const existing = await tx.execute(
    sql`select data from runtime.competition_matches where id=${walletId(owner)} for update`,
  );
  let w = existing[0]?.data as NightAssets | undefined;
  if (!w) {
    const migration = await previewNightMigration(tx, owner),
      legacy = migration.legacy;
    w = {
      version: 3,
      coins: migration.preservedCoins,
      xp: 0,
      revision: 0,
      inventory: {},
      permanent: [],
      active: {},
      loadouts: { solo: emptyLoadout(), duel: emptyLoadout() },
      signedDays: migration.signedDays,
      tasks: [],
      achievements: {},
      mail: [
        {
          id: migration.version,
          title: migration.veteran ? '老玩家补偿' : '欢迎加入夜航',
          body: `历史积分、进度与累计签到已保留。旧军需转换为新体系资格，按原版本目录价值补为 ${migration.conversionCoins} 金币；原始资产记录保留。附件只可领取一次。`,
          createdAt: at,
          read: false,
          claimed: false,
          reward: migration.reward,
        },
      ],
      videoOrders: [],
      videoDays: {},
      reservations: {},
      legacy,
      migratedAt: at,
    };
    if (legacy) {
      const inventory = legacy.inventory as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
      for (const [id, count] of Object.entries(inventory ?? {}))
        if (integer(count)) {
          w.inventory[`legacy:${id}`] = integer(count);
          if (id === 'coolant') w.inventory['cooling-match'] = integer(count);
        }
      const owned = legacy.owned as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
      for (const [id, count] of Object.entries(owned ?? {}))
        if (integer(count)) {
          const effect = ['power', 'hePower', 'heavyPower'].includes(id)
            ? 'power'
            : ['velocity', 'heVelocity', 'heavyVelocity'].includes(id)
              ? 'rate'
              : id === 'spread' || id === 'duration'
                ? 'radius'
                : id === 'emp'
                  ? 'emp'
                  : null;
          if (effect && !w.permanent.includes(effect)) w.permanent.push(effect);
        }
      for (const [id, count] of Object.entries(
        (legacy.trials as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {},
      ))
        if (integer(count)) w.inventory[`legacy-trial:${id}`] = integer(count);
    }
    await tx.execute(
      sql`insert into runtime.competition_matches(id,board,data,created_at) values(${walletId(owner)},${board},${JSON.stringify(w)}::jsonb,${at})`,
    );
  }
  if (w.version !== 3) fail('ASSET_VERSION');
  prepare(w, owner, at, rules);
  return w;
}
async function save(tx: Tx, owner: string, w: NightAssets) {
  w.revision++;
  await tx.execute(
    sql`update runtime.competition_matches set data=${JSON.stringify(w)}::jsonb where id=${walletId(owner)}`,
  );
}
const publicOrder = (order: VideoOrder) => ({
  ...(order.purpose ? { purpose: order.purpose } : {}),
  id: order.id,
  quantity: order.quantity,
  required: order.required,
  completed: order.completed,
  delivered: order.delivered,
  version: order.version,
  createdAt: order.createdAt,
  ...(order.attempt ? { attempt: order.attempt } : {}),
});
function snapshot(
  w: NightAssets,
  owner: string,
  at: number,
  rules: ProgressionRules,
) {
  const inventory: Record<string, number> = {},
    free: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const [id, n] of Object.entries(w.inventory)) {
    const canonical = id.replace(/^pack:gold:/, 'pack:general:');
    inventory[canonical] = (inventory[canonical] ?? 0) + n;
    free[canonical] = (free[canonical] ?? 0) + available(w, id);
  }
  return {
    playerId: owner,
    version: w.version,
    coins: w.coins,
    xp: w.xp,
    ...rules.levelProgress(w.xp),
    revision: w.revision,
    inventory,
    available: free,
    permanent: w.permanent,
    active: w.active,
    loadouts: w.loadouts,
    tasks: w.tasks,
    achievements: ACHIEVEMENTS.map((a) => ({ ...a, ...w.achievements[a.id] })),
    mail: w.mail,
    announcements: [
      {
        id: 'growth-v1',
        title: '成长与军需上线',
        body: '积分保持原规则。金币、经验独立记录；单人和双人分别配装。技能按冷却重复使用。',
        createdAt: cutoff,
      },
    ],
    videoOrders: w.videoOrders.map(publicOrder),
    signedDays: w.signedDays,
    signedToday: w.signedDays.includes(nightDay(at)),
    day: nightDay(at),
    serverNow: at,
    videoDailyLimit: 12,
    videosToday: w.videoDays[nightDay(at)] ?? 0,
    catalog: rules.catalog,
    packages: rules.packages,
    pools: rules.pools,
    quoteVersion: rules.version,
    adVerification: 'limited-client-callback',
    legacyPreserved: !!w.legacy,
  };
}
function drawPacks(rules: ProgressionRules, pool: string, quantity: number) {
  const weights = rules.pools[pool]?.weights ?? fail('INVALID_POOL');
  const grants: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (let n = 0; n < quantity; n++) {
    let roll = randomInt(weights.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0)),
      tier = 0;
    while (tier < weights.length - 1 && roll >= weights[tier]!)
      roll -= weights[tier++]!;
    const id = `pack:${pool}:${tier}`;
    grants[id] = (grants[id] ?? 0) + 1;
  }
  return grants;
}
function deliverPacks(w: NightAssets, grants: Record<string, number>) {
  for (const [id, count] of Object.entries(grants)) stock(w, id, count);
  return grants;
}
function spend(w: NightAssets, coins: number) {
  if (w.coins < coins) fail('INSUFFICIENT_COINS');
  w.coins -= coins;
}
export async function nightAssets(
  tx: Tx,
  owner: string,
  at: number,
  rules: ProgressionRules,
  command?: AssetCommand,
  development = false,
) {
  if (command?.kind === 'dev-adjust' && !development)
    throw new CompetitionError('DEV_TOOLS_DISABLED', 403);
  const w = await locked(tx, owner, at, rules),
    day = nightDay(at);
  const response = async (lastReceipt?: unknown) => {
    const rows = await tx.execute(
      sql`select data,created_at from runtime.competition_matches where board=${board} and id like ${eventId(owner, 'request:%')} and data->'result'->>'kind'='open' order by created_at desc,id desc limit 20`,
    );
    const openReceipts = rows.map((row) => {
      const result = (
        row.data as {
          result: {
            requestId: string;
            item?: string;
            quantity?: number;
            drops: Array<{ item?: string; coins?: number }>;
          };
        }
      ).result;
      return {
        requestId: result.requestId,
        at: Number(row.created_at),
        item: result.item ?? 'pack',
        quantity: result.quantity ?? result.drops.length,
        drops: result.drops,
      };
    });
    return {
      ...snapshot(w, owner, at, rules),
      devToolsEnabled: development,
      openReceipts,
      wheelTickets: w.wheelTickets ?? 0,
      wheelVideosToday: w.wheelVideoDays?.[day] ?? 0,
      wheelDailyLimit: 5,
      wheelPrizes: rules.wheelPrizes ?? [],
      ...(lastReceipt ? { lastReceipt } : {}),
    };
  };
  if (!command) {
    await save(tx, owner, w);
    return response();
  }
  const cmd = nightAssetCommandSchema.parse(command),
    key = eventId(owner, `request:${cmd.requestId}`),
    hash = signature(cmd);
  const previous = await tx.execute(
    sql`select data from runtime.competition_matches where id=${key}`,
  );
  if (previous[0]) {
    const receipt = previous[0].data as { hash: string; result: unknown };
    if (receipt.hash !== hash)
      throw new CompetitionError('REQUEST_ID_CONFLICT');
    return response(receipt.result);
  }
  if (cmd.kind === 'ad-complete') {
    const completed = await tx.execute(
      sql`select data from runtime.competition_matches where id=${eventId(owner, `ad:${cmd.attemptId}`)}`,
    );
    if (completed[0]) {
      const saved = completed[0].data as {
        orderId: string;
        result: Record<string, unknown>;
      };
      if (saved.orderId !== cmd.orderId) fail('INVALID_AD_ATTEMPT');
      const result = { ...saved.result, requestId: cmd.requestId };
      await save(tx, owner, w);
      await tx.execute(
        sql`insert into runtime.competition_matches(id,board,data,created_at) values(${key},${board},${JSON.stringify({ hash, result, at })}::jsonb,${at})`,
      );
      return response(result);
    }
  }
  let result: Record<string, unknown> = {
    kind: cmd.kind,
    requestId: cmd.requestId,
  };
  if (cmd.kind === 'dev-adjust') {
    const before = { coins: w.coins, xp: w.xp };
    const after = { coins: w.coins + cmd.coinsDelta, xp: w.xp + cmd.xpDelta };
    if (
      !Object.values(after).every(
        (value) => Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0,
      )
    )
      fail('INVALID_DEV_BALANCE');
    w.coins = after.coins;
    w.xp = after.xp;
    result = {
      ...result,
      before,
      after,
      coinsDelta: cmd.coinsDelta,
      xpDelta: cmd.xpDelta,
    };
  } else if (cmd.kind === 'buy') {
    const p =
      rules.catalog.find((p) => p.id === cmd.item) ?? fail('ITEM_UNAVAILABLE');
    if (
      rules.levelProgress(w.xp).level <
      (p.unlockLevel ?? rules.skills[p.effect]?.level ?? 1)
    )
      fail('LEVEL_LOCKED');
    if (
      p.term === 'permanent' &&
      (w.permanent.includes(p.effect) || cmd.quantity !== 1)
    )
      fail('ALREADY_OWNED');
    spend(w, p.price * cmd.quantity);
    if (p.term === 'permanent') w.permanent.push(p.effect);
    else stock(w, p.id, cmd.quantity);
    result = {
      ...result,
      item: p.id,
      quantity: cmd.quantity,
      coins: -p.price * cmd.quantity,
    };
  } else if (cmd.kind === 'checkin') {
    if (w.signedDays.includes(day)) fail('ALREADY_CLAIMED');
    w.signedDays.push(day);
    const reward = {
      coins: 100 + (w.signedDays.length % 7 === 0 ? 100 : 0),
      xp: 40,
      packs: 1,
    };
    add(w, reward);
    progress(w, day, ['checkin']);
    result = { ...result, reward };
  } else if (cmd.kind === 'buy-packs' || cmd.kind === 'ad-order') {
    const quote =
      rules.packages.find((p) => p.quantity === cmd.quantity) ??
      fail('INVALID_PACKAGE');
    if (cmd.kind === 'buy-packs') {
      spend(w, quote.coins);
      result = {
        ...result,
        coins: -quote.coins,
        packs: deliverPacks(w, drawPacks(rules, 'general', cmd.quantity)),
      };
    } else {
      if (cmd.purpose === 'wheel' && cmd.quantity !== 1)
        fail('INVALID_PACKAGE');
      const wheel = cmd.purpose === 'wheel';
      if (
        w.videoOrders.filter(
          (o) => !o.delivered && (o.purpose === 'wheel') === wheel,
        ).length >= (wheel ? 1 : 5)
      )
        fail('TOO_MANY_ORDERS');
      const order: VideoOrder = {
        ...(cmd.purpose === 'wheel' ? { purpose: 'wheel' as const } : {}),
        id: randomUUID(),
        quantity: quote.quantity,
        required: cmd.purpose === 'wheel' ? 1 : quote.videos,
        completed: 0,
        delivered: false,
        version: rules.version,
        createdAt: at,
        ...(cmd.purpose === 'wheel'
          ? {}
          : { grants: drawPacks(rules, 'general', quote.quantity) }),
      };
      w.videoOrders.push(order);
      result = { ...result, order: publicOrder(order) };
    }
  } else if (cmd.kind === 'ad-cancel') {
    const order =
      w.videoOrders.find((o) => o.id === cmd.orderId) ??
      fail('ORDER_NOT_FOUND');
    if (order.attempt?.id === cmd.attemptId) delete order.attempt;
  } else if (cmd.kind === 'ad-start' || cmd.kind === 'ad-complete') {
    const order =
      w.videoOrders.find((o) => o.id === cmd.orderId) ??
      fail('ORDER_NOT_FOUND');
    if (order.delivered) fail('ALREADY_CLAIMED');
    if ((w.videoDays[day] ?? 0) >= 12) fail('DAILY_VIDEO_LIMIT');
    if (order.purpose === 'wheel' && (w.wheelVideoDays?.[day] ?? 0) >= 5)
      fail('DAILY_WHEEL_LIMIT');
    if (cmd.kind === 'ad-start') {
      const playing = w.videoOrders.find(
        (o) =>
          !o.delivered && o.attempt && at - o.attempt.startedAt < 30 * 60000,
      );
      if (playing) fail('AD_IN_PROGRESS');
      order.attempt = { id: randomUUID(), startedAt: at };
      result = { ...result, orderId: order.id, attemptId: order.attempt!.id };
    } else {
      // shortcut: H5 exposes no server-verifiable receipt here; quota/order/time checks limit abuse, not prove viewing.
      if (
        !order.attempt ||
        order.attempt.id !== cmd.attemptId ||
        at < order.attempt.startedAt ||
        at - order.attempt.startedAt > 30 * 60000
      )
        fail('INVALID_AD_ATTEMPT');
      if (at - order.attempt!.startedAt < 10000)
        await recordNightAdAudit(tx, owner, at, 'ad-complete', 'FAST_CALLBACK');
      order.completed++;
      w.videoDays[day] = (w.videoDays[day] ?? 0) + 1;
      if (order.purpose === 'wheel') {
        w.wheelVideoDays ??= {};
        w.wheelVideoDays[day] = (w.wheelVideoDays[day] ?? 0) + 1;
      }
      delete order.attempt;
      progress(w, day, ['video']);
      if (order.completed === order.required) {
        order.delivered = true;
        if (order.purpose === 'wheel') {
          w.wheelTickets = (w.wheelTickets ?? 0) + 1;
          result.tickets = 1;
        } else
          result.packs = deliverPacks(
            w,
            order.grants ?? drawPacks(rules, 'general', order.quantity),
          );
        progress(w, day, ['exchange']);
      }
      result = { ...result, order: publicOrder(order) };
      await tx.execute(
        sql`insert into runtime.competition_matches(id,board,data,created_at) values(${eventId(owner, `ad:${cmd.attemptId}`)},${board},${JSON.stringify({ orderId: order.id, result, at })}::jsonb,${at})`,
      );
    }
  } else if (cmd.kind === 'wheel-spin') {
    if (!(w.wheelTickets ?? 0)) fail('NO_WHEEL_TICKETS');
    const prizes = rules.wheelPrizes;
    if (!prizes?.length) fail('ITEM_UNAVAILABLE');
    const roll = randomInt(prizes!.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.weight, 0));
    let cumulative = 0;
    const prizeIndex = prizes!.findIndex(
        (p) => (cumulative += p.weight) > roll,
      ),
      prize = prizes![prizeIndex]!;
    w.wheelTickets = (w.wheelTickets ?? 0) - 1;
    if (prize.coins) w.coins += prize.coins;
    const packs =
      prize.tier >= 0
        ? deliverPacks(w, { [`pack:general:${prize.tier}`]: 1 })
        : undefined;
    result = {
      ...result,
      prizeIndex,
      coins: prize.coins,
      ...(packs ? { packs } : {}),
    };
  } else if (cmd.kind === 'open' || cmd.kind === 'sell') {
    const p = rules.catalog.find((p) => p.id === cmd.item),
      pack = /^pack:(general|gold):([0-3])$/.exec(cmd.item);
    const keys = pack
      ? [`pack:general:${pack[2]}`, `pack:gold:${pack[2]}`]
      : [cmd.item];
    if (keys.reduce((sum, id) => sum + available(w, id), 0) < cmd.quantity)
      fail('INSUFFICIENT_INVENTORY');
    const consume = () => {
      let left = cmd.quantity;
      for (const id of keys) {
        const take = Math.min(left, available(w, id));
        stock(w, id, -take);
        left -= take;
      }
    };
    if (cmd.kind === 'sell') {
      const unit = pack
        ? rules.pools[pack[1]!]!.packSell
        : (p?.sell ?? (cmd.item.startsWith('legacy') ? 20 : 0));
      if (!unit) fail('ITEM_NOT_SELLABLE');
      consume();
      w.coins += unit * cmd.quantity;
      result = { ...result, coins: unit * cmd.quantity };
    } else {
      if (!pack) throw new CompetitionError('ITEM_NOT_PACK', 422);
      consume();
      const pool = rules.pools[pack[1]!]!,
        tier = Number(pack[2]);
      const drops: Array<{ item?: string; coins?: number }> = [];
      const choices = rules.catalog.filter(
        (p) =>
          p.term === 'match' &&
          p.rarity === tier &&
          p.category !== 'skill' &&
          pool.effects.includes(p.effect) &&
          (p.unlockLevel ?? 1) <= rules.levelProgress(w.xp).level &&
          !w.permanent.includes(p.effect),
      );
      for (let n = 0; n < cmd.quantity; n++) {
        if (!choices.length) {
          const coins = pool.coinDrops[tier]!;
          w.coins += coins;
          drops.push({ coins });
        } else {
          const prize = choices[randomInt(choices.length)]!;
          stock(w, prize.id, 1);
          drops.push({ item: prize.id });
        }
      }
      progress(w, day, ['open'], cmd.quantity);
      result = { ...result, item: cmd.item, quantity: cmd.quantity, drops };
    }
  } else if (cmd.kind === 'activate') {
    const p =
      rules.catalog.find((p) => p.id === cmd.item) ?? fail('ITEM_UNAVAILABLE');
    if (w.permanent.includes(p.effect)) fail('ALREADY_OWNED');
    if (p.term !== '24h' || available(w, p.id) < 1)
      fail('INSUFFICIENT_INVENTORY');
    stock(w, p.id, -1);
    w.active[p.effect] = Math.max(at, w.active[p.effect] ?? 0) + 86400000;
  } else if (cmd.kind === 'loadout') {
    if (!rules.validLoadout(cmd.loadout)) fail('INVALID_LOADOUT');
    for (const id of [
      ...cmd.loadout.mods,
      ...[cmd.loadout.weapon, cmd.loadout.skill].filter(
        (x): x is string => !!x,
      ),
    ]) {
      const p = rules.catalog.find((p) => p.id === id)!;
      if (
        !w.permanent.includes(p.effect) &&
        !(w.active[p.effect]! > at) &&
        !(available(w, id) > 0)
      )
        fail('INSUFFICIENT_INVENTORY');
    }
    w.loadouts[cmd.mode] = structuredClone(cmd.loadout);
  } else if (
    cmd.kind === 'claim-task' ||
    cmd.kind === 'claim-all' ||
    cmd.kind === 'alternate-task'
  ) {
    const tasks =
      cmd.kind === 'claim-all'
        ? w.tasks
        : w.tasks.filter((t) => t.id === cmd.taskId);
    if (!tasks.length) fail('TASK_NOT_FOUND');
    if (cmd.kind === 'alternate-task') {
      const t = tasks[0]!;
      if (t.event !== 'duel' || t.claimed || t.alternate)
        fail('TASK_NOT_REPLACEABLE');
      t.alternate = true;
      t.event = 'complete';
      t.name = '完成 3 次有效行动';
      t.target = 3;
      t.progress = 0;
    } else {
      let claimed = 0;
      for (const t of tasks)
        if (!t.claimed && t.progress >= t.target) {
          t.claimed = true;
          add(w, t.reward);
          claimed++;
        }
      if (!claimed) fail('NOT_CLAIMABLE');
      result.claimed = claimed;
    }
  } else if (cmd.kind === 'read-mail' || cmd.kind === 'claim-mail') {
    const mail =
      w.mail.find((m) => m.id === cmd.mailId) ?? fail('MAIL_NOT_FOUND');
    mail.read = true;
    if (cmd.kind === 'claim-mail') {
      if (mail.claimed) fail('ALREADY_CLAIMED');
      mail.claimed = true;
      add(w, mail.reward);
      result.reward = mail.reward;
    }
  } else {
    const a =
        ACHIEVEMENTS.find((a) => a.id === cmd.achievementId) ??
        fail('ACHIEVEMENT_NOT_FOUND'),
      state = w.achievements[a.id]!;
    if (state.claimed || state.progress < a.target) fail('NOT_CLAIMABLE');
    state.claimed = true;
    add(w, a.reward);
    result.reward = a.reward;
  }
  prepare(w, owner, at, rules);
  await save(tx, owner, w);
  await tx.execute(
    sql`insert into runtime.competition_matches(id,board,data,created_at) values(${key},${board},${JSON.stringify({ hash, result, at })}::jsonb,${at})`,
  );
  return response(result);
}
/** Daily bounded counters contain no token, nickname, IP or raw client payload. */
export async function recordNightAdAudit(
  tx: Tx,
  owner: string,
  at: number,
  kind: string,
  reason: string,
) {
  if (
    !['ad-order', 'ad-start', 'ad-complete'].includes(kind) ||
    ![
      'FAST_CALLBACK',
      'INVALID_AD_ATTEMPT',
      'ORDER_NOT_FOUND',
      'DAILY_VIDEO_LIMIT',
      'ALREADY_CLAIMED',
      'TOO_MANY_ORDERS',
    ].includes(reason)
  )
    return;
  const id = `night-ad-audit:${owner}:${nightDay(at)}:${kind}:${reason}`,
    data = { day: nightDay(at), kind, reason, count: 1, lastAt: at };
  await tx.execute(
    sql`insert into runtime.competition_matches(id,board,data,created_at) values(${id},'night-overwatch:private-ad-audit-v1',${JSON.stringify(data)}::jsonb,${at}) on conflict(id) do update set data=jsonb_set(jsonb_set(runtime.competition_matches.data,'{count}',to_jsonb((runtime.competition_matches.data->>'count')::bigint+1)),'{lastAt}',to_jsonb(${at}::bigint))`,
  );
}
/** Skip rows already held by gameplay: an asset read must never wait room -> identity in reverse. */
export async function reconcileNightLoadouts(
  tx: Tx,
  owner: string,
  at: number,
  rules: ProgressionRules,
  versions: { solo: string; duel: string },
  policy?: { state: string; killEpoch: number },
) {
  const epoch = policy?.killEpoch ?? 0,
    disabled = policy?.state === 'disabled';
  const runs = await tx.execute(
    sql`select id,data from runtime.competition_matches where data->>'kind'='ranked-run-v1' and data->>'game'='night-overwatch' and data->>'playerId'=${owner} and data->>'status'='active' and data->'loadout' is not null and data->'settlement' is null and (data->>'version'<>${versions.solo} or coalesce((data->>'killEpoch')::bigint,0)<>${epoch} or ${disabled}) order by id limit 20 for update skip locked`,
  );
  const rooms = await tx.execute(
    sql`select code,data from runtime.competition_rooms where data->>'game'='night-overwatch' and data->>'status'='playing' and data->>'loadoutBattle' is not null and data->'settledAt' is null and exists(select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(data->'players') p where p->>'id'=${owner}) and (data->>'version'<>${versions.duel} or coalesce((data->>'killEpoch')::bigint,0)<>${epoch} or ${disabled}) order by code limit 20 for update skip locked`,
  );
  const refunds = [
    ...runs.map((row) => ({
      owner,
      battle: `run:${(row.data as RankedRun).id}`,
    })),
    ...rooms.flatMap((row) => {
      const room = row.data as Room;
      return room.players.map((p) => ({
        owner: p.id,
        battle: room.loadoutBattle!,
      }));
    }),
  ].sort(
    (a, b) =>
      a.owner.localeCompare(b.owner) || a.battle.localeCompare(b.battle),
  );
  for (const item of refunds)
    await refundNightLoadout(tx, item.owner, at, item.battle, rules);
  for (const row of runs)
    await tx.execute(
      sql`update runtime.competition_matches set data=jsonb_set(data,'{status}','"finished"'::jsonb) where id=${row.id}`,
    );
  for (const row of rooms)
    await tx.execute(
      sql`update runtime.competition_rooms set data=jsonb_set(data,'{status}','"abandoned"'::jsonb) where code=${row.code}`,
    );
}
export async function reserveNightLoadout(
  tx: Tx,
  owner: string,
  at: number,
  battle: string,
  mode: 'solo' | 'duel',
  rules: ProgressionRules,
) {
  const w = await locked(tx, owner, at, rules),
    prior = w.reservations[battle];
  if (prior && ['reserved', 'consumed'].includes(prior.status))
    return prior.snapshot;
  const loadout = w.loadouts[mode],
    effects: string[] = [],
    items: string[] = [],
    skipped: string[] = [];
  for (const id of [
    ...loadout.mods,
    ...[loadout.weapon, loadout.skill].filter((x): x is string => !!x),
  ]) {
    const p = rules.catalog.find((p) => p.id === id)!;
    if (w.permanent.includes(p.effect) || (w.active[p.effect] ?? 0) > at)
      effects.push(p.effect);
    else if (p.term === 'match' && available(w, id) > 0) {
      effects.push(p.effect);
      items.push(id);
    } else skipped.push(id);
  }
  const snapshot: BattleLoadout = {
    version: rules.version,
    level: rules.levelProgress(w.xp).level,
    effects,
    skipped,
  };
  w.reservations[battle] = {
    snapshot,
    items,
    status: 'reserved',
    expiresAt: at + 24 * 3600000,
  };
  await save(tx, owner, w);
  return snapshot;
}
export async function consumeNightLoadout(
  tx: Tx,
  owner: string,
  at: number,
  battle: string,
  rules: ProgressionRules,
  release = false,
) {
  const w = await locked(tx, owner, at, rules),
    r = w.reservations[battle];
  if (!release && (!r || r.status === 'released' || r.status === 'refunded'))
    fail('LOADOUT_RESERVATION_EXPIRED');
  if (!r || r.status !== 'reserved') {
    // prepare() may have released an expired reservation in memory. Maintenance
    // must persist that release even when there is no consumption left to perform.
    if (release && r?.status === 'released') await save(tx, owner, w);
    return;
  }
  if (release) r.status = 'released';
  else {
    r.consumedItems = r.items.filter(
      (id) =>
        !w.permanent.includes(rules.catalog.find((p) => p.id === id)!.effect),
    );
    for (const id of r.consumedItems) stock(w, id, -1);
    r.status = 'consumed';
  }
  await save(tx, owner, w);
}
/** Only server invalidation calls this; a normal quit never refunds committed inventory. */
export async function refundNightLoadout(
  tx: Tx,
  owner: string,
  at: number,
  battle: string,
  rules: ProgressionRules,
) {
  const w = await locked(tx, owner, at, rules),
    r = w.reservations[battle];
  if (!r || r.status === 'released' || r.status === 'refunded') {
    if (r?.status === 'released') await save(tx, owner, w);
    return;
  }
  if (r.status === 'consumed')
    for (const id of r.consumedItems ?? r.items) stock(w, id, 1);
  r.status = 'refunded';
  await save(tx, owner, w);
}
export async function rewardNightBattle(
  tx: Tx,
  owner: string,
  at: number,
  battle: string,
  rules: ProgressionRules,
  input: {
    mode: 'solo' | 'duel' | 'bot';
    outcome: 'win' | 'loss' | 'draw';
    durationMs: number;
    valid: boolean;
    levelId?: string;
  },
) {
  const w = await locked(tx, owner, at, rules),
    id = eventId(owner, `battle:${battle}`);
  const prior = await tx.execute(
    sql`select data from runtime.competition_matches where id=${id}`,
  );
  if (prior[0]) return (prior[0].data as { receipt: BattleReward }).receipt;
  const valid = input.valid && input.durationMs >= 10000;
  const reward: Reward = valid
    ? input.mode === 'solo'
      ? {
          coins: input.outcome === 'win' ? 180 : 40,
          xp: input.outcome === 'win' ? 120 : 35,
        }
      : input.mode === 'duel'
        ? {
            coins:
              input.outcome === 'win'
                ? 200
                : input.outcome === 'draw'
                  ? 100
                  : 60,
            xp: input.outcome === 'win' ? 140 : 70,
          }
        : {
            coins: input.outcome === 'win' ? 100 : 30,
            xp: input.outcome === 'win' ? 80 : 25,
          }
    : { coins: 0, xp: 0 };
  add(w, reward);
  if (valid)
    progress(w, nightDay(at), [
      'complete',
      input.mode === 'solo' ? 'solo' : 'duel',
      ...(input.outcome === 'win' ? ['win'] : []),
    ]);
  prepare(w, owner, at, rules);
  await save(tx, owner, w);
  const receipt: BattleReward = {
    ...reward,
    totalCoins: w.coins,
    totalXp: w.xp,
    level: rules.levelProgress(w.xp).level,
    status: 'credited',
  };
  await tx.execute(
    sql`insert into runtime.competition_matches(id,board,data,created_at) values(${id},${board},${JSON.stringify({ receipt, input, at })}::jsonb,${at})`,
  );
  return receipt;
}
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { setTimeout } from 'node:timers/promises';
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { openDatabase } from '@coffeeeeffoc/service-kit';
import { assertResourceAuth, playerIdOf, type PlayerAuth } from './auth.js';
import {
  assertGameControl,
  gamePolicy,
  type CompetitionControl,
} from './control.js';
import { publicProfile } from './profile.js';
import { CompetitionError, type Room } from './types.js';
type Database = ReturnType<typeof openDatabase>['db'];
const hash = (value: unknown) =>
  createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(value)).digest('hex');
const seq = z
  .string()
  .regex(/^[1-9][0-9]{0,18}$/)
  .refine((value) => BigInt(value) <= 9223372036854775807n);
const text = z
  .string()
  .max(1000)
  .transform((value) => value.normalize('NFC').trim())
  .refine(
    (value) =>
      Array.from(value).length <= 120 && !/[\p{Cc}\p{Cf}]/u.test(value),
  );
export const chatSendSchema = z
  .object({
    requestId: z.uuid(),
    text,
    roomCode: z
      .string()
      .regex(/^(?:[0-9]{6}|[A-F0-9]{12})$/)
      .optional(),
  })
  .strict()
  .refine((value) => !!value.text || !!value.roomCode);
export const chatReportSchema = z
  .object({ id: seq, reason: z.string().trim().min(1).max(120) })
  .strict();
export const chatModerateSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('kind', [
  z.object({ kind: z.literal('remove'), id: seq }).strict(),
  z
    .object({
      kind: z.literal('mute'),
      playerId: z.uuid(),
      minutes: z.number().int().min(0).max(43200),
    })
    .strict(),
]);
/** A single channel lock gives cursors committed order across processes. No transaction is held while waiting. */
export function createNightChat(
  db: Database,
  now: () => number,
  control?: CompetitionControl,
  version?: string,
) {
  const access = async (auth: PlayerAuth) =>
    db.transaction(async (tx) => {
      const policy = await gamePolicy(control, tx, 'night-overwatch');
      await assertResourceAuth(tx, auth, 'night-overwatch', now());
      assertGameControl(policy, policy?.killEpoch, true);
      return policy;
    });
  const snapshot = async (epoch?: number) => {
    const rows =
      await db.execute(sql`select m.seq,m.body,m.room,m.created_at,p.id,p.display_name,p.profile,r.data as current_room
      from runtime.night_chat_messages m join runtime.competition_players p on p.id=m.player_id
      left join runtime.competition_rooms r on r.code=m.room->>'code'
      where not m.removed and m.created_at>${now() - 86400000} order by m.seq desc limit 100`);
    const messages = [...rows].reverse().map((row) => {
      const ref = row.room as { code: string; createdAt: number } | null;
      let room:
        | { code: string; createdAt: number; canJoin: boolean }
        | undefined;
      if (ref) {
        const data = row.current_room as Room | undefined;
        room = {
          ...ref,
          canJoin:
            !!data &&
            data.game === 'night-overwatch' &&
            data.version === version &&
            (epoch === undefined || (data.killEpoch ?? 0) === epoch) &&
            data.createdAt === ref.createdAt &&
            data.visibility !== 'private' &&
            data.status === 'waiting' &&
            data.players.length < 2 &&
            data.deadline > now(),
        };
      }
      const profile = publicProfile(row);
      return {
        id: String(row.seq),
        playerId: String(row.id),
        nickname: profile.nickname,
        text: String(row.body),
        at: Number(row.created_at),
        ...(room ? { room } : {}),
      };
    });
    return { messages, cursor: hash(messages), reset: true, serverNow: now() };
  };
  return {
    async list(
      auth: PlayerAuth,
      cursor?: string,
      waitMs = 0,
      cancelled = () => false,
    ) {
      const policy = await access(auth),
        until = Date.now() + Math.min(5000, Math.max(0, waitMs));
      let result = await snapshot(policy?.killEpoch);
      while (cursor === result.cursor && Date.now() < until && !cancelled()) {
        await setTimeout(Math.min(500, until - Date.now()));
        result = await snapshot(policy?.killEpoch);
      }
      const latest = await access(auth);
      return latest?.killEpoch === policy?.killEpoch
        ? result
        : snapshot(latest?.killEpoch);
    },
    async send(auth: PlayerAuth, value: unknown) {
      const input = chatSendSchema.parse(value),
        owner = playerIdOf(auth),
        signature = hash(input);
      await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
        const policy = await gamePolicy(control, tx, 'night-overwatch');
        await tx.execute(sql`select pg_advisory_xact_lock(7142902)`);
        await assertResourceAuth(tx, auth, 'night-overwatch', now());
        assertGameControl(policy, policy?.killEpoch, true);
        const previous = await tx.execute(
          sql`select signature from runtime.night_chat_messages where player_id=${owner} and request_id=${input.requestId}`,
        );
        if (previous[0]) {
          if (previous[0].signature !== signature)
            throw new CompetitionError('REQUEST_ID_CONFLICT');
          return;
        }
        await tx.execute(
          sql`insert into runtime.night_chat_limits(player_id) values(${owner}) on conflict do nothing`,
        );
        const limits = (
          await tx.execute(
            sql`select * from runtime.night_chat_limits where player_id=${owner} for update`,
          )
        )[0]!;
        if (Number(limits.muted_until) > now())
          throw new CompetitionError('CHAT_MUTED', 403);
        if (Number(limits.last_sent) && now() - Number(limits.last_sent) < 3000)
          throw new CompetitionError('CHAT_RATE_LIMIT', 429);
        let room: { code: string; createdAt: number } | null = null;
        if (input.roomCode) {
          const rows = await tx.execute(
            sql`select data from runtime.competition_rooms where code=${input.roomCode}`,
          );
          const current = rows[0]?.data as Room | undefined;
          if (
            !current ||
            current.game !== 'night-overwatch' ||
            current.version !== version ||
            (policy && (current.killEpoch ?? 0) !== policy.killEpoch) ||
            current.visibility === 'private' ||
            current.status !== 'waiting' ||
            current.deadline <= now() ||
            current.players.length >= 2 ||
            !current.players.some((p) => p.id === owner)
          )
            throw new CompetitionError('INVITATION_UNAVAILABLE', 422);
          room = { code: current.code, createdAt: current.createdAt };
        }
        await tx.execute(sql`insert into runtime.night_chat_messages(request_id,player_id,signature,body,room,created_at)
          values(${input.requestId},${owner},${signature},${input.text},${room ? JSON.stringify(room) : null}::jsonb,${now()})`);
        await tx.execute(
          sql`update runtime.night_chat_limits set last_sent=${now()} where player_id=${owner}`,
        );
        // Keep request digests for safe old retries, but remove expired public text and invitations.
        await tx.execute(sql`update runtime.night_chat_messages set removed=true,body='',room=null where not removed and
          (created_at<=${now() - 86400000} or seq<(select min(seq) from (select seq from runtime.night_chat_messages order by seq desc limit 100) recent))`);
      });
      return snapshot((await access(auth))?.killEpoch);
    },
    async report(auth: PlayerAuth, value: unknown) {
      const input = chatReportSchema.parse(value);
      await access(auth);
      const owner = playerIdOf(auth);
      return db.transaction(async (tx) => {
        await tx.execute(sql`select pg_advisory_xact_lock(7142902)`);
        await assertResourceAuth(tx, auth, 'night-overwatch', now());
        const old = await tx.execute(
          sql`select 1 from runtime.night_chat_reports where reporter_id=${owner} and message_seq=${input.id}::bigint`,
        );
        if (old[0]) return { reported: true };
        const count = await tx.execute(
          sql`select count(*)::int as n from runtime.night_chat_reports where reporter_id=${owner} and created_at>${now() - 86400000}`,
        );
        if (Number(count[0]?.n) >= 20)
          throw new CompetitionError('CHAT_RATE_LIMIT', 429);
        const row = (
          await tx.execute(
            sql`select * from runtime.night_chat_messages where seq=${input.id}::bigint and not removed and created_at>${now() - 86400000}`,
          )
        )[0];
        if (!row) throw new CompetitionError('MESSAGE_NOT_FOUND', 404);
        await tx.execute(
          sql`insert into runtime.night_chat_reports(reporter_id,message_seq,evidence,reason,created_at) values(${owner},${input.id}::bigint,${JSON.stringify(row)}::jsonb,${input.reason},${now()})`,
        );
        return { reported: true };
      });
    },
    async moderate(value: unknown) {
      const input = chatModerateSchema.parse(value);
      return db.transaction(async (tx) => {
        await tx.execute(sql`select pg_advisory_xact_lock(7142902)`);
        if (input.kind === 'remove')
          await tx.execute(
            sql`update runtime.night_chat_messages set removed=true,body='',room=null where seq=${input.id}::bigint`,
          );
        else
          await tx.execute(
            sql`insert into runtime.night_chat_limits(player_id,muted_until) values(${input.playerId},${now() + input.minutes * 60000}) on conflict(player_id) do update set muted_until=excluded.muted_until`,
          );
        return { applied: true };
      });
    },
    async reports() {
      return {
        reports: await db.execute(
          sql`select * from runtime.night_chat_reports order by created_at desc limit 100`,
        ),
      };
    },
  };
}
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { openDatabase } from '@coffeeeeffoc/service-kit';
import { CompetitionError, type Room } from './types.js';
import { rewardNightBattle, type ProgressionRules } from './night-assets.js';
type Database = ReturnType<typeof openDatabase>['db'];
type Tx = Parameters<Parameters<Database['transaction']>[0]>[0];
export const nightBoard = (board: string) =>
  `night-overwatch:${board}:night-records-v1`;
export const nightLedgerLock = (tx: Tx) =>
  tx.execute(
    sql`select pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('night-overwatch-ledger'))`,
  );
const clearsMigration = 'night-migration:highest-clear-v2';
async function clearsMigrated(tx: Tx) {
  const rows = await tx.execute(sql`select id from runtime.competition_matches
    where id=${clearsMigration} and data->>'status'='complete'`);
  return rows.length > 0;
}
/** Old verified fastest times remain useful; old events never award new points. */
export async function backfillNightClears(tx: Tx, at: number) {
  if (await clearsMigrated(tx)) return;
  await nightLedgerLock(tx);
  if (await clearsMigrated(tx)) return;
  await tx.execute(sql`insert into runtime.competition_best(board,player_id,score,secondary,match_id,updated_at)
    select 'night-overwatch:' || split_part(board,':',2) || ':night-records-v1',player_id,1,min(secondary),min(match_id),${at}
    from runtime.competition_results where board ~ '^night-overwatch:level-[a-z0-9-]+:night-input-replay-v[0-9]+$' and score>0
    group by player_id,split_part(board,':',2)
    on conflict(board,player_id) do update set secondary=least(runtime.competition_best.secondary,excluded.secondary)`);
  await refreshNightClears(tx, at);
  await tx.execute(sql`insert into runtime.competition_matches(id,board,data,created_at)
    values(${clearsMigration},${nightBoard('migration')},'{"status":"complete"}'::jsonb,${at})
    on conflict(id) do update set data=excluded.data,created_at=excluded.created_at`);
}
/** All players only during migration; each new clear refreshes its owner. Caller holds ledger lock. */
export async function refreshNightClears(
  tx: Tx,
  at: number,
  playerId?: string,
) {
  const player = playerId ? sql`and player_id=${playerId}` : sql``;
  await tx.execute(sql`insert into runtime.competition_best(board,player_id,score,secondary,match_id,updated_at)
    select ${nightBoard('clears')},player_id,max(substring(level from '[0-9]+$')::int),0,'night-clears:' || player_id,${at}
    from (select player_id,substring(split_part(board,':',2) from 7) as level from runtime.competition_best
      where board like 'night-overwatch:level-%:night-records-v1' and score>0 ${player}
      union select player_id,substring(split_part(board,':',2) from 7) as level from runtime.competition_results
      where board like 'night-overwatch:clear-%:night-records-v1' and score>0 ${player}) cleared
    where level in ('corridor-01','ambush-02','patrol-03') or level ~ '^operation-(00[4-9]|0[1-9][0-9]|1[01][0-9]|120)$'
    group by player_id
    on conflict(board,player_id) do update set score=excluded.score,updated_at=excluded.updated_at
    where runtime.competition_best.score<>excluded.score`);
}
export async function nightSummary(tx: Tx | Database, playerId: string) {
  const rows = await tx.execute(sql`select count(*)::int as games,
    count(*) filter(where data->>'outcome'='win')::int as wins,
    count(*) filter(where data->>'outcome'='loss')::int as losses,
    count(*) filter(where data->>'outcome'='draw')::int as draws
    from runtime.competition_matches where board=${nightBoard('history')} and data->>'playerId'=${playerId}`);
  const { wins, losses, draws, games } = rows[0]!;
  return {
    wins: Number(wins),
    losses: Number(losses),
    draws: Number(draws),
    games: Number(games),
    winRate: Number(games) ? Number(wins) / Number(games) : 0,
  };
}
export async function nightPoints(
  tx: Tx,
  eventId: string,
  playerId: string,
  points: number,
  at: number,
) {
  await nightLedgerLock(tx);
  const inserted =
    await tx.execute(sql`insert into runtime.competition_results(match_id,board,player_id,score,secondary,created_at)
    values(${`night-points:${eventId}`},${nightBoard('points')},${playerId},${points},0,${at}) on conflict do nothing returning player_id`);
  if (inserted.length)
    await tx.execute(sql`insert into runtime.competition_best(board,player_id,score,secondary,match_id,updated_at)
    select ${nightBoard('points')},${playerId},coalesce(sum(score),0),0,${eventId},${at}
    from runtime.competition_results where board=${nightBoard('points')} and player_id=${playerId}
    on conflict(board,player_id) do update set score=excluded.score,match_id=excluded.match_id,updated_at=excluded.updated_at`);
  const rows = await tx.execute(
    sql`select score from runtime.competition_best where board=${nightBoard('points')} and player_id=${playerId}`,
  );
  return Number(rows[0]?.score ?? 0);
}
export async function settleNightRoom(
  tx: Tx,
  room: Room,
  at: number,
  reason: 'elimination' | 'disconnect' | 'abandoned',
  progression?: ProgressionRules,
) {
  if (room.settledAt !== undefined || !room.startedAt || at < room.startedAt)
    return;
  await nightLedgerLock(tx);
  const matchId = room.id ?? room.code;
  const human = room.players.length === 2 && room.mode !== 'bot';
  for (const member of room.players) {
    const outcome =
      reason === 'abandoned'
        ? 'loss'
        : reason === 'disconnect'
          ? member.takenOver
            ? 'loss'
            : 'win'
          : member.result!.outcome!;
    const points =
      reason === 'abandoned'
        ? 0
        : human
          ? { win: 120, loss: 40, draw: 60 }[outcome]
          : { win: 40, loss: 10, draw: 20 }[outcome];
    if (human) {
      const opponent = room.players.find((other) => other.id !== member.id)!;
      const record = {
        id: matchId,
        code: room.code,
        playerId: member.id,
        opponentId: opponent.id,
        outcome,
        reason,
        startedAt: room.startedAt,
        finishedAt: at,
        durationMs: Math.max(0, at - room.startedAt),
        points,
      };
      await tx.execute(sql`insert into runtime.competition_matches(id,board,data,created_at)
        values(${`night-history:${matchId}:${member.id}`},${nightBoard('history')},${JSON.stringify(record)}::jsonb,${at}) on conflict do nothing`);
    }
    const totalPoints = await nightPoints(
      tx,
      `duel:${matchId}`,
      member.id,
      points,
      at,
    );
    const summary = await nightSummary(tx, member.id);
    if (human)
      await tx.execute(sql`insert into runtime.competition_best(board,player_id,score,secondary,match_id,updated_at)
      values(${nightBoard('win-rate')},${member.id},${summary.winRate},0,${matchId},${at})
      on conflict(board,player_id) do update set score=excluded.score,secondary=0,match_id=excluded.match_id,updated_at=excluded.updated_at`);
    member.settlement = {
      points,
      totalPoints,
      wins: summary.wins,
      games: summary.games,
      outcome,
      reason,
    };
    if (progression)
      Object.assign(
        member.settlement,
        await rewardNightBattle(
          tx,
          member.id,
          at,
          room.loadoutBattle ?? `duel:${matchId}`,
          progression,
          {
            mode: human ? 'duel' : 'bot',
            outcome,
            durationMs: at - room.startedAt,
            valid:
              reason === 'elimination' &&
              member.result?.participated === true &&
              !member.takenOver,
          },
        ),
      );
  }
  room.settledAt = at;
}
export async function nightHistory(
  tx: Tx | Database,
  playerId: string,
  cursor?: string,
) {
  let before: { at: number; id: string } | undefined;
  if (cursor) {
    try {
      before = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(cursor, 'base64url').toString('utf8'));
      if (
        !before ||
        !Number.isSafeInteger(before.at) ||
        before.at < 0 ||
        !/^(?:[0-9]{6}|[A-F0-9]{12}|[a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[1-5][a-f0-9]{3}-[89ab][a-f0-9]{3}-[a-f0-9]{12})$/.test(
          before.id,
        )
      )
        throw Error();
    } catch {
      throw new CompetitionError('INVALID_CURSOR', 422);
    }
  }
  const rows =
    await tx.execute(sql`select m.data,p.id as opponent_id,p.display_name as opponent_name from runtime.competition_matches m
    join runtime.competition_players p on p.id::text=m.data->>'opponentId'
    where m.board=${nightBoard('history')} and m.data->>'playerId'=${playerId}
    ${before ? sql`and (m.created_at,m.data->>'id') < (${before.at},${before.id})` : sql``}
    order by m.created_at desc,m.data->>'id' desc limit 21`);
  const matches = rows.slice(0, 20).map((row) => {
    const data = row.data as {
      id: string;
      code: string;
      outcome: string;
      reason: string;
      startedAt: number;
      finishedAt: number;
      durationMs: number;
      points: number;
    };
    return {
      id: data.id,
      code: data.code,
      opponent: {
        id: String(row.opponent_id),
        name: String(row.opponent_name ?? '新玩家'),
      },
      outcome: data.outcome,
      reason: data.reason,
      startedAt: data.startedAt,
      finishedAt: data.finishedAt,
      durationMs: data.durationMs,
      points: data.points,
    };
  });
  const last = matches.at(-1);
  return {
    summary: await nightSummary(tx, playerId),
    matches,
    ...(rows.length > 20 && last
      ? {
          nextCursor: Buffer.from(
            JSON.stringify({ at: last.finishedAt, id: last.id }),
          ).toString('base64url'),
        }
      : {}),
  };
}
